iti smṛtyantaram avalambya gārhasthyād eva saṃnyāsaḥ kartavya ity evamarthaḥ kramabheda iti varṇayanti | tat pūrvasūtreṇaiva pratipāditaṃ uta neti bhūsurair vicāraṇīyam || 3.2 ||
teṣāṃ gṛhastho yonir aprajanatvād itareṣām || 3.3 ||
caturṇām apy āśramāṇāṃ gṛhasthaḥ kāraṇam, aprajanatvād itareṣāṃ brahmacāryādīnām | vaikhānasārthā hetūktiḥ, brahmacāriṇo brahmacaryarakṣaṇād eva siddhatvāt, bhikṣor apy ūrdhvaretastvavidhānāt | evaṃ ca vaikhānasasya sapatnīkatvapakṣe ‘pi brahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam | adhikārād eva siddhe teṣāṃgrahaṇaṃ gṛhasthotpannānām eva teṣāṃ dharme ādhikāra ity evamartham | tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālāḥ pratyavasitāḥ parivrājakatāpasāḥ |
teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayet || iti |
“teṣāṃ gṛhastha eva yonir” ity ukte evakārād evetareṣāṃ brahmacaryarakṣaṇe siddhe evam abhidhānam eṣāṃ punar nivṛttir mā bhūd iti | yadi te gārhasthyaṃ prati nivṛttiṃ bhaveyuḥ tadā te caṇḍālā bhavanti | kutaḥ? udāhṛtaśātātapavacanāt | tatra – caṇḍālā bhavanti | pratyavasitāḥ pratinivṛttāḥ ye parivrājakatāpasāḥ naiṣṭhikavaikhānasāḥ tasmāt teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayed rājā grāmo vety arthaḥ || 3.3 ||
idānīm āśramadharmeṣu krameṇa vaktavyeṣu prāpteṣu sarvāśramasāmyād upakurvāṇasya pūrvam uktvā naiṣṭhikasya krameṇa prāptaṃ vaktum āha |
tatroktaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ || 3.4 ||
tatropanayanaprakareṇa yad agnīndhanādy uktaṃ tad asyāpi bhavatīty atideśaḥ |
nanu ca naiṣṭikasyaivedaṃ nopakurvāṇasyeti kathaṃ jñāyata iti |
ucyate – upakurvāṇasyoktatvāt, gṛhasthabhikṣuvaikhānasānāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt, naiṣṭikasyaiva bhavatīti |
tarhi brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ kim artham iti cet, yenopakurvāṇavratam anuṣṭhitaṃ tasyaiva naiṣṭhikatvasyādhikāra ity evamartham || 3.4 ||
atideśāt upakurvāṇavratam eva naiṣṭhikasyāpy anuṣṭhātavyam ity uktvā idānīm asya viśeṣavidhim āha |
ācāryādhīnatvam āntam || 3.5 ||
ācāryapāratantryaṃ taccittānuvidhāyitvaṃ ā maraṇāt kartavyam || 3.5 ||
kiṃ ca |
guroḥ karmaśeṣeṇa japet || 3.6 ||
guruśuśrūṣātiriktakāle japaṃ kuryāt | evaṃ ca japa evāsya viśiṣṭaṃ dharmasādhanam iti | ācārya iti prakṛte gurugrahaṇaṃ mātāpitror api śuśrūṣāprāpaṇārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ tapa ucyate |
na tair anabhyanujñāto dharmam anyaṃ samācaret || [MDh 2.229]
iti || 3.6 ||
gurvabhāve tadapatye vṛttiḥ || 3.7 ||
guror abhāve tadapatye śuśrūṣā, adhyāpayitur abhāve tadapatye śuśrūṣeti niyamārtham | gurugrahaṇaṃ samānajātīyāpatyagrahaṇārtham | apatyagrahaṇaṃ bhāryānivṛttyartham
|| 3.7 ||
tadabhāve vṛddhe sabrahmacāriṇy agnau vā || 3.8 ||
tadabhāve guruputrābhāve vidyādibhir vṛddhe, tadabhāve sabrahmacāriṇi samānacaraṇe, tadabhāve agnau | agneḥ śuśrūṣā jyotīrūpasyāgner dhyānam || 3.8 ||
kiṃ punar asyāśramasya phalam ity ata āha |
evaṃvṛtto brahmalokam avāpnoti jitendriyaḥ || 3.9 ||
evaṃ yathopadeśaṃ vartamāno brahmalokaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sthānam avāpnoti, sa cej jitendriyo bhavati | brahmacaryarakṣaṇopadeśāt upasthendriyasyoktatvāt idaṃ cakṣurādiviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.98]
uttareṣām apy etad eva pradhānam ity evamarthaṃ phalāvasāne vidhānam || 3.9 ||
idānīm anyeṣām api sāmānyadharmam āha |
uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi || 3.10 ||
yad brahmacāriṇa uktaṃ tad uttareṣām api bhavati, yad aviruddhaṃ dyūtādivarjanam | yad virudhyate tad vyāvartate, yathāgnikāryaṃ pravrajitasya, gurukulavāso vaikhānasasya, gṛhasthasya brahmacaryarakṣaṇam | caśabdaḥ uttarasya yad vakṣyamāṇaṃ tad apy aviruddhaṃ pūrveṣām api bhavatīti jñāpayati, yathā “na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha sambhāṣeta” [GDh 9.17] ityādi || 3.10 ||
anicayo bhikṣuḥ || 3.11 ||
idānīṃ kramaprāptasya gṛhasthasya dharmā vaktavyāḥ, tān vilaṅghya kimarthaṃ bhikṣor abhidhīyata iti cet, atrocyate-– bahuvaktavyo gṛhasthaḥ | tasyābhidhīyamānāḥ dharmāḥ aviruddhāḥ kecit kathaṃ nāma pūrveṣāṃ syur iti tadarthaṃ vyutkrameṇābhidhānam | tarhi vaikhānasasya vaktavya iti cet, upanyāsakrameṇoktatvād adoṣaḥ |
ato bhikṣos tāvad ucyate | anicayaśabdenaupacayikadravyasya tyāga ucyate, na nityadravyasya śikhāyajñopavītādeḥ |
nanu ca nityasyaiva tyāgo yuktaḥ | upavītaśabdena nyāsaviśeṣo ‘bhidhīyate –
uddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇāv upavīty ucyate budhaiḥ | iti | [MDh 2.63]
yajñārtham upavītaṃ yajñopavītam | asya yajñādhikārābhāvāt |
na kuṇḍyāṃ nodake saṅgo na cele na tripuṣkare |
nāgāre nāsane nānne yasya syān mokṣavit tu saḥ || [VaDh 10.23]
iti kuṇḍyādīnāṃ tyāgopadeśāt | jābālaśrutāv api: “nakhāni nikṛtya yajñopavītaṃ visṛjya” iti yajñopavītasya tyāgopadeśāc ceti |
ucyate – prathamaṃ tāvad yad uktaṃ yajñādhikārābhāvāt iti tatra yāgaśabdo devapūjanādāv api vartate | devapūjanaṃ tv asya dhyānena paramātmapūjanām asty eva | kutaḥ?
araṇyanityasya jitendriyasya
sarvātmakaprītinivartakasya |
adhyātmacintāgatamānasasya
dhruvā hy anāvṛttir upekṣakasya || [VaDh 10.17]
iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt, ātmayāga evādhikārāt | tathā ca śrūyate śrutau: “dharmādharmau havir manaḥ sruvaḥ prāṇo ‘dhvaryur indriyāṇy udgātāraḥ śabdādayo hotā etai ṛtvigbhis tena sruveṇa tad dhavir ahar ahar yatinā paramātmany agnau hotavyam” iti | ato yāgādhikārasadbhāvād adoṣaḥ | yac coktam: “na kuṇḍyāṃ nodake saṅgaḥ” [VaDh 10.23] iti tatrāpi kuṇḍikādīnāṃ vaicitryavarjanārthaḥ saṅgasyaiva parityāgaḥ, na kuṇḍikādeḥ | kutaḥ? tatraiva “yajñopavīty udakakamaṇḍaluhastaḥ” [VaDh 10.31] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tatra “sarvāśramāṇāṃ dharmiṣṭhaṃ” [VaDh 10.30] iti sarvāśramādhikārāt sāmānyatvena vartamāno ‘pi viśeṣadharmeṇa bādhyata iti cet, na, yadi sarvāśramādhikāras tatreṣyate | tatra prakaraṇe vākyārthavākyoccāraṇa-ghaṭanārthaṃ[18] “yajñopavītyudakakamaṇḍaluhastatvam” iti bhāvapratyayenaivāvakṣyat “paiśunamatsarābhimānāhaṃkārānārjavātmastavaparagarhadambha-lobhakrodhamohavivarjanaṃ sarvāśramāṇāṃ dharmiṣṭham” [VaDh 10.30] iti pūrvam uktvā tataś cābhāvapratyayena viśeṣoddeśena “yajñopavīty udakakamaṇḍaluhastaḥ” [VaDh 10.31] iti nirdeśaḥ sarvāśramādhikāranivṛttyartha iti jñātuṃ śakyate | tatas tatra sarvāśramādhikārābhāvāt pūrvāparavirodhaprasaṅgo mā bhūd iti “na kuṇḍyāṃ nodake saṅgaḥ” [VaDh 10.23] iti tatrāpi[19] saṅgamātrasyaiva tyāgaḥ kriyata iti grahītuṃ śakyata eveti | tadartha eva saṅgaśabdasyāpi nirdeśaḥ | tasmāt tenāpi na kuṇḍyādīnāṃ tyāgaḥ | yac coktam – “nakhāni nikṛtya yajñopavītaṃ visṛjya” iti tatrāpi prathamaṃ saṃnyāsāśramapraveśakāle purāṇasya yajñopavītasya tyāgamātraṃ kriyate, na tadānīṃ gṛhyamāṇasya pratiṣedhaḥ | kutaḥ? “nakhāni nikṛtya purāṇaṃ vastraṃ yajñopavītaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ tyaktvā navāni gṛhītvāśramaṃ praviśet” iti smṛtyantaraśravaṇāt | tasmād aupacayikataddravyasya tyāgaḥ[20] |
kiṃ ca – nityadravyatyāge bhikṣor ācamanābhāvaś ca prāpnoti, “ācamane yajñopavīti” [ĀpDh 1.15.1] ityārambhāt, tadabhāve ācamanasyāpy abhāvaḥ prāpnotīti |
“muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā” [GDh 3.22] iti muṇḍapakṣe ācamanāṅgaśikhāvarjitasyāpi ācamanaṃ yuṣmābhir abhyupagamyate, tadvad asmābhir apīti cet –
na, tatra vacanād adoṣaḥ “muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā” [GDh 3.22] iti | yuṣmākaṃ tu yajñopavītaṃ tyaktavyam iti vacanābhāvād doṣa eveti |
“bhikṣur anicayaḥ” iti vaktavye dharmāsyādāv abhidhānaṃ nityasyāgnihotradārāder api tyāgārtham | evaṃ ca gārhasthyād eva yaḥ pravrajet, tena śrautasya ca smārtasya cāgneḥ dāraputraprapañcayajñādisahitasya tyāgaḥ kartavyaḥ[21] | vaikhānasād eva yaḥ pravrajet, tatrāpi sapatnīkapakṣe prāptasya dārasya śrāmaṇakāgnisahitasya vihitasya[22] pañcayajñāder api tyāgaḥ kartavyaḥ |
kecin naiṣṭhikasyāpy anicayatvavidhānārtham ādāv abhidhānam iti varṇayanti |
tad ayuktaṃ, “guroḥ karmaśeṣeṇa japet” [GDh 3.6] iti japasyaiva pradhānatvenopadiṣṭatvāt, tenaivāgniparigrahāder apy abhāvo jñātuṃ śakyata eveti || 3.11 ||
ūrdhvaretāḥ || 3.12 ||
“uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] ity anena brahmacāriṇaḥ uktena brahmacaryarakṣaṇenāsyāpi siddher ūrdhvaretograhaṇaṃ yathā yathā retasaḥ kṣayo bhavati tathā tathopāyaḥ kartavya ity evamartham | upāyaś cālpānnābhyavahārādi | yathā cāha bhagavān vāsudevaḥ |
viṣayā vinivartante nirāhārasya dehinaḥ |
rasavarjaṃ raso ‘py asya paraṃ dṛṣṭvā nivartate || iti | [BhG 2.59]
tathālpānnābhyavahārābhiprāyeṇa smṛtyantare ‘py uktam “aṣṭau grāsā muner bhakṣaḥ” [BDh 2.18.13] ityādi | evaṃ ca brahmacāriṇaḥ saṃpūrṇabhojane na doṣaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
anaḍvān brahmacārī ca āhitāgniś ca te trayaḥ |
bhuñjānā eva sidhyanti naiṣāṃ siddhir anaśnatām || [VaDh 6.21]
iti || 3.12 ||
dhruvaśīlo varṣāsu || 3.13 ||
varṣāsv iti bahuvacanāt grāmād grāmāntaraṃ caturṣu māseṣu na gacched ity arthaḥ | ekatrāpi vasan na caṃkramaṇaprayāsaḥ syād iti śīlagrahaṇam | anenaivāsyāniketanatvaṃ sidhyati | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “grāmānte devagṛhe śūnyāgāre vṛkṣamūle vā” [VaDh 10.13] iti | śīlagrahaṇaṃ ca kurvann etat jñāpayati, nāvaśyam evaṃ bhavet | tataś ca durbhikṣarāṣṭrabhraṃśādau gacched apīti || 3.13 ||
bhikṣārthī grāmam iyāt || 3.14 ||
bhikṣā bhikṣyamāṇatvād annaṃ vāsaḥ kamaṇḍalvādiḥ, tadarthaṃ grāmaṃ gacchet | arthāc cheṣaṃ kālam araṇye devāyatanādau tiṣṭhed iti siddham || 3.14 ||
brahmacāribhikṣākāle prāpta āha |
jaghanyam anivṛttaṃ caret || 3.15 ||
jaghanyaṃ bhojanakriyātaḥ paścād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiś caret || iti | [MDh 6.56]
anivṛttaṃ yasmin gṛhe pratyākhyātaṃ tasmin dine tad grahaṃ punar na praviśed ity arthaḥ | cared iti smṛtyantaropasaṃgrahaṇārtham | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vidhūme sannamusale ekaśāṭhīparihito ‘jinena vā” [VaDh 10.8] iti || 3.15 ||
nivṛttāśīḥ || 3.16 ||
bhikṣādānābhivādanādāv āśiṣām aprayoktety arthaḥ | “hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī” [GDh 3.25] ity anenaivaital labhyam iti cet, na, kriyārūpatvād anugrahasya, vāgātmakatvād āśiṣaḥ || 3.16 ||
vākcakṣuḥkarmasaṃyataḥ || 3.17 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe[23] “vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ” [GDh 2.28] ity anenaiva siddhe atiśayārthaṃ punarvacanam | ato vāksaṃyamo maunam, anyatra svādhyāyajapapathipraśnadharmayoga-praśnebhyaḥ,
dharmayogaṃ pathipraśnaṃ svādhyāyaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
bhikṣārthaṃ dehivacanaṃ na nindati yater api ||
iti smṛtyantaravacanāt | cakṣuḥsaṃyamaḥ, ātmano vyādher apīkṣaṇaṃ na kartavyam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “upekṣakaḥ sarvabhūtānām” [VaDh 10.29] iti | karmasaṃyamaḥ yāvadvihitānuṣṭhānamātraṃ, kāmye pravṛttir mā bhūd iti || 3.17 ||
idānīm “anicayo bhikṣuḥ” [GDh 3.11] ityanena sarvasyaupacayikadravyasya parityāge sati pratiprasavaḥ kriyate |
kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt || 3.18 ||
kaupīnām iti nagnatocyate | tadāvaraṇamātraṃ vāsaḥ paridadhyāt | evaṃ ca praśastadvijātibhyas tāvanmātrapratigrahe na doṣa | tataś ca,
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo datvā yas tu nivartate |
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca pratigṛhṇāti yaḥ sadā ||
iti smṛtyantare yad doṣasaṃkīrtanaṃ tad asmād anyatreti draṣṭavyam || 3.18 ||
prahīṇam eke nirṇijya || 3.19 ||
eke manyante pratigrahadoṣaparihārārthaṃ prahīṇaṃ yad rathyādau tyaktaṃ tat prakṣālya paridadhyāt nirṇijyeti pratyātmikaśaucaprāpaṇārtham || 3.19 ||
nāviprayuktam oṣadhivanaspatīnām aṅgam upādadīta
|| 3.20 ||
oṣadhayaḥ phalapākāntāḥ | apuṣpeṇa ye phalavantas te vanaspatayaḥ | ubhayagrahaṇena sarve sthāvarā api gṛhyante | teṣām aviprayuktaṃ tato ‘napagataṃ śākhāpatrādi na gṛhṇīyāt | “hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī” [GDh 3.25] ity anena siddham iti cet, tatra jaṅgamavadha eva hiṃseti kecid vadanti | tadabhiprāyeṇoktatvād adoṣaḥ | upaśabdāt prayojayitāpi na syāt || 3.20 ||
dhruvaśīlatvopadeśād varṣābhyo ‘nyatra kāmacāre prāpte āha|
na dvitīyām apartu rātriṃ grāme vaset || 3.21 ||
ṛtuśabdena varṣā evocyante | apagate ṛtau dvitīyāṃ rātriṃ grāme na vaset | ekasmin grāme dinadvayaṃ na vased ity arthaḥ | grāmagrahaṇād araṇye na doṣaḥ[24] | apartugrahaṇād varṣāsu kadācit dvitīyām api vasato na doṣaḥ | varṣāśabdena hemantagrīṣmāv ucyete | tataś ca tasmin varṣākāle ekatraiva vased anyatra na vased ity arthasiddham || 3.21 ||
muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā || 3.22 ||
jaṭilapratiṣedhārtha ārambhaḥ | icchāto vikalpaḥ || 3.22 ||
varjayed bījavadham || 3.23 ||
bījānāṃ vrīhyādīnāṃ vadhaṃ avahananādi na kuryāt || 3.23 ||
samo bhūteṣu || 3.24 ||
sarvaprāṇiṣu madhyasthaḥ syāt ātmaparapakṣavibhāgavarjaka ity arthaḥ || 3.24 ||
hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhī || 3.25 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe “hiṃsām” [GDh 2.23] ity anenaiva siddhe atiśayārtham idaṃ punarvacanam | tataś cājñāte ‘pi prāṇivadhe ahar ahas tatparihārārthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam | tathā cāha uśanā: “ajñātavadhaśuddhyarthaṃ rātrāv ahas trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayet” iti | anugrahaśabdena sneha ucyate | tam api na kuryād ity arthaḥ || 3.25 ||
bhikṣor uktvā idānīṃ vaikhānasadharmam āha |
vaikhānaso vane mūlaphalāśī tapaḥśīlaḥ || 3.26 ||
vaikhānasaśabdaḥ kṛtanirvacanaḥ | so ‘raṇye vasan mūlādyaśanaśīlaḥ syāt | na saṃskṛtam annam aśnīyād ity arthaḥ | tapaḥśīlaḥ śarīrapariśoṣaṇaśīlaḥ | evaṃ ca mūlaphalādy api svalpam evāśnīyāt | smṛtyantaradarśanāt ṣoḍaśakṛtva eva “vānaprasthasya ṣoḍaśa” [VaDh 6.20] iti || 3.26 ||
śrāmaṇakenāgnim ādhāya || 3.27 ||
śrāmaṇako nāma vaikhānasānāṃ śāstram | tenāgnim ādhāya gṛhītvā sāyaṃ prātar homaṃ kuryād iti śeṣaḥ || 3.27 ||
agrāmyabhojī || 3.28 ||
mūlaphalam api grāmyaṃ na bhakṣayet || 3.28 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtarṣipūjakaḥ || 3.29 ||
grāmyānnasādhanāḥ gṛhasthasya pañca mahāyajñāḥ | asya cāgrāmyāhārapratiṣedhāt tannivṛttir mā bhūd ity ārambhaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
āraṇyair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || [MDh 6.5] || 3.29 ||
sarvātithiḥ pratiṣiddhavarjam || 3.30 ||
yāvad asya āśramaṃ gacchati tat sarvam asyātithir eva | “brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāhmaṇaḥ” [GDh 5.43] ityādivarṇaniyamaḥ saṃkhyāniyamo vā nāstīty arthaḥ | pratiṣiddhāḥ stenādayaḥ pratilomajāś ca, tadvarjam || 3.30 ||
vaiṣkam apy upayuñjīta || 3.31 ||
viṣkair vyāghrādibhir hatasya paśor māṃsaṃ vaiṣkam | tad upayuñjīta | apiśabdo garhāyām | tataś cāpady evopayuñjīta || 3.31 ||
na phālakṛṣṭam adhitiṣṭhet || 3.32 ||
araṇye ‘pi phālakṛṣṭapradeśaṃ nākrāmeta, āvāsaṃ tatra na kuryād ity arthaḥ | ākrāmed iti siddhe adhitiṣṭhed iti vacanaṃ pramādād ākramato doṣābhāvajñāpanārtham | kṛṣṭam iti siddhe phālagrahaṇaṃ kṣetrasamīpe vāsapratiṣedhārtham || 3.32 ||
grāmaṃ ca na praviśet || 3.33 ||
aphālakṛṣṭenāpi mārgeṇa janavāsaṃ na praviśet | caśabdād grāmāntaram api || 3.33 ||
jaṭilaś cīrājinavāsāḥ || 3.34 ||
jaṭilaḥ keśī | jaṭila eva syād iti niyamārtha ārambhaḥ | cīraṃ darbhādiniṣpannam | idam adhovāso draṣṭavyam | ajinaṃ carma | uttarīyam idam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “cīravāsā ajinottarīyaś ca” iti || 3.34 ||
nātisaṃvatsaraṃ bhuñjīta || 3.35 ||
mūlaphalam api gatasaṃvatsaraṃ na bhuñjīta | tathā ca manuḥ: “tyajed āśvayuje māsi” [MDh 6.15] ityādi | evaṃ ca saṃvatsaramātranicayaḥ siddhaḥ || 3.35 ||
vaikhānasasyoktvā idānīm ekīyamatenāśramabādhapakṣaṃ vaktukāma āha |
aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ[25] pratyakṣavidhānāt gārhasthasya gārhasthasya || 3.36 ||
ekāśrame bhavam aikāśramyam | tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇe | gārhasthasyeti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | gārhasthyam evācāryā icchanti | kutaḥ? pratyakṣavidhānāt upalabdhaśrutividhānād ity arthaḥ | yady api gṛhastho bhaved ity evaṃ na vihitam, tathāpy agnihotrādigṛhasthasādhyakriyāvidhānāt tasyaiva vidhānaṃ draṣṭavyam || 3.36 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ
caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
brahmacāryādīnāṃ dharmā uktāḥ, sāṃprathaṃ gṛhasthasya dharmā vaktavyā ity ata āha |
gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindetānanyapūrvāṃ yavīsasīm || 4.1 ||
gṛhastha iti bhāvisaṃjñayā vyapadeśaḥ, snātakasya yāvat pāṇigrahaṇaṃ tāvad aviruddhasya gṛhasthadharmasya praveśārtham | sadṛśī jātyā na kulataḥ |
vidyā praṇaṣṭā punar abhyupaiti
jātipraṇāśe tv iha sarvanāśaḥ ||
kulāpadeśena yathābhipūjyā
tasmāt kulīnāṃ striyam udvaheta ||
iti[26] smṛtyantare utkṛṣṭakulāyā evādhigamaśravaṇāt | bhāryāṃ bharaṇīyāṃ lakṣaṇayuktām ity arthaḥ | vindeta yācetety arthaḥ | ananyapūrvām anyasya vācāpy adattām ity arthaḥ | yavīyasīm ātmano hīnavayasīm ity arthaḥ || 4.1 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
asamānapravarair vivāhaḥ || 4.2 ||
samānapravaratvaṃ samānārṣeyatā, tadabhāvaḥ asamānapravaratvam, tair asmānapravarair vivāhaḥ kartavyaḥ | asamānaparvarām iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ yadi mātṛpakṣataḥ sagotrā bhavati tadānīm api doṣābhāvajñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca pitṛpakṣata eva sagotrāpratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ | [MDh 3.5]
ityādi || 4.2 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyaḥ || 4.3 ||
pitṛbāndhavāḥ pitur mātulabhaginyādayaḥ, tebhyaḥ saptamād ūrdhvaṃ vivāhaḥ | aṣṭamāt prabhṛti vā vivāhaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ || 4.3 ||
bījinaś ca || 4.4 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptamād ity anukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | bījaṃ yat parakṣetre uptaṃ tasya jātamātrād eva | anenāsamānapravaratvena prāptau satyāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ || 4.4 ||
mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt || 4.5 ||
ūrdhvaṃ vindeteti vartate | mātṛbāndhavāḥ mātulamātṛṣvasrādayaḥ | tatra pañcamād ūrdhvaṃ vivāhaḥ kartavyaḥ || 4.5 ||
idānīṃ vivāhavidhim āha |
brāhmo vidyācāritrabandhuśīlasaṃpannāya dadyād ācchādyālaṃkṛtām || 4.6 ||
brāhma iti vivāhasaṃjñā | vidyā trayī | cāritraṃ coditakarmānuṣṭhānam | bandhuḥ praśastābhijanaḥ | śīlaṃ dharma evābhimukhatvam | saṃpannaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | ācchādya vastrayugalena varam, alaṃkṛtāṃ bhūṣaṇādibhiḥ, dadyād udakapūrveṇa | ācchādya alaṃkṛtām ity uttarasminn api vivāhatraye draṣṭavyam || 4.6 ||
saṃyogamantraḥ prājāpatye saha dharmaś caryatām
iti || 4.7 ||
prājāpatya iti vivāhasaṃjñā | tasmin prājāpatye vivāhe saṃyogārtho mantraḥ saṃyogamantraḥ, tasmin taṃ pradānakāle uktvā dadyāt | ko ‘sau mantraḥ | “saha dharmaś caryatām” iti || 4.7 ||
ārṣe gomithunaṃ kanyāvate dadyāṭ || 4.8 ||
ārṣa iti vivāhasaṃjñā | tasmin purvaṃ vareṇa gomithune datte paścād ācchādyālaṃkṛtāṃ dadyāṭ
|| 4.8 ||
antarvedy ṛtvije dānaṃ daivaḥ || 4.9 ||
antarvedīti prakrāntayajñagrahaṇārtham | “ṛtvikśvaśura” [GDh 5.28] ityādau kriyottarakālam api ṛtvija iti vyavahāradarśanāt | nityadakṣiṇāto ‘dhikam iti jñāpanārthaṃ dānagrahaṇam | daiva iti vivāhasaṃjñā || 4.9 ||
alaṃkṛtyecchantyāḥ svayaṃ saṃyogo gāndharvaḥ
|| 4.10 ||
alaṃkṛtya svayam evātmānam, vakṣyati – “utsṛjya pitryān alaṃkārān” [GDh 18.21] iti tadviṣayam etad iti | kāmayamānā svayam eva vareṇa saṃyujyeta, sa gāndharvo vivāhaḥ || 4.10 ||
vittenānatiḥ strīmatām āsuraḥ || 4.11 ||
yatra dhanadānenānamanaṃ kanyāvatāṃ kriyate, sa āsuro nāma vivāhaḥ | kanyāvatām iti vaktavye strīgrahaṇaṃ kanyādānaphalanivṛttyartham || 4.11 ||
prasahyādānād rākṣasaḥ || 4.12 ||
yatra vikrameṇādānaṃ sa rākṣasaḥ || 4.12 ||
asaṃvijñātopasaṃgamanāt sa paiśācaḥ || 4.13 ||
yatrājñātām kanyāṃ suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā anyavyāpāre vā saktām upagacchati, sa paiśāco nāma vivāhaḥ | upagamanaśabda upagamanārtheṣv āliṅganādiṣu draṣṭavyaḥ, kṣatayonyāḥ saṃskārābhāvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ kanyāsv eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ || [MDh 8.226] iti || 4.13 ||
catvāro dharmyāḥ prathamāḥ || 4.14 ||
ādyāś catvāra eva dharmād anapetāḥ | ādyā iti vaktavye prathamagrahaṇaṃ yo yaḥ prathamaḥ sa sa praśasta ity evamartham | tataś ca pūrvasya pūrvasyābhāva eva uttarottaro draṣṭavyaḥ
|| 4.14 ||
ṣaḍ ity eke || 4.15 ||
eke tu[27] gāndharvāsurāv api dharmyau manyante | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “ṣaḍ vivāhāḥ brāhmo daiva ārṣo gāndharvaḥ kṣatro mānuṣaḥ” [VaDh 1.28] iti | evaṃ ca rākṣasapaiśācāv apy atyantanikṛṣṭau codanāsāmarthyāt pūrvāsaṃbhave draṣṭavyau || 4.15 ||
idānīṃ tāsu jātānāṃ putrāṇāṃ viśeṣārtham āha |
anulomāḥ anantaraikāntaradvyantarāsu jātāḥ savarṇāmbaṣṭhograniṣādadauṣyantapāraśavāḥ || 4.16 ||
anulomāḥ varṇānupūrvyeṇotpannāḥ | brāhmaṇasyānantarā kṣatriyā, ekāntarā vaiśyā, dvyantarā śūdrā, kṣatriyasyānantarā vaiśyā, ekāntarā śūdrā, tasya dvyantarā nāsti | vaiśyasyānantarā śūdrā, ekāntarā tasya nāsti | tataś ca brāhmaṇenānantarāyāṃ jātaḥ savarṇaḥ | kṣatriyeṇānantarāyāṃ jāta ambaṣṭhaḥ | vaiśyenānantarāyāṃ jāta ugraḥ | brāhmaṇenaikāntarāyāṃ jātaḥ niṣādaḥ | kṣatriyeṇaikāntarāyāṃ jāto dauṣyantaḥ | brāhmaṇena dvyantarāyāṃ jātaḥ pāraśavaḥ || 4.16 ||
pratilomāḥ sūtamāgadhāyogavakṣattṛvaidehakacaṇḍālāḥ || 4.17 ||
prātilomyenotpannāḥ pratilomāḥ | tāsv anantaraikāntaradvyantarāsv eva | kṣatriyasyānantarā brāhmaṇī, vaiśyasyānantarā kṣatriyā, śūdrasyānantarā vaiśyā | vaiśyasyaikāntarā brāhmaṇī, śūdrasya kṣatriyā | śūdrasya dvyantarā brāhmaṇī | kṣatriyasyānantarāyāṃ jātaḥ sūtaḥ | vaiśyasyānantarāyāṃ jāto māgadhaḥ | śūdrasyānantarāyāṃ jāta āyogavaḥ | vaiśyasyaikāntarāyāṃ jātaḥ kṣattā | śūdrasyaikāntarāyāṃ jāto vaidehakaḥ | śūdrasya dvyantarāyāṃ jātaś caṇḍālaḥ | evaṃ tāvad gautamamatam || 4.17 ||
idānīm anyeṣāṃ matena saṃjñābhedam āha |
brāhmaṇy ajījanat purtrān varṇebhya ānupūrvyāt brāhaṇasūtamāgadhacaṇḍālān || 4.18 ||
brāhmaṇī janitavatī brāhmaṇādibhyo varṇebhya ānupūrvyāt brāhmaṇād brāhmaṇam, kṣatriyāt sūtam, vaiśyān māgadham, śūdrāc caṇḍālam | saṃsārasyānāditvapradarśanāṛtham ajījanad iti bhūtanirdeśaḥ || 4.18 ||
tebhya eva kṣatriyā mūrdhāvasiktakṣatriyadhīvara-pulkasān || 4.19 ||
ajījanad ānupūrvyeṇeti ca vartate | tebhya eva brāhmaṇādibhyaḥ ānupūrvyāt kṣatriyā ajījanat janitavatī | kṣatriyāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jāto mūrdhāvasiktaḥ | kṣatriyāyāṃ kṣatriyāj jātaḥ kṣatriyaḥ | vaiśyāt kṣatriyāyāṃ jāto dhīvaraḥ | śūdrāt kṣatriyāyāṃ jātaḥ pulkasaḥ || 4.19 ||
tebhya eva vaiśyā bhṛjyakaṇṭhamāhiṣyavaiśya-vaidehakān || 4.20 ||
ajījanad ānupūrvyeṇeti vartate | evaṃ vaiśyāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jāto bhṛjyakaṇṭhaḥ | vaiśyāyāṃ kṣatriyāj jāto māhiṣyaḥ | viśyāyāṃ vaiśyāj jāto vaiśyaḥ | vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrāj jāto vaidehakaḥ
|| 4.20 ||
pāraśavayavanakaraṇaśūdrān śūdrayety eke || 4.21 ||
etebhya evānupūrvyāj janitavatī | śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ pāraśavaḥ | kṣatriyāj śūdrāyāṃ jāto yavanaḥ | vaiśyāc chūdrāyāṃ jātaḥ karaṇaḥ | śūdrāc chūdrāyāṃ jātaḥ śūdra ity eva saṃjñābhedena eke ācāryā varṇayanti | ekīyamatena saṃjñābhedakathanaṃ ca deśāntareṣv etat saṃjñopalabdhāv api na jātibhedo mantavya ity evamarthaḥ || 4.21 ||
eṣām api ca |
varṇāntragamanam utkarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ saptamena
|| 4.22 ||
varṇaśabdenotpādakabījam ucyate | yo varṇa utpādakaḥ tadvarṇaḥ saṃbhaved ity arthaḥ | saptamena yugaparivartanena | antaraśabdena kṣetram ity ucyate | tataś ca kramo draṣṭavyaḥ, utkarṣeṇotpādakavarṇatvam apakarṣeṇa kṣetravarṇatvam iti | tataḥ strītaḥ utkarṣo mantavyaḥ | puruṣād apakarṣaś ca | tad yathā — brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyāṃ mūrdhāvasiktā jāyate | sāpi mūrdhāvasiktā tatrotkṛṣṭabījaguṇā brāhmaṇenaiva duhitaram utakṛṣṭabījaguṇāṃ janayati, sāpi brāhmaṇenaivety evaṃ saptame yuge brāhmaṇy eva bhavatīty evam utkarṣaḥ | tathā brāhmaṇena kṣatriyāyāṃ mūrdhāvasikto jāyate | sa kṣatriyayā saṃbadhyamānaḥ pūrvavat kṣatriya eva bhavatīty apakarṣaḥ | evaṃ śūdrāpatyādāv api sarvatra draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ śreyasā cet prajāyate |
aśreyāṃś chreyasīṃ jātiṃ prāpnoty ā saptamād yugāt ||
śūdro brāhmaṇatām eti brāhmaṇaś caiti śūdratām |
kṣatriyāj jātam evaṃ ca vidyād vaiśyāt tathaiva ca || [MDh 10.64–65]
iti || 4.22 ||
pañcamenācāryāḥ || 4.23 ||
pañcamenaiva yugaparivartanena varṇāntaragamanaṃ bhavatīti kecid ācāryā varṇayanti | idam api vṛttāpekṣayā draṣṭavyam || 4.23 ||
sṛṣṭyantarajānāṃ ca || 4.24 ||
sṛṣṭiś catvārā varṇāḥ | tadantaraṃ mūrdhāvasiktādiḥ | tebhyo jātāḥ sṛṣṭyantarajāḥ | varṇāntaragamanam utkarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ saptamena pañcamenācāryā ity anukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | tataś ca tatrāpy anulomapratilomān kalpayitvā utpādakakṣetrajātyantaragamaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam || 4.24 ||
pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ || 4.25 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ, sṛṣṭer api pratilomāj jātaḥ sūtādiḥ dharmahīnaḥ tadantare pratilomāj jāto viśeṣata eveti | dharmaśabdenopanayanaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca anulomānām upanayanaṃ siddham | vedādhyayane tu pratiṣedho draṣṭavyaḥ | kutaḥ – “anulomānām upanayanam eva na vedādhyayanaṃ na pañcayajñaṃ naupāsanaṃ nāgnihotraṃ na samitkāryaṃ na saṃdhyopāsanam upanayanam api tūṣṇīm eva” ityādismṛtyantaradarśanāt |
kecic cakāraṃ na paṭhanti | teṣām uttarasūtrāt “śūdrāyāṃ ca” [GDh 4.26] ity ataś cakāram adhyāhṛtya yojanīyam “sṛṣṭyantarajānām” [GDh 4.24] ity asmin sūtre |
apare “sṛṣṭyantarajānāṃ pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ” ity ekam evedaṃ sūtram iti vyācakṣate |
sarvatra vyākhyānaṃ tulyam eva || 4.25 ||
śūdrāyāṃ ca || 4.26 ||
anulomānām api śūdrāyāṃ jāta upanayanādirahitaḥ | eṣa ca savarṇāmbaṣṭhaniṣādānām evopanayanam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “savarṇam upanīya dharnurvedaṃ śikṣayet, ambaṣṭham upanīya vaidyaśāstram, niṣādam upanīya hastisikṣāṃ śikṣayet, anyeṣām upanayanābhāvaḥ” iti | kecid ambaṣṭhāyāṃ savarṇāj jātam upanīya nṛttaṃ śikṣayed iti varṇayanti | ugrādayo vṛttiviśeṣeṇa jñātavyāḥ | ugro hantā vyālamṛgādīnām, dauṣyanto matsyaghātakaḥ, pāraśavo bhadrakālīpūjakaḥ, sūto hayaśikṣakaḥ, māgadho vaṇikpathasevī, paṭṭakṛd āyogavaḥ, kṣattā cākrikaḥ, sa eva lavaṇavikretā, vaidehako ‘japālaḥ ityādi || 4.26 ||
asamānāyāṃ ca śūdrāt patitavṛttiḥ || 4.27 ||
utkṛṣṭajātāyāṃ śūdrāj jātaḥ patitavat sparśanapratigrahādau varjanīya ity arthaḥ || 4.27 ||
antyaḥ pāpiṣṭhaḥ || 4.28 ||
śūdreṇa brāhmaṇyāṃ jātaḥ pāpatamaḥ | ataḥ so ‘tyantaṃ parihartavyaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “caṇḍālamāgovālavyajanād gacchet” ityādi |
tatrānulomānām upanayanavidhānāt, anyāyenotpannānāṃ tadayogyatvāt yady api “sadṛśīṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta” [GDh 4.1] ity atra samānajātiṣv eva coditaḥ, tathāpi prathamaniviṣṭasya nikṛṣṭajātiṣv api krameṇa vivāho ‘stīti jñāpayati | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tisro brāhmaṇasya bhāryā varṇānupūrvyeṇa dve rājanyasya ekaikā vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ” [VaDh 1.24] iti | “pratilomaśūdrābhyāṃ jāto dharmahīnaḥ” iti vaktavye “śūdrāyāṃ ca” [GDh 4.26] iti pṛthak sūtrakaraṇāt śūdrāvivāhasyāpi kathaṃcid yogyatāṃ darśayati | tataś ca prathamam uttamajātiṣūtpannaputrasya kāminaḥ śūdrāvivāho ‘pi mantravarjo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śūdrām apy eke mantravarjam” [VaDh 1.25] iti | tatrāpiśabdasya nindārthatvād utpannaputrasyeti tatrārthaḥ | “pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ” [GDh 4.25] ity anena nikṛṣṭasyottamāvivāhābhāvaṃ darśayati | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
channotpannās tu ye kecit prātilomyaguṇāśritāḥ |
guṇācāraparibhraṃśāt karmabhis tān vijānīyuḥ || [VaDh 18.7]
ityādi | channotpannatvena prātilomyena vivāhābhāva ukta iti tatrārthaḥ || 4.28 ||
vivāhavidhiṃ ca putrotpādanaṃ coktvā idānīṃ vivāhastutyartham āha |
punanti sādhavaḥ putrāḥ || 4.29 ||
punanti duḥkhād rakṣantīty arthaḥ | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
pud iti narakasyākhyā duḥkhaṃ ca narakaṃ viduḥ |
pudi trāṇāt tataḥ putram ihecchanti paratra ca || iti |
sādhavaḥ vivāhenaivotpannāḥ tasmāj jāratvena vā prātilomyena vā putrotpādanaṃ na kartavyam | putrāḥ śikṣitāḥ | kutaḥ? “tasmāt putram anuśiṣṭaṃ lokyam āhuḥ” [BṛĀrUp 1.5.17] iti śruteḥ
|| 4.29 ||
tripuruṣam ārṣāt || 4.30 ||
ārṣavivāhoḍhāyā jātaḥ pitrādipuruṣatrayaṃ narakād uddharatīty arthaḥ || 4.30 ||
daśa daivāt || 4.31 ||
daivavivāhotpanno daśa puruṣam || 4.31 ||
daśaiva prājāpatyāt || 4.32 ||
ubhayatra daśeti mā bhūd ity evamarhaṃ “prājāpatyāc ca” iti noktam | evaśabdo vispaṣṭārthaḥ
|| 4.32 ||
daśa pūrvān daśāparān ātmānaṃ ca brāhmīputro brāhmīputraḥ || 4.33 ||
brāhmavivāhenotpanno brāhmaḥ putraḥ | atikrāntān daśa bhaviṣyāṃś ca daśa punāti | upanyāsakramaṃ vihāya vyutkrameṇa phalābhidhānaṃ kanyādātur api pratiphalabhūyastvadarśanārtham, yathā jābālaśrutiḥ: “yaḥ saṃpannāya putrīṃ dadyāt so ‘gniṣṭomaphalam avāpnoti” iti | tathā kauṣītakabrāhmaṇam api: “tasya putrā dāyam upayanti suhṛdaḥ sādhukṛtyā dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyām” iti || 4.33 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye paurāṇike caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
apatyotpādanārthaṃ karmārthaṃ ca bhāryāpariṇayanam uktam | tatra pūrvam apatyotpādanavidhim āha |
ṛtāv upeyāt || 5.1 ||
ṛtur garbhagrahaṇasāmarthyena strīgatenopalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ ṣoṣaśāhorātraḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir itaraiḥ sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ || iti | [MDh 3.46]
tasmin upeyād upagacchet, prakṛtāṃ bhāryām, praharṣayan na tu paśuvat, upopasargayogāt |
nāyaṃ vidhiḥ, kāmata evārthaprāptatvāt | nāpi niyamaḥ | ko ‘sau niyamaḥ?[28] pakṣe prāptasya punarvacanaṃ niyamaḥ, icchātaḥ pravṛttiḥ, ṛtau gamane cāgamane ca prāpte ṛtāv upeyād eveti | asmin pakṣe gṛhīte anṛtau na vidhir na pratiṣedha iti pravṛttir eva bhavati marīcādivat | marīcaṃ bhakṣayen na bhakṣayed iti na kvacid dharmaśāstre śrūyate | tathāpi śiṣṭā bhakṣayanti, tadvad atrāpi pravṛttir eva bhavatīti “sarvatra vā” [GDh 5.2] ity asyānārambhaḥ prāpnoti, agamane prāyaścittaṃ ca |
nanu cobhayam apīṣyate, “yatra tu prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ na tatra śāstram asti, tadanuvartamāno narakāya rādhyati” itīcchātaḥ pravṛttau narakapātaśravaṇāt, tannivāraṇārthaḥ “sarvatra vā” [GDh 5.2] ity asyārambhaḥ | smṛtyantare agamane prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate |
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati | [=BDh 4.1.18]
ghorāyāṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ yujyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
naitad evam | “yatra tu prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ” ityādes tāvad ayam arthaḥ | yatra yasminn anuṣṭhāne prītyupalabdhitaḥ pravṛttiḥ phalaṃ jñātvā tatprītyā pravṛttiḥ, phalābhisaṃdhinā pravṛttir ity arthaḥ | tasyāṃ pravṛttau na śāstram asti atyantaṃ na phalatīty arthaḥ | tadanuvartamānaḥ phalābhisaṃdhinānuṣṭhānaṃ kurvan narakāya rādhyati narakaṃ gacchatīti | saṃdhyopāsanādeḥ phalābhisaṃdhityāgenānuṣṭhānārtham idaṃ vacanam | tathā ca bhagavān vāsuvevaḥ “mā karmaphalahetur bhūḥ” [BhG 2.47] ityādi | yadi sarvatra prītipravṛttinivāraṇaṃ syāt tadānīṃ marīcādīnām api śāstraṃ vaktavyam, śāstreṇa vinā narakapatanaṃ bhavatīti | tasmāt “sarvatra vā” [GDh 5.2] ity etad api śāstreṇānugrahārthaṃ na vaktavyam | yac coktam “ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryām” [BDh 4.1.18] ityādi, tad api ṛtusnātāyā bhāryāyā ātmadarśanārthaṃ vacanam | kutaḥ? jābāliśrutidarśanāt | evaṃ ca śrūyate: “ṛtusnātā bhāryā yaṃ pūrvaṃ paśyet tādṛśaṃ putraṃ janayati | tasmāt saṃnidhau bhartaiva prathamam ātmānaṃ darśayet” iti | tataś cāgamane prāyaścittābhāvāt niyamena doṣābhāvaḥ prāpnoti | prāptasya punarvacanaṃ niyama iti yeṣāṃ pakṣaḥ, teṣāṃ niyamāśaṅkāpi nopapadyate, ṛtugamanasyaiva prāptyabhāvāt, icchātaḥ pravṛttiḥ sarvatra bhavatīti |
tasmāt parisaṃkhyārtham idaṃ sūtram | kā ca parisaṃkhyā? tatra cānyatra ca prāptau vacanam | icchātaḥ pravṛtteḥ ṛtāv anṛtau ca prāptau satyām ṛtāv eva gamanaṃ kartavyam iti | idam apy anarthakam “sarvatra vā” ity ārambhād iti cet, na, vyavasthitavikalpārthatvāt | vyavasthā caiva draṣṛṭavyā – yadi putrārthaiva pravṛttiḥ tadānīm ṛtāv evopeyāt | ratyarthā cet “sarvatra vā” it | ṛṇatrayaśrutyā putrotpādanārtham anuṣṭhānārthaṃ ca bhāryāgrahaṇaṃ na ratyarthm iti cen, na “ratiputraphalā nārī” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt, “yā syād anaticāreṇa ratiḥ sā dharmasaṃśritā” iti [VaDh 12.23] vāsiṣṭhadarśanāt, “svadāratuṣṭa eva bhavet” ity auśanasadarśanāt, “api nāryo vijaniṣyamāṇāḥ patibhiḥ saha śayīran” iti vājasaneyaśrutidarśanāt, “virakto bhikṣur bhaved itaro gārhasthyaṃ praviśet” iti ca smṛtyantaradarśanāt,
kāmatas tu pravṛttānām imā syuḥ kramaśo ‘varāḥ | [MDh 3.12]
iti manudarśanāt, ṛṇatrayaśrutyāpi vaikalpikācāryapakṣa evānṛtau brahmacaryopadeśāt | samuccayapakṣe tu yajñaprajābhyām ity udāharaṇatvenopapadyamānatvāt, gārhasthyacodanāyāḥ smṛtivirodho mā bhūd iti | tasmād anaṣṭarāgasya ratyartham api dāragrahaṇam asty eva | tataś ca yadi jāyāpatī viraktau bhavataḥ gamanaṃ ca putrārtham eva tadānīm ṛtāv evopeyāt | saraktayoḥ “sarvatra vā” ity upapadyata iti | tarhi ṛtāv upagamanam akurvato doṣābhāvaḥ prāpnoti, sa doṣa iti cen, na asmābhir api doṣābhāvasyaiveṣyamāṇatvāt | evam api putrārthina anṛtau gamanaṃ kurvataḥ prāyaścittaṃ prāpnoti, sa doṣa iti cen, na tasyaiva parisaṃkhyāphalatvāt | yeṣāṃ śāstraprāptasya punarvacanaṃ parisaṃkhyeti teṣām apy upapadyate | tad yathā – “jātaputro ‘gnīn ādadhīta,” “tribhir ṛṇavān jāyate” iti śruteḥ, “punanti sādhavaḥ putrāḥ” iti ca smṛteḥ | putrotpādanārthaṃ garbhagrahaṇasāmarthyād ṛtāv eva gamane prāpte anutpannaputrasya punar idaṃ vacanam anṛtukālīnagamanapratiṣedhaparaṃ vijñāyata iti | tasmāt parisaṃkhyaiveyaṃ yady upeyād ṛtāv eveti |
nanu yadi putrārthaṃ gamanaṃ tadā utpannaputrasya na prāpnoti iti |
ucyate – bahuputrasyābhīṣṭatvāt | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ |
putrapautrapratiṣṭhasya bahvapatyasya jīvataḥ |
ityādi | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi patim ekādaśaṃ kṛdhi” iti | tasmāt parisaṃkhyārthaṃ sūtram iti sthitam || 5.1 ||
sarvatra vā pratiṣiddhavarjam || 5.2 ||
parvādīni pratiṣiddhāni | yathāha manuḥ |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || iti | [MDh 4.128]
vāśabdo vyavasthitavikalpārthaḥ |
anye tu vyācakṣate – “ṛtāv upeyād” [GDh 5.1] ity anena ṛtāv eva sakṛd gamanaṃ codyate | “sarvatra vā” ity anena sarvasmin kāle sakṛd gamanam iti |
tad ayuktam “ṛtāv upeyād dvir ekato vā anṛtau kāmataḥ” iti śrutidarśanāt || 5.2 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyarṣibhūtapūjakaḥ || 5.3 ||
ete pañca mahāyajñāḥ | tatra devapūjakaśabdena vaiśvadevam ucyate | pitṛpūjaka iti pitrarthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayen nityam iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthaṃ pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam || iti | [MDh 3.83]
annāsaṃbhave payo dadyāt, tadasaṃbhave mūlam, tadasaṃbhave phalam, sarvāsaṃbhave udakam | tathāha manuḥ |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhya prītim āvahan || iti | [MDh 3.82]
tatrāpi sarvāsaṃbhave udakam iti draṣṭavyam | manuṣyapūjaka iti atithipūjanam ucyate | tatrāpy annāsaṃbhave āsanārthaṃ tṛṇaṃ dadyāt, tadasaṃbhave saṃveśanārthaṃ bhūmim, śītāpanodārtham agnim, paṅkāpakarṣaṇārtham udakam, madhurāṃ ca vāṇīm | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tṛṇabhūmyagnyudakavāksūnṛtānasūyāḥ satāṃ geheṣu nocchidyante kadācana” [VaDh 13.61] iti | svayam api ca vakṣyati atithipūjanam | taccheṣaḥ vasiṣṭhoktaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ | bhūtapūjaka iti vaiśvadevottarakāladigdevatābhyo baliharaṇam | ṛṣipūjaka iti nityasvādhyāyaḥ | agniparigrahaṇāt prāg eṣām abhidhānam anagnikasyāpi laukike ‘gnau prāptyartham | tataś ca śūdrasya pākayajñapakṣe ‘pi laukike ‘gnau draṣṭavyam | tathā avakīrṇihomaś ca || 5.3 ||
nityasvādhyāyaḥ || 5.4 ||
nityasvādhyāya iti brahmayajño ‘bhidhīyate | pūrvam ṛṣipūjaka ity anenābhihita eva itarānuṣṭhānāśaktāv api sa nityaṃ viguṇo ‘pi kartavyaḥ | pūrvoktenaiva prakāreṇa ṛṣiyajña iti vaktavye smṛtyantaroktasya vidheḥ prāpaṇārtham evam abhidhānam | tathā cāha uśanā: “atha brahmayajñe darbheṣv āsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitrīpūrvaṃ vedānām ekadeśaṃ paṭhet vedānām ādiṃ vā ṛgyajuḥsāmnām eka vā trir vā praṇavam aitihāsikaṃ ślokaṃ vā samāhitaḥ” ityādi || 5.4 ||
pitṛbhyaś codakadānam || 5.5 ||
cakārād devatānām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca | udakam iti siddhe dānagrahaṇaṃ tarpaṇapūrvatvajñāpanārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
devatānām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca tarpayitvā samāhitaḥ |
paścād dadyāt pitṝṇāṃ ca svadhāpūrvaṃ tilaiḥ saha || iti |
nityaṃ pitṛbhya uadkadānacodanayaiva ahar ahaḥ snānam apy arthāt siddham, tatpūrvakatvād udakadānasya | tathā ca manuḥ |
nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam | iti | [MDh 2.176]
udakadānaṃ dvividham, yad apsu dīyate yad ākāśe ceti |
nanu ca bhūmyām eva codyate, nāpsu, yathāha bhṛguḥ |
nodakeṣu na pātreṣu na kruddho naikapāṇinā |
nopatiṣṭhati tat toyaṃ yad bhūmyāṃ na pradīyate || iti |
tathā bṛhaspatir api |
udake nodakaṃ dadyāt pitṛbhyas tu kadācana |
uttīrya tu śucau deśe kuryād udakatarpaṇam || iti |
naitad evam – smṛtyantare viruddhābhidhānāt, viṣayavibhāgakathanāc ca | yathā cāha uśanā |
āpo devagaṇāḥ sarve āpaḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tasmād apsu jalaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti |
tatra kātyāyano viṣayavibhāgam āha |
devānāṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ ca jale dadyāj jalāñjalim |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ sthale dadyāj jalāñjalim || iti |
tarpaṇādyudakadānāntam ubhābhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ draṣṭavyam | udakadāne tāvad adoṣaḥ, jalāñjalim iti vacanāt | tarpaṇe tu nopapadyate, naikapāṇinety asya viruddhavākyaśravaṇāt | tathā ca bhṛguḥ |
ubhābhyām api hastābhyām udakaṃ tarpayan dvijaḥ |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti kālasūtram avākchirāḥ || iti |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, vibhāgavākyaśravaṇāt | tathā cāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
śrāddhe secanakālaś cet ekapāṇiṃ pradāpayet |
tarpaṇe tūbhayaṃ kuryād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || iti |
tasmād ekam apsv eva codyate | ākāśe yac codyate tad aparam | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
nābhimātre jale sthitvā cintayann ūrdhvamānasaḥ |
āgacchantu me pitara imaṃ gṛhṇantv apo’ñjalim ||
tris trir añjalim ākāśe uccair uccataraṃ budhaḥ |
uktvā coktvā kṣiped vāri vāgyato dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
pitṝṇāṃ sthānam ākāśaṃ dakṣiṇā dik tathaiva ca |
tasmāt tathā sadā dattaṃ pitṝṇāṃ syāt tad akṣayam || iti |
pitṝṇāṃ yad dīyate tat sarvaṃ tilair darbhaiś ca saha draṣṭavyam, yathāha bhṛguḥ |
tiladarbhayutaṃ vāri svadhayā yat pradīyate |
tat sarvam amṛtaṃ bhūtvā pitṝṇām akṣayaṃ bhavet || iti |
tasmād eva pitṛbhya udakadānaṃ kartavyam || 5.5 ||
yathotsāham anyat || 5.6 ||
brahmayajñodakadānābhyāṃ yad anyad avaśiṣṭaṃ gṛhasthadharmarūpaṃ tad yathāśakti kuryāt, utsāhavacanāt | tataś ca mukhyāsaṃbhave amukhyenāpi kartavyam, udāhṛtaṃ ca “payomūlaphalair vāpi” [MDh 3.82] ityādi || 5.6 ||
bhāryādir agnir dāyādir vā || 5.7 ||
bhāryāśabdena vivāho labyate, agniśabdenaupāsanam, vaivāhikam agnim aupāsanarūpeṇa dhārayed ity arthaḥ |
kecid evaṃ vyācakṣate – jātakāgner eva pāraṃparyāgatasya vivāhottarakālam aupāsanarūpeṇa dhāraṇārtham abhidhānam iti |
tad ayuktam – “vivāhaṃ kurvan laukikam agnim ādhāya” it gṛhyasmṛteḥ | yadi jātakāgnir evāgacchati tadānīṃ tatra laukikagrahaṇaṃ na karoti | tataś cāśucitvāt jātakāgnis tyaktavya eva |
yadā vā asya bhrātṛbhiḥ pitrā vā saha vibhāgaḥ tatra vāgniparigrahaṇaṃ kuryāt | vivāhottarakālam api dravyāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ, karmaṇāṃ dravyasādhyatvāt | yo hi dravyavān sa vivāhakāle parigṛhṇīyāt niḥsvo dāyakāla iti |
kecid vivāhāt prāg api dāyādikam agniṃ parigṛhṇīyād iti vyācakṣate |
tad ayuktam, “vidhuraḥ punar niviṣṭaḥ punar agniṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt abhāryasyāgnyabhāvāt” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt |
katham avagamyate smārtasyaiva dhāraṇaṃ na śrautasyeti | “jātaputro ‘gnīn ādadhīta” iti śruteḥ, tatrāpy agnīn iti bahuvacanaprayogāt || 5.7 ||
tasmin gṛhyāṇi || 5.8 ||
tasminn evaupāsane gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi puṃsavanādīni kuryāt | grahaṇapakṣe idam | agṛhītaś cel laukike ‘gnāv eva draṣṭavyāni || 5.8 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyayajñāḥ svādhyāyaś ca || 5.9 ||
devayajño vaiśvadevaḥ | pitrarthaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ pitṛyajñaḥ | manuṣyayajño ‘tithipūjanam | eteṣām agnisaṃbandho ‘pi vaiśvadevasya prasiddhaḥ, pitṛyajñasya tadbhojanārthaḥ pākaḥ, manuṣyayajñasyāpy atithibhojanārthaḥ pākaḥ, svādhyāyaśabdena brahmayajña ucyate | tasyaikīyamata evāgnisaṃbandha iti visamāsaḥ | tathā cāha uśanā: “udakasamīpe atha brahmayajñaḥ” ity ārabhya brahmayajñam uktvā “agnisamīpe ity ekeṣām” iti | eṣāṃ gṛhyoktakarmatvād eva siddham iti cet, na, āśaṅkānivṛttyarthatvāt punar abhidhānasya | kāśaṅketi cet, devayajñādīnāṃ pūrvaṃ laukike ‘gnau vidhānāt tatraiva teṣām adhikāraḥ, naupāsane | “tasmin gṛhyāṇi” [GDh 5.8] ity anenāpi tadvarjitānāṃ gṛhyakarmaṇām eva grahaṇam, teṣāṃ pūrvaṃ pṛthagabhidhānād iti kasyacid āśaṅkā syād iti | evaṃ ca teṣām ubhayatrādhikāraḥ | tataś cāgnim ato ‘pi laukike ‘gnau kṛte na doṣaḥ | tataś ca bādarāyaṇasūtram: “agnim ato ‘pi vacanena niṣiddhāḥ pañcayajñāḥ āhitāgneḥ” iti || 5.9 ||
balikarma || 5.10 ||
anena bhūtayajña uktaḥ | āśaṅkānivṛttyartham evāsyābhidhānam | cakāraḥ pūrvasūtrād adhikriyate tasminn evety anukarṣaṇārthaḥ | vaiśvadevānantaram eva kartavyaṃ na pṛthag ity evamarthaṃ pṛthak punaḥ karaṇam | tathā ca gṛhyakāraḥ: “pūrvaṃ devānāṃ hutvā paścād baliṃ haret” iti | pūrvavad bhūtayajña iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ vaiśvadevāt pṛthagghaviḥsūcanārtham | tataś ca siddhasyānnasya vaiśvadevārthaṃ bhūtayajñārthaṃ caikadeśaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | tathā ca jaiminiḥ: “aśanasya balī vardhayitvā” iti dvivacanaṃ sāyaṃ prātaḥ kartavyārtham | karmagrahaṇaṃ vaiśvadevasyāpi sāyaṃ prātaḥ kartavyārtham | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “sāyaṃ prātar vaiśvadevaṃ baliharaṇaṃ ca” iti, “atha sāyaṃ prātaḥ siddhasya haviṣyasya juhuyāt” ityādi || 5.10 ||
anuktagṛhyakārāṇām api sāmānyapraveśanārtham idānīṃ vaiśvadevam āha |
agnāv agnir dhanvantarir viśve devā prajāpatiḥ sviṣṭakṛd iti homāḥ || 5.11 ||
homagrahaṇād eva labhyamānasyāgneḥ susamiddhe ‘gnau homārthaṃ punarvacanam | homā iti bahuvacanam ekāhutipratiṣedhārtham | homagrahaṇaṃ ca svāhākārapradarśanārtham | itikaraṇaṃ caturthyupasaṃgrahaṇārtham | visamāso mantrāntaraprasiddhyarthaḥ | tataś cāgnaye svāhā, dhanvatantraye svāhā, viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svāhā, prajāpataye svāhā | sviṣṭakṛcchabdo ‘gnyupapadaś caturthyanto draṣṭavyaḥ, agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti | etāvān vaiśvadevaḥ || 5.11 ||
idānīṃ bhūtayajñavidhim āha |
digdevatābhyaś ca yathāsvam || 5.12 ||
pūrvaṃ digdevatābhyo baliṃ haret | indrāgniyamaniṛtivaruṇavāyusomeśānānāṃ prācyādipradakṣiṇam, ūrdhvaṃ viṣṇuḥ, atho ‘nantaḥ indrāya nama iti mantro ‘tra draṣṭavyaḥ | caśabdāt tatpuruṣebhyaś ca | tataś cendrapuruṣebhyo nama ityādi draṣṭavyam | yathāsvaṃ yathāsthānam ity arthaḥ || 5.12 ||
dvārṣu marudbhyaḥ || 5.13 ||
bahuvacanāt sarveṣu | marudbhyo nama iti draṣṭavyaḥ | atra tatpuruṣacodanā nāsti, cakārābhāvāt || 5.13 ||
gṛhadevatābhyaḥ praviśya || 5.14 ||
praviśyeti vacanāt pūrvaṃ gṛhād bahir draṣṭavyam | tataś ca gṛhād bahir ekasmin deśe gomayena maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā digdevatābhyo baliharaṇaṃ kṛtvā tato bāhyāntareṣu ca dvāreṣu baliharaṇaṃ kṛtvābhyantaram eva praviśya gṛhadevatābhyo baliṃ haret | tāḥ śryādayaḥ, yathāha uśanā:
śayanasya śiraḥsthāne śriyai nama iti dadyāt pādasthāne bhadrakālyai nama iti, koṣṭhāgāre dhanapataye nama iti, stambhanilaye rudrāya nama iti, goṣṭhe mitrāya nama iti, ulūkhalamusalayor varuṇāya nama iti, kūpe ahaye budhnyāya nama iti, peṣaṇyām agnisamīpe caitābhyo devatābhyo nama iti dadyāt |
iti || 5.14 ||
brahmaṇe madhye || 5.15 ||
gṛhamadhye brahmaṇe nama iti dadyāt || 5.15 ||
adbhya udakumbhe || 5.16 ||
adbhyo nama ity udakumbhe dadyāt || 5.16 ||
ākāśāyety antarikṣe || 5.17 ||
ākāśāya nama ity antarikṣe ‘ṅkaṇapradeśe dadyāt | itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī | tataś ca mṛtyave nama ity annaśālāyām, ajāyaikapade nama iti phalakeṣu ceti draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.17 ||
naktaṃcarebhyaś ca sāyam || 5.18 ||
sāyaṃ cet phalakābaliharaṇād ūrdhvam ākāśe naktaṃcarebhyo nama iti dadyāt | cakāraḥ pūrvasamuccayārthaḥ, itarathā nakṃcarebhya eva syād iti || 5.18 ||
svasti vācya bhikṣādānam appūrvam || 5.19 ||
svastīti bhikṣukaṃ vācayitvā tata udakadānapūrvam asmai bhikṣādānaṃ kartavyam | bhikṣādāne striyā adhikārāt tasyā eva ca yācitatvād atas tām eva kārayed gṛhasthaḥ | kecid vyācakṣate vaiśvadevānantaram eva vidhānād agrabhaikṣasyāyaṃ vidhir na sāmānyasyeti | teṣāṃ pakṣe svastivācanaṃ puruṣeṇaiva kārayitavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 5.19 ||
dadātiṣu caivaṃ dharmyeṣu || 5.20 ||
dadātiśabdena hiraṇyādidānam ucyate | dharmyagrahaṇān na dṛṣṭārthaṃ mitrādibhyo dānam | evam eva udakapūrvaṃ dadyāt | cakārād eva siddhe evaṃśabdaḥ samastaprāpaṇārthaḥ | tataś ca yathā bhikṣādānaṃ strīkṛtam api bhartur anujñayā pramāṇaṃ bhavati, tadvad dhiraṇyādidānam api pramāṇaṃ bhavati | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
strīkṛtāny apramāṇāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpadi |
viśeṣato gṛhakṣetradānadharmān na vikrayāḥ ||
etāny eva pramāṇāni bhartā yady anumanyate || [NSm 1.22–23]
ityādi | bhikṣādānam api bhartur anujñayā vinā na kāryam | tathā ca manuḥ |
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kāryaṃ kiṃcid gṛheṣv api || [MDh 5.147]
iti || 5.20 ||
samadviguṇasāhasrānantāni phalāny abrāhmaṇabrāhmaṇaśrotriyavedapāragebhyaḥ || 5.21 ||
pātraviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣapratipādanārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | samaṃ īṣatphalam ity arthaḥ | abrāhmaṇāya dattaṃ hiraṇyādi bhaikṣaṃ vā | abrāhmaṇaśabdena upanītamātra ucyate | tathā cāha uśanā: “yena vedā anadhītās tadvratāni ca nānuṣṭhitāny abrāhmaṇa eva saḥ” iti | abrāhmaṇaśabdaṃ brāhmaṇa eva prayuktavān | dviguṇaṃ bhavati brāhmaṇāya dattam | brāhmaṇaśabdenānuṣṭhitavedavrato anadhītaveda ucyate | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yaś ca kāṣṭhamayo hastī yaś ca carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
yaś ca vipro ‘nadhīyānas trayas te nāmadhārakāḥ || [VaDh 3.11]
śrotriyāya dattaṃ sahasraguṇaṃ bhavati | śrotriyaśabdena ekaśākhādhyāyī ucyate | yathāha baudhāyanaḥ: “ekāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati” [=ĀpDh 2.6.4] iti | anantaṃ vedapāragāya dattam | vedapāragaśabdena sāṅgavedādhyāyy ucyate | yathāha kaṇvaḥ: “yena sāṅgo vedo ‘dhītaḥ sa vedapārago bhavati” iti | eṣāṃ pūrvaṃ phalābhidhānāt | evaṃ jātiviśeṣād api draṣṭavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
śūdre samaphalaṃ dānaṃ vaiśye ca dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
kṣatriye triguṇaṃ prāhur amitaṃ brāhmaṇe bhavet ||
iti || 5.21 ||
evaṃ phalaviśeṣāpekṣayā dānaphalam uktvā idānīṃ tatprasaṅgenānyam api
dānaprakāram āha |
gurvarthaniveśauṣadhārthavṛttikṣīṇayakṣymāṇādhyaya-nādhvasaṃyogavaiśvajiteṣu dravyasaṃvibhāgo
bahirvedi || 5.22 ||
gurugrahaṇaṃ mātṛpitṛbhṛtyānāṃ copalakṣaṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam adhvagaṃ sarvavedasam |
gurvarthaṃ pitṛbhṛtyārthaṃ svādhyāyārthyupatāpinau || [MDh 11.1]
ityādi | niveśanārthaṃ ca | evaṃ yācitvāpi niveśaḥ kartavyaḥ | idam api samuccayapakṣe draṣṭavyam | oṣadhārtham arthagrahaṇe prastute punar arthagrahaṇam ebhyo vidyāviśeṣato ‘rthasyāpi gurulāghavajñāpanārtham, niḥsvebhya eva dātavyam ity evamarthaṃ vā | yathāha manuḥ |
niḥsvebhyo deyam etebhyo dānaṃ vidyāviśeṣataḥ | [MDh 11.2]
ityādi | vṛttikṣīṇaḥ āpannaḥ | yakṣyamāṇaḥ nityayajñārtham idaṃ vacanam | tataś ca “alaṃ somāya, nāsomayājī, sa somaṃ pātum arhati” ity evamādīni smṛtyantaravākyāni kāmyayajñe draṣṭavyāni | yācitvāpi yakṣyamāṇatvān nityayajñasya | adhyayanārthaṃ vedādhyayanārtham, adhvasayogaḥ adhvani vartamānaḥ, kṣīṇaḥ pathyadānārthaṃ ca saṃyogagrahaṇāt | vaiśvajito viśvajitā yeneṣṭam | ebhyaḥ saṃvibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ hiraṇyāder api, dravyagrahaṇāt | niyamena kartavyam, samupasargaprayogāt | vaiśvajitasya ā saṃvatsarāt yācñāpratiṣedhāt aprārthayamānāyaiva deyam | itarebhyo ‘prārthayamānebhyo vā prārthayamānebhyo vā dadyāt | dravyavibhāgaś ca yathātmanaḥ kuṭumbavyasanaṃ na bhavati tathā kartavyaḥ, vyupasargaprayogāt | tataś caikadeśo dātavya ity arthaḥ | bahirvedigrahaṇād antarvedy anyebhyo ‘pi | evaṃ ca niyamena kartavyatvāt tad akurvato na kevalaṃ phalābhāva eva | kiṃ tarhi | pratyavāyaś ca bhavati | tarhi tat kurvato ‘pi tatpratyavāyābhāva eva prayojanaṃ na phalārtham iti cet, na, vidyāviśeṣata iti liṅgāt pātraviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣo ‘stīti jñāpayati | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
guruniveśauṣadhārthaṃ svādhyāyārthaṃ ca bhārata |
adhvany api pravṛttasya yāgārthaṃ ca tathaiva ca ||
kṛtavaiśvajitasyāpi vṛttikṣīṇasya pārthiva |
datvā vidyāviśeṣeṇa svargaloke mahīyate ||
ityādi | tataś ca saṃdhyopāsanādivad draṣṭavyam | yathā saṃdhyopāsanam akurvataḥ pratyavāyaḥ, kurvataḥ pratyavāyābhāvaś ca phalaprāptiś ceti || 5.22 ||
bhikṣamāṇeṣu kṛtānnam itareṣu || 5.23 ||
uktavyatirikteṣu bhikṣamāneṣu kṛtānnaṃ dadyāt | “itarebhyaḥ” iti kecit paṭhanti, teṣām apy ayam evārthaḥ |
nanu ca “svasti vācya bhikṣādānam” [GDh 5.19] ity anenaiva siddhatvād etan na vaktavyam iti |
ucyate – itikartavyatāmātraṃ tat | idaṃ punar niyamena bhikṣā dātavyety evamartham iti | evaṃ cet idam api saṃdhyopāsanādivad draṣṭavyam, aviśeṣeṇoktatvāt | dānaṃ ca bahirvedy antarvedi ca | kṛtānnagrahaṇāt taṇḍulāder aniyamaḥ | “bhaikṣam itareṣu” ity vaktavye, “bhikṣamāṇeṣu” iti vacanāt “bhavati bhikṣāṃ dehi” ityādibhir mantrair prārthayamāneṣv eva niyamaḥ | atha vā bhikṣārhāṇām evāyaṃ niyama iti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
avratā hy anadhīyānā yatra bhaikṣacarā dvijāḥ |
taṃ grāmaṃ daṇḍayed rājā corabhaktaprado hi saḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.4]
tatra bhikṣādāne yater haste ‘py udakadānaṃ kartavyam | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
yatihaste jalaṃ dadyād bhaikṣaṃ dadyāt punar jalam |
bhaikṣaṃ parvatamātraṃ syāt taj jalaṃ sāgaropamam ||
|| iti || 2.23 ||
dānāpavādam adhunocyate |
pratiśrutyāpy adharmasaṃyukte na dadyāt || 5.24 ||
adharmasaṃyuktaḥ gurvādyartham uddiśya ya prārthayate na ca karoti tasmai pratiśrutam api na dadyāt | apiśabdāt saṃakalpitam api || 5.24 ||
adharmasaṃyuktatvaṃ na yasya kasyacid vacanāt pratipattavyam ity āha |
kruddhahṛṣṭabhītārtalubdhabālasthaviramūḍha-mattonmattavākyāny anṛtāny apātakāni || 5.25 ||
krodhaharṣabhayarogalobhāviṣṭānāṃ bālādīnāṃ ca vacanenārdham asaṃyuktatvaṃ nādhyavaseyam, apramāṇatvāt teṣāṃ vacanasya | vākyagrahaṇam anyatrāpy eṣāṃ vacanasyāprāmāṇyaprasiddhyartham || 5.25 ||
prāsaṅgikam uktvā idānīṃ prakṛtam āha |
bhojayet pūrvam atithikumāravyādhitagarbhiṇī-suvāsinīsthavirān jaghanyāṃś ca || 5.26 ||
ātmana prathamam etān bhojayet | atithir vakṣyamāṇaḥ | kumāro bālaḥ | vyādhito rogārtaḥ | suvāsinyo garbhayuktā duhitaraḥ | sthavirā vṛddhāḥ | jaghanyāḥ paricārakāḥ | caśabdād dīnānāthādayaḥ upasaṃgṛhyante | visamāsaḥ kramaniyamārthaḥ | tataś ca yathānugrāhyam etān bhojayet || 5.26 ||
ācāryapitṛsakhīnāṃ tu nivedya vacanakriyā || 5.27 ||
yo hy etasmin kāle ācāryādīnāṃ kaścid gṛham āgacchet tataḥ siddham annaṃ nivedya tasmai taduktasya vacanasya karaṇaṃ yat te bruvate tat kuryād ity arthaḥ | tuśabdo niyamārthaḥ, tad eva kuryān na kvacid api svatantreṇa bhavitavyam iti | vacanakriyety uktatvād yadi te na kiṃcid vadanti tadānīṃ svatantreṇa bhavitavyam || 5.27 ||
ṛtvigācāryaśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānām upasthāne madhuparkaḥ || 5.28 ||
ṛtvigādīnāṃ tu gṛhāgamane sati madhuparkeṇa pūjā kartavyā prathamam, upasthānagrahaṇān na dvitīyādau | madhuparkavidhiś ca gṛhye draṣṭavyaḥ || 5.28 ||
saṃvatsare punaḥ || 5.29 ||
pūjitās te yadi saṃvatsarāt punar āgaccheyus tadā punar api madhuparko deyaḥ, nārvāk || 5.29 ||
yajñavivāhayor arvāk || 5.30 ||
arvāg api saṃvatsarād yajñe vivāhe cāgatānāṃ madhuparko deyaḥ | yajñe ṛtvijāṃ śrutita eva madhuparkadānasya siddhatvāt śvaśurādyartha ārambhaḥ || 5.30 ||
rājñaś ca śrotriyasya || 5.31 ||
rājñaś copasthāne madhuparko deyaḥ śrotriyaś cet | pṛthaggrahaṇād arvāg api saṃvatsarāt | cakārād vastraṃ ca || 5.31 ||
aśrotriyasyāsanodake || 5.32 ||
yadi rājā aśrotriyo bhavati tasyāsanodakamātreṇaiva pūjā kartavyā || 5.32 ||
śrotriyasya tu pādyam arghyam annaviśeṣāṃś ca prakārayet || 5.33 ||
brāhmaṇasya śrotriyasya | kuta etat? punaḥ śrotriyagrahaṇāt | vaiśyārthaṃ cet, na, “vaiśyo madhuparkeṇa na mānyaḥ tasmāt taṃ bhojayed eva” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | sa yadi vṛttasaṃpanno bhavati tasyopasthāne pādaprakṣālanārthaṃ pūjārthaṃ codakaṃ deyam | pādyam arghyam iti visamāsān madhuparkaś ca annaviśeṣāḥ mudgodanādibhedabhinnāḥ | cakārād upadaṃśādiviśeṣāś ca | propasargād ghṛtabahulam | tathā ca śrutiḥ; “brāhmaṇāyābhyāgatāya sarpiṣmad annaṃ pacati” iti || 5.33 ||
asāmarthye tu sati –
nityaṃ vā saṃskāraviśiṣṭam || 5.34 ||
yad evāsya gṛhe nityam annaṃ tad evopadaṃśena bahulena viśiṣṭaṃ sādhāraṇāt || 5.34 ||
madhyato ‘nnadānam avaidye sādhuvṛtte || 5.35 ||
yas tv aśrotriyo brāhmaṇaḥ sādhuvṛtto bhavati vṛttavān bhavati tasminn upasthite madhyamena saṃskāreṇānvitaṃ deyam annam | annagrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham, annam eva deyaṃ na pādyādīni | aśrotriyatvād eva saṃskāraviyogo mā bhūd ity evamarthaṃ dānagrahaṇam || 5.35 ||
viparīte tu tṛṇodakabhūmi || 5.36 ||
tuśabdād ubhayaviparīte | tataś ca yadi vidvān asādhuvṛtto bhavati tasyāpi “nityaṃ vā saṃskāraviśiṣṭaḥ” [GDh 5.34] ity evaṃ draṣṭavyam | na tṛṇodakabhūmimātram eva deyam || 5.36 ||
svāgatam antataḥ || 5.37 ||
svāgataṃ sūnṛtāṃ vācam ity arthaḥ | antataḥ āpadi tṛṇodakīdānam asaṃbhave sati sūnṛtāṃ vācam uccārayed ity arthaḥ || 5.37 ||
pūjānatyāśaś ca || 5.38 ||
sūnṛtavacanād uttarakālaṃ pūjā kartavyā | anavajñākaraṇam eva pūjā | tataś cāvajñā na kartavyety arthaḥ | anatyāśaś ca grāsamātram apy abhojayitvā svayam antataḥ āpadi na bhoktavyam ity arthaḥ || 5.38 ||
śayyāsanāvasathānuvrajyopāsanāni sadṛkchreyasoḥ samāni || 5.39 ||
ātmanā tulyasya cādhikasya cātitheḥ śayyādīny ātmanulyāni kartavyāni | śayyā khaṭvādi, āsanaṃ phalakādi, āvasatham apavarakādi, anuvrajyā anugamanam, upāsanaṃ namaskārādi | anuvrajyopāsanayor ātmatulyatvāsaṃbhavād ātmanas tulyasya cādhikasya cānuvrajyopāsanaṃ ca kartavyam ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | śayyāsanādi tv ātmanaḥ sāmyena kartavyam | ātmana eva sāmyaṃ na parasparayoḥ, uttamasya saṃbhāvanīyatvāt | tathā ca vakṣyati: “samāsamābhyāṃ viṣamasame pūjātaḥ | anarcitaṃ ca” [GDh 17.18–19] iti | tataś ca sadṛkśreyasor ātmāpekṣayā tulyāni parasparāpekṣayā atulyānīti draṣṭavyam[29] || 5.39 ||
alpaśo ‘pi hīne || 5.40 ||
īṣaddhīne ‘pi samāni kartavyāni || 5.40 ||
atithilakṣaṇam āha[30] |
asamānagrāmo ‘tithir aikarātriko ‘dhivṛkṣasūryopasthāyī || 5.41 ||
anyagrāmajaḥ ekarātravasanaśīlaḥ madhyāhnād ūrdhvam upasthito ‘tithir avagantavyaḥ | evaṃrūpāya niyamena dātavyam | adāne ca pratyavāyaḥ | visamāsād ekalakṣaṇahīno ‘pi na bhavati | adhivṛkṣasūryaśabdena sāyaṃkāla ucyate | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “sāyam āgatam atithiṃ nāparādhyāt” [VaDh 8.4] iti || 5.41 ||
kiṃ punar atithir vaktavya ity ata āha |
kuśalānāmayārogyāṇām anupraśno ‘ntyaṃ
śūdrasya || 5.42 ||
ānupūrveṇātithiḥ praṣṭavyaḥ | kuśalaṃ brāhmaṇasya, anāmayaṃ kṣatriyasya, ārogyaṃ śūdrasya | tataḥ vaiśyasya smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchet kṣatrabandhum anāmayam |
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdram ārogyam eva ca || [MDh 2.127]
iti || 5.42 ||
brāhmaṇasyānatithir abrāhmaṇo ‘yajñe saṃvṛtaś
cet || 5.43 ||
brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyādir uktalakṣaṇayoge saty api nātithiḥ | ataś ca niyamato na deyam | ayajñe yajñād anyatra | yajñe tu bhavaty eva | sa ced brāhmaṇaḥ saṃvṛtaḥ daridro bhavati | yajñād anyatrāpi prabhūtānnasya tu bhavaty eva | brāhmaṇasyeti vacanāt kṣatriyāder bhavaty eva | brāhmaṇasyābrāhmaṇaniṣedhād brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇo bhavaty eva || 5.43 ||
bhojanaṃ tu kṣatriyasyordhvaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ || 5.44 ||
tuśabdaḥ pakṣasūcanārthaḥ | atithipakṣe bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu kṣatriyaṃ bhojayed iti || 5.44 ||
anyān bhṛtyaiḥ sahānṛśaṃsārtham
ānṛśaṃsārtham || 5.45 ||
anyān vaiśyādīn bhṛtyair dāsabhṛtyakarmakarādibhiḥ saha bhojayet sati vibhave | yady apy atithir na bhavati, tathāpy ānṛśaṃsārtham anukampārthaṃ pratyākhyāne nṛśaṃsatā loke syād iti || 5.45 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ
ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
idānīm atithipūjanaprasaṅgenānyeṣām apy anyaṃ pūjāprakāram āha |
pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ samavāye ‘nvaham || 6.1 ||
pādayor upaśleṣaṇam upasargadvayaprayogāt smṛtyantaropadiṣṭena vidhinā kartavyam | yathāha manuḥ: “vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇam” [MDh 2.72] ityādi | samavāye saṃgame | anvahaṃ pratidinam | kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | vakṣyamāṇānāṃ saṃgame iti draṣṭavyam || 6.1 ||
abhigamya tu viproṣya || 6.2 ||
viprakṛṣṭadeśāntarapratyāgatenābhimukhyena tadgṛhaṃ gatvā kartavyam | tuśabda ādaropasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tataś ca na yādṛcchikadarśanam udīkṣeta || 6.2 ||
keṣāṃ punar idaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
mātṛpitṛtadbandhūnāṃ pūrvajānāṃ vidyāgurūṇāṃ tadgurūṇāṃ ca || 6.3 ||
mātāpitarau prasiddhau | tadbandhavaḥ mātulamātāmahapitṛvyapitāmahādayaḥ | pūrvajāḥ ātmano bhrātaro jyeṣṭhā | vidyāguravaḥ ye aṅgādhyāpayitāraḥ, pūrvaṃ vedādhyāpayitur uktatvāt | tadguravaḥ teṣāṃ pitrādīnāṃ ye vidyāguravaḥ | cakārād gurusakhyādīnām api | eṣāṃ samavāye anvaham abhigamya ca viproṣya | pūrvajādīnāṃ visamāsaḥ tacchvaśurādigurūpasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ || 6.3 ||
saṃnidhāne parasya || 6.4 ||
yadaikasmin deśe bahūnāṃ samavāyaḥ, tadā yaḥ paraḥ pradhānaḥ tasya prathamaṃ kartavyam | sa coktaḥ: “ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām” [GDh 2.57] ityādinā || 6.4 ||
svanāma procyāham ayam ity abhivādo
jñasamavāye || 6.5 ||
svanāma pitrādikṛtam | tat prakarṣeṇoccārya tadanantaram ahaṃśabdaṃ coktvā ayam iti pratyakṣopadeśaḥ, tadartho ‘smiśabdaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī | ante, guror nāmnaḥ sthāne[31] prakṛtatvāt bhośabdaś ca | “devadattanāmāham asmi bho” iti prayojyaḥ | asminn upasaṃgrahaṇakāle evam abhivādaḥ | ābhimukhyenoccāraṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | jñasamavāye pratyabhivādanavidhijñasamavāya ity arthaḥ | pratyabhivādanavidhiś ca smṛtyantare draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro ‘bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno ‘nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.125]
tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “āmantrite svaro ‘ntyaḥ plavate saṃdhyakṣaram apragṛhyam āyāvbhāvaṃ cāpadyate, tathā bhobhāv iti” [VaDh 13.46] | āyuṣmān bhava saumya viṣṇumitrā a āyuṣmān bhava saumya dāśarathāya, ityevamādayaḥ prayogāḥ pratyabhivādanavidhau draṣṭavyāḥ | ajñasamavāye tu upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam | evam abhivādanaṃ ca | kiṃ tatra kartavyam iti cet – abhivādaye ity etāvad eva vaktavyam | abhivādanam eva kuryāt, yathāha āpastambaḥ: “dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ śrotrasamaṃ prasārya brāhmaṇo ‘bhivādayīta, uraḥsamaṃ rājanyo, madhyasamaṃ vaiśyo nīcaiḥ śūdraḥ prāñjaliṃ plāvanaṃ ca” [ĀpDh 1.5.16–17] ityādi | evaṃ ca “vyatyastapāṇinā” [MDh 2.72] ityādir upasaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ āpastambokto ‘bhivādanavidhir ity avagantavyaḥ || 6.5 ||
strīpuṃyoge ‘bhivādane ‘niyamam eke || 6.6 ||
jāyāpativiṣaye jāyayā bhartur abhivādane kartavye prāpta ity arthaḥ | yady api bhartā pratyabhivādanajñaḥ, tathāpy aniyamam eke icchanti | aham ayam ity evamādiyamo nāsti, yena kenacit prakāreṇa kartavyam iti | na tu gautamaḥ, sa tu aham ayam ity evamādinā niyamenaiva kartavyam iti | vaidagdhyāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 6.6 ||
nāviproṣya strīṇām amātṛpitṛvyabhāryābhaginīnām
|| 6.7 ||
aviproṣya na strīṇām upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | mātā prasiddhā | pitṛvyabhāryā pitur anujasya bhāryā | bhaginī sodarā jyeṣṭhā | etā varjayitvā | āsām anvaham upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryān mātaraṃ bhaginīṃ tathā |
pitṛvyabhāryāṃ ca tathā so ‘nvahaṃ tv abhigamya tu || iti |
anyāsāṃ gurusthānīyānāṃ viproṣya pratyāgatenābhivādanam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ vā kartavyam | tatrātyantagurusthānīyānāṃ mātulabhāryādīnām upasaṃgrahaṇam anyāsām abhivādanam iti draṣṭavyam || 6.7 ||
nopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ bhrātṛbhāryāṇāṃ śvaśrvāś ca || 6.8 ||
viproṣya pratyāgatenāpy atyantagurusthānīyadvāreṇa prāptaṃ pratiṣidhyate | tataś cābhivādanaṃ siddham | visamāsaḥ sakhyupasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ | śvaśurasakhibhāryopasaṃgrahaṇārthaś caśabdaḥ || 6.8 ||
ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānāṃ tu yavīyasāṃ pratyutthānam anabhivādyāḥ || 6.9 ||
ṛtvigādīnām ātmano yavīyasām upasthitānāṃ pratyutthānamātreṇaiva pūjā kartavyā | yasmād anabhivādyāḥ, ato nopasaṃgrāhyā api | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | samānavayasaś ca nābhivādyāḥ, viśeṣato yavīyāṃsa iti || 6.9 ||
tathānyaḥ pūrvaḥ pauro ‘śītikāvaraḥ śūdro ‘py apatyasamena || 6.10 ||
yathaite nābhivādyāḥ pratyuttheyās tathāyam apy anyaḥ ṛtvigādibhyaḥ pūrvo vayasā, pauraḥ samānasthānavāsī, aśītikāvaraḥ dṛḍham aśītivarṣastha ity arthaḥ | tataś ca tatprabhṛty eva draṣṭavyaḥ śūdro ‘pi | apiśabdāt kṣatriyavaiśyāv api | apatyasamena brāhmaṇena kṣatriyeṇa vaiśyena vā anya asamānajātīyo ‘pi | anyaśabde saty api apatyasamaśabdaḥ samānajātīyaviṣaya eveti kasyacid āśaṅkā syād iti || 6.10 ||
avaro ‘py āryaḥ śūdreṇa || 6.11 ||
avaro ‘py atyantanikṛṣṭavayasko ‘pi āryo dvijātiḥ śūdreṇa pratyuttheyaḥ | śūdragrahaṇaṃ vaiśyādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam, tataś ca śūdreṇa brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyāḥ pratyuttheyāḥ, vaiśyena brāhmaṇakṣatriyau, kṣatriyeṇa brāhmaṇa iti draṣṭavyaḥ | namaskārasamuccayārthaḥ apiśabdaḥ || 6.11 ||
nāma cāsya varjayet || 6.12 ||
asyagrahaṇaṃ vīpsārtham asyāsyeti, uttamasyottamasya nikṛṣṭo nikṛṣṭo na nāmoccārayet | nirupapadam iti śeṣaḥ | caśabdāt parihasanaṃ ca | tataś cottama uttamo nikṛṣṭasya nikṛṣṭasya nāmakāraṃ gṛhṇīyāt || 6.12 ||
rājñaś cājapaḥ preṣyaḥ || 6.13 ||
rājñaś ca nāma varjayet preṣyaḥ preṣaṇakaraṇaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ | nikṛṣṭatvāt prāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | sa ced ajapaḥ aśrotriya iti yāvat | cakārād vaiśyasyāpi | śūdrasya tu na doṣaḥ || 6.13 ||
bho bhavann iti vayasyaḥ samāne ‘hani jātaḥ || 6.14 ||
bho bhavann ity evam abhidhātavyaḥ | bhobhavanśabdau ca kāryāpekṣayā prayoktavyau | etāvaty eva pūjā | yo vayasyaḥ sakhā tasya lakṣaṇaṃ samāne ‘hani jāta iti | ahaḥśabdo ‘tra saṃvatsaraparyāyaḥ, “abdaḥ saṃvatsaram ahar jalam” iti naighaṇṭuke saṃvatsaraparyāyaśravaṇāt | tataś ca samāne saṃvatsare jātaḥ | loke ‘pi ca tulyasaṃvatsarajāteṣu samānavayastvaṃ prasiddham | tataś cordhvaṃ saṃvatsarād vṛddho bhavati || 6.14 ||
daśavarṣavṛddhaḥ pauraḥ || 6.15 ||
bho bhavann iti vartate | bho bhavann iti vayasyavad abhibhāṣya ity arthaḥ | daśavarṣavṛddho ‘pi pauraḥ samānasthānavāsī || 6.15 ||
pañcabhiḥ kalābharaḥ || 6.16 ||
samānasthānavāsy api yaḥ kalābhir gāndharvacitrapatracchedādibhiḥ kalāvidyādibhir jīvati sa pañcavarṣair vṛddho ‘pi vayasyavad abhibhāṣyaḥ || 6.16 ||
śrotriyaś cāraṇas tribhiḥ || 6.17 ||
śrotriyaḥ mantrabrāhmaṇādhyāyī | cāraṇa samānacaraṇaḥ |
kecin nṛttagītādiśīlaṃ varṇayanti |
tad ayuktam, kalābharaśabdenaiva gatatvāt || 6.17 ||
rājanyavaiśyakarmā vidyāhīnaḥ || 6.18 ||
rājanyakarmaṇā vā vaiśyakarmaṇā vā ya upajīvati sa utkṛṣṭakarmāpi tatsthaḥ tatkarmoparamaṇāt prāg atyantavṛddho ‘pi | yaś ca vidyāhīnaḥ so ‘pi vidyotkṛṣṭasya || 6.18 ||
dīkṣitaś ca prāk krayāt || 6.19 ||
yo vayasyatvayukto ‘pi dīkṣitaḥ saḥ somavikrayāt prāg eva vayasyaḥ | tataḥ paraṃ mānya ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | cakārād uttamāśramastho ‘pi trivṛtprāśanāt prāg eveti sāmarthyād draṣṭavyam | tathāha uśanā: “yena trivṛtprāśitaḥ sa sarveṣāṃ gurur bhavati” iti || 6.19 ||
vittabandhukarmajātividyāvayāṃsi mānyāni || 6.20 ||
vittādīnāṃ sākṣānmānyatvāsaṃbhavāt tadvanto mānyā iti draṣṭavyam | vittavān dhanāḍhyaḥ, bandhumān viśiṣṭasodaryādibahulaḥ, karmavān yathoktakārī, jātimān abhijanayuktaḥ, vidyāvān adhītavedaśāstraḥ, vayovān prakṛṣṭavayāḥ || 6.20 ||
parabalīyāṃsi || 6.21 ||
yad yat paraṃ tat tat pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād gurutaraṃ vijñeyam | tataś ca vittavato bandhumān mānyaḥ | ubhābhyām api karmavān ityādi yojyam | mānam abhivādanādi || 6.21 ||
śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ || 6.22 ||
śrutaṃ vedārthaparijñānam |
nanu ca pūrvasūtra evaitadanta upadeṣṭavyam | tataś ca parabalīyastvād evaitat sidhyatīty ataḥ pṛthag upadeśo ‘narthaḥ |
ucyate – viśeṣārtho ‘yaṃ pṛthagārambhaḥ | viśiṣṭaṃ śrutam atrābhipretam | tac ca vedārthaparijñānam | pūrvaṃ vedamātrapāṭha eva | tadviśeṣābhidhāyy eva tuśabdo ‘pi | tataś cāyaṃ sarvair api mānyaḥ || 6.22 ||
kutaḥ |
tanmūlatvād dharmasya || 6.23 ||
yac chrutavān ācarati tad api dharmasya mūlatvena pūrvam eva pratipāditam: “tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle” [GDh 1.2] iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
vedo ‘khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnām ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca || iti | [MDh 2.6]
atha vā vedārthaparijñānapūrvakatvāt smṛteḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
śrutiṃ paśyanti munayaḥ smaranti tu tathā smṛtim |
iti || 6.23 ||
śruteś ca || 6.24 ||
śruter api sa eva garīyān | śrutir api manunoktaṃ śaiśavabrāhmaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam |
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝn śiśur āṅgirasaḥ kaviḥ |
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān || [MDh 2.151]
ityādi | śrutyudāharaṇopādānaṃ śrutasyādhyayanaprakarṣe sati balīyastvajñāpanārtham || 6.24 ||
cakridaśamīsthānugrāhyavadhūsnātakarājabhyaḥ patho dānam || 6.25 ||
cakryādīnām atadguṇair anyaiḥ patho dānenopakartavyam | cakraṃ śakaṭaṃ tataḥ cakrī | daśamīstho daśamyāṃ daśāyāṃ vartamānaḥ atyantavṛddha ity arthaḥ | anugrāhyo rogārtaḥ | vadhūr garbhiṇītyevamādiḥ | snātako vidyāvratasnātakaḥ | rājā abhiṣiktaḥ || 6.25 ||
rājabrāhmaṇayoḥ samāgame –
rājñā tu śrotriyāya śrotriyāya || 6.26 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣaṇārthaḥ | yadi rājālpataraḥ syāt śrotriyas tv adhikasaṃpannaḥ prakṛta eva snātakaḥ, tatas tasmai rājñā panthā deyaḥ | evaṃ cānyai rājña eva deyaḥ | pūrveṣām api yathānugrāhyaṃ parasparasaṃnidhāne draṣṭavyam || 6.26 ||
iti gautamabhāṣye maskarīye ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ
saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
“śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ” [GDh 6.22] iti vidyāprādhānyam uktam | tadadhigamaś ca brāhmaṇāt kartavyaḥ, tasyaiva pravacanādhikārāt | yadā tu pravacanasamarthabrāhmaṇābhāvaḥ tadā katham ity āha |
āpatkalpo brāhmaṇasyābrāhmaṇād vidyopayogaḥ || 7.1 ||
āpatkalpa ityādhyāyaparisamāpter adhikāraḥ kriyate | āpat pravacanasamarthabrāhmaṇābhāvaḥ, tatra yaḥ kalpaḥ vidhiḥ saḥ āpatkalpaḥ | āpady api kṣatriyavaiśyayor brāhmaṇād eva vidyādhigamaprāpaṇārthaṃ brāhmaṇagrahaṇam | abrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyavaiśyāḥ, itareṣām adhyayanādhikārābhāvāt | tatrāpi prathamaṃ kṣatriyāt tato vaiśyād iti draṣṭavyam, “tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ” [GDh 7.7] iti darśanāt | vidyāś caturdaśa | tathā smṛtyantaram |
aṅgāni vedāś catvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyās tv etāś caturdaśa || iti |
upayogaḥ svīkaraṇam, yujeḥ saṃbandhavacanatvāt | anayaivānyathānupapattyā kṣatriyavaiśyayor adhyāpanam anumīyate | tad apy adṛṣṭārtham eva,
ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā |
yājanādhyāpane caiva viśuddhāc ca pratigrahaḥ || iti | [MDh 10.76]
brāhmaṇasyaiva dṛṣṭārthaṃ netarayor iti manunā pratiṣiddhatvāt | abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam iti vaktavye vidyopayoga ity ārambhaḥ āpadi abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam eva kartavyam iti niyamārthaḥ | tataś ca brahmahatyādau sarvasvadānacodanāyāṃ pratigrahasamarthabrāhmaṇābhāve ‘pi kṣatriyāder na dātavyam || 7.1 ||
idānīm adhyayanadvāreṇa samastaśuśrūṣāprāptāv āha |
anugamanaṃ śuśrūṣā || 7.2 ||
gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato ‘nugamanamātram eva śuśrūṣā | nānyad abhivādanādi kartavyam | adhyayanakālād anyad etad ucyate, tatrārthaprāptatvāt || 7.2 ||
samāpte brāhmaṇo guruḥ || 7.3 ||
samāpte adhyayane ‘nugamanamātram api na kartavyam | brāhmaṇasyaiva tenādhyāpakena kartavyam, “anugamanaṃ śuśrūṣāsamāpteḥ” iti vaktavye “brāhmaṇo gurur” iti vacanāt || 7.3 ||
idānīm anyo ‘py āpatkalpa ucyate |
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām || 7.4 ||
sarvagrahaṇenāpraśastās traya eva varṇā gṛhyante praśastānām anāpadi vidhānāt, śūdrasya ‘vṛttiś cen nāntareṇa śūdrāt” [GDh 17.5] ityārambhāt | pañcamyantaḥ sarvaśabdaḥ prāpnotīti cet – sakāśād ity adhyāhārān na doṣaḥ | sarveṣām iti bahuvacanam anulomopasaṃgrahārtham | brāhmaṇasyaitad ucyate, adhikārāt, “tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ” iti ca liṅgāt | tataś cāsyām avasthāyāṃ garhitayājanādibhir na doṣaḥ, śāstracodanāsāmarthyāt | tathāha manuḥ |
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanāmbusamā hi te || [MDh 10.103]
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt brāhmaṇas tv anayaṃ gataḥ || [MDh 10.102]
ityādi | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “tasmād yayā kayā ca vidhayā bahvannaṃ prāpnuyāt” [TaitUp 3.10.1] iti || 7.4 ||
pūrvaḥ pūrvo guruḥ || 7.5 ||
eṣāṃ yājanādīnāṃ yo yaḥ pūrvaṃ nirdiṣṭaḥ sa sa uttarasmād gurur jñeyaḥ | tataś ca uttareṇottareṇa jīvanāsaṃbhave pūrveṇa pūrveṇa jīved iti kramapratipādanārthaṃ pūrvavacanam | kramalaṅghane prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ sann iti vaktavye gurugrahaṇāt || 7.5 ||
tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ || 7.6 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | pūrvaprakārāyāḥ brāhmaṇavṛtter alābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ[32] savādinā upajīvanaṃ rakṣaṇādinā vā | tadgrahaṇam atyantanirguṇāpi brāhmaṇavṛttir eva varam iti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
śreyān svadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmāt svanuṣṭhitāt |
paradharmeṇa jīvan hi sadyaḥ patati jātitaḥ || iti | [MDh 10.97]
“tathā vā kṣatravṛttiḥ” ity ukte abhāve vaikalpikena vāśabdenaiva brāhmaṇavṛttyabhāve kṣatravṛttir iti labhyamāne alābhagrahaṇaṃ kṣatravṛttyā jīvato ‘pi yadā brāhmaṇavṛttir eva jivanāyālaṃ bhavati tadā kṣatravṛttim utsṛjya brāhmaṇavṛttir evāśrayitavyeti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇas tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyā bhṛte jane |
utsṛjet kṣatravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ || NSm 1.55]
|| 7.6 ||
tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ || 7.7 ||
alābhagrahaṇaṃ vṛttisaṃkaro mā bhūd iti | evaṃ ca saṃkare ‘pi prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tadgrahaṇam uktavyākhyānam | atra tu vidhānābhāvād eva uttamavṛttyāśrayaṇaprasaṅga eva nāsti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “na tu kadācij jyāyasīm” [VaDh 2.23] iti || 7.7 ||
tasyāpaṇyam || 7.8 ||
tadgrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva na kṣatriyasya vaiśyavṛttisthitasyeti | evaṃ ca vaiśyasyārthasiddhaṃ vikreyatvaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ tyaktvā datvā pratiṣiddhasyāpratiṣiddhasya cādānam | tadabhāve apaṇyam api vikreyam || 7.8 ||
kiṃ tad apaṇyam ity ata āha |
gandharasakṛtānnatilaśāṇakṣaumājināni || 7.9 ||
gandham anulepanam, rasas tailādi, kṛtānnaṃ modakādi, tilāḥ prasiddhāḥ, śāṇaṃ śaṇavikāraḥ, kṣaumaṃ kṣumā atasī tadvikāraḥ | vikārapratiṣedhāt prakṛter apratiṣedhaḥ | ajinaṃ carma || 7.9 ||
raktanirṇikte vāsasī || 7.10 ||
raktaṃ lākṣādivikṛtam, nirṇiktaṃ prakṣālitam || 7.10 ||
kṣīraṃ savikāram || 7.11 ||
kṣīraṃ payaḥ vikāraiḥ saha dadhyādibhiḥ | iha vikāragrahaṇāt tilavikārāṇām apratiṣedho vijñāyate | atha vā yatra kevalasya kṣīrasyaiva nirdeśaḥ tatra savikārasyāpi grahaṇam ity evamarthaṃ vikāragrahaṇam | yaḥ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ tasmin śāṇakṣaumayor vikāranirdeśaḥ prakṛtipratiṣedhārtha iti vaktavyam || 7.11 ||
mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsa-
tṛṇodakāpathyāni || 7.12 ||
mūlaṃ haridrādi, phalaṃ kramukaphalādi, puṣpaṃ damanakādi, auṣadhaṃ masūrādi, madhu mākṣikādi | rasagrahaṇenaiva madhunaḥ pratiṣedhe siddhe “sarvathā tu vṛttir aśaktau” [GDh 7.22] ity atrāpi pakṣe pratiṣedhārthaṃ punar vacanam | māṃsodake prasiddhe | tṛṇaṃ prasiddham | apathyaṃ viṣam || 7.12 ||
paśavaś ca hiṃsāsaṃyoge || 7.13 ||
paśavo gavādayaḥ hiṃsāsaṃyoge na vikreyāḥ | yoga iti siddhe upasargārambhād yajamānahaste ‘pi na vikreyāḥ | tatra hiṃsāyāṃ doṣābhāvād hiṃsāsaṃyogo nāstīti cet, satyam, sa tu yajamānasyaiva netarasyeti | caśabdān mayūrādīni ca || 7.13 ||
puruṣavaśākumārīvehataś ca nityam || 7.14 ||
puruṣo manuṣyaḥ, tadgrahaṇaṃ striyā api, jātivacanatvāt | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “puruṣaśīrṣam upadadhāti na striyāḥ” iti | vaśā vandhyā gauḥ | kumārī gaur eva | manuṣyakumāryāḥ strītvāt puruṣagrahaṇenaiva siddhatvāt | vehat garbhaghātinī, sāpi gaur eva, pūrvoktenaiva nyāyena | caśabdaḥ kākavandhyādyupasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ | nityam, hiṃsāsaṃyogād anyatrāpīti kecit | tad ayuktam, hiṃsāsaṃyoge paśutvād eva siddhe punar ārambhasāmarthyād evānyatra labdhuṃ śakyata iti | nityaśabdena dravyaṃ viśeṣayanti nityam evaṃrūpā iti | tad apy ayuktam, puruṣādayaḥ kadācid apy anevaṃrūpā na bhaveyur iti | tataś ca nityaśabdo ‘nyeṣāṃ tilādīnām anityatvasūcanārthaḥ | tataḥ svayaṃ kṛṣyotpāditānāṃ tilādīnāṃ vikreyatvam, anyathotpāditānām avikreyatvam iti draṣṭavyam | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāmaṃ vā svayaṃ kṛṣyotpādy tilān vikrīṇīran” [VaDh 2.31] iti | tilān api vikrīṇīran kiṃ punar anyān iti tatrārthaḥ || 7.14 ||
bhūmivrīhiyavājāvyaśvarṣabhadhenvanaḍuhaś
caike || 7.15 ||
bhūmiḥ gṛhaṃ “kṣayo veśma gṛhaṃ bhūmir niketanam” ityādi naighaṇṭuke gṛhaparyāyaśravaṇāt | vrīhiḥ prasiddhaḥ, yavaś ca, tadgrahaṇaṃ dhānyopalakṣaṇam | ajaḥ chāgaḥ | aviḥ prasiddhaḥ | aśvo hayaḥ | ṛṣabhaḥ puṅgavaḥ | dhenur dogdhrī | anaḍvān śakaṭavahanasamartho balīvardaḥ | ete na vikeyāḥ hiṃsāsaṃyogād anyatrāpīty eke ācāryāḥ, na tu gautamaḥ | uṣṭropasaṃgrahaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | yathāha uśanā: “uṣṭrarṣabhahayā na vikreyāḥ” iti | nityatvānukarṣaṇārthaś ca ity eke
|| 7.15 ||
nimayas tu || 7.16 ||
kartavya iti śeṣaḥ | nimayaḥ dravyābhyāṃ pratiṣiddhābhyāṃ parivṛttiḥ | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, samato hīnato vāpi nimayaḥ kartavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “samato hīnato vāpi nimātavyaḥ” iti
|| 7.16 ||
rasānāṃ rasaiḥ || 7.17 ||
nimaye kartavye rasānāṃ tailādīnāṃ rasair guḍādibhir eva nimayaḥ kartavya iti niyamārthaḥ sutrārambhaḥ || 7.17 ||
paśūnāṃ ca || 7.18 ||
tṛtīyāntapaśuśabdaprāpaṇārthaś cakāraḥ, paśūnāṃ paśubhir iti | pratiṣiddhavikrayapaśuviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | apratiṣiddhānāṃ vikreyatvād eva siddhatvāt || 7.18 ||
na lavaṇakṛtānnayoḥ || 7.19 ||
lavaṇaṃ rasāntareṇa na nimātavyam | rasānāṃ rasair iti niyamāt prāptaṃ pratiṣidhyate | kṛtānnaṃ yena kenacid api dravyāntareṇa, prāptyabhāvāt || 7.19 ||
tilānāṃ ca || 7.20 ||
nimayapratiṣedhānukarṣārthaś caśabdaḥ – tilānāṃ[33] yena kenacid api nimayo nāstīty arthaḥ | āpadi dhānyena samena nimayābhyanujñānārthaṃ pṛthaggrahaṇam | tathāha manuḥ:[34] “tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ” [MDh 10.94] iti || 7.20 ||
idānīṃ kṛtānnasya pratiprasavārtham āha |
samenāmena tu pakvasya saṃpratyarthe || 7.21 ||
samena tulyenāmena saha pakvasya nimayo bhavati | saṃpratyarthe yadi tasminn eva kāle kṛtānnam upabhujyate | tuśabdaḥ samaviśeṣavācī, tataś ca taṇḍuleneti draṣṭavyam |
kecit – tenaiva prakāreṇa nimayaḥ kartavyaḥ nānyeneti niyamārthaṃ tuśabdam āhuḥ |
tad ayuktam, sūtrārambhasāmarthyād eva niyamasiddheḥ | yadi yena kenacin nimayo bhavet tadānīm “nimayas tu” iti sāmānyavihitenaiva siddhatvād anārambhaḥ prāpnoti | tataś ca rasādīni yāni dravyāṇi tāni dravyaviśeṣair eva nimātavyāni | śeṣāṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ yena kenacid iti draṣṭavyam | taṇḍulaiḥ prasthādiparimitaiḥ saha tanmātrair eva pakvānnasya niyamaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca “gandharasakṛtānna” ity atra kṛtānnaśabdas taṇḍuleṣv api vartata iti jñāpayati | anyathā vikrayapratiṣedhābhāvān nimayārambo na prāpnoti, yasmāt pratiṣiddhānām eva nimayārambha iti || 7.21 ||
sarvathā tu vṛttir aśaktāv aśaudreṇa || 7.22 ||
pratiṣiddhavikrayanimayajātyantaravṛttisamuccayair api jīvet | aśaktau atyantāpadi, aśaudreṇa tatrāpi śūdravṛttim ucchiṣṭāśanaśilpavṛttyādi varjayet | āpadvihitasya kiṃcit pāpasūcanārthas tuśabdaḥ | udāhṛtaṃ ca smṛtyantaram: “kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ” iti || 7.22 ||
tad apy eke prāṇasaṃśaye || 7.23 ||
tad api śūdrakarma maraṇasaṃdehe prāpte eke icchanti, prāṇānām avaśyarakṣaṇīyatvāt | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ prāṇāḥ saṃsthitihetavaḥ |
tān nighnatā kiṃ na hataṃ rakṣatā kiṃ na rakṣitam || iti |
prāṇagrahaṇena ca tadadhīnavṛttīnāṃ putradārādīnām api grahaṇam “prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ” iti darśanāt | ekegrahaṇān na tu gautamaḥ, asyām apy avasthāyāṃ garhitatvād iti | iha prāṇasaṃśayagrahaṇāt vijñāyate anyāpy āpad astīti | tataś ca yasyām avasthāyāṃ pūrvakalpopadeśaḥ sā nityānāṃ karmaṇām anuṣṭhānasāmarthyalakṣaṇā vijñeyā | yathāha
manuḥ |
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca yaḥ |
na nirvapati pañcānām ucchavasan na sa jīvati || [MDh 3.72]
iti || 7.23 ||
tadvarṇasaṃkarābhakṣyaniyamas tu || 7.24 ||
tadgrahaṇena śūdra ucyate | saṃkaraḥ sahāsanādinā | abhakṣyaṃ taducchiṣṭādi, tadyogyaṃ vā laśunādi, tayor niyamo varjanam | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī – asyām avasthāyāṃ viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇasyeti || 7.24 ||
prāṇasaṃśaye brāhmaṇo ‘pi śastram ādadīta || 7.25 ||
prāṇagrahaṇena putradārahiraṇyāder api grahaṇam, “prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ” iti darśanāt | apiśabdād vaiśyaśūdrāv api | kṣatriyasyārthasiddhatvāt rakṣaṇādau pravṛtteḥ | yad vā kṣatriyasyāpi || 7.25 ||
rājanyo vaiśyakarma vaiśyakarma || 7.26 ||
kṣatriyo ‘pi svakarmaṇā jīvitum aśaktaḥ san vaiśyavṛttim ātiṣṭhet tasyāpy apaṇyapratiṣedho nāstīty uktam | yathā brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyavṛttir eva kṣatriyasyāpi vaiśyavṛttiḥ tathā vaiśyasyāpi śūdravṛttiḥ, yathā brāhmaṇasya vaiśyavṛttir evaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi śūdravṛttir ity evaṃ kalpanā na bhaved iti jñāpanārthaḥ sūtrārambhaḥ | kutaḥ? yadi brāhmaṇavad itareṣām abhīṣṭaṃ syāt[35] tadā sarve anantaraikāntarāṃ vṛttim ātiṣṭheyur ity avakṣyat | tad anuktvā “rājanyo vaiśyakarma” ityārambhāt kṣatriyasyaivāpadi vihitā vṛttiḥ na vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ, kṣatriyasyāpi na śūdravṛttir iti jñāpayati | tataś ca kṣatriyo ‘pi vaiśyavṛttim ātiṣṭhed āpadi | tathā ca manuḥ |
jīved etena rājanyaḥ sarveṇāpy anayaṃ gataḥ || [MDh 10.95]
iti || 7.26 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
aṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha yady āpadvihite karmaṇi kaścid doṣāparijñānāt lobhād vā prasajyeta tato ‘sau kena nivāryaḥ | rājabrāhmaṇābhyām upadeśadaṇḍābhyām | yatas tāv eva –
dvau loke dhṛtavratau rājā brāhmaṇaś ca
bahuśrutaḥ || 8.1 ||
dvāv iti prakarṣavācī na saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ dvivacananirdeśād eva dvitvasiddheḥ | asti cātiśayārthaḥ saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ, yathā: “asmin grāme dvāv eva brāhmaṇau” iti | tatra gamyate – anye ‘pi brāhmaṇāḥ santi, dvāv evānuṣṭhānaparāv iti | atrāpi rājabrāhmaṇayor atiśayena dhṛtavratatvārtham, nānyeṣāṃ pratiṣedhārtham | lokagrahaṇaṃ vipsārtham, lokasaṃghāte loke rāṣṭra ity arthaḥ | anyathā sarvakarmaṇāṃ lokādhikāratvād anarthakam evaitat syād iti | evaṃ ca yathā pratirāṣṭraṃ rājñā bhavitavyaṃ tathā bahuśrutenāpi bhavitavyam iti gamyate | dhṛtavratau vratānāṃ karmaṇāṃ dhārayitārau | rājā kṣatriyaḥ, sa cābhiṣekādiguṇayuktaḥ “sa mūrdhābhiṣikto rājā bhavati” iti śaṅkhavacanāt | brāhmaṇaś ca bahuśrutaḥ | saty api brāhmaṇasya prādhānye rājñaḥ pūrvanirdeśo bahuśrutatvasya brāhmaṇaviśeṣaṇārtham | rājñe ‘pi bahuśrutatvānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāra | tataś ca rājño ‘pi bahuśrutatvena bhavitavyam, brāhmaṇasya viśeṣeṇeti draṣṭavyam | bahuśrutaḥ bahv anena śrutam iti | rājabrāhmaṇau bahuśrutāv iti vaktavye visamāsanirdeśo rājño ‘py atiśayena śauryādiguṇayogārthaḥ | tataś ca tau dhṛtavratau syātām iti śeṣaḥ || 8.1 ||
yataḥ |
tayoś caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasyāntaḥsaṃjñānāṃ ca calanapatanasarpaṇānām āyattaṃ jīvanam || 8.2 ||
tayoḥ samuditayoḥ samānanirdeśāt | caturvidhasya manuṣyajātasya cāturvarṇyasyety arthaḥ | antaḥsaṃjñāḥ anulomapratilomāḥ cakārāt devānāṃ ca, “itaḥ pratidānād dhi devā upajīvanti” iti śruteḥ | calanāḥ sthāvarāḥ vṛkṣādayaḥ, patanāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ, sarpaṇā kīṭādayaḥ | eteṣām āyattam ādhīnaṃ jīvanaṃ prāṇadhāraṇam | kathaṃ? brāhmaṇenānugṛhyamāṇā varṇāśramiṇaḥ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthakriyānuṣṭhānāt vṛṣṭyādinimittadvāreṇa lokopakāre vartanta iti tāvad brāhmaṇāyattam | tathā ca manuḥ |
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate | [MDh 3.76]
ityādi | rājā ca paripanthinigrahadvāreṇa hiṃsānigrahadvāreṇa ca sākṣāj jīvanahetur iti || 8.2 ||
na ca jīvanamātram eva tayor āyattam, kiṃ tu –
prasūtī rakṣaṇam asaṃkaro dharmaḥ || 8.3 ||
prasūtir abhivṛddhiḥ, sā ca tadāyattā, rājabrāhmaṇadaṇḍadhāraṇopadeśābhyāṃ yathoktakāritvāt vṛṣṭyādidvāreṇa rogādyupadravābhāvāc ca vṛddhir bhavatīti | corādinigrahād rakṣaṇaṃ ca daṇḍadhāraṇaprāyaścittopadeśāt, saṃkarābhāvaś ca yamaniyamopadeśāt | vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane ca daṇḍadhāraṇād dharmo ‘pi tadāyatta eva || 8.3 ||
idānīṃ kiyatā punaḥ śrutena bahuśruto bhavatīty ata āha |
sa eṣa bahuśruto bhavati || 8.4 ||
sa eṣa iti parokṣapratyakṣopadeśaḥ | “śrutaṃ tu sarvebhyo garīyaḥ” [GDh 6.22] ity atrāpy evaṃrūpasyaiva grahaṇam iti | bahuśruto bhavati saṃpadyata ity arthaḥ | bhavatigrahaṇaṃ ca yady apy eṣāṃ lokādīnām īṣadvijñānaṃ bhavati kiṃcid anyad api śrutam, tathāpi bhavaty evety evamartham, anyathā sa eva bahuśruta ity eva siddhatvāt || 8.4 ||
lokavedavedāṅgavit || 8.5 ||
lokaśabdena lokavyavahārā grāmamaryādādaya ucyante | vedaśabdena upavedā āyurvedadhanurvedādayaḥ ucyante | dvitīyena vedaśabdena catvāro vedā ucyante | aṅgaśabdena vyākaraṇaśikṣāchandovicitikalpasūtrajyautiṣaniruktāny ucyante | vicchabdaḥ pratyakam abhisaṃbadhyate | vedavvid ity anenaiva vedapāṭho ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ, smṛtyantare anadhītavedasyānyavidyāpratiṣedhāt | yathāha manuḥ |
yo ‘nadhītya dvijo vedān anyatra kurute śramam |
sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ || [MDh 2.168]
iti || 8.5 ||
vākovākyetihāsapurāṇakuśalaḥ || 8.6 ||
vākovākyam uktipratyuktirūpaṃ tarkaśāstram ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
yas tarkeṇānusaṃdhatte sa dharmaṃ veda netaraḥ || iti | [MDh 12.106]
itihāso bhāratarāmāyaṇam | purāṇam – yatra sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārā ucyante tat purāṇaṃ brahmāṇḍādi | eteṣv api kuśalaḥ nipuṇaḥ artheṣv eva na pūrveṣv iva granthārthayoḥ, kriyāpadāntarārambhāt | evaṃ ca na kevalaṃ veda eva paṭhitavyaḥ, aṅgaṃ ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “sāṅgo vedo ‘dhyetavyaḥ” iti || 8.6 ||
tadapekṣas tadvṛttiḥ || 8.7 ||
tadapekṣaḥ vedaprasiddhārthānuvacanaśīlaḥ | tadvṛttiḥ tadabhihitakarmānuṣṭhānaparaḥ || 8.7 ||
catvāriṃśatā saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ || 8.8 ||
te ca vakṣyamāṇāḥ garbhādhānādayaḥ, taiś copetaḥ | vakṣyamāṇatvād eva siddhe saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ samuccayārtho mā bhūd iti, bahuvacanasya prayogāt triprabhṛtiṣu kṛtārthateti | “yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘pi” [GDh 8.25] ity atrāpiśabdāt samuccayo ‘pi gamyata iti cet, na, ekadeśaśabdasya saṃskārāvayavavācitvenāpi kṛtārthatvaprasaṅgāt | ata iha samuccayābhidhānāt yatra ekadeśagrahaṇena katipayasaṃskāragrahaṇasiddhiḥ || 8.8 ||
triṣu karmasv abhirataḥ || 8.9 ||
tāni sāṅgādhyayanadānejyālakṣaṇāni “dvijātīnām adhyayanam” [GDh 10.1] ity atroktāni | teṣv abhirataḥ satatānuṣṭhānaśīlaḥ | “tadvṛttiḥ” [GDh 8.7] ity anenaivānuṣṭhāne siddhe viśeṣārthaḥ punar ārambhaḥ | viśeṣaś cātiśayena tatsevanam, adṛṣṭārthatvajñāpanaṃ vā || 8.9 ||
ṣaṭsu vā || 8.10 ||
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahaiḥ saha ṣaḍ bhavanti | eṣām apy adṛṭārthatvajñāpanārthaḥ punarārambhaḥ || 8.10 ||
sāmayācārikeṣv abhivinītaḥ || 8.11 ||
sāmayikāḥ smārtāḥ upanibaddhāḥ | ācārikāḥ śiṣṭācaritāḥ padārthāḥ anupanibaddhāḥ | teṣūbhayavidheṣu karmasv abhivinītaḥ jñātvānuṣṭhānarataḥ || 8.11 ||
evaṃrūpo brāhmaṇaḥ |
ṣaḍbhiḥ parihāryo rājñā avadhyaś cābandhyaś cādaṇḍyaś cābahiṣkāryaś cāparivādyaś cāparihāryaś ceti || 8.12 ||
abuddhipūrve saty apy aparādhe vadhādiyogye, buddhipūrvakasya prasaṅgābhāvāt tadvṛttitvāt, ṣaḍbhiḥ parivarjanīyaḥ | ṣaḍgrahaṇād aparādhasamuccaye ‘pi | [vadhaḥ] punaḥ punas tāḍanam iti, tasyāprasaṅgāt | bandho nigalādiyogaḥ | daṇḍo dhanāpaharaṇam | bahiṣkaraṇaṃ grāmādibhyo nirvāsanam | parivādo doṣasaṃkīrtanam | parihāras tyāgaḥ | anumantā prayojayitā ca na syād ity evamarthaḥ cakāraḥ | itikaraṇaṃ prakāravāci | tataś ca saṃbhāṣaṇādi na kartavyam || 8.12 ||
“catvāriṃśatā saṃskāraiḥ” [GDh 8.8] ity uktam | ke punas te iti tān āha |
garbhādhānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarma-nāmakaraṇānnaprāśanacaulopanayanam || 8.13 ||
garbhādhānādayaḥ pratigṛhyam āmnātāḥ | ihābhidhānaṃ tu saṃskāratvajñāpanārtham | manunāpi caiṣāṃ saṃskāratvam uktam: “niṣekādiḥ śmaśānāntaḥ” [MDh 2.16] ityādi | garbhādhānam ṛvāv eva “saṃveśane hutvā” ityādi pratigṛhyaṃ draṣṭavyam | puṃsavanaṃ tṛtīye māsi codyate prathame garbhe nānyatra, tatra tu vyakte garbhe | pratigarbhaṃ kecid icchanti | kecid ekam eva | tathā sīmantonnayanam api | tat caturthe ṣaṣṭhe aṣṭame vā codyate | jātakarmāpi stanaprāśanānnābhivardhanād vā prāg draṣṭavyam | nāmakaraṇaṃ daśame dvādaśe vāhni puṇye nakṣatre vā | annaprāśanaṃ ṣaṣṭhe māse saṃvatsare vā | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “saṃvatsare ‘nnaprāśanaṃ ṣaṣṭhe vā” iti | caulaṃ saṃvatsare tṛtīye vā | upanayanam uktam | upaniṣkramaṇaṃ ca annaprāśana evāntarbhūtatvān noktam | tad uktaṃ manunā |
māse caturthe kartavyaṃ śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt | [MDh 2.34]
iti || 8.13 ||
kiṃ ca |
catvāri vedavratāni || 8.14 ||
brahmagrahaṇārthatvāt pṛthag upadeśaḥ | prativedaṃ catvāri catvāri gṛhītavyānīty evamarthaṃ vedagrahaṇam || 8.14 ||
kiṃ ca |
snānaṃ sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogaḥ pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyabhūta-brahmaṇām eteṣāṃ ca || 8.15 ||
