snānaṃ samāvṛttiḥ | sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogo vivāhaḥ | visamāso nāvaśyam anantaram eva vivāha iti jñāpanārthaḥ | vivāha iti vaktavye sahadharmacāriṇīgrahaṇaṃ dāraparigrahasya karmārthatvajñāpanārtham | evaṃ cākṛtadārasya vidhurasya ca pañcayajñādyabhāve na doṣaḥ | pañcānāṃ yajñānāṃ devayajñādīnām anuṣṭhānaṃ karaṇam | anuṣṭhānābhisaṃbandhārthaḥ ṣaṣṭhyā nirdeśaḥ | anuṣṭhānagrahaṇaṃ ca nāgniṣṭomādivat kadācid eva | kiṃ tu ahar ahar ity evamartham | yady api devayajñādaya eva pañca mahāyajñāḥ, tathāpi devādīnām upādānaṃ paṇca saṃskārā it jñāpanārtham, anyathā pañcayajñānuṣṭhānam eka eva saṃskāra ity āśaṅkā syād iti | devādayo vyākhyātāḥ | “eteṣāṃ ca” ity ātmaguṇasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | tataś ca pañca yajñā apy ātmaguṇasahitā eva puruṣārthaprāpakāḥ, na pṛthagbhūtā iti siddhaṃ bhavati | eteṣām iti vakṣyamāṇānām upalakṣaṇam, noktāḥ pañca yajñā eva | kutaḥ? yadi pañcayajñānām evātmaguṇasahitatvam iṣyate, tadānīṃ saguṇānāṃ pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānam ity avakṣyat | tataś ca vakṣyamāṇā apy ātmaguṇasahitā eva brahmasāyujyaprāpakā iti draṣṭavyam || 8.15 ||
aṣṭakā pārvaṇaḥ śrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇy āgrahāyaṇī caitry āśvayujīti sapta pākayajñasaṃsthāḥ || 8.16 ||
“ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās trayo ‘parapakṣāḥ teṣām ekaikasminn ekaikā bhavati” iti yā ucyante tā aṣṭakāḥ | pārvaṇaḥ (sthālīpākaḥ) parvaṇi bhavatvāt pārvaṇaḥ | śrāddhaṃ māsiśrāddham ucyate śrāvaṇīśabdena śrāvaṇapaurṇamāsīyuktatvād upākaraṇam ucyate | āgrahāyaṇīśabdena āgrahāyaṇam ucyate | caitrīśabdena śūlagavaḥ | āśvayujīti indradhvajahomaḥ | saptagrahaṇam ekaikasya gaṇanārtham | pākagrahaṇaṃ sthālīpākasaṃbandhārtham | yajñagrahaṇam agnisaṃbandhārtham | saṃsthāḥ samyaksthitāḥ gṛhya evoktā ity arthaḥ || 8.16 ||
agnyādheyam agnihotraṃ darśapaurṇamāsāv āgrayaṇaṃ cāturmāsyāni nirūḍhapaśubandhaḥ sautrāmaṇīti sapta haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ || 8.17 ||
śrutiprasiddhakarmānuvādaḥ saṃskāratvārthaḥ | atrāgrayaṇaśabdenāgrahāyaṇeṣṭir ucyate, itarasya pūrvam uktatvāt | agnyādheyam ity ādhānam ucyate | agnihotraṃ nāma haviryajñaḥ śrutau prasiddhaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsau tu saṃskāramadhye eka eva | itare prasiddhāḥ | saptagrahaṇasyoktaṃ prayojanam | śeṣasyāpy uktam eva || 8.17 ||
agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo ‘tirātro ‘ptoryāma iti sapta somasaṃsthāḥ || 8.18 ||
uktenaiva nyāyena sūtraṃ vyākhyeyam | pākayajñādīnāṃ visamāso bhinnaphalatvajñāpanārthaḥ
|| 8.18 ||
ity ete catvāriṃśat saṃskārāḥ || 8.19 ||
ity ete ityuktopasaṃhāraḥ | catvāriṃśad iti niyamārthaḥ, etāvanta eva saṃskārā iti | tataś ca samastasmārtaśrautakarmasaṃpadupeto bhavati || 8.20 ||
athāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ || 8.20 ||
vakṣyanta iti śeṣaḥ | athaśabdo ‘nantaravācī, saṃskārānantaram ātmaguṇā vakṣyanta ity arthaḥ | sarva evaitair garbhādhānādibhiḥ samuccīyante, na tu yathālābham ity evamartham aṣṭaugrahaṇam iti kecit | tad ayuktam, “yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘py aṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ” ity atrāṣṭaugrahaṇasyānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | tataś ca saṃjñārthaṃ vacanam, yatra yatrāṣṭāv ātmaguṇā ucyante tatra tatra dayādīnām eva grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti | samuccitānām eva praveśo yathā syād ity evamarthaṃ gurusaṃjñākaraṇam || 8.20 ||
dayā sarvabhūteṣu kṣāntir anasūyā śaucam anāyāso maṅgalam akārpaṇyam aspṛheti || 8.21 ||
dayā hitacintanam | tathā purāṇa uktam |
ātmavat sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ śivāya hitāya ca |
vartate satataṃ hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtsnā hy eṣā dayā smṛtā || iti |
sarvagrahaṇāt sthāvareṣv api | kṣāntiḥ bāhyābhyantaradvandvasahiṣṇutā | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
ākruṣṭo ‘bhihato vāpi nākrośen nāpi tāḍayet |
aduṣṭo vāṅmanaḥkāyaiḥ sā titikṣā kṣamā smṛtā || iti |
anasūyā guṇavatām upari pradveṣābhāvaḥ | tathāha |
yo dharmam arthaṃ kāmaṃ vā labhate mokṣam eva vā |
na dviṣyāt taṃ sadā prājñaḥ anasūyeti sā smṛtā || iti |
śaucaṃ dravyādiśaucam | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
dravyaśaucaṃ manaḥśaucaṃ vākśaucaṃ kāyikaṃ tathā |
śaucaṃ caturvidhaṃ proktam ṛṣibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || iti |
anāyāsaḥ ātmapīḍānārambhaḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
yadārambhe bhavet pīḍā nityam atyantam ātmanaḥ |
tad varjayed dharmyam api anāyāsaḥ sa ucyate || iti |
maṅgalaṃ praśastam eva | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
praśastācaraṇaṃ nityam apraśastavivarjanam |
etad dhi maṅgalaṃ proktaṃ munibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ || iti |
akārpaṇyam āpady apy adīnatā saṃvibhāgarucitvaṃ ca | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ bhaved dīno na kasyacit |
saṃvibhāgaruciś ca syād akārpaṇyaṃ tad ucyate ||
aspṛhāviṣayasaṃtoṣaḥ paradravyānabhilāṣaś ca | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
vivarjayed saṃtoṣaṃ viṣayeṣu sadā naraḥ |
paradravyābhilāṣaṃ ca sāspṛhā kathyate budhaiḥ || iti |
itikaraṇaṃ samāptyartham, etāvān puruṣasya saṃskāraḥ kartavya iti | visamāsaḥ phalapṛthaktvajñāpanārthaḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
kṣamāvān jayate bhūmiṃ dayāvān sukham aśnute |
anasūyur labhet svargaṃ śaucenādhyātmam eva ca ||
maṅgalād api saṃpūjyaḥ iha loke paratra ca |
surasāmyam anāyāsāt akārpaṇyāt prakṛtau layam ||
aspṛho labhate nityam anantaṃ sukham eva ca |
sarvais tu brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ saṃskārais tu tathaiva ca || iti |
evaṃ sarvālābhe yathālābhaparigraho ‘pi kartavyaḥ, pṛthagbhūtasyāpi phalaśravaṇāt || 8.21 ||
yasyaite catvāriṃśat saṃskārā na cāṣṭāv ātmaguṇā na sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchati || 8.22 ||
yasya tu puruṣasya | ete iti saṃskāraparāmarśo guṇānāṃ gauravārthaḥ | catvāriṃśad iti saṃhitānām apy asāmarthayajñāpanārtham | na cāṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ, cakārāt samastā vyastāś ca | etad api guṇastutiḥ | na sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchati | brahmā nāma devatāviśeṣaḥ | sāyujyam aikātmyam, sālokyaṃ samānasthānavāsitvam | nindā ca guṇastutyarthaiva | prādhānyāt sāyujyasyaiva pūrvam upadeśaḥ || 8.22 ||
yasya tu khalu saṃskārāṇām ekadeśo ‘py aṣṭāv ātmaguṇāḥ atha sa brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ sālokyaṃ ca gacchati gacchati || 8.23 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, ekadeśagrahaṇenopanayanasyaiva grahaṇam iti | khalv ity avadhāraṇe, sa eva gacchatīti | apiśabdāt samastā api | athaśabdād ānantaryeṇaiva gacchatīti | ekadeśayogāt sālokyam, samastayogāt sāyujyam iti draṣṭavyam | iyam api guṇastutiḥ || 8.23 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye aṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
rājabrāhmaṇayor atiśayena vratadhāraṇam uktam | sāṃprataṃ vratasvarūpakathanārthaṃ tadavadhikālāvadhāraṇārthaṃ cāha |
sa vidhipūrvaṃ snātvā bhāryām adhigamya yathoktān gṛhasthadarmān prayuñjāna imāni vratāny anukarṣet snātakaḥ || 9.1 ||
sa iti rājabrāhmaṇayoḥ pratyekaṃ parāmarśārtham | vidhipūrvaṃ śāstrīyena vidhinā, vidyayā vratenobhayena vā pūrvagrahaṇāt, anyathā vidhivad ity eva vaktavyam | snātveti bhāryādhigamavacanād evākṣiptam apy ucyate gṛhasthāśramābhyupagama eva snānaṃ nānyāśramapratipattāv iti jñāpanārtham | bhāryām adhigamya dāraparigrahaṃ kṛtvā |
tatra snānānantaraṃ bhāryādhigamaḥ kartavyaḥ, ktvāpratyayanirdeśāt, anāśramiṇaś cāvasthānasyāyuktatvāt, it kecid vyācakṣate |
tad ayuktam, ānantaryāsaṃbhavāt parādhīnatvāt kanyālabhasya, smṛtyantare ca vivāhakālaniyamāt | yathāha uśanā: “triṃśadvarṣo vahet kanyām” iti | tatrāpy ānantarye sati snānakālavidhānād eva siddhatvān na vaktavyam, samācārāc ca | na hi kaścit snānānantaraṃ vivāham akṛtvā prāyaścittaṃ kurvann upalabhyate, śiṣṭair vā nindyata iti, udagayanāpūryamāṇapakṣapuṇyāhapratīkṣaṇopadeśāc ca, mānave “sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam” [MDh 11.1] ity akṛtavivāhasyāvasthānopadeśāt, tatrāpi “gurvarthaniveśa” [GDh 5.22] ity atroktatvāt | ktvāpratyayas tu pūrvakālatāmātra eva vaiyākaraṇaiḥ smaryate | ānantaryeṇa saṃkīrtanam apy akṛtasnānasya gārhasthyapratiṣedhārthaṃ snātvaiva vivāhaḥ kartavya iti ||
yat tūktam anāśramiṇo ‘vasthānam ayuktam iti, tatrocyate – naivāyam anāśramī | katham? samāptabrahmacaryo gurvanujñayā snāto yāvatā kālena yatnaṃ kṛtvā bhāryām adhigacchati tāvantaṃ kālaṃ snātakavratādhigato 'viplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāntarbhūta eveti | tadartha eva “gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīm” [GDh 4.1] ity atrākṛtavivāhe ‘pi gṛhasthaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ |
na cāniyatakālam evāvasthānam, smṛtyantare āyurvibhāge nāśramapratipattividhānāt | yathāha manuḥ: “caturtham āyuṣo bhāgam” [MDh 4.1] ityādi | yathodāhṛtauśanasavacanāc ca, “pūrṇacatvāriṃśadvarṣo gṛhī bhavet” iti vyāghravacanāc ca, “snātako ‘bdatrayaṃ na tiṣṭhet” iti kaṇvavacanāc ca | tatra durjñeyatvād āyurvibhāgasya śatavarṣacaturbhāgo gṛhyate | so ‘py ekavedādhyāyinaḥ | yad uktam “catvāriṃśadvarṣaḥ” iti tad vedatrayādhyāyinaḥ | yady asau prāg eva parisamāptavidyaḥ | aparisamāptavidyaś ced yāvatā kālena parisamāpya snāto bhavet, tasmād ūrdhvaṃ varṣatrayam akṛtadāro na tiṣṭhed ity evaṃ niyatakālam evāvasthānam | tasmād ayaṃ gṛhasthāntarbhūta eva | tataś ca,
anāśramī na tiṣṭhet tu dinam ekam api dvijaḥ |
iti dakṣavacanasya vidhuraviṣayatvaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra dinam ekam apīti sati saṃbhava iti śeṣaḥ, parādhīnatvāt kanyālābhasya | atha vā,
anāśramī na tiṣṭhet tu dinam ekam api,
yatnam akṛtveti śeṣaḥ | tatra pūrvasmin pakṣe saṃbhave saty akartur doṣaḥ, asaṃbhave na doṣaḥ | uttarasmin pakṣe yatnam akartur doṣaḥ |
yathoktān gṛhasthadharmān prayuñjānaḥ imāni vratāny anukarṣet | tatraivoktatvād eva siddhe ihopadeśo “gṛhasthaḥ sadṛśīm” [GDh 4.1] ity uktānām api snātakavratāntarbhāvārtham | tatas tadatikrame ‘pi smṛtyantaroktasnātakavratalopaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
snātakavrataloke tu prāyaścittam abhojanam | iti | [MDh 11.203]
anuktā api gṛhasthadharmāḥ santīti kasyacid āśaṅkhā syād iti tannivṛttyarthaṃ yathoktagrahaṇam | tatrokagrahaṇenaiva siddhe ye brahmacāridharmāḥ “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] ity anenādhikriyante teṣām api grahaṇārthaṃ yathāgrahaṇaṃ tadatikrame ‘pi snātakavratalopaprāyaścittaṃ yathā syād iti |
nanu ca bhāryām adhigamya yathoktān dharmān prayuñjāna iti vaktavyam, bhāryādhigamanottarakālaṃ gṛhasthadharmair evādhikṛtatvād iti |
ucyate – gṛhasthasnātakadharmayoḥ pṛthaktvajñāpanārthaṃ gṛhasthagrahaṇam | uktā eva gṛhasthadharmāḥ, vakṣyamāṇās taiḥ sahitāḥ snātakadharmā iti pṛthaktvajñāpanārthaṃ rājabrāhmaṇayor eva niyamena snātakavratānuṣṭhānaṃ tadatikrame prāyaścittaṃ ca, itareṣāṃ tadanuṣṭhāne 'bhyudayaḥ, akaraṇe pratyavāyaprāyaścittābhāvaś cety evamarthaṃ ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
snātakavratalope tu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
rājabrāhmaṇayor eva netareṣāṃ kadācana || iti |
atrāpi “dvau loke dhṛtavratau” [GDh 8.1] iti viśeṣeṇa vratadhāraṇokter etad eva tātparyam |
imāni vakṣyamāṇāni vratāny anukarṣed yāvajjīvaṃ kuryāt | avaśyakartavyāni na phalārthānīty evamarthaṃ vratagrahaṇam | evaṃ ca snātakaḥ śaśvad brahmalokān na cyavata iti phalopadeśo “jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo jayeta” ity atra phalopadeśavad draṣṭavyaḥ | vakṣyamāṇāny eva vratāni na vratāntarāṇīty evam āśaṅkānivṛttyartham imānīty asyopādānam | vratatvenānuṣṭhāne siddhe ‘nukarśed iti yāvajjīvārtham | snātaka ity asyopādānaṃ[36] saṃjñārtham – yo vakṣyamāṇavratāny anutiṣṭhed asau gṛhasthaḥ snātaka ity ucyata iti | anyathā “sa vidhipūrvaṃ snātvā” ity anenaiva siddhatvān na vaktavyam || 9.1 ||
kāni punas tānīty ata āha |
nityaṃ śuciḥ sugandhasnānaśīlaḥ || 9.2 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | nityaṃ śucir ācamanādinā | nityagrahaṇād āturāvasthāyām api | sugandhair āmalakādibhiḥ snātuśīlaṃ yasyāsau sugandhasnānaśīlaḥ | “snānadantadhāvana” [GDh 2.19] iti brahmacāriprakaraṇe pratiṣiddhasya pratiprasavaḥ | śīlagrahaṇād asaṃbhave kevalasnānenāpi na doṣaḥ | visamāsaḥ snātakasya yāni nityaṃ dhāryāṇi teṣām apy upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
snātakānāṃ tu nityāni antarvāsas tathottaram |
yajñopavīte dve yaṣṭiḥ sodakaś ca kamaṇḍaluḥ || iti | [VaDh 12.14]
sugandhasnānaśīla iti suśabdopādānāt snānottarakālam apy ālepanaṃ kartavyam || 9.2 ||
sati vibhave na jīrṇamalavadvāsāḥ syāt || 9.3 ||
saty anyasaṃbhave jīrṇaṃ malavac ca vāso na dhārayet || 9.3 ||
na raktam ulbaṇam anyadhṛtaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt || 9.4 ||
raktaṃ kusumbhādinā, ulbaṇam uddhatārgham araktam, anyadhṛtaṃ guruvarjaṃ taducchiṣṭābhyanujñānāt | pṛthagārambhasāmarthyād āpady api na dhārayet | pratyekaṃ varjanārtho visamāsaḥ || 9.4 ||
na sragupānahau || 9.5 ||
pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā srag mālā, upānahau lākṣādirakte, na tu kaṣāyeṇa | sūtradvayārambho yathāsaṃkhyanivṛttyarthaḥ || 9.5 ||
nirṇiktam aśaktau || 9.6 ||
anyāsaṃbhave tu vāsaḥprabhṛti kṛtaśaucaṃ dhārayet | aśaktir anyāsaṃbhava iti || 9.6 ||
na rūḍhaśmaśrur akasmāt || 9.7 ||
śmaśrugrahaṇaṃ nakhādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | rūḍhagrahaṇān na vapanam, kiṃ tu samatvena kalpanāmātram| tathā ca manuḥ: “kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ” [MDh 6.52] iti | tatra keśakalpanā tu malāpakarṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā ślakṣṇatāpādanam | yathāha uśanā: “keśādīni nirmalāni ślakṣṇāny atyantasugandhīni snātako dhārayet” iti || 9.7 ||
nāgnim apaś ca yugapad dhārayet || 9.8 ||
yugapad ekakālam | visamāso ‘pām uttarasūtrābhisaṃbandhārthaḥ | annasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
na dhārayed apaś cāgnim apaś cānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
yugapat snātako nityaṃ tadbhāryā ca tathaiva ca || iti || 9.8 ||
nāñjalinā pibet || 9.9 ||
apaḥ prakṛtatvāt | dvihastasaṃyogo ‘ñjaliḥ | tataś caikahastena na doṣaḥ | kṣirādes tv apratiṣedhaḥ | aviśeṣān na kiṃcit pibed iti kecit | teṣāṃ pūrvasūtravisamāsaprayojanaṃ mṛgyam || 9.9 ||
na tiṣṭhan || 9.10 ||
tiṣṭhaṃś ca na pibet | tiṣṭhaṃś ceti pratiṣedhe siddhe punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ kṣīrādīnām apy upasaṃgrahārthaḥ || 9.10 ||
uddhṛtodakenācāmet || 9.11 ||
uddhṛtam udakaṃ yeneti uddhṛtodakaśabdena kamaṇḍalur ucyate, tenācāmet || 9.11 ||
na śūdrāśucyekapāṇyāvarjitena || 9.12 ||
na śūdrajātyā, aśucināpy aśūdrajātyāpi, ekahastena śucinā aśūdrajātyāpi nācāmet | anenāprāptapratiṣedhena pūrvasutrasya niyamābhāvaṃ jñāpayati | tataś ca vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ – kamaṇḍalunā, aśūdreṇa śucinobhayahastāvarjitena vācāmed iti | evaṃ ca kamaṇḍalunaivācamanasya niyamābhāvāt taddhāraṇasyāpy anityatvaṃ jñāpayati, ācamanārthatvāt taddhāraṇasyeti || 9.12 ||
na vāyvagniviprādityāpo devatā gāś ca prati paśyan vā mūtrapurīṣāmedhyāni vyudasyet || 9.13 ||
na vāyvādyabhimukhaḥ anabhimukho ‘pi caitān avalokayan mūtrādi visarjayet | devatāśabdena tatpratikṛtayo lekhyādirūpā ucyante | devatāgrahaṇenaiva vāyvādīnām api siddhe teṣām upādānaṃ nādhikopasaṃgrahārtham | tataś candragrahanakṣatrādidarśane na doṣaḥ | caśabdāt smarann api caitān na kuryāt | mūtrapurīṣayor amedhyatvāt amedhyasya punar grahaṇam amedhyaviśeṣārtham | tataḥ śuklanirasanādy api na kartavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 9.13 ||
naitā devatāḥ prati pādau prasārayet || 9.14 ||
punar devatāgrahaṇaṃ gobrāhmaṇayor devatātvārtham | pādāv iti dvivacanaṃ na vivakṣitam, ekam api na prasārayet kiṃ punaḥ dvau || 9.14 ||
na parṇaloṣṭāśmabhir mūtrapurīṣāpakarṣaṇaṃ
kuryāt || 9.15 ||
loṣṭaśabdena mṛd gṛhyate | tataś cānyair apakarṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 9.15 ||
na bhasmakeśatuṣakapālāmedhyāny adhitiṣṭhet || 9.16 ||
bhasmādīni nākrāmet | keśā manuṣyāṇām | kapālāni bhinnabhāṇḍāvayavāḥ | amedhyaṃ mūtrādi
|| 9.16 ||
na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta || 9.17 ||
mlecchāḥ pārasikādayaḥ | aśucayaḥ āryā api | adhārmikāḥ patitādayaḥ | taiḥ saha na saṃmantrayet | tṛtīyānirdeśād eva sahabhāve siddhe ‘tiśayārthaṃ sahaśabdasyopādānam | tataś cānena sahaikakāryatāṃ gatvā na saṃbhāṣeta | mārgapraśnādau tu na doṣaḥ || 9.17 ||
saṃbhāṣya puṇyakṛto manasā dhyāyet || 9.18 ||
yadi tv ajñānāt kāryavaśād vā saṃbhāṣeta tataḥ puṇyakṛtaḥ sukṛtakāriṇaḥ, bahuvacanaprayogāt bahūn, manasā cintayet | manograhaṇam avayavanirūpaṇena smaraṇārthan || 9.18 ||
brāhmaṇena vā saha saṃbhāṣeta || 9.19 ||
puṇyakṛd eva brāhmaṇaḥ | sahagrahaṇād brāhmaṇo yadi bhāṣeta | anantarānuṣṭhānārtham aprakaraṇe prāyaścittavidhānam, lāghavārthaṃ vā || 9.19 ||
adhenuṃ dhenubhavyeti brūyāt || 9.20 ||
adhenur vandhyā gauḥ, tāṃ dhenubhavyety evam abhidadhyāt | itikaraṇaḥ prakārāvarodhārthaḥ, mā bhūd anyeṣām apy evaṃrūpāṇāṃ saṃśabdanam iti | etad eva prayojanam itikaraṇānāṃ vakṣyamāṇānām api || 9.20 ||
abhadraṃ bhadram iti || 9.21 ||
abhadram abhajanīyam aśrīkatvād amaṅgalatvād vā | tad bhadram ity evam abhidadhyāt | yathā durbhagaṃ subhagam iti || 9.21 ||
kapālaṃ bhagālam iti || 9.22 ||
kapālaṃ bhinnabhāṇḍāvayavaṃ bhagālam iti brūyāt || 9.22 ||
maṇidhanur itīndradhanuḥ || 9.23 ||
indradhanur manidhanur iti brūyāt | vacanaprāmāṇyād eṣām evābhidhānam || 9.23 ||
gāṃ dhayantīṃ parasmai nācakṣīta || 9.24 ||
gāṃ pāyayantīṃ parasmai nācakṣīta | paragrahaṇād ātmīyām ācakṣīta || 9.24 ||
na caināṃ vārayet || 9.25 ||
enām iti parakīyām eva | cakārāt parītādāv api || 9.25 ||
na mithunībhūtvā śaucaṃ prati vilambeta || 9.26 ||
mithunībhūtvā striyam āsevya śaucaṃ prati na vilambeta, sadya eva śaucaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | vītyupasargād yāvatā kālena ratir na nivartate tāvantaṃ kālaṃ vilambetāpi | na maithunaviṣaya eva śaucavilambanam | kiṃ tu mūtrapurīṣādiviṣaye ‘pi na vilambanaṃ kāryam ity evamarthaṃ pratigrahaṇam |
nanu “nityaṃ śuciḥ” [GDh 9.2] ity anenaivaitat siddham |
atrocyate – atra śaucaśabdena malāpakarṣaṇamātram ucyate, ācamanārhodakāsaṃbhave tallepāpakarṣaṇaṃ yena kenacit kartavyam ity evamarthaḥ sūtrārambha iti | evaṃ ca “nityaṃ śuciḥ” [GDh 9.2] ity asya sati saṃbhave nityaṃ śuciḥ syād ity eṣo ‘rtho labhyate | tataś cāsaṃbhavād aśuceḥ sato na doṣaḥ || 9.20 ||
na ca tasmin śayane svādhyāyam adhīyīta || 9.27 ||
yasmin śayane maithunam ācaritaṃ tasmin śayane nādhīyīta svādhyāyagrahaṇād vedam | caśabdāt pūjyāṃś ca tatra nopaveśayet || 9.27 ||
na cāpararātram adhītya punaḥ pratisaṃviśet || 9.28 ||
paścime rātribhāge ‘dhītya punar na svapyāt | punargrahaṇāt pūrvam asuptasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | apararātragrahaṇāt pūrvarātre na doṣaḥ | yathā ca pūrvaṃ saṃviṣṭaḥ tathā punar na saṃviśet na tu viśramārtham apīti pratigrahaṇam || 9.28 ||
nākalyāṃ nārīm abhiramet || 9.29 ||
asvasthaśarīrām analaṃkṛtaśarīrāṃ vā akalyāṃ nārīṃ striyam nābhiramayet | abhīty upagamārtham | tataḥ vinodārthāyāḥ krīḍāyāḥ apratiṣedhaḥ || 9.29 ||
na rajasvalām || 9.30 ||
udakyāṃ ca nopeyāt |
nanu ca “udakyāgamane trirātram” [GDh 23.35] iti prāyaścittavidhānād eva pratiṣedhaḥ siddhyati |
ucyate – “trirātraṃ rajasvalāśucir bhavati” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt trirātrāntargamane prāyaścittam uktam | iha tūrdhvam anivṛtte rajasi pratiṣedhaḥ |
anye tv akālapravṛtte rajasi rajasvalām āhuḥ |
tad ayuktam, kālapravṛtte eva rakte rajaḥśabdapravṛtteḥ, tasyaivāśucikaratvāc ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
akāle yad bhavet strīṇāṃ raktam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
kāle tu tad rajaḥ proktaṃ tasmāt tatraiva sāśuciḥ || iti |
tathā ca bhārgavīyam api |
akāle yad bhavet strīṇāṃ śuci tat tu vinirdiśet |
āśuddhaṃ tad vijānīyān māsi māsi bhavet tu yat || iti |
tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
striyaḥ pavitram atulaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhicit |
māsi māsi rajo hy āsāṃ duritāny apakarṣati || iti | [VaDh 28.4]
tathā ca śrutiḥ: “saiṣā bhrūṇahatyā māsi māsy āvir bhavati” iti | tasmāt trirātrād ūrdhvaṃ pratiṣedhārthaḥ | evaṃ ca trirātrād ūrdhvam apy anuparate rajasy eṣāśucir eva | tathā ca manuḥ |
rajasy uparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā || iti | [MDh 5.65]
tatra trirātrād ūrdhvam anuparate ‘pi sparśayogyaiva, rajasvalā caturthe ‘hani snāyād iti sparśanārthatvāt snānacodanāyāḥ | rajasvalāṃ ceti siddhe uttarārthaḥ pṛthag ārambhaḥ || 9.30 ||
na caināṃ śliṣyet || 9.31 ||
enām iti trirātrābhyantararajasvalopasaṃgrahārtham | “na rajasvalām” [GDh 9.30] iti pṛthagārambhasyāpy etad eva prayojanam | evaṃ trirātrād ūrdhvam anuparate ‘py āliṅgane na doṣaḥ |
nanu ca “patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśava” [GDh 14.29] ityādinā sparśapratiṣedhād āliṅganasyāpy arthataḥ pratiṣedhaḥ sidhyatīti |
tatrocyate – āliṅgane kṛte snātakavratalopaprāyaścittapraveśārtha ārambha iti | cakāra enāṃ cākalyāṃ ceti samuccayārthaḥ || 9.31 ||
na kanyām || 9.32 ||
akṛtavivāhāṃ nāliṅgayet, udakapūrvaṃ labdhām api | kanyāṃ ceti siddhe pṛthagyogakaraṇam aduṣṭabāvenāliṅgane na doṣa iti jñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca – duṣṭabhāvenāliṅganamātreṇa kanyāyā dūṣaṇaṃ bhavati | tathā ca paiśāce vivāhe saṃgamanaśabdenāliṅganam evocyata iti yuktam |
atra kim ayaṃ kanyādūṣaṇaprāyaścittenādhikriyate, uta snātakavratalopaprāyaścitteneti |
atrocyate – snātakavratalopaprāyaścittenaivādhikāraḥ, na kanyāgamanaprāyaścittena, smṛtyantare “retaḥsekaḥ” ity ārambhāt | yathāha manuḥ |
retaḥsekaḥ svayonīṣu kumāriṣv antyajāsu ca | [MDh 11.58]
iti || 9.32 ||
agnimukhopadhamanavigṛhyavādabahirgandhamālya-
dhāraṇapāpīyasāvalekhanabhāryāsahabhojanāñjanty-
avekṣaṇakudvārapraveśapādapādadadhavanāsandīstha-
bhojananadībāhutaraṇavṛkṣaviṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇa-
prāṇavyāyacchanāni varjayet || 9.33 ||
agnimukhopadhamanaṃ agner mukhenopadhmānam | vigṛhya vādo laukiko vākkalahaḥ, jalparūpasya brahmacāriprakaraṇa eva siddhatvāt | bahirgandhamālyadhāraṇam, nirgandhamālyadhāraṇam, tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “nāgandhāṃ srajaṃ dhārayed anyatra hiraṇyasrajaḥ” iti | pāpīyasāvalekhanam aśucidravyāvayavena kāṣṭhādinā śiraḥprabhṛtikaṇḍūyanam | bhāryāsahabhojanaṃ tayā sahaikādhārasthānnabhyavahāraḥ | “ardho vā eṣa ātmano yat patnī” ity ardhavādadarśanāt prāptau satyāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ, na punar anyayā sahābhyanujñānam, smṛtyantare strīśūdrocchiṣṭasāmānyapratiṣedhāt | añjantyavekṣaṇaṃ tailādyabhyaṅgasādhanadravyasevāyāṃ bhāryāvalokanam | kudvārapraveśaṇaṃ gṛhāder aprasiddhadvārapraveśaḥ | pādapādadhāvanaṃ pādasya pādena prakṣālanam | āsandīsthabhojanaṃ pīṭhikādyavasthitādhārānnopayogaḥ | nadībāhutaraṇaṃ bāhubhyāṃ tatpāragamanam | nadīgrahaṇaṃ taṭākādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | vṛkṣasyārohaṇam, viṣamasya kūpāder avarohaṇam avataraṇam | prāṇavyāyacchanaṃ prāṇoparodhaḥ ullaṅghanādibhiḥ | etāni varjayet || 9.33 ||
na saṃdigdhāṃ nāvam adhirohet || 9.34 ||
saṃdigdhām atibhāradoṣāpahatatvāt pāragamane, tāṃ nādhirohet | adhiśabdāt nākrāmed api, niṣprayojanatvāt || 9.34 ||
katipayadharmān śarīrarakṣaṇahetūn uktvā athedānīm –
sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyet || 9.35 ||
sarvāpāyebhyaḥ śarīraṃ rakṣet | yathā – “naikaḥ prapadyetādhvānam” [MDh 4.60], “nārīsahāyaḥ” ityādi | sarvagrahaṇāt pratiṣiddhasevanenāpi | evaśabdād grahaśāntyādibhir api | ātmaśabdena śarīram ucyate, tadrakṣaṇam api yathā na vinaśyati tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | yady api kāla eva vināśakaḥ, tathāpi durjñeyatvād āyuṣaḥ pauruṣahīno na bhaved ity abhiprāyaḥ | tathā jyotiḥśāstraṃ |
āyurjñānaṃ vayojñānaṃ garbhiṇīnāṃ ca lakṣaṇam |
ṛṣayaś cāpi muhyanti kiṃ punar māṃsacakṣuṣaḥ || iti |
kecid anyathā vyācakṣate – sarvopāyaiḥ paramātmānaṃ gopāyed vijājīyāt, dhātūnām anekārthatvāt | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “ātmā mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ” [BṛĀrUp 2.4.5] iti |
anye tu – ātmaśabdena paramātmakṣetrajñajīvātmabhūtātmānaś catvāra ucyanta ity āhuḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
yo ‘syātmanaḥ kārayitā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ pracakṣate |
yaḥ karoti tu karmāṇi sa bhūtātmocyate budhaiḥ ||
jīvasaṃjño ‘natarātmā yaḥ sahajaḥ sarvadehinām |
yena vedayate sarvaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca janmasu || [MDh 12.12–13]
praśāsitāraṃ sarveṣm aṇīyāṃsam aṇor api |
rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāttaṃ puruṣaṃ param || [MDh 12.122]
iti || 9.35 ||
na prāvṛtya śiro ‘hani paryaṭet || 9.36 ||
prāvṛtya vastrādinā śiraḥ pidhāya divā samantāc caṅkramaṇaṃ na kuryāt | alpāvakāśaṃ gacched api, pariśabdaprayogāt | tatra śirovedanāparihārārthaṃ na doṣaḥ, “sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyet” [GDh 9.35] ity uktatvāt || 9.36 ||
prāvṛtya tu rātrau || 9.37 ||
arthalabdhasyāpi niyamārtha ārambhaḥ | alpāvakāśam api prāvṛtyaiva gacched iti || 9.37 ||
mūtrocchāre ca || 9.38 ||
kuryād iti śeṣaḥ | cakārāt prāvṛtyaiva || 9.38 ||
kiṃ ca |
divā kuryād udaṅmukhaḥ || 9.39 ||
divā ced udaṅmukhaḥ kuryāt | “na vāyvagniviprādityāpo devatā gāś ca pratipaśyan vā” [GDh 9.13] ity abhimukhanivāraṇāt, asya kvacid anityatāṃ darśayati | yady ayaṃ niyamaḥ tadānīm ādityābhimukhatāprāptyabhāvād iti | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
chāyāyām andhakāre ca prāṇabādhabhayeṣu[37] ca |
yathāsukhamukhaḥ kuryād iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || iti |
[cf. MDh 4.5; VaDh 6.13]
chāyādiṣv apy ādityādyabhimukhatā varjanīyety abhiprāyeṇa “na vāyvagni” [GDh 9.13] ityādisūtram ārabdham iti draṣṭavyam || 9.39 ||
saṃdhyayoś ca || 9.40 ||
udaṅmukhatvānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ || 9.40 ||
rātrau tu dakṣiṇābhimukhaḥ || 9.41 ||
tuśabdaś caśabdasyārthe | so ‘pi kuryād iti kriyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ || 9.41 ||
na bhūmāv anantardhāya || 9.42 ||
ayajñiyaiḥ tṛṇaiḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “bhūmim ayajñāṅgais tṛṇair ācchādya mūtrapurīṣe kuryāt” iti | bhūmigrahaṇād iṣṭakādau na doṣaḥ || 9.42 ||
na tiṣṭhan || 9.43 ||
tiṣṭhaṃś ca mūtrapurīṣotsargaṃ na kuryāt || 9.43 ||
nārāc cāvasathāt || 9.44 ||
gṛhasamīpe na kuryāt | caśabdād devāyatanasabhādisamīpe ca || 9.44 ||
na bhasmakarīṣakṛṣṭacchāyāpathikāmyeṣu || 9.45 ||
gṛhādiviprakṛṣṭeṣv api bhasmādiṣu na kuryāt | chāyā upajīvyacchāyā | karīṣaṃ gomayam | kāmyaṃ kamanīyapradeśaḥ āpaṇādiḥ | kāmyeṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ smṛtyantaropadiṣṭopasaṃgrahārtham | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ:
na nadyāṃ mehanaṃ kuryān na pathi na ca bhasmani |
na gomaye na kṛṣṭe nopte na śāḍvalopajīvyacchāyāsu || [VaDh 6.12]
iti || 9.45 ||
pālāśam āsanaṃ pāduke dantadhāvanam iti ca varjayet
|| 9.46 ||
pālāśam āsanādi na kuryāt | itikaraṇād anyad apy evaṃrūpaṃ rathādi, visamāsāt śayanaṃ ca | caśabdād darvyādi gṛhopakaraṇaṃ ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ gṛhopakaraṇāni ca |
varjayet pāduke caiva pālāśaṃ dantadhāvanam ||
iti || 9.46 ||
sopānatkaś cāśanāsanābhivādananamaskārān
varjayet || 9.47 ||
upānatpādo bhojanāsanābhivādanadevatāpraṇāmān varjayet | caśabdāt pādukāsthaś ca || 9.47 ||
na pūrvāhṇamadhyaṃdināparāhṇān aphalān kuryād yathāśakti dharmārthakāmebhyaḥ || 9.48 ||
pūrvāhṇādyaharvibhāgān yathāsāmarthyaṃ dharmādibhiḥ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ nāvarundhyāt | kuryāc chaktyeti vaktavye yathāgrahaṇaṃ samuccayenāpy āsevanaprāpaṇārtham | caturthīnirdeśāc ca dharmādibhir iti draṣṭavyam | tatra pūrvāhṇe yathāsaṃbhavaṃ japādi kuryāt, madhyāhne arthāharaṇopāyam, aparāhṇe gandhamālyādisevanam iti || 9.48 ||
teṣu tu dharmottaraḥ syāt || 9.49 ||
teṣu dharmārthakāmeṣu dharmapradhānaḥ syāt | itarayor āsevanāsaṃbhave ‘pi niyamena dharmaḥ kartavya ity abhiprāyaḥ | trīn api dharmānubandhinaḥ kuryād iti tuśabdaḥ || 9.49 ||
na śiśnodarapāṇipādavākcakṣuścāpalāni kuryāt || 9.50 ||
cāpalaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | śiśnacāpalam akāle maithunecchā | udaracāpalam anekarasābhilāṣaḥ | pāṇicāpalaṃ śilpakarmaśikṣābhilāṣaḥ | pādacāpalaṃ vṛthāṭanam | vākcāpalam apṛṣṭenānyāyena pṛṣṭena vā dharmādikathanam | tathāha manuḥ |
nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyān na cānyāyena pṛcchataḥ | iti | [MDh 2.110]
cakṣuścāpalam anavadhir darśanecchā[38] |
nanu ca “vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ” [GDh 2.28] ity anena kiṃcit punaruktam iti |
naitad evam, tatra vāksaṃyatatvenānibandhapralāpitvam, bāhusaṃyatatvena loṣṭamardanādi, udarasaṃyatatvena amitāpathyabhuktvam iti || 9.50 ||
chedanabhedanavilikhanavimardanāvasphoṭanāni nākasmāt kuryāt || 9.51 ||
chedanaṃ tṛṇādīnām, bhedanaṃ ghaṭādeḥ, vilikhanaṃ nakhādibhiḥ kuḍyabhūmyādīnām, vimardanaṃ loṣṭādīnāṃ saṃgharṣaṇam, avasphoṭanam aṅgulyādīnāṃ sandhisaṃśabdanam – etāni viśiṣṭena nimittena vinā na kuryāt | brahmacāriprakaraṇe bāhusaṃyatatvena siddhe chedanādīnāṃ kāraṇe saty abhyanujñānārtham || 9.51 ||
nopari vatsatantīṃ gacchet || 9.52 ||
yayā rajjvā vatso badhyate sā vatsatantī | tām upary ullaṅghya na gacchet | vatsaśabdo gojātyupalakṣaṇam || 9.52 ||
na kulaṃkulaḥ syāt || 9.53 ||
gṛhād gṛhaṃ na gacched ity arthaḥ | atha vā vinā kāraṇena gṛhāt gṛhaṃ na gacchet | kulam eva vā kulaṃ yasya saḥ kulaṃkulaḥ | saty api dharmādhikāre pitṛkula eva na tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ || 9.53 ||
na yajñam avṛto gacchet || 9.54 ||
anupāmantrito yajñaṃ neyāt || 9.54 ||
darśanāya tu kāmam || 9.55 ||
kāmam iti nāvaśyam, evam api gacchato lāghavam avaśyaṃbhāvīti | tuśabdo niyamārthaḥ, darśanārtham eva na tu bhojanārtham apīti || 9.55 ||
na bhakṣān utsaṅge bhakṣayet || 9.56 ||
bhakṣāḥ pṛthukādayaḥ | yeṣām utsaṅge bhakṣaṇaṃ prasaktaṃ tān utsaṅgopari kṛtvā na bhakṣayet
|| 9.56 ||
na rātrau preṣyāhṛtam || 9.57 ||
rātrau preṣyeṇa śūdrādinā āhṛtaṃ yat kiṃcin na bhakṣayet | kuta etat? bhakṣān iti prakṛte ekavacananirdeśāt | divānītasyāpratiṣedhaḥ || 9.57 ||
uddhṛtasnehavilayanapiṇyākamathitaprabhṛtīni cāttavīryāṇi nāśnīyāt || 9.58 ||
uddhṛtasneham āttāgramaṇḍaṃ yad[39] dadhyādi | vilayanaṃ ghṛtādiniṣpannam | piṇyākam āttarasam, itarasya doṣābhāvāt | tathā cārthavādaśrutiḥ: “vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyām anāttasāraṃ piṇyākaṃ dadyuḥ” iti | mathitaṃ takram, tad apy uddhṛtanavanītam, itarasya doṣābhāvāt | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “dadhi nirmathyānāttanavanītam aśnīyāt” iti | prabhṛtigrahaṇāt kīlāṭakucakādīni | rātryanukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | etāni rātrau nāśnīyād iti |
atha vā – uddhṛtasneha iti pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | uddhṛtasnehaṃ yad vilayana, yac ca piṇyākam, yāni ca mathitaprabhṛtīni | prabhṛtigrahaṇena dadhikṣīre ucyete | tayor uddhṛtasnehatvam āttāgramaṇḍatvam | uddhṛtasnehāni nāśnīyād iti siddhe vilayanādīnām upādānam anyeṣām āmraphalaguḍādīnām uddhṛtasnehānām abhyanujñānārtham | etāni rātrau nāśnīyāt, divā na doṣaḥ |
āttavīryāṇīti vīryaśabdena sārabhūtam ucyate | tad āttaṃ yasmin tad āttavīryam ucyate kadalīphalatvagādi | pṛthag ārambhād divāpi |
nanu cābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprakaraṇa evedaṃ vaktavyam iti |
atrocyate – evaṃ vyatikrame snātakavratalopaprāyaścittaṃ syāt, nābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittam ity atropādānam iti | apreṣyāhṛtāny api nāśnīyād rātrāv iti vilayanādīnāṃ pṛthag ārambhaḥ || 9.58 ||
sāyaṃ prātas tv annam abhipūjitam anindan bhuñjīta
|| 9.59 ||
antarā bhojanapratiṣedhārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ, arthaprāptatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
sāyaṃ prātar dvijātīnām aśanaṃ śruticoditam |
nāntarā bhojanaṃ kuryād agnihotrasamo vidhiḥ || iti |
tuśabdo viśeṣavācī, annaviśeṣasya vipratiṣedho na modakādīnām apīti | abhipūjitaṃ namaskāraḥ | abhiśabdaprayogāt samantrakaṃ darśayati | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “rocata iti sāyaṃ prātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti | anindan kadannatvādidoṣeṇākutsayan bhuñjīta || 9.59 ||
na kadācid rātrau nagnaḥ svapet || 9.60 ||
kadācit nimitteṣv api | rātrāv eveti niyamārthaṃ rātrigrahaṇam, yadi divāpīṣyate tadānīṃ na kadācin nagnaḥ svaped ity ukte sarvaṃ siddhyatīti | tataś ca divā na doṣaḥ | kiṃ ca – anenaitad api jñāpayati – divāsvapnapratiṣedho ‘sya nāstīti | anyathā tadānīm api na kadācin nagna svaped ity ukte divāsvapnapratiṣedhād evārthato divālabhyamānatvāt pāriśeṣyād rātrāv eva bhavatīti rātrigrahaṇam anarthakaṃ syād iti || 9.60 ||
snāyād vā || 9.61 ||
pṛthagārambhād divāpi | vāśabdāj jalāvataraṇam api | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “nagno jalaṃ nāvataret” iti || 9.61 ||
idānīm ānantyād ācārasyāśeṣato vaktum aśakyatvāt saṃkṣipyāha |
yac cātmavanto vṛddhāḥ samyagvinītā dambhalobhamohaviyuktā vedavida ācakṣate tat samācaret || 9.62 ||
caśabdas tuśabdasyārthe, so ‘pi viśeṣavācī, adṛṣṭam uddiśyeti | ātmavanto jñānakarmasamuccayaniṣṭhā ity arthaḥ | vṛddhāḥ vedāvasthitatattvajñānāḥ | samyagvinītāḥ gurubhiḥ suśikṣitācārāḥ | dambho lokarañjanārthaṃ dharmasevanam | lobho ‘nyāyena paradravyāditsā | moho lokaviruddhajñānam, etair viyuktā avyāptāḥ | vedavidaḥ adhītasakalavedāḥ | yad adṛṣṭārtham uddiśyācakṣate: “tvaṃ mama gobhyo yavasaṃ dehi” ityādi | dṛṣṭārthatvam “mama gobhyo yavasaṃ datvā āḍhakaṃ gṛhāṇa” ityādi | tat sakalam, amāyayā, yattador nityasaṃbandha iti yacchabdopādānād eva siddhe punas tacchabdena parāmarśāt | tad ācaret kuryāt prasanno bhūtvā, samupasargaprayogāt | sarvaguṇayogābhāve dviguṇatriguṇādiyoge kartavyam ity evamartho visamāsaḥ | ekasya niyogakaraṇād eva kṛtārtho na syāt, kiṃ tu bahūnāṃ niyogakaraṇād eveti bahuvacanaprayogaḥ | evaṃvidho yadi svayam eva niyuṅkte tad eva kartavyaṃ na puruṣāntaraniyogaḥ, vadantīti vaktavye ācakṣata iti gurusūtrakaraṇāt | ā maraṇāt punaḥ punaḥ kartavyam, kartavyam iti vaktavye ācared ity ārambhāt saṃbhūya vā pṛthag vā ekaviṣaye nānāviṣaye vā yo niyogaḥ kartavyaḥ kṛtsno ‘sau kartavya eva, ātmavatāṃ vṛddhānāṃ samyagvinītānāṃ dambhalobhamohaviyuktānāṃ vedavidāṃ vacanaṃ kartavyam iti vaktavye evam abhidhānāt || 9.62 ||
yogakṣemārtham īśvaram adhigacchet || 9.63 ||
alabdhalābho yogaḥ, labdhasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ, arthaśabdaḥ prayojanavācī, yogakṣemaprayojanam iti | īśvaraśabdena rājocyate | taṃ gacchen na kārpaṇyena, adhiśabdaprayogāt || 9.63 ||
nānyam anyatra devagurudhārmikebhyaḥ || 9.64 ||
anyaḥ ubhayakaraṇāsamarthaḥ | aprāptipūrvakapratiṣedhārambhād rājñaḥ anyo ‘pi ya ubhayakaraṇasamarthaḥ so ‘dhigantavya iti jñāpayati | anyatra varjayitvā, devatā pratimā, guruḥ pitrādiḥ, dhārmikaḥ jñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānaśīlaḥ, teṣām abhigamanasyādṛṣṭārthatvāt
|| 9.64 ||
prabhūtaidhodakayavasakuśamālyopaniṣkramaṇam āryajanabhūyiṣṭham analasasamṛddhaṃ dhārmikādhiṣṭhitaṃ niketanam āvasituṃ yateta
|| 9.65 ||
bahukāṣṭhodakatṛṇadarbhapuṣparamyanirgamanabhūmim, brāhmaṇādivarṇatrayabahulam, anuṣṭhānaśīlajanasaṃkīrṇam, nyāyavṛttarājaparigṛhītaṃ ca sthānam āśrayituṃ prakarṣeṇa yatnaṃ kuryāt | samuccayābhāve yathālābhaguṇayoge ‘pi nivased ity asamāsaḥ | evaṃvidham eva dharmāya rataye ca paryāptaṃ bhaviṣyatīti || 9.65 ||
praśastamaṅgalyadevāyatanacatuṣpathādīn pradakṣiṇam āvarteta || 9.66 ||
praśastaṃ gobrāhmaṇaprajñātavanaspatyādi | maṅgalyaṃ dadhighṛtapūrṇakumbhādi | devāyatanaṃ devatāsthānam | catuṣpathaḥ prasiddhaḥ | eteṣāṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ yāyāt, nāpasavyam, yathaitāni gacchataḥ pradakṣiṇenātikrāmanti, na punar ekaikasya bhramaṇam, kuryād iti vaktavye āvartetety ārambhāt || 9.66 ||
manasā vā tatsamagram ācāram anupālayed āpatkalpaḥ
|| 9.67 ||
yadi tvaritatvāt pradakṣiṇaṃ na karoti, tato manodhyānamātreṇaiva saṃpādayed ity eke varṇayanti |
tad ayuktam, manasā vānupālayed ity etāvataiva labhyamānatvāt | tasmān manasā vā – tacchabdopādānād yacchabdo ‘dhyāhartavyaḥ – yat “nityaṃ śuciḥ” ityādyācārajātum uktaṃ, tatsamagram, – samagraśabdasyopādānam anantarasyaiva mā bhūd iti, ācāragrahaṇaṃ gṛhasthadharmasyāpi grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd iti – anupālayec cintayed ity arthaḥ | āpatkalpaḥ āpadvidhiḥ | āpat tvaritatvāt tatkāryāsaṃbhavaḥ |
abhāvavaikalpikārthenaiva vāśabdenāyam artho labhyata ity āpatkalpagrahaṇam anarthakam iti cet, na, āpadvihitakarmānuṣṭhānād akaraṇadoṣābhāvamātraṃ bhavati nābhyudayo ‘pīty evamartham āpatkalpagrahaṇam iti | adhyāyānte vaktavye atropādānam “satyadharmāryavṛttaḥ” [GDh 9.68] ityādivakṣyamāṇavarjanārtham || 9.67 ||
satyadharmāryavṛttaḥ || 9.68 ||
satyadharmaś cāsāv āryavṛttaś ceti satyadharmāryavṛttaḥ | satyadharmā satyasaṃdhaḥ | yad vadati saṃkalpayati vā tad eva karotīty arthaḥ | yena vṛttenāryāṇāṃ sādhūnāṃ priyo bhavati priyabhāṣaṇādinā tad asya vṛttam ity āryavṛttaḥ || 9.68 ||
śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ śaucaśiṣṭaḥ śrutinirataḥ syāt || 9.69 ||
śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ satām adhyāpayitā na tv ayogyānām | jalādibhiḥ śaucaśiṣṭaḥ tatparaḥ | “nityaśuciḥ, na mithunībhūtvā śaucaṃ prati vilambeta” [GDh 9.2, 26] iti ca siddhe “manasā vā” [GDh 9.67] ity āpatkalpānupraveśo mā bhūd iti śaucasya punarupadeśaḥ | śrutinirato vedābhyāsaparaḥ | śrutigrahaṇaṃ manvādinivṛttyartham || 9.69 ||
nityam ahiṃsro mṛdur dṛḍhakārī damadānaśīlaḥ
|| 9.70 ||
nityagrahaṇāt nimitte ‘pi sati ahiṃsraḥ prāṇipīḍāvarjanaśīlaḥ | mṛduḥ aparādheṣv api pratyāpattiśīlaḥ | dṛḍhakārī ārabdhasya samāpayitā, ālocitakāryārambhako vā[40] | damaśīlaḥ indriyāṇāṃ svaviṣayātiprasakter nivārayitā | dānaśīlaḥ alpadhanāpi saṃvibhāgaruciḥ | sarvāsaṃbhave yathāsaṃbhavānuṣṭhāne ‘pi kṛtārthatājñāpanārtho visamāsaḥ || 9.70 ||
evamācāro mātāpitarau pūrvāparān saṃbandhān duritebhyo mokṣayiṣyan snātakaḥ śaśvad brahmalokān na cyavate na cyavate || 9.71 ||
evamācāra evaṃkarmā snātaka iti vyavahitena saṃbandhaḥ | prakṛtasyāpi punarvacanaṃ snātakavratānām etat phalam iti jñāpanārtham | mātāpitarau “pitā mātrā” ity ekaśeṣaḥ prāpnotīti cen, na, dvāv asya pitarau, tayor gṛhyamāṇatvāt – ācārya utpādakaś ceti | pūrvasaṃbandhāḥ pitāmahādayaḥ | aparasaṃbandhā mātāmahādayaḥ | etān pāpebhyo mocayitum icchan mokṣayati caitān, svayaṃ ca brahmalokān nāpaiti | ācāragrahaṇam aśeṣasnātakadharmaphalārtham, mā bhūn nityaṃ śucir ityāder eveti | śaśvadgrahaṇaṃ āpekṣikanityatvanivṛttyartham | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptijñāpanārthā || 9.71 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye navamo ‘dhyayaḥ
daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānam || 10.1 ||
adhyayanaṃ vedasya grahaṇadhāraṇābhyāsajapādirūpam | ijyā yāgaḥ devapitṛpūjā | dānaṃ yathāśakty arthijanasya dravyasaṃvibhāgaḥ | asamāsaḥ samuccayārthaḥ, dvijātīnāṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ mā bhūd iti | adhikagrahaṇād evaital labhyata iti cet, ekaikasya ekaikadharmaprādhānyajñāpanārtham ity adoṣaḥ | tad yathā – brāhmaṇasyādhyayanaṃ pradhānam, yathāha manuḥ |
japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyet brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ | [MDh 2.87]
ityādi | kṣatriyasyejyā | tathāha “yajeta rājā kratubhiḥ” [MDh 7.79] iti | vaiśyasya dānam, tathā cāha |
dadyāc ca sarvabhūtebhyo ‘py annam eva prayatnataḥ | iti | [MDh 9.333]
pūrvasya pūrvasya vā balīyastvajñāpanārtho ‘pi visamāsaḥ | yathāha uśanā |
dānāc chataguṇo yāgo yāgāc chataguṇo japaḥ || iti |
tathā manunāpi yāgāj japasya prādhānyaṃ darśitam |
ye pākayajñāś catvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || iti | [MDh 2.86]
atra dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānam ity upanayanottarakālam eva dānasya vidhānāt, anupanītena yat kṛtaṃ dānaṃ tasyāsiddhatvaṃ draṣṭavyam || 10.1 ||
brāhmaṇasyādhikāḥ pravacanayājanapratigrahāḥ
|| 10.2 ||
brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijāter adhikārād ādhikyaṃ pūrveṣu satsu bhavatīti |
nanu “vaiśyasyādhikam” [GDh 10.48], “rājño ‘dhikam” [GDh 10.7] iti ca vakṣyati | tataś ca brāhmaṇasyaivaitad bhavatīti brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam iti |
ucyate – pūrvavan naite āśramadharmāḥ, kiṃ tu varṇadharmā iti jñāpanārtham | tataś cānupanītasyāpi pratigrahādhikāro ‘sti | yājanādhyāpane tv asaṃbhavān na staḥ | brāhmaṇasya pravacanayājanapratigrahāś ceti vaktavye adhikagrahaṇam ete vṛttyarthā api bhavantīti jñāpanārtham | pūrvasūtravad asamāso na kṛtaḥ samuccayenāpy anuṣṭhānārtham ekārthatvād vikalpo mā bhūd iti | pravacanam anuvacanam | śeṣaṃ nigadavyākhyānam || 10.2 ||
pūrveṣu niyamas tu || 10.3 ||
prathameṣv adhyayanādiṣv avaśyakartavyatā tataś cākriyāyāṃ doṣaḥ kriyāyāṃ cābhyudayaḥ | pravacanādiṣv aniyamaḥ, tataś ca kriyāyām abhyudaya akriyāyāṃ pratyavāyābhāvaś ca | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī kṣatriyavaiśyayor apy eteṣu niyamaḥ, brāhmaṇasya viśeṣata iti | ata evaivaṃ sūtraṃ na kṛtam: “dvijātīnām adhyayanam ijyā dānaṃ niyamataḥ” iti, uttareṣv api niyamādhikāraḥ prasajyeteti || 10.3 ||
ācāryajñātipriyagurudhanavidyāniyameṣu brahmaṇaḥ saṃpradānam anyatra yathoktāt || 10.4 ||
ācārya uktalakṣaṇaḥ | jñātir mātṛpitṛsaṃbandhaḥ | priyaḥ sakhā | guruḥ pitā jyeṣṭhaḥ ṛtvik ityādiḥ | dhananiyamo dhanaparivartanam | vidyāniyamo vidyāntareṇa parivartanam | brahmagrahaṇaṃ vedārtham | tataś cāṅgādāv aniyamaḥ | saṃpradānam adhyāpanam, kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | anyatra yathoktāt “anujñāta upaviśet” ityādikāyāḥ śuśrūṣāyāḥ | anyatroktād iti vaktavye yathāgrahaṇaṃ yena vidhinā adhyayanam uktaṃ tasyaivāyam apavādo na punaḥ “śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ” [GDh 6.69] ity asyāpi | tataś ca dhanavidyāniyamenāpy ayogyāya na deyam || 10.4 ||
kṛṣivāṇijye cāsvayaṃkṛte || 10.5 ||
kṛṣivāṇijye prasiddhe, te anyena kārite brāhmaṇasyādhike | caśabdāt paśupālanaṃ cāsvayaṃkṛtam eva || 10.5 ||
kusīdaṃ ca || 10.6 ||
kusīdam upacayārtho dhanaprayogaḥ, tad asvakṛtam eva, caśabdaprayogāt | pṛthaggrahaṇaṃ cāsya svayaṃkṛtasyāpy anujñānārtham | tathā ca “kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā” [GDh 12.26] iti vakṣyati || 10.6 ||
rājño ‘dhikaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām || 10.7 ||
rājñaḥ kṣatriyasyābhiṣekavato rakṣaṇaṃ pālanaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ sarvaprāṇinām | sarvaśabdaprayogāt sthāvarāṇāṃ ca taddvāreṇa vṛttir bhavatīty adhikagrahaṇam || 10.7 ||
tat punaḥ kathaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
nyāyyadaṇḍatvam || 10.8 ||
lokāviruddhadaṇḍadhāritvam, na punar aiśvaryābhimānāt svecchayā daṇḍapātanam | vakṣyati ca: “daṇḍo damanād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet” [GDh 11.30] iti | adāntanigraheṇa itareṣāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ siddhyatīti || 10.8 ||
kiṃ cānyat |
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇān śrotriyān || 10.9 ||
brāhmaṇān brahmaguṇasaṃpannān śrotriyān śrutimataḥ, sāmarthyāt svaviṣayavāsinaḥ | bharaṇam annādidānena || 10.9 ||
nirutsāhāṃś cābrāhmaṇān || 10.10 ||
nirutsāhatvam upajīvanārthakarmānuṣṭhānāsamarthatvam | abrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ | iha cābrāhmaṇagrahaṇāt pūrvasūtre labdham api brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ kriyate, kathaṃ nāmāśrotriyāṇām api brāhmaṇānāṃ jīvanāsamarthānāṃ bharaṇaṃ syād iti | caśabdād dīnānāthāṃś ca || 10.10 ||
akarāṃś copakurvāṇāṃś ca || 10.11 ||
akarāḥ pravrajitāḥ abrāhmaṇā eva, teṣām evādhikārāt | brāhmaṇapravratijānāṃ tu pratigrahānadhikārād eva noktam | tāṃś ca sādhanādidānena bibhṛyāt | caśabdād vyādhibhayādyupahatāṃś ca | upakurvāṇā lokopakāriṇo vaidyādayaḥ | ātmīyaparicārakā iti kecit | tad ayuktam, arthasiddhatvāt teṣām | cakārāt kālajñāṃś ca || 10.11 ||
yogaś ca vijaye || 10.12 ||
vijaye kartavye yogaḥ kartavyaḥ | alabdhalābho yogaḥ | vāllabhyādinā yair āṭavikādibhiḥ karo na dattaḥ tān yatnato gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | cakārāl labdhaparirakṣaṇaṃ ca | corādayo yathā na haranti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca pūrvaṃ yogaṃ kṣemaṃ ca kṛtvā paścād arijaye yatnaḥ kartavya iti draṣṭavyam || 10.12 ||
bhaye viśeṣeṇa || 10.13 ||
bhayam anyābhibhavaṃ tasmin viśeṣeṇātyantaṃ yatnato yogakṣemau kartavyau || 10.13 ||
caryā ca rathadhanubhyām || 10.14 ||
caryā rāṣṭrasya sarvato ‘ṭanam | rathagrahaṇaṃ hastyaśvādivāhanopalakṣaṇam, dhanurgrahaṇaṃ khaḍgādīnām | cakārād ekasminn avasthānaṃ ca | caryāvasthānayor vyavasthā kāryavaśād draṣṭavyā || 10.14 ||
saṃgrāme saṃsthānam anivṛttiś ca || 10.15 ||
saṃgrāme yuddhe saṃsthānaṃ nirbhayasyāvasthānam, anivṛttiś ca tatraiva prāṇaparityāgaḥ | cakārād yuddhaṃ ca | visamāsād guptāśṛayatvaṃ ca || 10.15 ||
idānīṃ nityam ahiṃsra ity asyāpavādam āha |
na doṣo hiṃsāyām āhave || 10.16 ||
āhavo yuddham | prakṛtasyāpi punarvacanaṃ viśeṣārtham – yasminn anyo ‘nyam āhūya puruṣāḥ pravartante tatraiva na doṣo nānyāvasthāyām iti || 10.16 ||
anyatra vyaśvasārathyāyudhakṛtāñjaliprakīrṇakeśa-parāṅmukhopaviṣṭasthalavṛkṣārūḍhadūtagobrāhmaṇa-vādibhyaḥ || 10.17 ||
evaṃvidhān hiṃsato doṣaḥ | viśabdaḥ tribhiḥ saṃbadhyate – vyaśvaḥ, visārathiḥ, vyāyudha iti | kṛtāñjaliḥ baddhāñjaliḥ | prakīrṇakeśo vimuktakeśaḥ | parāṅmukhaḥ anyamukhaḥ | upaviṣṭaḥ āsīnaḥ | sthalārūḍhaḥ parvatārūḍhaḥ | vṛkṣārūḍhaḥ prasiddhaḥ | dūto ‘haṃ brāhmaṇo ‘ham iti ca yo vadati, yaś ca govacchabdaṃ karoti, ebhyo ‘nyatra na doṣaḥ || 10.17 ||
kṣatriyasyābhiṣekaguṇayuktasya dharma uktaḥ | idānīm itarasyāha |
kṣatriyaś ced anyas tam upajīvet tadvṛttyā || 10.18 ||
anyo jātimātrasaṃbandhaḥ taṃ rājānam upajīvet tadvṛttyā “caryā ca rathadhanubhyām” [GDh 10.14] ityādinā || 10.18 ||
jetā labheta sāṃgrāmikaṃ vittam || 10.19 ||
yadi rājñā niyuktaḥ paraṃ nirjitya dhanam āsādayati sa tat sāṃgrāmikaṃ dhanaṃ labheta
|| 10.19 ||
asyāpavādam āha |
vāhanaṃ tu rājñaḥ || 10.20 ||
vāhanaṃ hastyādi | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | mahārghaṃ yad ratnādi tad api rājña eva, viśeṣato vāhanam iti | tataś ca vāhanaviṣaye svalpam api draṣṭavyam || 10.20 ||
uddhāraś cāpṛthagjaye || 10.21 ||
apṛthagjayaḥ sarājako jayaḥ tatroddhāro rājñaḥ sāradravyam | vāhanasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ
|| 10.21 ||
anyat tu yathārhaṃ bhājayed rājā || 10.22 ||
śauryādyapekṣayā yodhebhyo dadyāt | rājagrahaṇam evaṃ kurvann anurāgād rājā saṃpadyata iti | svayam eva vibhajanārthaṃ vā, na parair iti || 10.22 ||
adhunā rakṣaṇanimittām asya vṛttim āha |
rājñe balidānaṃ karṣakair daśamam aṣṭamaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ
vā || 10.23 ||
niyuktāya deyam iti rājagrahaṇam | pratisaṃvatsaraṃ deyam iti baligrahaṇam | dānaṃ kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | karṣakair yāvantaḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ, na tu vaiśyenaiva | daśamaṃ vāṣṭamaṃ vā ṣaṣṭhaṃ vā adhamamadhyamottamabhūbhāgakrameṇa vyavasthitavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 10.23 ||
paśuhiraṇyayor apy eke pañcāśadbhāgaḥ || 10.24 ||
paśupālanenopajīvataḥ sakāśāt paśūnāṃ pañcāśadbhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | hiraṇyaṃ vārdhuṣikasakāśāt |
samārghaṃ dhanam uddhṛtya mahārghaṃ ya prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ || [VaDh 2.41]
iti vārdhuṣikasya pratiṣedhād evāsyābhāvaḥ prāpnotīti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ, “kāmaṃ pariluptakṛtyāya pāpīyase dadyātām” [VaDh 2.43] iti vasiṣṭhena prakārāntareṇābhyanujñānāt | ekegrahaṇān na tu gautamaḥ, tatra yeṣām apratiprasavaḥ teṣu na gṛhṇīyāt | yeṣāṃ pratiṣedhābhāvād eva vārdhuṣikatvaṃ syāt, teṣu gṛhṇīyād ity evaṃ draṣṭavyam || 10.24 ||
viṃśatibhāgaḥ śulkaḥ paṇye || 10.25 ||
paṇyaṃ paṇanīyaṃ yad vaṇigbhir vikrīyate hiṃgvādi teṣu viṃśatibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | śulkagrahaṇaṃ saṃjñārtham, tataś ca “prātibhāvyavaṇikśulka” [GDh 12.38] ityādau vyavahārasiddhiḥ || 10.25 ||
mūlaphalapuṣpauṣadhamadhumāṃsatṛṇendhanānāṃ ṣāṣṭhyaḥ || 10.26 ||
mūlaṃ haridrādi, phalaṃ marīcādi, puṣpaṃ kusumbhādi, auṣadham abhayādi, tṛṇaṃ yat kiṃcit kaṭādi | śeṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ || 10.26 ||
kasmāt punar etad rājño deyam ity āha |
tadrakṣaṇadharmitvāt || 10.27 ||
teṣāṃ karadāyināṃ rakṣaṇaṃ tadrakṣaṇam, sa eva dharmaḥ yasyāsau tadrakṣaṇadharmī, tasya bhāvas tadrakṣaṇadharmitvam, tasmāt tadrakṣaṇadharmitvāt tacchīlatvād ity arthaḥ | vacanagamye ‘rthe hetuvacanaṃ deśakālāpekṣayā uktaparimāṇād apy alpatarabhāgagrahaṇārtham, itarathā sarvam eva rājā rakṣatīti sādhāraṇo ‘yaṃ hetuḥ syād iti | tathā cāha uśanā: “deśakālalābhānurūpataḥ karān prakalpayet” iti || 10.27 ||
teṣu tu nityayuktaḥ syāt || 10.28 ||
teṣu tu balyādāneṣu sarvadā saty api kāryavyagratve tatparo bhavet | tuśabdo viśeṣavācī anyeṣv api dravyārjanopāyeṣu dharmād anapeteṣu tatparo bhavet, atra viśeṣata iti || 10.28 ||
adhike na vṛttiḥ || 10.29 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | kuṭumbapoṣaṇād adhikaṃ yat kośarūpeṇānupraviṣṭaṃ tasmin kośe vṛttir na syāt, kuṭumbapoṣaṇārtham, anyatrāpadaḥ, tasmin na gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
kuṭumbapoṣaṇaṃ kuryān nityaṃ kośaṃ ca vardhayet |
anyatrāpattitaḥ kośaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt kadācana || iti |
kecid vyācakṣate – adhikena rakṣaṇadvārāgatena jīvanaṃ syād iti | tatra “rājño ‘dhikam” ity anena punaruktaprasaṅgo ‘sti uta nāstīti nirūpaṇīyam || 10.29 ||
śilpino māsi māsy ekaikaṃ karma kuryuḥ || 10.30 ||
śilpino lohakārādayaḥ māsi māsy ekaikam ahar ātmānurūpaṃ rājñaḥ karma kuryuḥ, tad eva teṣāṃ śulkaṃ nānyat kiṃcid iti || 10.30 ||
etenātmopajīvino vyākhyātāḥ || 10.31 ||
ātmopajīvino naṭanartakādayaḥ, te ‘py ekam aho rājñaḥ karma kuryur iti śilpyātmopajīvina iti vaktavye pṛthaggrahaṇam ātmopajīvinām alpopakāritvād anityatvārtham | ātmopajīvinaś cety evam api na kṛtam, smṛtyantare ‘pi śilpino yad yad uktaṃ tasya sarvasyāpy anupraveśārtham, yathāha uśanā: “śilpino māsi māsi karmaikaṃ proktam, tadabhāve kārṣāpaṇaṃ vā dadyāt” iti
|| 10.31 ||
naucakrīvantaś ca || 10.32 ||
cakraṃ śakaṭam, naucakrābhyāṃ ya upajīvanti | bahuvacanāt vardhakināpitādayaḥ | cakārāt vanyamṛgaghātakādayaḥ | pūrvavad anityā mā bhūd iti pṛthaggrahaṇam || 10.32 ||
bhaktaṃ tebhyo dadyāt || 10.33 ||
tebhyaḥ śilpiprabhṛtibhyaḥ bhaktaṃ bhojanaṃ śulkaṃ dadyāt | tadgrahaṇam anantarāṇām eva mā bhūd iti || 10.33 ||
paṇyaṃ vaṇigbhir arghāpacayena deyam || 10.34 ||
māsi māsy ekaikam ity anuvartate | śulkād adhikam idam | arghāpacayaḥ arghāvaramūlyam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
śulkaṃ dadyāt tato māsam ekaikaṃ paṇyam eva ca |
arghārdhāvaramūlyena vaṇijas te pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
pranaṣṭam asvāmikam adhigamya rājñe prabrūyuḥ
|| 10.35 ||
pranaṣṭaṃ svāmisakāśād apagatam asvāmikam avijñāyamānasvāmikam – jñāyamānasvāmikaṃ tu svāmina eva kathayet | adhigamya labdhvā rājñe nāmātyādibhya prabrūyuḥ, praśabdād amāyayā kathayeyuḥ, idam asmin deśe evaṃ cāsāditam, anviṣyatāṃ kasyaitad iti | bahuvacanaprayogāt sāmantādibhir api vaktavyam eveti || 10.35 ||
evaṃ kathitam |
vikhyāpya saṃvatsaraṃ rājñā rakṣyam || 10.36 ||
vikhyāpya paṭahakenāvaghuṣya amukajātīyaṃ vastv āsāditam, yasyaitat sa svayam upatiṣṭhatām iti | saṃvatsaraṃ nirupadravaṃ rakṣyaṃ pālanīyam | prāk saṃvatsarād yadi svāmy āgacchati tasmai deyam, rakṣaṇasya tadāyattatvāt adhikārāc ca labdhasyāpi rājaśabdasyopanyāsaḥ tadrakṣaṇe atyāptā niyoktavyā iti | tathā cāha uśanā: “vidyābhijanayuktān pūrvadṛṣṭapramāṇān vṛddhān nidhipālane niyuñjyāt” iti || 10.36 ||
ūrdhvam adhigantuś caturthaṃ rājñaḥ śeṣaḥ || 10.37 ||
saṃvatsarāt parato yenākhyātaṃ tasmai caturtham, rājñaḥ śeṣam | ākhyātuś caturthaṃ datvā śeṣaṃ svayaṃ gṛhṇīyāt uta niyuktāya deyam iti saṃdehaḥ syād iti tannirākaraṇārthaṃ śeṣaṃ rājña ity uktam || 10.37 ||
atha yo ‘rvāk saṃvatsarān mamedam iti prabrūyāt sa svāmīti katham avagamyate ity ata āha |
svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu
|| 10.38 ||
eteṣu kāraṇeṣu pratipāditeṣu jñāyamāneṣu sa svāmī bhaved iti | rikthaṃ pituḥ sakāśāt prāptam | krayo mūlyena vikrītam | saṃvibhāga utsannaṃ yad dāyādi svarūpeṇa prāptam | parigrahaḥ strīdhanam | ananyapūrvasya svīkaraṇam, yathā nadyādiṣu vṛkṣādir ity eke tatrādhigamena punar uktam asti uta nāstīti vicāraṇīyam | adhigamaḥ ākarādibhyo labdhaṃ ratnādi || 10.38 ||
etāni tāvat sarvavarṇānāṃ sādhāraṇāni |
brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdham || 10.39 ||
pratigrahopāttam, taddharmatvāt | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yājanādhyāpanopāttam api draṣṭavyam iti
|| 10.39 ||
kṣatriyasya vijitam || 10.40 ||
nigṛhya śastreṇa nirjitaṃ yat tat kṣatriyasyādhikam || 10.40 ||
nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || 10.41 ||
nirviṣṭaṃ karmaṇopāttam, vaiśyasya kṛṣyādinā, śūdrasya tu śuśrūṣādinā | adhikam iti vartate
|| 10.41 ||
pranaṣṭādhigatād adhigantuś caturtham uktam | tad viśinaṣṭi |
nidhyadhigamo rājadhanam || 10.42 ||
nidheḥ pūrvanihitasyādhigamo lābhaḥ sa rājña eva, na tato ‘dhigantuś caturthaḥ | evaṃ ca nidher anyad dravyaṃ yat tasmād eva caturthāṃśa iti draṣṭavyam |
nanu ca nidher yad anyad dravyaṃ rājñaḥ kathaṃ tatprāptiḥ, adhigamasya sādhāraṇatvenoktatvāt yena pūrvam adhigamyate tenaiva tasya gṛhyamāṇatvād iti |
atrocyate – yasminn anubhūtacihnāni dṛśyante tad rājñe kathayet itarat svayaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti evaṃ cānubhūtacihnāni muṣitvā gṛhṇato doṣaḥ | yathāha lokākṣiḥ: “anubhūtacihnāni muṣitvā gṛhṇataḥ pūrvasāhasaṃ daṇḍaḥ taddravyadviguṇaṃ ca rājā haret” iti asati dhanagrahaṇe nidhyadhigamo rājña ity ukte arājña iti pratiṣedho ‘py āśaṅkyeta || 10.42 ||
na brāhmaṇasyābhirūpasya || 10.43 ||
abhirūpaḥ ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇaś ced adhigacchet ṣaṭsu karmasu vartamāno na rājā haret” [VaDh 3.14] iti, tasya karmasādhanatvāt || 10/43 ||
abrāhmaṇo ‘py ākhyātā ṣaṣṭhaṃ labhetety eke || 10.44 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ | ākhyātā pūrvaṃ jñāpayitā | ṣaṣṭham aṃśaṃ labhetety eke manyante, cāritrakleśaphalaṃ hi tad iti | eka iti vacanān na tu gautamaḥ, aparimitasāratvān nidhīnām | ato yat kiṃcid asya deyam iti na tu ṣaṣṭha evāṃśa iti | apiśabdād brāhmaṇo ‘py anabhirūpa iti
|| 10.44 ||
rakṣyaṃ bāladhanam ā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt samāvṛtter vā || 10.45 ||
bāla aprāptaṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ | tadgrahaṇam anyeṣām api rakṣaṇāsamarthānām upalakṣaṇam | taddhanaṃ bandhubhyo rakṣet | anyebhyas tu rakṣaṇasya “rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām” [GDh 10.7] ity anenaiva siddhatvāt | ā vyavahāraprāpaṇāt rakṣaṇasāmarthyopajananāt, ā samāvṛtter vā saty api sāmarthyalakṣaṇe | vikalpas tu adhyayanādyabhiyogāpekṣayā varṇanīyaḥ || 10.45 ||
corahṛtam apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet || 10.46 ||
corādyapahṛtaṃ tata ācchidya svāmina eva pratyarpayet | na tu “jetā labheta sāṃgrāmikam” [GDh 10.19] ity anena kaṇṭakamardanavyājena vā kiṃcid upajīvet | ācchidyeti vaktavye apajityeti vacanaṃ yo ‘py anya ācchinatti asāv apy amuṣmai dāpanīya iti | svāmine dadyād iti vaktavye “yathāsthānaṃ gamayet” ity ārambhaḥ svāmyabhāve ‘pi tadbhṛtyebhyo yathārhato ‘rpayed iti | coragrahaṇaṃ balātkaraṇāder apy upalakṣaṇam || 10.46 ||
kośād vā dadyāt || 10.47 ||
anapajaye, durgadeśādiṣu gatatvāt, yāvanmātram apahṛtaṃ tat svadhanād dadyāt | kośād veti noktam, yathāsthānaṃ gamayet kośād vā gamayed iti mā bhūd āśaṅketi || 10.47 ||
vaiśyasyādhikaṃ kṛṣivaṇikpāśupālyakusīdam || 10.48 ||
kṛṣyādi prasiddham | kṛṣiś ca vaṇik ceti dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | vaṇig iti vāṇijyaṃ karmocyate
|| 10.48 ||
śūdraś caturtho varṇaḥ || 10.49 ||
śūdra iti saṃjñā śrutito dravatīti | varṇa iti trayāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ varṇasaṃjñāpratipattyartham | ataś caturṇām eva varṇasaṃjñā, nāntarajānām | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “catvāro varṇā brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ” [VaDh 2.1] iti || 10.49 ||
ekajātiḥ || 10.50 ||
nāsya dvitīyā jātir vidyate “upanayanam iti dvitiyaṃ janma” iti tasyāpi jātisaṃjñātvāt | evaṃ cāsyopanayanapratiṣedhaḥ | tataś ca tasyaiva pratiṣedhāt tasmāt prāktanānām amantrakāṇāṃ yathākālam anuṣṭhānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā ca gṛhyakāravacanam: “śūdrasyāpi niṣekapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇopaniṣkramaṇānnaprāśanacaulāny amantrakāṇi yathākālam upadiṣṭāni” iti | uparitānāṃ tūpanayanamūlatvād eva pratiṣedhasiddhiḥ || 10.50 ||
tasyāpi satyam akrodhaḥ śaucam || 10.51 ||
tasya śūdrasya | apiśabdāt utkṛṣṭā avāntarajāś ca gṛhyante | dvijātīnāṃ brahmacāriprakaraṇe snātakadharme coktānām api varṇadharmaprāpaṇārthaḥ ihānuvādaḥ anupanītānām api praveśo yathā syād iti | satyaṃ yathābhūtārthavāditvam | akrodhaḥ parānabhidrohabuddhiḥ | śaucaṃ mṛdādibhiḥ | asteyendriyasaṃyamādīnām upasaṃgrahārtho visamāsaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
ahiṃsā satyam akrodhaḥ śaucam indriyasaṃyamaḥ |
etān sāmāsikaṃ dharmam || iti | [MDh 10.63]
tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “sarveṣāṃ satyam akrodho dānam ahiṃsā prajananaṃ ca” [VaDh 4.4]
iti || 10.51 ||
ācamanārthe pāṇipādaprakṣālanam evaike || 10.52 ||
ācamanārthe snānārthe pāṇipādaprakṣālanamātram eka icchanti, amantrakaṃ snānaṃ kartavyam eveti gautamaḥ | vikalpaś ca sacchūdrāpekṣayā varṇanīyaḥ | tathāha uśanā: “sacchūdraḥ snāyāt, asacchūdraḥ pāṇipādau prakṣālayet” iti | nityasnānasyāyam | naimittikasya tu pakṣāntarābhāvāt snānam eva kartavyam |
atha vā ācamanam evocyate | tadarthaṃ pāṇipādaprakṣālanam evaika icchanti, na tu gautamaḥ | pāṇipādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā sakṛt pānaṃ ca kartavyam iti tasyābhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ: “strīśūdrau tu sakṛt sakṛt” [MDh 5.137] iti | vikalpas tu pūrvavad varṇanīyaḥ || 10.52 ||
śrāddhakarma || 10.53 ||
“atha śrāddham” ityādi [GDh 15.1] vakṣyati | karmagrahaṇaṃ samastaśrāddhopasaṃgrahārtham | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham athāparam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || iti |
śrāddhagrahaṇenaiva labhyamānatvāt karmagrahaṇaṃ samastavidhyupasaṃgrahārtham ity eke | kecin[41] mā bhūn mantrapratiṣedhāt tatsādhyavidhipratiṣedho ‘pīti || 10.53 ||
bhṛtyabharaṇam || 10.54 ||
bhṛtyadārādipoṣaṇam | arthaprāptam apy etad ucyate, mā bhūd ucchiṣṭāśanavidhānād abhṛtyatvam iti || 10.54 ||
svadāravṛttiḥ[42] || 10.55 ||
upanayanamūlatvād uparitanānāṃ sarveṣāṃ pratiṣedhe prāpte dāraparigrahamātrasya pratiprasavaḥ kriyate | samānajātīyaivāsya bhāryā na tu pratilometi svaśabdasyopādānam | tataś ca brāhmaṇādīnāṃ vijātīyaparigrahaḥ siddho bhavati | tathāha manuḥ |
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte |
te ca svā caiva rājñaḥ syus tāś ca svā cāgryajanmanaḥ || iti | [MDh 3.13]
svadāra iti siddhe vṛttiśabdasyopādānaṃ devarādiniyogapratiṣedhārtham, svadāra eva vṛttiḥ na paradāra iti | asamāso gurvarthādidānopasaṃgrahārtham | gārhasthyam evāśramo ‘sya netarāṇīti pṛthagyogakaraṇam || 10.55 ||
paricaryā cottareṣām || 10.56 ||
paricaryā paricaraṇaṃ śuśrūṣā uttareṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānām | caśabdāt kṛṣyādi ca | sacchūdrasya śuśrūṣā, itarasya kṛṣyādir it kecit | gautamasya tāvad ayam abhiprāyaḥ – yaḥ sacchūdras tenobhayam apy avirodhena kartavyam, itareṇa kṛṣyādy eveti | itareṣām iti siddhe uttaragrahaṇaṃ yo ya uttaras tatra tatra phalabhūyastvajñāpanārtham | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “pūrvasmin pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasaṃ bhūyaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.1.8] iti || 10.56 ||
idānīṃ yasya śuśrūṣā sa kathaṃ vartata ity ata āha |
tebhyo vṛttiṃ lipseta || 10.57 ||
yān paricaret tebhya eva jīvanaṃ labdhum icchet | evaṃ ca vṛttyarthina eva paricaryā nāvaśyaṃ sarvasyeti siddham || 10.57 ||
kiṃ punar ebhyaḥ prārthayet, tair evāsmai kiṃ deyam, ity āha |
jīrṇāny upānacchatravāsaḥkūrcādīni || 10.58 ||
jīrṇāny upabhuktāni | ādiśabdāc cānyāni cālpasārāṇi | yathāha manuḥ |
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ jīrṇāni vasanāni ca |
pulākāś caiva dhānyānāṃ jīrñṇāś caiva paricchadāḥ || iti | [MDh 10.125]
chatragrahaṇaṃ śikhipatrakṛtānām apy upalakṣaṇam | anyat prasiddham || 10.58 ||
ucchiṣṭāśanaṃ śilpavṛttiś ca || 10.59 ||
pānīyasyāniyamaḥ, aśanaśabdasyopādānāt | ucchiṣṭaṃ bhuktaśiṣṭam | tad apy akṛtadārasyaiva, itarasya tu pākayajñādhikārād apravṛtteḥ | kuta etat | asamāsanirdeśāt, smṛtyantarāc ca | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ śūdrāyāgṛhamedhine |
gṛhasthāya tu dātavyam anucchiṣṭaṃ dine dine || iti |
śilpavṛttiḥ takṣāyaskārādikarmopajīvanam | caśabdāt kārukarmāṇi | visamāsaḥ āpatkalpapradarśanārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
aśaknuvaṃs tu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām |
putradārātyayaṃ prāpto jīvet kārukakarmabhiḥ || iti | [MDh 8.250]
śuśrūṣāṃ kartum aśaknuvan śilpavṛttyopajīvet | tayāpi jīvanāsaṃbhave kāruvṛttir iti draṣṭavyam | śilpakārukarmaṇoś bhedo lokād avagantavyaḥ | kārukarmāṇi nāpitarajakacitrakārādīni, pratilomakarmatvād anulomakarmabhyo ninditāni | śilpāni tu takṣāyaskārādīni anulomakarmatvāt kārukarmabhyo viśiṣṭānīti || 10.59 ||
yaṃ cāryam āśrayīta bhartavyas tena kṣīṇo ‘pi || 10.60 ||
ya brāhmaṇādim āśrayet paricaret tenāsau poṣaṇīyaḥ | caśabdād anāryaṃ svajātīyam api karmāntareṇāryāṇām eva paricaraṇopadeśāt | yaṃ cāśrayeteti vaktavye āryagrahaṇaṃ samānajātīye ‘pi sādhūnāṃ viśeṣajñāpanārtham | teneti tasyāvaśyakartavyatāsūcanārtham | kṣīṇo ‘pi vyādhijarādyupahato ‘pīty arthaḥ || 10.60 ||
tena cottaraḥ || 10.61 ||
tena ca śūdreṇottaro yam āśrayeta | evaṃ ca vṛttikṣīṇasya śūdrāśrayaṇam apy astīti jñāpayati | āśrayaṇam apy ātmasaṃdarśanamātram | yathāha jātūkarṇiḥ |
yo nīcam āśrayed āryaḥ ātmānaṃ darśayet sadā |
ātmānaṃ dāsavan matvā caren nīco ‘pi taṃ prati ||
caśabdād anāśrito ‘py avasannaḥ | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ |
daridro brāhmaṇo dānto vedānāṃ caiva pāragaḥ |
śūdreṇāpi sadā hy eṣa bhartavyo ‘nāśrito ‘pi san || iti |
tena cetara iti vaktavye uttargrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇa eva niyamato bhartavyaḥ, itaro ‘niyamata iti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ |
bibhṛyād brāhmaṇṃ nityaṃ sarvayatnena buddhimān |
anyaṃ cāpy ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ śūdro ‘pi dravyavān sadā ||
iti || 10.61 ||
tadartho ‘sya nicayaḥ syāt || 10.62 ||
tadarthaḥ uttarapoṣaṇārthaḥ asya śūdrasya nicayaḥ saṃcayaḥ syād bhavati | yasmād ity adhyāhartavyam | evaṃ cāsya anyārtho dravyaparigrahaḥ pratiṣiddho bhavati | hetuvacanam api pūrvavad asāradravyeṇa bharaṇaṃ na bhavati, kiṃ tu svakuṭumbāvirodhena sarvasvadāneneti
|| 10.62 ||
anujñāto ‘sya namaskāro mantraḥ || 10.63 ||
anujñātaḥ śrutau śiṣṭair vāsya sacchūdrasya na tv asataḥ | namaskāro nāma mantraḥ | so ‘yam |
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca mahāyogibhya eva ca |
namaḥ svadhāyai svāhāyai nityam eva bhavaty uta || iti |
asya pitṛkārye viniyogaḥ | yathāha gṛhyakāraḥ: “brāhmaṇān upaveśya devatādikaṃ mantraṃ japet” iti | mantragrahaṇaṃ namaskāra evāsyānujñāta iti mā bhūd āśaṅketi || 10.63 ||
pākayajñaiḥ svayaṃ yajetety eke || 10.64 ||
pākayajñāḥ pākasādhyāḥ aṣṭakādayaḥ taiḥ svayaṃ yajeta na paraṃ niyuñjyāt | evaṃ cānyasya paraniyogasiddhiḥ | ekegrahaṇāt na tu gautamaḥ | so ‘yaṃ vikalpaḥ sacchūdrāsacchūdraviṣayo vyākhyeyaḥ || 10.64 ||
na kevalaṃ śūdra evaivaṃ kuryāt, kiṃ tarhi |
sarve cottarottaraṃ paricareyuḥ || 10.65 ||
sarve nikṛṣṭāḥ adhikaṃ varṇaṃ śuśrūṣeyuḥ |
nanu ca śūdrasyoktatvāt brāhmaṇasya cottarābhāvāt kṣatriyavaiśyārthaḥ ārambhaḥ | ato bahuvacanānupapattiḥ “paricareyuḥ sarve” iti |
ucyate – samānajātīyam apy adhikaguṇaṃ hīnaḥ paricared iti bahuvacanam | sarvaśabdād anulomāś cāpi, anyathā varṇādhikārān na bhavati iti | śūdrasyāpi svajātau guṇādhikaparicaraṇārthaś cakāraḥ || 10.65 ||
āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe karmaṇaḥ sāmyaṃ || 10.66 ||
āryasya utkṛṣṭajāter anāryasya nikṛṣṭajāteḥ vyatikṣepe vyatikare parasparavyatyāse karmaṇaḥ ācārasya sāmyaṃ samatvaṃ tayor avagantavyam | āryo ‘pi brāhmaṇādiḥ anāryakarmā na śūdreṇa paricaraṇīyaḥ | anāryo ‘pi śūdro ya uktakārī sa āśrayaṇīyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ śūdrān adhikṛtyāha |
dharmepsavas tu dharmajñāḥ satāṃ vṛttim anuṣṭhitāḥ |
mantravarjaṃ na duṣyanti praśaṃsāṃ prāpnuvanti ca || iti | [MDh 12.127]
evaṃ cānena na jātisāmyam uktam, kiṃ tu jātibalam āśritya kutsitaṃ na kartavyaṃ | itaro ‘pi hīno ‘ham iti kṛtvānādaro na kartavya iti | vyatyaye sati vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane ca yad vakṣyati prāyaścittaṃ tad draṣṭavyam || 10.66 ||
iti maskarīye gautamasūtrabhāṣye daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ekādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe rājā nivārayitety uktam | adhunā taddharmān āha |
rājā sarvasyeṣṭe brāhmaṇavarjam || 11.1 ||
rājā pūrvam uktaḥ sarvasya viṣayavāsino janasya, nigrahānugrahācārabhoganiyogeṣu prabhuḥ, nigrahe aparādhinām, anugrahe ‘nugrāhyāṇām, ācāre maryādāvasthāne bhoge karagrahaṇe, niyoge puruṣāṇāṃ śiṣṭakaraṇe | asyāpavādaḥ – brāhmaṇavarjaṃ brāhmaṇān muktvety arthaḥ | apacāre ‘pi sataḥ sāntvapūrvam etān svadharme sthāpayet, na tv itaravarṇavan nigraheṇeti draṣṭavyam | ārambhaprayojanaṃ sarvakriyāsu svatantratākhyāpanārtham | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
asvatantrāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svatantraḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ | [NSm 1.29]
iti || 11.1 ||
īśatve ‘pi sati –
sādhukārī syāt sādhuvādī || 11.2 ||
sādhukārī śāstrābhihitāviruddhācaraṇaśīlaḥ | sādhuvādī vyavahāradarśanakāle pakṣānāśritavādī | yady apy asya sarveśatvam uktam, tathāpi śāstroktamaryādāṃ ca na laṅghayet, parasvāpaharaṇaparadyūtādau na pravartayed ity arthaḥ | tataḥ śāstrāvirodhipadārthaviṣaya evāsya svatantratā nāviśeṣeṇeti || 11.2 ||
kiṃ ca |
trayyām ānvīkṣikyāṃ cābhivinītaḥ || 11.3 ||
taryī ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇā | ānvīkṣikī nyāyavidyā ātmavidyā ca | anayor abhivinītaḥ kuśalaḥ | visamāsād daṇḍanītyā ca | caśabdād vārtāyāṃ ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
traividyebhyas trayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca tadvidaḥ |
ānvīkṣikīṃ cātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhaṃ ca lokataḥ || [MDh 7.43]
iti || 11.3 ||
śucir jitendriyo guṇavatsahāyopāyasaṃpannaḥ || 11.4 ||
śucir alubdhaḥ | jitendriyaḥ mṛgayādivyasanarahitaḥ | guṇavatsahāyaḥ guṇavadbhir vidyācāritrādibhir guṇair yuktaiḥ sahāyair mantrisāmantadūtādibhir upetaḥ | upāyair deśakālāvasthādyapekṣayā prayuktaiḥ sāmādibhiś ca yuktaḥ | guṇavatsahāyopāyavān iti vaktavye saṃpannagrahaṇam anisrāvamantratvopasaṃgrahārtham || 11.4 ||
samaḥ prajāsu syāt || 11.5 ||
samo vyavahāre dveṣyapriyayos tulyabhāvaḥ syāt || 11.5 ||
hitaṃ cāsāṃ kurvīta || 11.6 ||
hitaṃ cāsāṃ taṭākakhananasetubandhanārāmādīn pratigrāmaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | caśabdād dyūtamadyādīny ahitāni nivartayet || 11.6 ||
tam upary āsīnam adhastād upāsīrann anye brāhmaṇebhyaḥ || 11.7 ||
tam evaṃguṇasaṃpannaṃ rājānam upari sthitaṃ nīcaiḥ samīpataḥ severan kṣatriyādayaḥ | abrāhmaṇā iti vaktavye “anye brāhmaṇebhyaḥ” iti guṇavadbrāhmaṇopasaṃgrahaṇārtham, na tu jātimātrasyeti | tad adhastād āsīrann iti siddhe uparigrahaṇam utsavādiṣv apy uparibhāvārtham
|| 11.7 ||
te ‘py enaṃ manyeran || 11.8 ||
te ‘pi guṇavanto brāhmaṇā api enam adhastāt sthitaṃ manyeran pūjayeyur āśīrvādādibhiḥ lokapālāṃśa iti matvā | yathā ca manuḥ |
yasmād eṣa surendrāṇāṃ mātrābhyo nirmito nṛpaḥ | [MDh 7.5]
ityādi | apiśabdān nīcaiḥ sthitā aguṇavanto brāhmaṇāś ca || 11.8 ||
varṇān āśramāṃś ca nyāyato ‘bhirakṣet || 11.9 ||
varṇā anupanītā brāhmaṇādayaḥ | uttarakālam āśramāḥ | nyāyataḥ lokaśāstrāviruddhena mārgeṇa yathaiṣāṃ śāstravihitakarmānuṣṭhānopadravo lokavyavasthābhaṅgaś ca na bhavati tathā rakṣed ity arthaḥ | abhigrahaṇam ābhimukhyārtham, tataś ca svayam eva vicārya rakṣet | yeṣāṃ varnatvam āśramatvaṃ ca nāsti pratilomānām, teṣām api rakṣaṇārtho visamāsaḥ | yeṣāṃ varṇatvaṃ nāsti āśramatvam evānulomānāṃ teṣām apy upasaṃgrahārtham āśramagrahaṇam | teṣāṃ tu “śūdras caturtho varṇaḥ” [GDh 10.49] ity atra varṇatvanirākaraṇāt | “pratilomāt tu dharmahīnaḥ” [GDh 4.25] ity atropanayanavidhānād āśramatve saty api varṇatvānapagamāc ca varṇagrahaṇenaiva labhyamānatvād iti | cakārād devatāpratimāś ca | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
brāhmaṇān kṣatriyān vaiśyān śūdrān antarajāṃs tathā |
devatāpratimāś cāpi rakṣed bhūpaḥ prayatnataḥ || iti |
“rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām” [GDh 10.7] iti corādibhyo rakṣaṇasyoktatvāt | anyonyāsaṃkarārtham ihopadeśaḥ || 11.9 ||
calataś caitān svadharme sthāpayet || 11.10 ||
etān pūrvoktān yady ālasyādinā ye calanti na kurvanti tān nigṛhya svadharmam eva kārayed ity arthaḥ | cakārāt pratiṣiddhasevane ca || 11.10 ||
kasmād evaṃ karotīty āha |
dharmasya hy aṃśabhāg bhavatīti vijñāyate || 11.11 ||
hiśabdo hetvarthaḥ | yasmād rakṣaṇataḥ dharmasyāṃśo bhavatīti arakṣaṇato ‘py adharmasyety arthasiddham | aṃśaḥ ṣaṣṭho bhāgaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
sarvato dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājño bhavati rakṣaṇāt |
adharmād api ṣaḍbhāgo bhavaty asya hy arakṣataḥ || iti | [MDh 8.304]
amūrtasya dharmasya vibhāgāsaṃbhavāt, yāvān varṇāśramāṇāṃ dharma utpadyate tataḥ ṣaṣṭhāṃśaparimāṇo rājño ‘pi vihitakarmānuṣṭhānād utpadyata iti draṣṭavyam | itikaraṇaś copasaṃhārapradarśanārthaḥ, yataḥ etad evam ato rakṣet sthāpayec ceti | vijñāyata iti śrutisūcanārtham | tad api sarvadharmāṇāṃ śrutimūlatvād rakṣaṇe gauravotpādanārtham
|| 11.11 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ ca puro dadhīta || 11.12 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ puraḥ agre sarvakāryeṣu kuryāt | caśabdāt senāpatiṃ ca kuryāt || 11.12 ||
vidyābhijanavāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannam || 11.13 ||
vidyāśabdenātharvaveda ucyate, sa eva śāntyādikarmasamartha iti | abhijanaḥ cāritram, tadvān kārye pravartata iti | vāksaṃpannaḥ saṃskṛtavaktṛtvena sarvaṃ rañjatīti | rūpasaṃpannaḥ priyadarśanatvāc chraddheyavākyo bhavatīti | vayaḥsaṃpannaḥ nātiyuvā nātisthaviraḥ, asāv eva mahatkarmayogyo bhavatīti | śīlasaṃpannaḥ kṣamāyuktaḥ tasyāsādhyaṃ nāstīti || 11.13 ||
kiṃ cānyat |
nyāyavṛttaṃ tapasvinam || 11.14 ||
nyāyavṛttaḥ aviruddhalokācāropetaḥ, saḥ kṛcchre ‘pi rājānaṃ na parityajatīti | tapasvī ṛtāv eva gamanaśīla ity arthaḥ, so ‘bhyantarapraveśādiṣu yogyo bhavatīti | anye ‘pi sahāyā evaṃrūpā eva kartavyā iti yogavibhāgaḥ | visamāso dharmaśāstrakuśalopasaṃgrahārthaḥ || 11.14 ||
tatprasūtaḥ karmāṇi kurvīta || 11.15 ||
tacchabdaḥ sarvasahāyopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | prasūtaḥ abhyanujñātaḥ | tair vicāryety arthaḥ | karmāṇi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthāni kurvīta || 11.15 ||
kiṃ ca |
brahmaprasūtaṃ hi kṣatram ṛdhyate na vyathata iti ca vijñāyate || 11.16 ||
hiśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmād brāhmaṇānujñayā kṣatrakarmapravartamānam ṛdhyate avaśyaṃ tatphalena yujyata ity arthaḥ | vidhinaiva kṛtatvāt pātradoṣādyabhāvān na vyathate anyathā na bhavatīty arthaḥ | iticaśabdau śrutidvayasūcanārthau | vijñāyta iti śrutisūcanārtham | vicāryaiva kartavyānīty evamarthaḥ śrutyupanyāsaḥ || 11.16 ||
kiṃ ca |
yāni ca daivotpātacintakāḥ prabrūyus tāny ādriyeta
|| 11.17 ||
daivacintakāḥ jyotiḥśāstravidaḥ | utpātacintakāḥ śakunanimittajñāḥ | prabrūyuḥ niścayena brūyuḥ | caśabdād guravaś ca | tāny ādriyeta śraddadhīta, tadanurūpaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 11.17 ||
kasmāt? yataḥ |
tadadhīnam api hy eke yogakṣemaṃ pratijānate || 11.18 ||
daivādhīnā puruṣakārādhīnā vā karmaphalaprāptiḥ | daivaśabdena pūrvajanmopāttaṃ karmocyate, puruṣakāraśabdenaitajjanmakṛtaṃ karma | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ |
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ karma daivam ity abhidhīyate |
etajjanmakṛtaṃ yat tu puruṣakāram ihocyate || iti |
tatra puruṣakārahīnānām api paṅgvādīnāṃ nidhānādilābhadarśanāt, prakṛṣṭakarmakṛtām api phalādarśanād daivādhīnam eva phalaṃ na puruṣakārādhīnam iti manyamānāḥ tadadhīnam iti pratijānate | tacchabdena daivotpātakacintakavacanam, tenaivādṛṣṭaphalavijñānāt | tadāyattaṃ yogakṣemam | alabdhalābho yogaḥ, labdhasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ | yogas tāvat tadadhīnaḥ nimittena tallābhālābhavijñāne sati tadanurūpayatnasya karaṇākaraṇāt | kṣemaś cāpi nimittena upadravajñāne sati śāntyādinā tadapanetuṃ śakyatvāt | pratijānate pratijñāṃ kurvanti | anyābhiprāyasūcanārtho ‘piśabdaḥ |
anye py evaṃ bruvate – puruṣakāram antareṇānnādisaṃbandhasyānupapadyamānatvāt, puruṣakāra eva pravṛttānāṃ kadācid eva niṣphalatvadarśanāt, sarvadā vyabhicārābhāvāc ca puruṣakārādhīnam eveti manyamānāḥ daivotpātakavacanasya kadācid avybhicāradarśanān na tadadhīnam iti |
ekegrahaṇād ubhayasmād iti gautamaḥ | naikāntena daivasya prādhānyam, na ca puruṣakārasya | kiṃ tarhi | kadācid daivaṃ puruṣakāram apekṣate, tathā puruṣakāro ‘pi daivam iti | yathā puruṣakāro daivam apekṣate kṛṣyādau, na daivaṃ varṣādikam antareṇa kṛṣyādīnāṃ puruṣakāreṇaiva phalasiddhir iti | tathā annādilābhe daivaṃ puruṣakāram apekṣate, tam antareṇa tatsaṃbandhānupapatteḥ | gautamasyāyam abhiprāyaḥ – daive daivotpātacintakavacanam ādriyeta, puruṣakāre tv āptavacanam iti || 11.18 ||
śāntipuṇyāhasvastyayanāyuṣyamaṅgalasaṃyuktāny ābhyudayikāni vidveṣaṇasaṃvananābhicāra-dviṣadvyṛddhiyuktāni ca śālāgnau kuryāt || 11.19 ||
saṃyuktaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | śāntisaṃyuktaṃ daivotpātasūcitāpāyanivṛttyarthaṃ yat kriyate grahaśāntyādi | puṇyāhasaṃyuktaṃ dinadoṣanāśanārthaṃ vivāhādau yat kriyate, taddoṣanāśanāt tat karma śubham eva bhavatīti | svastyayanasaṃyuktaṃ yātrādau yat kriyate | tathāha vyāsaḥ |
yātrāyāṃ vāsanaṃ daivaṃ nārāsiṃhaṃ tathā yudhi |
bhaye dhyāyed vāsudevaṃ trayaṃ svastyayanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
āyuṣyasaṃyuktaṃ janmanakṣatre āyuṣyavṛddhyarthaṃ yat kriyate | maṅgalasaṃyuktam arthādyarthaṃ yat kriyate śrīhomādi | etāny evābhyudayikāny abhyudayahetutvāt | svayam evābhyudayahetutvād evaṃ na vaktavyam iti cet, tatkartur doṣābhāvasūcanatvād adoṣaḥ | evaṃ coparitanānāṃ kartuḥ kiṃcit prāyaścittam astīti jñāpayati | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
rājño ‘bhyudayikaṃ karma yas tu kuryād dvijottamaḥ |
parapīḍākaram uktvā na sa pāpena lipyate ||
parapīḍākaraṃ kṛtvā viproktād vātha niṣkrayāt |
rājanyānumatād vāpi śuddhir uktā manīṣibhiḥ || iti |
parapīḍākaratvād uparitanānāṃ pṛthag upanyāsaḥ | vidveṣaṇayuktaṃ yenāsya paraḥ prakṛtīnāṃ vidviṣṭo bhavati | saṃvananayuktaṃ yenāsya śatruḥ praṇipatati | ābhicārayuktaṃ yenāsya śatrur mriyate | vigatā ṛddhiḥ vyṛddhiḥ, vidviṣṭo vyṛddhiḥ dviṣadvyṛddhiḥ tayā yuktaṃ yenāsya paro vigataiśvaryo bhavati uccāṭanādi | upari ṛtviggrahaṇād etāni purohitaḥ kuryāt śālāgnau | caśabdāt laukike ca | tatra pūrvoktāni laukike, itarāṇi śālāgnāv iti draṣṭavyam || 11.19 ||
yathoktam ṛtvijo ‘nyāni || 11.20 ||
putrakāmeṣṭyādīny anyāni yathoktam ṛtvijaḥ kuryuḥ | rājā dakṣiṇāṃ prati lubdho mā bhūd iti yathoktagrahaṇam || 11.20 ||
tasya ca vyavahāro vedo dharmaśāstrāṇy aṅgāny upavedāḥ purāṇam || 11.21 ||
vyavahriyate aneneti vyavahāraḥ | tasya prajāpālanādhikṛtasya rājño vyavasthāsādhanāny etāni | vedādīni prasiddhāni | vede “gartā rug iva sanaye dhanānām” [RV 1.124.7] ityādivyavahāradarśanāt, dharmaśāstre varṇāśramādipratipādanāt, aṅge śabdādivyavasthāpratipādanāt, upavede astrādivyavahārapratipādanāt, purāṇe jagatsthitivijñānāt | visamāsaḥ pūrvasya pūrvasya balīyastvajñāpanārthaḥ | tataś ca pūrvottaravirodhe pūroktam evācaret | vyavahāratattvadarśane vedādīny aṅgāni bhavantīti vyavahāraśabdenocyante | etāny ālocya vyavahārān sthāpayed ity arthaḥ || 11.21 ||
kiṃ ca |
deśajātikuladharmāś cāmnāyair aviruddhāḥ pramāṇam
|| 11.22 ||
deśadharmaḥ kautukamaṅgalādiḥ | jātidharmaḥ ābhīrādīnāṃ dantarāgābhāvādiḥ | kuladharmo “dakṣiṇataś cūḍā vasiṣṭhānām” ityādi | āmnāyair vedadharmaśāstrādibhiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ bahuvacanaprayogāt, aviruddhā abādhitāḥ pramāṇam | vakṣyamāṇānāṃ karṣakādīnām apy āmnāyāviruddhatvajñāpanārthaś cakāraḥ | deśadharmādīn api samyak jñātvā āmnāyāvirodhena gṛhṇīyāt | virodhe tyaktvā ity evamartham upadeśaḥ || 11.22 ||
karṣakavaṇikpaśupālakusīdikāravaḥ sve sve varge
|| 11.23 ||
pramāṇam iti vartate | rājani praṣṭum aśakye svavarga eva praṣṭavya ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | karṣakāḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ | vaṇijaḥ prasiddhāḥ | paśupālā gopālāḥ | kusīdino vārdhuṣikāḥ | kāravo ‘yaskārādayaḥ | svavarga iti vaktavye vīpsāvacanaṃ na kevalaṃ karṣakādaya eva svavarge pramāṇam, kiṃ tu brāhmaṇādayo ‘pīti | tatrāpi smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt tryavarā eva praṣṭavyāḥ, “svavarge tryavarā dharmān prabrūyuḥ” iti prajāpativacanāt | “āmnāyair aviruddhā pramāṇam” [GDh 11.22] ity uktatvāt yadi te tadviruddhaṃ brūyus[43] tadā sarvair brāhmaṇasakāśaṃ gantavyam, teṣām eva tadavirodhena vaktuṃ sāmartham iti | “tasya vyavahāraḥ” [GDh 11.21] ity uktatvāt | tatrāpy aparitoṣe rājasakāśaṃ gantavyam iti draṣṭavyam | tatra tenāpy āmnyāyāvirodhena vaktavyatvāt ekasyaiva tadavirodhajñāpanasāmarthyābhāvāt, tajjñaiḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha brūyād ity arthasiddham | tathā ca manuḥ |
vyavahāraṃ didṛkṣus tu brāhmanaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ | [MDh 8.1]
ityādi || 11.23 ||
idānīṃ yadi karṣakādayo rājānam eva gaccheyus tadā kathaṃ rājā paricchinattīty āha |
tebhyo yathādhikāram arthān pratyavahṛtya dharmavyavasthā || 11.24 ||
tebhyaḥ karṣakādibhyaḥ, yathādhikāraṃ yo yatrādhikṛtaḥ prativargaṃ sthitaḥ ity arthaḥ | arthān ācārān pratyavahṛtya svayam eva samyak pṛthak pṛthag vicāryety arthaḥ | kuta etat? avahṛtyeti vaktavye pratyavahṛtyety ārambhāt | tato dharmavyavasthā kartavyeti śeṣaḥ | tattvaparicchedaḥ kartavyaḥ ity abhiprāyaḥ | brūyād iti vaktavye vyavasthāgrahaṇaṃ rājñā paricchinnasya punar nivṛttir mā bhūd iti | evaṃ cāparitoṣe[44] ‘nyatra siddhasyāpi punaḥ punaḥ karaṇam astīti jñāpayati | dharmabuddhyaiva vyavasthāpayet nārthāśayeti dharmagrahaṇam || 11.24 ||
nyāyādhigame tarko ‘bhyupāyaḥ || 11.25 ||
nyāyayuktasyādhigame avadhāraṇe tarkaḥ anumānam abhyupāyaḥ prathamopāya ity arthaḥ | tathāha nāradaḥ |
yathā nayaty asṛkpātair mṛgasya mṛgayuḥ padam |
tathānumānena nayed dharmasya nṛpatiḥ padam || [= MDh 8.44]
iti || 11.25 ||
tenābhyūhya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet || 11.26 ||
tenānumānenābhyūhya | yathāha manuḥ |
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣitena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ || iti | [MDh 8.26]
yathāsthānaṃ yathātattvaṃ gamayet prāpayet || 11.26 ||
vipratipattau traividyavṛddhebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya niṣṭhāṃ gamayet || 11.27 ||
vipratipattiḥ ekasyaiva tarkeṇāvadhāraṇāsāmarthyam, atisaukṣmyāt kāryasya ubhayathā liṅgadarśanāc ca, tasyām, traividyavṛddhāḥ sakalavedatrayapāragāḥ, tebhyaḥ pratyavahṛtya avagamya asyedaṃ liṅgaṃ asya paricchedakam, pūrvam asyedaṃ liṅgam idānīṃ liṅgāntaram idaṃ kiṃ paricchinattīty evamādi vicārya tattvaṃ jñātvety arthaḥ | āmnāyādyavirodhaparijñāpanārthaṃ ye sahāyā uktās ta evānūdyante traividyalakṣaṇaprāpaṇārtham | yathāha manuḥ |
mantrajñair mantribhiś caiva vinītaḥ praviśet sabhām || iti | [MDh 8.1]
tatraiva vaktavyam iti cen, naiṣa doṣaḥ, āmnāyāviruddhavicāraṇāyām eva na kevalaṃ tair vicāraḥ kriyate kiṃ tu liṅgasaṃdehe ‘pi cety evamartha ihopadeśa iti | asati liṅgasaṃdehe virodhe cāsati rājña eva paricchedakatvaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ sabhāyās tadubhayārthatvād iti | liṅgasaṃdehe traividyavṛddhair eva prāpayet na svayam, yathāsthānaṃ gamayed ity adhikārād eva siddhe niṣṭhāṃ gamayed iti punar ārambhāt || 11.27 ||
kim evaṃ kurvato bhavatīty āha |
tathā hāsya niḥśreyasaṃ bhavatīti || 11.28 ||
tasya nirṇayaṃ kurvataḥ niḥśreyasam ubhayalokavijayitvam amutra dharmaprāptyā, ihāpi janānurāgād iti | itiśabdo hetvarthaḥ, yasmād evam iti || 11.28 ||
evaṃ rājño bhavati, traividyavṛddhānāṃ tu sahāyarūpeṇa sthitānāṃ kiṃ bhavatīti tadanurūpāṃ śrutir udāharati |
brahma kṣatreṇa pṛktaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyān dhārayatīti vijñāyate || 11.29 ||
brahma traividyalakṣaṇaṃ kṣatreṇa rājñā pṛktaṃ sahitam, rājñā saha dharmavivecanaṃ kurvad ity arthaḥ | nirupadravakarmānuṣṭhānadvāreṇa “itaḥ pradānaṃ devā upajīvanti” ity evaṃ devapitṝn dhārayati āhutyādidvāreṇa, annaniṣpattyā manuṣyān | ato rājasu praśnavivecanaṃ dharmāyeti vaktavyam || 11.29 ||
atha tv evam api vyavasthāṃ kurvataḥ te dauḥśīlyān nāvatiṣṭhante, tataḥ |
daṇḍo damanād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet || 11.30 ||
damayaty adāntān damyante vāsminn iti daṇḍaḥ | yataś ca damanayogād daṇḍa ity evaṃ manvādaya āhuḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
devadānavagandharvā rakṣāṃsi patagoragāḥ |
te ‘pi bhogāya kalpante daṇḍenaiva nipīḍitāḥ || iti | [MDh 7.23]
ato yo yathā damyate vācā dhanādinā vā, yathāha manuḥ |
dhigdaṇḍo prathamaṃ kuryād vāgdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍo tv ataḥ param || iti | [MDh 8.129]
evaṃ kuryād dauḥśīlyādinā vikarmasthān || 11.30 ||
evaṃ kṛtāḥ prajāḥ kathaṃ pravartanta ity āha |
varṇā āśramāś ca svakarmaniṣṭhāḥ pretya karmaphalam anubhūya tataḥ śeṣeṇa viśiṣṭadeśajātikularūpāyuḥśruta-vṛttavittasukhamedhaso janma pratipadyante || 11.31 ||
varṇā brāhmaṇādayaḥ anupanītāḥ, āśramāḥ brahmacāryādayaḥ, svakarmaniṣṭhāḥ svakarmānuṣṭhānaparāḥ, pretya paraloke, karmaphalam anubhūya upabhujya tatphalopabhogasyāvaśeṣeṇeha viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhaṃ janma pratipadyante | viśiṣṭaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate – viśiṣṭadeśa āryāvartādiḥ, viśiṣṭajātir brāhmaṇādiḥ, viśiṣṭaṃ kulam adhyayanādisaṃpannam, viśiṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ priyadarśanatvam, viśiṣṭam āyuḥ samagraśatavarṣādi, viśiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ vedārthavijñānasahitam, viśiṣṭaṃ vṛttaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ cāritram, viśiṣṭaṃ vittaṃ gavāśvādilakṣaṇam, viśiṣṭaṃ sukham abhilaṣitapadārthasyāvighnenānubhavasiddhiḥ, medhaḥśabdenārogyam ucyate, viśiṣṭārogyam abhilaṣitabhakṣaṇe ‘py arogotpattiḥ | evaṃ ca bruvatā nityakarmaṇāṃ ca kartā tatphalasya copabhoktā śarīravyatirikta ātmā pratipādito bhavati | tathā ca śrutiḥ:
sa eṣa mahān aja ātmā yo ‘yaṃ vijñānamayaḥ kartā bhoktā sa u kṣetrajño ‘mṛtamayaḥ prāṇe pratiṣṭhitaḥ || iti | [cf. BṛĀrUp 4.4.22]
tathā ca manuḥ |
yo hy ātmanaḥ kārayitā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ pracakṣate |
yaḥ karoti tu karmāṇi sa bhūtātmocyate budhaiḥ || [MDh 12.12]
ityādi | tasya ca nityasya sataḥ karmakṣayāt tadārabdhasya śarīrasya patane tatkṛtaṃ karmaphalaṃ svargādi tatkarmaṇaivārabdhena tatphalopabhogayogyena śarīreṇāhaṃkārendriyaśaktimanaḥprāṇasahitenānubhūyauṣadhādidvāreṇa annamayo bhūtvā retorūpeṇa pariṇamya tatsthena vāyunā pūrvaṃ mātur udaraṃ praviṣṭenākṛṣṭaḥ san tacchoṇitasahitaḥ pūrvānubhuktaśiṣṭakarmānugṛhītasya śarīrasyopādānaṃ janmety ācakṣate |
evaṃ vyavasthite sūtrārthe codyate āśramagrahaṇam anarthakaṃ varṇānām evāśramavidhānāt āśramatve ‘pi varṇatvānapagamāc ca | svagrahaṇaṃ cānarthakam, anuṣṭhānād eva svatvasiddheḥ | karmagrahaṇam apy anarthakam, karmaphalam anubhūyety atra karmagrahaṇenaivārthasiddhatvāt | niṣṭhāgrahaṇam apy anarthakam, buddhipūrvatvena vinā tatkaraṇāsaṃbhavāt | pretyety api na kartavyam, janma pratipadyanta ity anenaivārthasiddhatvāt | phalam anubhūyeti vaktavye karmagrahaṇam atiricyate, phalasya karmasādhyatvāt | tata ity etad apy anarthakam, adhikārād eva karmaṇaḥ parāmarśasiddheḥ | śeśānupapattiś ca, kṛtsnasya karmaṇaḥ phalopabhogenopakṣīṇatvāt | na hi phaladānābhimukhībhūtasya karmaṇa ekadeśenāvasthānam upapadyate | na hi kṣīrādyekadeśena dadhyādikāryārambhaṇam iti | na ca ghṛtaghaṭādivad vāsanāśeṣaḥ śeṣaśabdavācyaḥ, amūrtatvāt karmaṇo vāsanāśeṣānupapatteḥ | na cāmutrānuṣṭhitaṃ karmeha śeṣaśabdenocyate, tatrādhikārābhāvāt, upayuktetarārthatvāsaṃbhavāc ca | na hi phalopabhogavicchedasyaiva śeṣatvam adhyavasituṃ yuktam, śrutahānyaśrutakalpanāprasaṅgāt, karmaṇo hi phalaṃ śrutaṃ na phalavicchedasya | na ca akṣīṇe karmaṇy upabhogavicchedaḥ saṃbhavati, tasya karmāśritatvāt | yathā pradīpas tailāśritaḥ tailakṣayād eva kṣīyate | api ca tasyopasaṃhāraḥ śalabhādayo vidyante na tu karmaphalasya | tasmān na phalavicchedasya śeṣaśabdavācyatā | na ca karmaphalasyaiva vibhāgaḥ kartuṃ śaknoti – anenāmutrasukham anubhokṣye anena viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhaṃ lapsye iti, niṣpannatvāt | aniṣpannasya hi vibhāgaḥ śrūyate – idaṃ vrīhīṇāṃ kṣetram idaṃ yavānām iti, na tu niṣpannā vrīhayo yavā kriyanta iti | yadi ca svakarmaniṣṭhā apy amutra phalopabhogaśeṣeṇeha janma pratipadyante evaṃ mokṣābhāvaprasaṅge śruter virodo bhavati – “brahmavid āpnoti param” [TaitUp 2.1], “tam eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti” [ŚveUp 3.8] ityādikāyāḥ | yadi karmaśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭadeśādiprāptilakṣaṇaṃ janma bhavati, tato jagadvaicitryānupapattiprasaṅgaḥ, dṛśyate hi tad iti | prāptasya ca karmaṇaḥ phalābhyupagame puruṣakāro ‘pi niṣpala eveti | tasmāc cheṣaśabdavācyābhāvāt sūtreṇa samastenāpi prakrāntasyārthasyānupapadyamānatvāt asamañjasam idam sūtram iti ||
tatra samādhiḥ – ubhayagrahaṇaṃ tāvat kartavyam, varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca dharmabhedāt | prāg upanayanād varṇāḥ ūrdhvam āśramā iti | asti cānupanītānām api kaiścid varṇadharmair adhikāra iti | asamāso dvandvaparigrahārtham, mā bhūt samānādhikaraṇāśaṅketi, dandvapakṣe tūbhayapadapradhānatvād ubhayor upādānaṃ siddhaṃ bhavati iti | sṛṣṭyantrajopasaṃgrahārthaś caśabdaḥ, teṣām api svakarmānuṣṭhānād utkarṣo labhyata iti | tathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo ‘nupaskṛtaḥ |
strībālābhyupapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam || iti | [MDh 10.62]
svagrahaṇam api prāthamikakalpopasaṃgrahārtham | āpadvihitasya prāyaścittavidhānād evaitat siddham iti cen, na, varṇāntarakarmānuṣṭhāna eva prāyaścittavidhānam, na svakarmaṇa eva viguṇasyeti | tathā coktaṃ nāradavākyam |
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇas tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyā mṛte jane |
utsṛjet kṣatravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ || [NSm 1.55]
