ityādi | tasmād viguṇasya karmaṇaḥ phalapratiṣedhārthaṃ svagrahaṇam | evaṃ ca viguṇaṃ yat karma na tat phalasādhanam, prāyaścittavidhānābhāvāt nāpi doṣasādhanaṃ ceti draṣṭavyam | niṣṭhāgrahaṇaṃ sātatyārtham, yo hy ā śarīranipātanāt karma karoti sa evānena phalena saṃbadhyata iti | pretyagrahaṇam avaśyabhāvi phalam iti jñāpanārtham, yathā dṛṣṭaphalair iha siddhis tathā avaśyaṃ tatrāpy adṛṣṭaphalair iti | na tu kṛtaṃ karma niṣphalaṃ bhavatīti | karmagrahaṇaṃ tu kevalaṃ karma kṛta evānena phalena saṃbandhaḥ, na jñānakarmasamuccayakāriṇa iti | asau mukta eva bhavatīti | anenaiva mokṣābhāvaprasaṅgacodyam api parihṛtaṃ draṣṭavyam | tata ity etad api karmanānātvapratipādanārtham | tasya tasya karmaṇaḥ śeṣenātirekeṇa | ataś ca karmaṇāṃ tāratamyāt phalavaicitrye sati jagadvaicitryam upapadyata iti | tāratamyaṃ ca karmaṇāṃ deśakālaśaucadravyaśraddhāpātrābhyāsaviśeṣād iti śeṣaśabdaś ca karmasamudāyāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | kathm? yat karma svargaphalaṃ tatraiva phalaṃ dadāti, yat tv aihikaphalaṃ tena viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhaṃ janma bhavati, sa ca tasya samudāyasya śeṣa iti | pradhānakarmaṇo ‘gnihotrāder aṅgabhūtaṃ yat karma tac cheṣaśabdenocyate | yathā – “godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayet” [ĀpŚr 1.16.2], “khādiraṃ vīryakāmasya yūpaṃ kuryāt” [ṢaḍviṃBr 4.4] ityādi | tatra pradhānakarmaphalam amutropabhujyate, aṅgaphalaṃ iheti | vṛkṣavad vaitad draṣṭavyam | vṛkṣo hi bahubhiḥ mūlair jāyate | sa yadā chinnapradhānabhūyiṣṭhamūlo bhavati tadālpaniḥsāramūlatvād bhāram asahamānaḥ patati, evaṃ karmaṇy api pradhānarūpe upabhogād upakṣīṇe śiṣṭarūpaṃ kartāraṃ tatra sthāpayitum aśaknuvad viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbaddhaṃ phalaṃ dadātīti | ghṛtaghaṭavad vā draṣṭavyam | yathā – ghṛtaghaṭe upayukte ‘pi snehavāsanāśeṣād agninā saṃyukto jvalati, evaṃ karmakṛd api vāsanāśeṣād eva viśiṣṭadeśādy anubhaviṣyatīti | sāvayavatvāc ca karmaṇo vāsanopapadyata eveti, sāvayavatvam api kāryatvād iti |
yat tūktaṃ puruṣakāro ‘narthaka iti, tatrocyate – puruṣakārayuktasyaiva phalasaṃbandhaḥ, tadvihīnasyānnādisaṃbandho ‘pi nāstīti | tasmāt phaladānakāraṇaṃ pūrvaśiṣṭaṃ saṃbandhakāraṇaṃ puruṣakāra iti nānarthakaḥ puruṣakāra iti sarvam idaṃ[45] sūtram upapadyata eveti || 11.31 ||
viśvañco viparītā naśyanti || 11.32 ||
ye punaḥ na svayaṃ karma[46] kurvanti rājñā ca na sthāpyante te viśvañcaḥ tiryaggatayo naśyanti, viśiṣṭadeśādisaṃbandhābhāvāt | viparītāḥ svakarmaniṣṭhetarāḥ | evaṃ ca bruvatā ye svakarmaniṣṭhās teṣām uttamagatitvam, ye rājñā svadharme sthāpyante teṣāṃ madhyamagatitvam, ye svakarmaniṣṭhā na bhavanti na rājñā ca sthāpyante teṣām adhamagatitvaṃ ca darśitaṃ bhavati | tathā cokaṃ purāṇe |
śubhair[47] bhavati devatvaṃ vyāmiśrair janma mānuṣam |
aśubhaiś cāpy adhojanma karmabhir labhate naraḥ || 11.32 ||
tān ācāryopadeśo daṇḍaś ca pālayate || 11.33 ||
tān ājñān avihitānuṣṭhānena pratiṣiddhasevanayā ca naśyato varṇāśramiṇaḥ ācāryopadeśo rakṣati, itarāṃs tu daṇḍa iti || 11.33 ||
tasmād rājācāryāv anindyāv anindau || 11.34 ||
tataś ca rakṣaṇahetutvāt kāraṇe sati pāruṣyādy api vaktavyam | ataḥ paruṣam apy upadiśann ācāryaḥ, ugram api dhārayan rājā ca, atyantopakāritvād akutsanīyau || 11.34 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye ekādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
“daṇḍo damanād ity āhus tenādāntān damayet” [GDh 11.30] iti sāmānyenābhihitam | tatra kvāparādhe kiyān daṇḍa iti tad vaktavyam ity āha |
śūdro dvijātīn atisaṃdhāyābhihatya ca vāgdaṇḍa-pāruṣyābhyām aṅgaṃ mocyo yenopahanyāt || 12.1 ||
śūdra uktaḥ | sa dvijātīn upanītān brāhmaṇādīn atisaṃdhāya buddhipūrvam atikramya na tu parihāsādinā | abhihatya ca abhiśabdo buddhipūrvajñāpanārthaḥ | abhihatya tāḍayitvā | krameṇa vāgdaṇḍapāruṣyābhyām | tatra vākpāruṣyeṇātisaṃdhāya daṇḍapāruṣyeṇābhihatyeti kramo draṣṭavyaḥ | vākpāruṣyeṇātikramaṇam atikramya paruṣādivacanam | paruṣaśabda ugraparyāyaḥ | ugrayā vācā atikramya ugreṇa ca daṇḍenābhihatya cety arthaḥ | tataś ca upalādinā na doṣaḥ | aṅgaṃ śarīrāvayavaḥ mocyaḥ chedyaḥ yena hastādinā upahanyāt pīḍayet | evaṃ ca vināpi daṇḍena daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ bhavatīti jñāpayati | vākpāruṣye vākchedanam | hastādinā daṇḍapāruṣye tadaṅgacchedanam iti draṣṭavyam | caśabdād ubhayāparādhe ubhayaṃ mocyaḥ || 12.1 ||
āryastryabhigamane liṅgoddhāraḥ svaharaṇaṃ ca
|| 12.2 ||
āryāṇāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ āryavṛttā cet strī | kuta etat stryabhigamana iti vaktavye āryastryabhigamana ity ārambhāt evaṃ ca veśyārūpeṇa sthitāyām adaṇḍyaḥ | striyām evābhigamanaprasiddher āryāgamana ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇam āryaparigṛhītāyāṃ śūdrāyām apīty evamartham | abhigamane kṛte abhiśabdo buddhipūrvārthaḥ | tataś ca svapnādāv abuddhasya tathaiva kṛtasya laghutaro daṇḍo draṣṭavyaḥ | liṅgasyoddhāraḥ utpāṭanaṃ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ ca kartavyam | talliṅgoddhāro dhanasya ceti vaktavye svaharaṇaṃ cety abhidhānāt | cakāraḥ samuccayārthaḥ, vikalpo mā bhūd ity asamāsaḥ, kṣatriyavaiśyastrīgamane yathāsaṃkhyenaikaikam, brāhmaṇasya tūbhayam iti || 12.2 ||
goptā ced vadho ‘dhikaḥ || 12.3 ||
yadi tāsāṃ rakṣitā bhavati tatas tadabhigamane pūrvoktam ubhayaṃ kṛtvā adhikagrahaṇāt, pramāpaṇaṃ kartavyam || 12.3 ||
atha hāsya vedam upaśṛṇvatas trapujatubhyāṃ śrotrapratipūraṇam udāharaṇe jihvācchedo dhāraṇe śarīrabhedaḥ || 12.4 ||
pañcavarṣād ūrdhvam ayam, athaśabdopādānāt | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
vedaṃ śrutvā tu pañcābdaḥ śūdraś ced daṇḍabhāg bhavet |
aprāptapañcavarṣo na daṇḍam arhati kutracit || iti |
haśabdo buddhipūrvasūcanārthaḥ | na goptur evety evamartham asya śabdasyopādānam | vedaṃ sāṅgam | kutaḥ? “sāṅgo vedaḥ strīśūdrasakāśe nādhyetavyaḥ” iti gṛhyasmṛtidarśanāt | upaśabdena samīpavācinā akṣaraśravaṇa eva doṣa iti darśayati | tataś ca | dhvanimātraśravaṇe na doṣaḥ | haśabdena buddhipūrva eva daṇḍavidhānāt pramādāt kadācid akṣaraśravaṇe ‘pi doṣābhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ | trapujatubhyāṃ trapunā jatunā ceti draṣṭavyam | taptābhyām iti ca draṣṭavyam, pūraṇopadeśasāmarthyāt | śrotradvayaparigrahārthaḥ pratiśabdaḥ udāharaṇe dvijātibhiḥ saha jihvācchedaḥ kartavyaḥ | dhāraṇe svayam evodāharaṇa ity arthaḥ | paraśunā śarīrabhedaḥ kartavyaḥ || 12.4 ||
āsanaśayanavākpathiṣu samaprepsur daṇḍyaḥ
|| 12.5 ||
āsanādiṣv āryais tulyatvaṃ svecchayā kāmayamānaḥ | āsanādiṣūcchritādiguṇeṣu samatvaṃ vāksāmyaṃ samakāloccāraṇam, pathi sāmyaṃ pṛṣṭhato muktvā saha gamanam | daṇḍaḥ sthānādyapekṣayā śatād arvāg draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.5 ||
śataṃ kṣatriyo brāhmaṇākrośe || 12.6 ||
kṛta iti śeṣaḥ | ākrośe vākpāruṣye | daṇḍapāruṣye tu dviguṇaṃ kalpyam, smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
vākpāruṣye kṛte yasya yathā daṇḍo vidhīyate |
tasyaiva dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kārayen maraṇād ṛte || iti |
tathā cāha uśanā: “avagūraṇe vākpāruṣyatulyaṃ tāḍane taddviguṇaṃ śoṇitotpādane triguṇam” ityādi | śataṃ kārṣāpaṇānāṃ kṣatriyo daṇḍyaḥ brāhmaṇākrośe || 12.6 ||
adhyardhaṃ vaiśyaḥ || 12.7 ||
brāhmaṇākrośa iti vartate | ardhenādhikaṃ yac chataṃ tadadhyardhaṃ taddaṇḍyo vaiśyaḥ
|| 12.7 ||
brāhmaṇas tu kṣatriye pañcāśat || 12.8 ||
ākruṣṭe iti vartate | brāhmaṇena kṣatriye ākruṣṭe brāhmaṇa aśrotriyaś cet | kutaḥ | tuśabdopādānāt, pañcāśad daṇḍyaḥ | samavarṇeṣu dvādaśa vā kalpya avacanīyeṣu dviguṇaṃ kalpyam | tathā[48] ca manuḥ |
samavarṇe dvijātīnāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame |
vādeṣv avacanīyeṣu tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || [MDh 8.269]
|| 12.8 ||
tadardhaṃ vaiśye || 12.9 ||
vaiśye ākruṣṭe brāhmaṇaḥ pañcaviṃśatir daṇḍyaḥ || 12.9 ||
śūdre na kiṃcit || 12.10 ||
ākruṣṭe na kiṃcid brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ | atha vā,[49] avacanād eva siddham iti cen, na, kṣatriyavaiśyayor daṇḍaprāpaṇārthatvāt | evaṃ ca tadviṣaye daṇḍaḥ kalpyaḥ, kṣatriye caturviṃśatipaṇam, ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ vaiśya iti | tathāha uśanā: “śūdram ākruśya kṣatriyaś caturviṃśatidaṇḍabhāg vaiśyaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat” ityādi[50] || 12.10 ||
brāhmaṇarājanyavat kṣatriyavaiśyau || 12.11 ||
yat brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ parasparākrośa uktaṃ tat kṣatriyavaiśyayor api draṣṭavyam | kṣatriye śataṃ vaiśye pañcāśad iti | evam antarajānām api draṣṭavyam | tathāha jamadagniḥ: “mātṛtulyam anulomānāṃ pitṛtulyaṃ pratilomānām” iti || 12.11 ||
sāhasadaṇḍam uktvā steya idānīm āha |
aṣṭāpādyaṃ steyakilbiṣaṃ śūdrasya || 12.12 ||
steyena yad upāttam adharmakāraṇāt tad dravyaṃ kilbiṣaśabdenocyate steyakilbiṣaṃ steyadhanam ity arthaḥ | tadaṣṭaguṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ, “samaprepsur daṇḍyaḥ” [GDh 12.5] ity atra daṇḍyaśabdasya nadīsrotonyāyenādhikṛtasyātra ṣaṣṭyanurūpārthaṃ daṇḍa ity evaṃ bhavatīti | kilbiṣaśabdena vā daṇḍa ucyate | steyakilbiṣaṃ steyadaṇḍa ity arthaḥ | sa gṛhītaḥ syād aṣṭaguṇa iti | tatra brāhmaṇasuvarṇavarjaṃ draṣṭavyam, tasya mahāpātakamadhye upadeśād daṇḍagauravaṃ bhavatīti || 12.12 ||
dviguṇottarāṇītareṣāṃ prativarṇam || 12.13 ||
itareṣāṃ vaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇānām, prātilomyena prativarṇam ity ārambhāt, dviguṇottarāṇi daṇḍaḥ uttaram uttaraṃ dviguṇam iti | vaiśyasya ṣoḍaśaguṇam, kṣatriyasya dvātriṃśadguṇam, brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇam iti | sarvatra dhanine dhanārpaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, “corahṛtam apajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet” [GDh 10.46] ity uktatvāt || 12.13 ||
viduṣo ‘tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvam || 12.14 ||
jātyutkarṣavad vijñānotkarṣād api daṇḍabhūyastvaṃ draṣṭavyam, “tat svajātivihitād adhyardhaṃ viduṣo jñeyam” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | upapannaṃ caitat, yato ‘asau jñātvātikrāmatīti | viduṣo daṇḍabhūyastvam iti siddhe atikramagrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham, cauryaviṣaya evedam, na sāhasaviṣaye | prāyaścittaviṣaye ‘pīti | evaṃ ca bruvatā daṇḍavidhānāt prāyaścittasyāpi gurulaghubhāvakalpanāstīti pradarśitaṃ bhavati | sāhasaprakaraṇe ca yad uktam “śrotriyasyārdhadaṇḍaḥ" iti tac copapannam || 12.14 ||
asyāpavādam āha |
phalaharitadhānyaśākādāne pañcakṛṣṇalam alpe
|| 12.15 ||
phalānām āmrādīnām, haritadhānyasya vrīhyādeḥ apakvasyety arthaḥ | śākasya ca mūlakāder apaharaṇe māṣamātradaṇḍaḥ | kutaḥ “pañcakṛṣṇalako māṣaḥ” [MDh 8.134] iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | alpe udarapūraṇamātre | adhike anyadravye vā “aṣṭāpādyam” [GDh 12.12] ity etad eva draṣṭavyam || 12.15 ||
paśupīḍite svāmidoṣaḥ || 12.16 ||
paśubhir upahate sasyādau yasya paśavas tasya daṇḍo vakṣyamāṇaḥ || 12.16 ||
pālasaṃyukte tu tasmin || 12.17 ||
pālānvite pāla eva daṇḍabhāg ity arthaḥ | tuśabdaḥ pālena kārite daṇḍabhūyastvajñāpanārthaḥ | bhūyastvam api dviguṇam, “pālakārite dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt || 12.17 ||
pathi kṣetre ‘nāvṛte pālakṣetrikayoḥ || 12.18 ||
pālasyāyatnakaraṇād itarasyāpi vṛter akaraṇāt tāv ardham ardhaṃ dadyātām ity arthaḥ || 12.18 ||
idānīṃ paśuviśeṣāpekṣayā daṇḍaviśeṣam āha |
pañca māṣā gavi || 12.19 ||
māṣaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya viṃśatibhāgaḥ | tathāha uśanā |
māṣo viṃśatibhāgas tu jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya tu |
kākaṇī tu caturbhāgo māṣasyaiva prakīrtitaḥ || iti |
te pañca gavy aparādhe daṇḍyaḥ || 12.19 ||
ṣaḍ uṣṭrakhare || 12.20 ||
uṣṭrāparādhe kharāparādhe ca pratyekaṃ ṣaṇmāṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.20 ||
aśvamahiṣyor daśa || 12.21 ||
atrāpi pratyekaṃ daśa māṣā daṇḍaḥ || 12.21 ||
ajāviṣu dvau dvau || 12.22 ||
bahuvacanaṃ vatsanivṛttyartham | tat pūrvatrāpi draṣṭavyam | dvau dvau māṣau daṇḍaḥ | ante vīpsābhidhānaṇaṃ pūrve ‘bhyanuṣaṅgārtham, itarathā saṃdehaḥ syād iti || 12.22 ||
sarvavināśe śadaḥ || 12.23 ||
sarvavināśe prarohaṇasāmarthye yāvad vināśitaṃ tāvan niṣpattim avekṣya kṣetrasvāmine śadaḥ phalaṃ deyam | rājñaś cānurūpo daṇḍaḥ || 12.23 ||
śiṣṭākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ ca nityaṃ celapiṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ svaharaṇam || 12.24 ||
nityaṃ śiṣṭākaraṇe nityaṃ vihitasyānanuṣṭhāne, na sakṛt, pratiṣidhasyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇāder āsevane, na pramādāt, celapiṇḍād bhaktācchādanamātrād yad anyat tasyāpahāraḥ kartavyaḥ | bhaktācchādanaṃ tu yāvad anyasya dravyasyāgamanakālas tāvad asya moktavyam | tato ‘pi yady atikrāmati punar asya svam apahartavyam eveti | evaṃ kṛte daṇḍabhayāt svakarmaṇy eva pravartata iti | cakārāt prāyaścittaṃ ca kārayitavyam || 12.24 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe uktasyādattādānapratiṣedhasyedānīm apavādam āha |
go ‘gnyarthe tṛṇam edhān vīrudvanaspatīnāṃ ca puṣpāṇi svavad ādadīta phalāni cāparivṛtānām || 12.25 ||
gavārthaṃ tṛṇāni | agnigrahaṇena śrautasya smārtasyāpi grahaṇaṃ na laukikasya, tadartham edhān kāṣṭhāni | vīrudhāṃ karavīrādīnāṃ puṣpāṇi – vanaspatiśabdo ‘tra vṛkṣaparyāyaḥ, sākṣād vanaspatīnāṃ puṣpāsaṃbhavāt | yathāha manuḥ |
apuṣpāḥ phalavanto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | [MDh 1.47]
puṣpāṇi devārcanārthāni nānubhavārthāni, go’gnisāhacaryāt | cakārāt patrāṇi brāhmaṇabhojanārthāni | vīrudvanaspatipuṣpāṇi ceti vaktavye oṣadhārthaṃ mūlāder api brāhmaṇārtham asamāsaḥ | svavat yathā teṣāṃ pīḍā na bhavati tathā gṛhītavyam iti | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
pakvaṃ pakvaṃ pracinvīta mūlacchedaṃ tu varjayet |
mālākāra ivārāme na yathāṅgārakārakaḥ || iti |
phalāni cāmraphalādīni | āpatsūcanārthaṃ pṛthag abhidhānam | cakārāc chākaṃ ca | aparivṛtānām anārāmīkṛtānām | ubhayaviśeṣaṇaṃ cedam | evaṃ cārāme tṛṇāder api pratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ
|| 12.25 ||
idānīṃ nyāyenāpi paradravyādānaṃ steyam eveti tatparihārārtham idam ārabhyate |
kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā viṃśatiḥ pañcamāṣikī māsam
|| 12.26 ||
kusīdaṃ dravyaprayogaḥ | tatra vṛddhir upacayaḥ dharmyā dharmād anapetā yadā viṃśatiḥ kārṣāpaṇānāṃ māsaṃ prayuktā bhavanti tadā tasyāḥ pratimāsaṃ pañcamāṣaparimāṇā vṛddhir bhavati, pratimāsaṃ niṣkasyārdhaguñjāmātrā vṛddhir ity arthaḥ | māṣaśabdena vrīhiparimitaṃ vastv abhidhīyata iti | evaṃ ca manunoktasyāśītibhāgasyaiva grahaṇaṃ saṃpadyate | tathāha manuḥ |
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ vṛddhiṃ sṛjed vittavivardhinīm |
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyān māsaṃ vārdhuṣikaḥ śateḥ || iti | [MDh 8.140]
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ “kāmaṃ vā pariluptakṛtyāya pāpīyase dadyātām” [VaDh 2,43] iti | suvarṇakārṣāpaṇapakṣe catuḥṣaṣṭibhāgo bhavati: “te suvarṇaṃ tu ṣoḍaśa” [YDh 1.358] iti || 12.26 ||
nātisāṃvatsarīm eke || 12.27 ||
māsam atikramya saṃvatsaravṛddhir na grāhyety eke manyante | etad uktam bhavati – māsi māsi yad grahītavyaṃ tat sarvaṃ saṃbhūya saṃvatsare paripūrṇa eva gṛhṇāmīty evaṃ matvā pratisaṃvatsaraṃ na grahītavyam iti | pratisaṃvatsarapratiṣedhāt pratimāsaṃ pratiṣāṇmāsaṃ cābhyanujñāyate | tataś ca māsi māsi ṣaṇmāse ṣaṇmāse vā grahītavyaṃ na pratisaṃvatsaram ity ekeṣām abhiprāyaḥ | pratimāsavṛddhigrahaṇe caitad draṣṭavyam | yāvad dhanasaṃvyavahāras tāvad grāhyaiveti gautamaḥ | tadvicchede tu “nātisāṃvatsarīm” iti vyavasthitavibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.27 ||
cirasthāne dvaiguṇyaṃ prayogasya || 12.28 ||
cirasthāne agṛhītāyāṃ vṛddhau tayā vṛddhyā yāvatā kālena dviguṇaṃ bhavati tataḥ paraṃ na vardhate | prayogagrahaṇaṃ prayogāntare dvaiguṇyavyatikramārtham | yathāha manuḥ |
kusīdavṛddhiḥ dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛd āhitā || iti | [MDh 8.151]
hiraṇyasyaiva dviguṇānatikramaṇam iti jñāpanārthaṃ sthānagrahaṇam | dhānyādayo dviguṇam apy atikramya vardhanta eva | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “dviguṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ triguṇaṃ dhānyaṃ dhānyenaiva rasā vyākhyātā, puṣpamūlaphalāni ca tulādhṛtam aṣṭaguṇam” [VaDh 2.44–47] iti | evaṃ ca bruvatā kusīdavṛddhir dharmyeti yoktā vṛddhis tasyāḥ sārvatrikatvaṃ pradarśitaṃ bhavati, anyathā prasaṅgābhāvād dhiraṇyasyaiva siddhatvāt tadarthaṃ sthānagrahaṇam anarthakam iti | tataś ca sarvatrāśītibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ | ciragrahaṇaṃ saṃvatsarasahasrair api na vardhata iti || 12.28 ||
bhuktādhir na vardhate || 12.29 ||
bhoga eva tatra vṛddhir bhavatīti || 12.29 ||
kiṃ ca |
ditsato ‘varuddhasya ca || 12.30 ||
dātum icchato dhanine sa yadā dhanī vṛddhilobhād vyājena na gṛhṇāti tato ‘pi na vardhate | yo datum icchati tena sākṣimal lāñchanā saṃsthā kartavyā, anabhijñānād buddhivyāpārasya | evam avaruddhasya ca niḥsvo bhūtvā deśāntaragatasyety arthaḥ | cakāraḥ pūrvaviśeṣaṇāśaṅkā mā bhūd iti | taddivasād ārabhyeti draṣṭavyam | kecid rājādinā nigalabandhanam āhuḥ tatra bhūmidevāḥ pramāṇam || 12.30 ||
idānīm āpadi svayaṃ kṛtā vṛddhiprakārā abhyanujñāyante |
cakrakālavṛddhiḥ || 12.31 ||
vṛddhiśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | cakravṛddhiḥ – vṛddhir api punar vardhate yasmin dravyaprayoge sā cakravṛddiḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
vṛddher api punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhir udāhṛtā | iti | [NSm 1.89]
kālavṛddhiḥ – asmāt kālāt prāk kevalaṃ vā prakṛtavṛddhyā vā mama dātavyam | yadi na dadāsi tatas tasmād ūrdhvaṃ triguṇaṃ ca caturguṇaṃ vā deyam iti samayaṃ kṛtvā yasmin bahvī vṛddhir gṛhyate sā kālavṛddhiḥ | prakṛtalabdham api vṛddhigrahaṇaṃ kriyate svayaṃ kṛtāyā eveyaṃ kalpanā na pañcamāṣaparimitāyā iti | sahanirdeśa ekayā jīvanāsaṃbhave dvābhyām api jīvane na doṣa iti |
nanu ca “kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā viṃśatiḥ pañcamāṣikī māsam” [GDh 12.26] ity atraiva vaktavyam | atraiva pṛthagupadeśād āpadarthatvaṃ ca jñāyate | “nātisāṃvatsarīm” [GDh 12.27] ity etad api vyākhyānato viśeṣapratipattir ity anena nyāyena vyākhyānata eva pañcamāṣaparimitavṛddhiviṣayam iti jñātuṃ śakyata iti |
atrocyate – kusīdasya svayaṃkaraṇapakṣe āpady anāpadi ca asvayaṃkṛtapakṣe cānāpadi pañcamāṣaparimitām eva gṛhṇīyāt nāsvayaṃkṛtapakṣa āpadi cakrādivṛddhim api, anenābhiprāyenopadeśa iti | “nātisāṃvatasarīm” [GDh 12.27] ity asyāpi vispaṣṭīkaraṇārthas tatopadeśa iti || 12.31 ||
kāritākāyikāśikhādhibhogāś ca || 12.32 ||
kāritā śāstrānapekṣayā yasminn ātmanaiva kriyate pratisaṃvatsaraṃ niṣkasya tatpādam ardhaṃ vā deyam ity evamādiḥ sā kāritā vṛddhiḥ | kāyikā abhyupagatāyā vṛddher adhikaṃ divase divase māsi māsi saṃvatsare saṃvatsare vā yasmin gṛhyate sā kāyikā | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
kāyāvirodhinī śaśvat paṇapādādikādhikā | iti | [NSm 1.88]
śikhā dhānyasya, yathā caturṇāṃ prasthānāṃ pañcama ityādi | adhibhogaḥ vṛddhyartham ahar ahar yasmin ghṛtādi gṛhyate sādhibhogavṛddhiḥ | vṛddhiśabdānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | gauravapradarśanārthaḥ pṛthagnirdeśaḥ || 12.32 ||
kusīdaṃ paśūpajalomakṣetraśadavāhyeṣu nātipañcaguṇam || 12.33 ||
adhikṛtam api kusīdagrahaṇaṃ kriyate anyarūpo ‘yaṃ prayoga iti jñāpanārtham | paśūpajaṃ ghṛtādi tat krītvā amuṣmin kāle mūlyaṃ dātavyam, tasminn adīyamānaṃ vardhata iti yat paribhāṣitam ity abhiprāyaḥ | vatsasya grahaṇaṃ mā bhūd ity upaśabdaprayogaḥ | loma ūrṇādi | prakṛtigrahaṇena vikārāṇām api grahaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | tasyāpi krayamūlyasyādīyamānasyaiva | kṣetraśadaḥ[51] kṣetraṃ yadā parimitena dhānyena prayujyate tasmin kāle asya śulkam[52] adīyamānaṃ vardhata iti | vāhyaḥ prāpaṇanimittaṃ yathā balīvardādibhiḥ kasyacid dhānyaṃ prāpayati amuṣmin kāle ‘sya śulkam adīyamānaṃ vardhata iti | eteṣu yat kusīdam upari savṛddhikaṃ dātavyam iti prayuktam ity arthaḥ | tat pañcaguṇatvaṃ nātivartate | paśūpajagrahaṇenaiva siddhasya lomnaḥ punar grahaṇaṃ mayūrapatrakṛtānām api grahaṇārtham, “pakṣāṇāṃ patraromāṇām agne brūhi mayā śrutam” iti śrutidarśanāt | “cirasthāne dvaiguṇyaṃ prayogasya” [GDh 12.28] ity atraiva vaktavyam iti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ – eṣāṃ cakravṛddhiprayogābhāvajñāpanārtha ihopadeśa iti | pañcaguṇam ity anena siddhe nātigrahaṇaṃ prayogāntare atikramaṇasūcanārtham || 12.33 ||
idānīm anyo vyavahāra ucyate |
ajaḍāpogaṇḍadhanaṃ daśavarṣabhuktaṃ paraiḥ saṃnidhau bhoktuḥ || 12.34 ||
jaḍaḥ unmugdhacittaḥ saṃvyavahārāyogyaḥ | pogaṇḍaḥ aprāptavyavahāraḥ | tau muktvā yad anyasya dhanaṃ paraiḥ asaṃbaddhaiḥ tatsamakṣaṃ – svāminy ekagrāmasthita evety arthaḥ – bhujyate tat daśavarṣabhoktur eva bhavati, na his sati svāmitve iyantaṃ kālam upekṣaṇam upapadyata ity abhiprāyaḥ | jaḍabāladhanaṃ tu nāhriyate | tadgrahaṇam ādhyādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | yathāha nāradaḥ |
ādhiḥ sīmā bāladhanaṃ nikṣepopanidhiḥ striyaḥ |
rājasvaṃ śrotriyasvaṃ ca nopabhogena jīryati || iti | [NSm 1.73]
atra ajaḍadhanaṃ na parigaṇitam iti cet, viṃśativarṣeṇāpi jīrṇatvaṃ mā bhūd iti | yathāha nāradaḥ |
pratyakṣaparibhogāc ca svāmino dvidaśāḥ samāḥ |
ādhyādīny api jīryeyuḥ strīnarendradhanād ṛte || iti | [NSm 1.74]
evaṃ ca viṃśativarṣāt bāladhanaṃ jīryaty eva | saṃnidhāv ity anenaiva pareṣām arthasiddhatvāt paragrahaṇaṃ saṃbandhibhuktasyānaticārajñāpanārtham | saṃnidhigrahaṇād anyagrāmasthasya na doṣaḥ || 12.34 ||
asyāpavādaṃ bhoktṛviśeṣeṇāha ||
na śrotriyapravrajitarājanyapuruṣaiḥ || 12.35 ||
śrotriyādibhir bhujyamānam api nāpahriyate, upekṣākāraṇopapatteḥ śrotriyapravrajitau dharmatṛṣṇayopekṣyete, rājapuruṣo bhayeneti | pravrajitasya katham upapadyata iti ced, atrocyate – kasyacid gṛhasya śūnyatvena sthitasya pravrajitasya śūnyagṛhavāsanopadeśād upapadyata eveti || 12.35 ||
adhunā bhujyamānadravyaviśeṣeṇāpavādam āha |
paśubhūmistrīṇām anatibhogaḥ || 12.36 ||
paśuśabdena sarve catuṣpādā yajñiyā gṛhyante, “catuṣpādā vai paśavo yajñiyāḥ” iti śrutidarśanāt | bhūmiḥ kṣetrārāmādiḥ, na gṛham, “daśavarṣād veśmano bhoktur na bhūmiḥ” ity auśanasavacanāt | striyaḥ paricārikāḥ | eṣām anatibhogaḥ, daśavarśānubhavena na svāmyaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
anāgamaṃ tu yad bhuktaṃ vatsarāṇāṃ śatair api | iti | [NSm 1.75]
idam api vacanaṃ paśubhūmistrīviṣayatvena vyākhyātam iti || 12.36 ||
rikthabhāja ṛṇaṃ pratikuryuḥ || 12.37 ||
yasya dhanaṃ ye bhajanti gṛhṇanti ta eva tadṛṇaṃ dadyur ity arthaḥ, na dāyādamātreṇa | putrastrīhāriṇām api grahaṇārthaṃ bahuvacanam | tathāha nāradaḥ |
dhanastrīhāriputrāṇām ṛṇabhāg yo dhanaṃ haret |
putro ‘satoḥ strīdhaninoḥ strīhārī dhanaputrayoḥ || iti | [NSm 1.20]
evaṃ ca rikthabhāktve saty asati ca putreṇāvaśyaṃ dātavyaṃ strīhāriṇā ca anyais tu rikthabhāgbhir eva | putrāṇāṃ ca madhye yeṣāṃ rikthabhāktvaṃ ca vidyate ta eva ṛṇaṃ dadyuḥ na putramātreṇeti draṣṭavyam || 12.37 ||
idānīṃ putraṃ praty apavādam āha |
prātibhāvyavaṇikśulkamadyadyūtadaṇḍā na putrān adhyābhaveyuḥ || 12.38 ||
pratibhuvas trayaḥ | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
pādadarśī ca viśvāsyo hy arthadaś ceti kīrtitāḥ | iti |
atra dānapratibhuvaṃ muktvā itarayor grahaṇam | kutaḥ?
dānapribhuvi prete dāyādān api dāpayet | [MDh 8.161]
iti manuvacanāt | pratibhūnimittaṃ yad ṛṇaṃ tat prātibhāvyam | vaṇig vāṇijyanimittaṃ cety arthaḥ | yathā mūlyaṃ dadāmīti paribhāṣya kasyacit sakāśe dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vāṇijyakaraṇārthaṃ deśāntaraṃ gatvā mriyeta, putreṇa tad aprāptaṃ ca bhavati tatas tad ṛṇaṃ putrasyopari na bhavatīty abhiprāyaḥ | śulkam āsurādivivāhe śulkaṃ pratiśrutya vivāhaṃ kṛtvā yady asau mriyate tac chulkaṃ putrasopari na bhavatīti | madyaṃ madyanimittaṃ brāhmaṇasya pratiṣiddhatvāt kṣatriyādiviṣayam etad draṣṭavyam | dravyaṃ dadāmīti madyapāne kṛte dravyam adatvā ‘sau mriyeta tad api putrasya na bhavatīti | tathā dyūtanimittaṃ ca ṛṇam | tathā daṇḍanimittaṃ ca vyavahārāpajayanimittam | naite putrān adhyābhaveyuḥ putrāṇām upari na bhavatīty arthaḥ | rikthabhāktve sati draṣṭavyam || 12.38 ||
nidhyanvādhiyācitāvakrītādhayo naṣṭāḥ sarvān aninditān apuruṣāparādhena || 12.39 ||
nidhiḥ nikṣepaḥ, anvādhir upanidhiḥ, yācitam utsavādiṣu yācñayā ānīta ābharaṇādi, avakrītam adattamūlyam ardhadattamūlyaṃ vā, ādhir gopyādhiḥ kāṃsyādir ity arthaḥ, ete coraharaṇāgnidāhādinā naṣṭā santo na kevalaṃ putrān eva adhyābhaveyuḥ | kiṃ tarhi? yeṣāṃ sakāśe nikṣepādayaḥ kṛtāḥ tān api, sarvagrahaṇāt | aninditān apramādakāriṇa ity arthaḥ | apuruṣāparādhena yadi naśyanti | puruṣāparādhaḥ puruṣapramādaḥ ālasyādiḥ | evaṃ ca ninditaiḥ pramādakāribhiḥ te dātavyā eva || 12.39 ||
yadi caiṣāṃ putrādīnāṃ pitryam ṛṇaṃ na pratikuryāt kaścit, tatah steno bhavatīti, asya “aṣṭāpādyaṃ steyakilbiṣam” [GDh 12.12] iti daṇḍaś ca vihita ity anenaiva prasaṅgena hiraṇyastenasyāpi daṇḍa ucyate |
stenaḥ prakīrṇakeśo musalī rājānam īyāt karmācakṣāṇaḥ
|| 12.40 ||
stenaḥ suvarṇastenaḥ, anyadravyāpahāriṇas tu “aṣṭāpāyam” iti uktatvāt, smṛtyantradarśanāc ca |
suvarṇastenakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyāt māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti || [MDh 11.99]
daṇḍaprakaraṇe gamanopadeśaḥ prāyaścittārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
rājabhir dhṛtadaṇḍas tu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargam āyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā || iti | [MDh 8.318]
steye kṛte steno bhavati | steyaḥ parasvāpahāraḥ | buddhipūrvaviṣaya evedam | abuddhipūrvakasteyaṃ nopapadyata iti | mahaty apahṛte tv etad draṣṭavyam, viśeṣānārambhāt | prakīrṇakeśo muktakeśo mṛtasamam ātmānaṃ manyamānaḥ | musalī svavadhasādhanamusalahasto rājānaṃ ca gacchet, karma kathayan steno ‘ham asmīti || 12.40 ||
tataḥ evam upasthitaḥ |
pūto vadhamokṣābhyām || 12.41 ||
pūtaḥ śuddho bhavatīti | vadhān māraṇād vā mokṣād vā | vadhas tāḍanam, tad api sakṛd eva, smṛtyantaradarśanāt |
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam | iti | [MDh 11.100]
tatra rājñā yadi niḥśaṅkena tāḍito na mriyeta tato ‘pi pūta eva bhavatīti draṣṭavyam | abrāhmaṇaviṣayaṃ cedaṃ tāḍanam – “na śārīro brāhmaṇadaṇḍaḥ” [GDh 12.43] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapasaiva vā | iti | [MDh 11.100]
atraivakārāt brāhmaṇas tapasaiveti bhāṣyakṛtā vyākhyātam | tataś ca abrāhmaṇasya tāḍanam, brāhmaṇasya mokṣa iti draṣṭavyam | tathāpi brāhmaṇasya svayaṃ gamanapakṣe mokṣaḥ | yas tu balād ānīyate tasya mokṣaḥ tapaś ca draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatra svayaṃ gamanapakṣe daṇḍaś ca prāyaścittaṃ ceti draṣṭavyam | tapas tv aśrotriyabrāhmaṇasya sāntapanaṃ draṣṭvayam, aśrotriyabrāhmaṇasya suvarṇaharaṇaṃ cet | tathāha manuḥ |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ niryāty ātmaviśuddhaye | iti | [MDh 11.124]
niryāty eti sarvasteyaśeṣatvenānvetīti draṣṭavyam | śrotriyasya āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “steyaṃ kṛtvā surāṃ pītvā gurudāraṃ ca gatvā brahmahatyām akṛtvā caturthakālāmitabhojinaḥ syuḥ” [ĀpDh 1.25.9–10] ityādi | kathaṃ śrotriyasya gauravam iti cet, “viduṣo ‘tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvam” [GDh 12.14] iti uktatvāt | śrotriyabrāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe tv aśrotriyasya caitad eva śrotriyasya ṣaḍvarṣaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā: “suvarṇasteyakṛt ṣaḍvarṣaṃ brāhmaṇo vrataṃ caret” iti | kṣatriyasyāpi yadā prāyaścittaṃ samuccīyate tadā aśrotriyasuvarṇaharaṇe śrotriyasya bhārgavīyaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brāhmaṇasya hared yas tu carec cāndrāyaṇādikam | iti |
aśrotriyasya pūrvam uktam auśanasaṃ draṣṭavyam | śrotriyasvaharaṇe tv etad eva dviguṇam “śrotriyasvaharaṇe dviguṇam” iti kaṇvavacanāt | vaiśyasyāpi kṣatriyavad draṣṭavyam – “vaiśyasya kṣatriyavac coradaṇḍaḥ” ity auśanasavacanāt | śrotriyasvaharaṇe –
tapasāpanunutsus tu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam |
cīravāsā dvijo ‘raṇye caret brahmahaṇo vratam || [MDh 11.101]
iti manunokta vā draṣṭavyam | śūdrasya prāyaścittaṃ sarvatra “niṣkālako vā ghṛtākto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛty ātmānam avadāhayet” [VaDh 20.42] iti vasiṣṭhokaṃ draṣṭavyam | apahṛtadravyavaśād api prāyaścittasya gurulaghubhāvaḥ kalpyaḥ | kutaḥ?
tathā parimeyānāṃ śatād abhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ |
iti liṅgāt | kārṣāpaṇamūlyād adhika eva rājñā tāḍanam | itaratra sarvatra tu mokṣa eva draṣṭavyaḥ | kārṣāpaṇamūlyād adhika eva samastaprāyaścittapraveśaḥ | “ardhe ardhaṃ pāde pādam” iti ca draṣṭavyam | śūdrasya ca vaiśyavat kalpyam, “anyūnabhāve vaiśyavac chūdrasya kalpyam” iti śaṅkhavacanād iti || 12.41 ||
aghnannn enasvī rājā || 12.42 ||
dayādiyogād aghnan enasvī rājā bhavati | corasya yāvat pāpaṃ tāvad asya bhavatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “stenasyāpnoti kilbiṣam” [NSm 19.56] iti | | yathā vadhārhasyāvadhe doṣa evaṃ mokṣārhasyāmokṣe doṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “eno rājānam ṛcchaty utsṛjantaṃ sakilbiṣam” [VaDh 19.46] iti || 12.42 ||
na śārīro brāhmaṇadaṇḍaḥ || 12.43 ||
svayam upasthitasyāpi yaḥ śārīro daṇḍaḥ vadhaḥ sa brāhmaṇasya nāsti | tasmān na kartavyaḥ |
nanu ca karmaviyogādidaṇḍāntaravidhānād eva śarīrasya nivṛttir bhavaty eveti |
ucyate – satyam | evamārambhas tu anyatrāparādhe brāhmaṇasya śarīradaṇḍapratiṣedhārtha iti || 12.43 ||
tarhi katham asau śāsya ity ata āha |
karmaviyogavikhyāpanavivāsanāṅkakaraṇāni || 12.44 ||
yathā punas tat karma na karoti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | vikhyāpanaṃ cauryacihnena sarvataḥ paribhramaṇakaraṇam | vivāsanaṃ nirviṣayīkaraṇam | aṅkanaṃ cihnakaraṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhaga kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye tu śvapadaṃ kāryo brahmaghnas tv aśirāḥ pumān || iti | [MDh 9.237]
eṣām abhyāsāpekṣayā yathārho daṇḍo draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.44 ||
avṛttau prāyaścittī saḥ || 12.45 ||
acore evaṃ kṛte sa rājā prāyaścittī bhavati | prāyaścittaṃ tu smṛtyantarād draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “daṇḍyotsarge rājaikarātram upavaset trirātraṃ purohitaḥ kṛcchram adaṇḍadaṇḍane purohitas trirātraṃ rājā ca” [VaDh 19.40–43] iti || 12.45 ||
corasamaḥ sacivo matipūrve || 12.46 ||
corasya sacivaḥ sahāyaḥ buddhipūrve pratiśayānnādidāne coravan nigrāhyaḥ | evaṃ cābuddhipūrve na doṣaḥ || 12.46 ||
pratigrahītāpy adharmasaṃyukte || 12.47 ||
matipūrva ity vartate | pratigrahītāpy adharmasaṃyukte, stenasya prakṛtatvād eva siddhe adharmasaṃyuktagrahaṇaṃ yo yat pāpaṃ karoti tasya sakāśāt matipūrvaṃ pratigrahītur api avakīrṇiprāyaścittam ityādi | abuddhipūrve tu laghutaraṃ kalpyam || 12.47 ||
puruṣaśaktyaparādhānubandhavijñānād daṇḍaniyogaḥ
|| 12.48 ||
puruṣavijñānāj jātyādi jñātvety arthaḥ | śaktir durbalatvādiḥ | aparādhaḥ sāhasabhāṣaṇādi | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “steno ‘nupraveśān na duṣyate | śastradhārī sahoḍho vraṇasaṃpannaś ca vyapadiṣṭas tv ekeṣām” [VaDh 19.38–39] iti | anubandhaḥ adhyavasāyaḥ | etān avadhārya daṇḍadhāraṇaṃ kartavyam || 12.48 ||
anujñānaṃ vā vedavitsamavāyavacanād vedavitsamavāyavacanāt || 12.49 ||
vedavidāṃ saṅghavacanān mokṣo vā kartavyaḥ | saṅghaḥ samavāyaḥ caturṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ vā | yathāha manuḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi ya brūyur vedapāragāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ || iti |
vakṣyamāṇadaśāvarapariṣadanujñayā vā | evaṃ ca[53] svayaṃkaraṇe[54] doṣo draṣṭavyaḥ || 12.49 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
trayodaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vākpāruṣye sapratipattau daṇḍa uktaḥ | yadā tu vipratipadyante tadā katham ity ata āha |
vipratipattau sākṣinimittā satyavyavasthā || 13.1 ||
vipratipattir nānāvidhā pratipattiḥ arthipratyarthinoḥ | tasyāṃ satyāṃ sākṣihetukā satyavyavasthā satyasthāpanā kartavyā | sākṣiṇaḥ pañcaprakārāḥ, yathāha nāradaḥ |
likhitaḥ smāritaś caiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca |
gūḍhaś cottamasākṣī ca sākṣī pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | [NSm 1.130]
tān vicārya tattvāvabodha ity arthaḥ | sākṣinimittaṃ satyam iti vaktavye vyavasthāgrahaṇaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ vipratipattau bahūnāṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇam | tatrāpi saṃdehe tair eva śapathapūrvaṃ pratipāditam ity evamartham || 13.1 ||
te punaḥ kiyantaḥ kiṃlakṣaṇā ity ata āha |
bahavaḥ syur aninditāḥ svakarmasu prātyayikā rājñām
|| 13.2 ||
bahavaḥ tryavarāḥ syur ity arthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
tryavaraiḥ sākṣibhir bhāvyaṃ nṛpabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau || iti | [MDh 8.61]
syurgrahaṇaṃ saṃdehavyudāsārtham | aninditāḥ svakarmasu vihitānuṣṭhānā ity arthaḥ | svagrahaṇaṃ dhanaputrādāv api | yathāha manuḥ: “gṛhiṇaḥ putriṇo maulyāḥ” [MDh 8.62] ityādi | karmasv iti bahuvacanam āpadvihitasyāpy upasaṃgrahārtham | prātyayikā rājñām, pratyayena ye caranti te prātyayikāḥ, rājñāṃ bahuṣu karmasu parīkṣitāḥ sarvatrāpramattā eveti niścitā ity arthaḥ | bahuvacanād rājaniyuktānāṃ sabhāsadādīnām api || 13.2 ||
niṣprītyabhitāpāś cānyatarasmin || 13.3 ||
niṣprītayo nirabhitāpāś ca, na kṛtasnehā na śatravaś cety arthaḥ | cakārān nārthasaṃbandhinaś ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
nārthasaṃbandhino nāptā na sahāyā na vairiṇaḥ | [MDh 8.64]
ityādi | anyatarasminn arthini pratyarthini vety arthaḥ || 13.3 ||
api śūdrāḥ || 13.4 ||
evaṃvidhāḥ śūdrā api | kiṃ punar dvijātaya iti | evaṃ ca guṇavaddvijādyabhāve evaṃvidhāḥ śūdrā api bhavantīti draṣṭavyam || 13.4 ||
brāhmaṇas tv abrāhmaṇavacanād anavarodhyo ‘nibaddhaś cet || 13.5 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādiḥ | abrāhmaṇagrahaṇād eva siddhe brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ śrotriyaparigrahārtham | itaro grāhya eveti | tuśabdāt anye ‘pi liṅgino na grāhyā viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇā iti | abrāhmaṇavacanād anavarodhyo na grāhyaḥ, asya vṛttāntasyāsau brāhmaṇaḥ sākṣīty abrāhmaṇenoke rājñāsau sākṣitvena na grāhya ity arthaḥ | anibaddhaś cet brāhmaṇaḥ patrake ‘nāropitaś cet | āropito bhavaty evety abhiprāyaḥ || 13.5 ||
nāsamavetā apṛṣṭāḥ prabrūyuḥ || 13.6 ||
samuditā yāvantas te samavetā api rājñā prāḍvivākena vā apṛṣṭāḥ na prabrūyuḥ | ā praśnāt tadvyāpāraṃ prati maunina eva bhaveyur iti praśabdopādānam || 13.6 ||
kiṃ ca |
avacane ca doṣiṇaḥ syuḥ || 13.7 ||
praśnakāle ‘py avacane cakārād anyathā vacane ca doṣiṇo daṇḍabhājaḥ syuḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
sabhā vā na praveṣṭavyā vaktavyaṃ vā samañjasam |
abruvan vibruvan vāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī || iti | [MDh 8.13]
tasmāt tathyam eva vaktavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ || 13.7 ||
na kevalaṃ tathyavacane daṇḍābhāvamātraṃ viparyaye daṇḍabhāktvam eva[55] | kiṃ tarhi |
svargaḥ satyavacane viparyaye narakaḥ || 13.8 ||
satyavacananimittād dharmāt svargaś ca bhavati, viparyaye anṛtavacananimittād adharmān narakaś ca bhavati | vacanagrahaṇaṃ sākṣiṇām etat phalam, na tu vyavasthāhetor nṛpasyeti
|| 13.8 ||
idānīṃ yatra pañcaprakārā api sākṣiṇo na bhavanti tatra katham ity ata āha |
anibaddhair api vaktavyam || 13.9 ||
nibaddhāḥ niścayena baddhāḥ smṛtāḥ, pañcavidhatvena smṛtāḥ ity arthaḥ | ye pañcavidhatvena na smaryante te anibaddhāḥ, yena kenacit prakāreṇa tadvyāpārajñā tadgrāmasthā ity arthaḥ | tair api vaktavyam | apiśabdo nibaddhābhāvasūcanārthaḥ | anibaddhā apīty ukte ‘pi sākṣiprakaraṇatvāt sākṣiṇaḥ syur iti sāmarthyalabdhatvād eva samastārthe labhyamāne vaktavyagrahaṇam – tatrāpi yeṣāṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇatvena nirdiṣṭhaṃ teṣām eva vacanaṃ grāhyam, nārthasaṃbandhināṃ vacanam, tataś cārthasaṃbandhādidoṣarahitair eva vaktavyam iti
|| 13.9 ||
na pīḍākṛte nibandhaḥ || 13.10 ||
atra bandhaśabdena vyavasthā ucyate | niścayaṃ bandho nibandhaḥ | pīḍākṛte hiṃsādau | na nibandhaḥ na pratiṣedho ‘stīty arthaḥ | pañcaprakārair anyaiś ca arthasaṃbandhādidūṣaṇayuktair api vaktavyam evety abhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca uśanā: “sāhase sarve bhavanti” iti || 13.10 ||
tathā |
pramattokte ca || 13.11 ||
pramattaḥ ajñātaḥ, vañcanayā vā, praśabdopādānāt | yadā nigūḍhāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ kṛtvā upāyair enaṃ rahasy āpādayanti tataḥ tatsthāḥ arthasaṃbandhādidūṣaṇayuktā api sākṣiṇo bhavantīti | pramattenokte paribhāṣita ity arthaḥ | cakārāt strīgamanavyāpāre ‘pi | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
steye ca sāhase caiva saṃsarge ca striyas tathā |
garādīnāṃ prayoge ca na doṣaḥ sākṣiṣu smṛtaḥ || iti |
viparyaye naraka uktaḥ | saḥ na kevalaṃ teṣām eva || 13.11 ||
kiṃ tarhi |
sākṣisabhyarājakartṛṣu doṣo dharmatantrapīḍāyām
|| 13.12 ||
yatra dharmatantrapīḍā kriyate samyagvyavahāradarśanāt tatra yasyaiva[56] [yathaiva] doṣeṇa kartā lipyate, evaṃ sākṣyādayo ‘pi |
nanu ca sākṣiṇo doṣa ukta eva | kim arthaṃ punar ihābhidhānam iti |
ucyate – yadīha[57] sākṣigrahaṇaṃ na kriyate tataḥ sabhyādīnām asākṣika eva vyavahāre doṣa uktaḥ syāt sasākṣike ‘pi doṣaprāpaṇārtham | yāvatā doṣeṇa kartā saṃbadhyate tāvān doṣaḥ sabhyādīnām api svāparādhāt pṛthak pṛthag upapadyate na tu khaṇḍaśa iti draṣṭavyam | tatraśabdena lokavyavahāra ucyate | tataś ca tatpīḍā ca na kartavyeti tadgrahaṇam || 13.12 ||
śapathenaike satyakarma || 13.13 ||
yadā tu sākṣyādipramāṇāntarapratyastamayaḥ tadā śapathena kośādinā eke satyakarma satyavyavasthāpanam icchanti | śapathaś ca prayujyamānasya, itareṇa vyavasitatvāt | satyam iti vaktavye karmagrahaṇaṃ vyavahārād anyatrāpi laukikavyavahārasaṃdehe śapathasyānupraveśārtham | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “anyām udīya yadi yātudhāno asmi” ityādi | gautamas tu nyāyenaiva | tatra pramāṇaleśe sati sapatham, asati nyāya eveti draṣṭavyam || 13.13 ||
tad devarājabrāhmaṇasaṃsadi syād abrāhmaṇānām
|| 13.14 ||
tac chapathaṃ devasaṃsadi devasaṇnidhau | devaśabdena devatāpratimāḥ ādityādayo vā ucyante | rājā vyavahāradarśī | brāhmaṇāḥ sabhyādayaḥ | tadgrahaṇaṃ laukikavyavahāranivṛttyartham | evaṃ ca vyavahāre devatādisaṃnidhau, laukike yatra kutracid iti draṣṭavyam | arthāntaranimitto vyavahāraḥ, aparādhanimitto laukika iti | abrāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ | syādgrahaṇaṃ kramanivṛttyartham | tatra devatālābhe viprasaṃsadi, tadalābhe rājasaṃsadi draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
abrāhmaṇān nṛpaḥ samyak kārayed devasaṃnidhau |
śapathaṃ tadalābhe ca brāhmaṇasya samīpataḥ ||
tadalābhe nṛpasyātha bhagavān kāśyapo ‘bravīt || iti |
alābho ‘pi kāryāntaravyagrāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | aviprāṇām iti vaktavye gurusūtrakaraṇam aśrotriyabrāhmaṇasyāpi devatāsaṃnidhipraveśārtham | evaṃ ca śrotriyabrāhmaṇasya satyaśabdoccāraṇamātrād eva śapathaṃ bhavatīti draṣṭavyam |
smṛtyantaropasaṃgrahārthaṃ syādgrahaṇam ity eke | yathāha manuḥ |
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || iti | [MDh 8.113]
devarājabrāhmaṇasamakṣam iti vaktavye saṃsadigrahaṇaṃ devatāyāḥ saṃnihitasthānopasaṃgrahārtham | sabhāyāś ca chalādidoṣarahitāyāḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
na sā sabhā yatra na santi vṛddhā
na te vṛddhā ye na vadanti dharmam |
nāsau dharmo yatra na satyam asti
na tat satyaṃ yac chalenānuviddham || [NSm Mātṛkā 3.17]
iti || 13.14 ||
sākṣiṇāṃ sāmānyena doṣa uktaḥ | viśeṣārtham idānīm āha |
kṣudrapaśvanṛte sākṣī daśa hanti || 13.15 ||
kṣudrapaśavaḥ ajāvikādayaḥ | tadviṣaye ‘nṛtavacane daśa puruṣān hanti | aviśeṣāt svabandhūn hantīti jñāyate | daśasvabandhuhanane yāvat pāpaṃ tāvad asya bhavatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ sarvatra | tataś ca tadanurūpeṇa daṇḍaprāyaścittakalpanam | sākṣigrahaṇaṃ sabhyādīnām apy upalakṣanārtham || 13.15 ||
go’śvapuruṣabhūmiṣu daśaguṇottarān || 13.16 ||
uttaram uttaraṃ daśaguṇaṃ bhavati | goviṣaye śataguṇam | aśvaviṣaye sahasraguṇam ityādi |
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte || [MDh 8.98]
ityādi yad uktaṃ smṛtyantare tatparigrahaviśeṣāpekṣayā prayojanāpekṣayā vā draṣṭavyam
|| 13.16 ||
sarvaṃ vā bhūmau || 13.17 ||
bhūmiviṣaye śatasahasraṃ sarvaṃ vā manuṣyajātam | kṛtākṛtakṣetrabhedena vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ
|| 13.17 ||
haraṇe narakaḥ || 13.18 ||
bhūmer iti vipariṇāmaḥ | aviśeṣāt ā kalpāt bhavati | doṣavacanaprasaṅgenaitad uktam
|| 13.18 ||
idānīṃ prakṛtam āha |
apsu maithunasaṃyoge ca || 13.19 ||
apsu kūpādyādhārāvasthitāsu maithunasaṃyoge ca parastrīgamanādāv ity arthaḥ | saṃyogagrahaṇāt kanyālakṣaṇādāv api | cakāraḥ abgrahaṇaṃ maithunaviśeṣaṇaṃ mā bhūd ity evamarthaḥ | evaṃ ca haraṇe ‘pi narako draṣṭavyaḥ || 13.19 ||
paśuvan madhusarpiṣoḥ || 13.20 ||
madhughṛtaviṣaye kṣudrapaśvanṛtatulyo doṣaḥ || 13.20 ||
govad vastrahiraṇyadhānyabrahmasu || 13.21 ||
vastrādiviṣaye ‘nṛte gavānṛtavad doṣaḥ brahmānṛtam adhītavedam anadhītavedam ity
ukte || 13.21 ||
yāneṣv aśvavat || 13.22 ||
hastyādiyānanimitte ‘nṛte aśvānṛtatulyo doṣaḥ | yāneṣv iti bahuvacanam acetanānāṃ śibikādīnām apy upasaṃgrahārtham |
kecid anyathā vyācakṣate – paśuvan madhusarpiṣor ity ārabhya paśvanṛtavad doṣaḥ madhusarpiṣor haraṇe | vastrādiharaṇe ca govad doṣaḥ | tatra brahmaśabdena vedāṅgāni, likhitāni pustakāny āhuḥ | yānaharaṇe cāśvānṛtatulyo doṣa iti || 13.22 ||
yadi tv evam api doṣe ukte sākṣyanṛtam eva vakti tataḥ |
mithyāvacane yāpyo daṇḍyaś ca sākṣī || 13.23 ||
anṛta ukte yāpyaḥ garhyaḥ sarvalokasamakṣaṃ saṃvyavahāravicchedanārtham | puruṣaśaktyādivijñānāt tanmūlyapramāṇena daṇḍyaś ca bhavati | arthaprayojanāpekṣayā parigrahaviśeṣāpekṣayā ca samastavyastaparikalpanārthaś cakāraḥ | adhikārād eva siddhe sākṣigrahaṇaṃ sa eva yāpyo daṇḍyaś ca | sabhyādayo daṇḍyā eveti || 13.23 ||
asyāpavādam āha |
nānṛtavacane doṣo jīvanaṃ cet tadadhīnam || 13.24 ||
yadā satyavacanāt parasyāvaśyabhāvī jīvanacchedaḥ tadā sākṣiṇānṛtam api vaktavyam | tasminn ukte na pūrvokto doṣaḥ || 13.24 ||
na tu pāpīyaso jīvanam || 13.25 ||
tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ etaj jñāpayati – avaśyaṃbhāvyanṛtavacane doṣaḥ, tathāpi tu vadhanimittāt satyād anṛtam eva viśiṣṭam alpataradoṣatvād iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatroktārthaṃ bhaved vadhaḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tad dhi tatra viśiṣyate || iti | [MDh 8.104]
tad evaṃ manyeta – jīvann apy ayaṃ pāpakarmaratir eva bhavati steyādy eva karotīti tadā satyam eva vaktavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | pāpīyasa ity ātiśāyanikapratyayanirdeśaḥ | parapīḍā kartavyeva satyaṃ vaktavyam, na vihitākaraṇamātre pratiṣiddhakaraṇamātre veti || 13.25 ||
uktāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ, kena praṣṭavyā ity ata āha |
rājā prāḍvivāko brāhmaṇo vā śāstravit || 13.26 ||
pṛcchatīti prāṭ | pṛṣṭaṃ vivektīti vivākaḥ | pṛcchati vā asau vivādinaḥ[58] sākṣiṇo vā yad[59] uktaṃ taṃ vicārayatīti prāḍvivākaḥ | sa rājaiva bhavet, śrānte tasminn anyakarmapravṛtte brāhmaṇo vā śāstravid bahuśruta ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
yadā svayaṃ na kuryāt tu nṛpatiḥ kāryadarśanam |
tadā niyuñjyād vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kāryadarśinam || [MDh 8.9]
iti || 13.26 ||
evam uktalakṣaṇam |
prāḍvivākam adhyābhavet || 13.27 ||
adhi ābhavet upary enaṃ gacched ity arthaḥ | idam api brāhmaṇapakṣe, rājñas tu “tam upary āsīnam adhastāḍ upāsīran” [GDh 11.7] ity uktatvāt | tam adhyābhaved iti siddhe prāḍvivākagrahaṇaṃ niyamārtham, tam evoparisthaṃ gacchen na sahāyam api[60] | āṅgrahaṇam arthinam eva paricchedayen na punar utpādayed iti | tathā ca manuḥ: “notpādayet svayaṃ kāryam” [MDh 8.43] ityādi || 13.27 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣetāpratibhāyām || 13.28 ||
yadābhiyuktasyārthinaḥ sākṣiṇāṃ vā pratibhānaṃ na bhavati apratipattir bhavati tadā saṃvatsaraṃ teṣāṃ pratīkṣeta | saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ yathāpratibhaṃ paricchedayed ity arthaḥ
|| 13.28 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
dhenvanaḍutstrīprajananasaṃyukte ca śighram || 13.29 ||
dhenvādisaṃbandhe vyavahāre na saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣeta, kālakṣepe dhenvādyupabhogakālo ‘tīto bhavatīti | dhenuḥ sakṣīrā gauḥ | anaḍvān balīvardaḥ, asāv api saṃvatsarabhogena jīryatīti | strīprajananasaṃyukte ‘pi ca, saṃyuktagrahaṇasya pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyamānatvāt | strīprajananaṃ kanyāvivāhaḥ, saṃvatsarapratīkṣaṇe tasyāpi kālātikramo bhavatīti | vivāha iti vaktavyam iti cen, na prajananayogyagrahaṇārthatvāt | evaṃ cāsamarthāyāḥ saṃvatsarapratīkṣaṇe ‘pi na doṣaḥ | strīgrahaṇaṃ savarṇajātyupasaṃgrahārtham | tataś cāsavarṇāyāṃ pratīkṣaṇaṃ kartavyam iti sūcayati | cakāraḥ pratyekam iti sūcanārthaḥ | dhenusaṃyukte anaḍutsaṃyukte ityādi, mā bhūt samuditānām eva viṣaya iti | śīghragrahaṇaṃ savarṇajātyupasaṃgrahārtham || 13.29 ||
ātyayike ca | 13.30 ||
śīghraṃ nirṇayaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | atyayakarma[61] yathā sārdham uktasya sārdho gacchati, mriyamāṇāvastho vānyataro bhavati, grāmopaplavo vā bhavatītyādi | atyayavaśāt dhenvādiyuktād api pūrvaṃ vicāraṇīyam iti pṛthak karaṇam | caśabdaḥ steyasāhasadaṇḍapāruṣyādyupasaṃgrahaṇārthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
sāhasasteyapāruṣyago’bhiśāpātyayastriyām |
vivādayed sadya eva kālo ‘nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ || [YDh 2.12]
iti || 13.30 ||
sarvadharmebhyo garīyaḥ prāḍvivāke satyavacanaṃ satyavacanam || 13.31 ||
sarvadharmebhyaḥ śrutismṛtyupadiṣṭebhyo dharmasādhanebhyo gurutaraṃ prāḍvivāke vyavahāradraṣṭari yathābhūtārthadarśitvam ity arthaḥ | prāḍvivāke vā saṃnihite sākṣyādīnāṃ yat satyavacanam iti || 13.31 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye trayodaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
caturdaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vihitākaraṇe daṇḍaprāyaścitte ukte | kasmin kāle vihitam akurvato neṣyete ity ata āha |
śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram anṛtvigdīkṣita-brahmacāriṇāṃ sapiṇḍānām || 14.1 ||
śavanimitto ‘śucibhāvo daśāhorātrāṇi bhavati | keṣāṃ? sapiṇḍānām | tatsvarūpaṃ vakṣyati | ṛtvigādivarjitānām | ṛtvig yājakaḥ pravṛttakarmātra gṛhyate dīkṣitasāhacaryāt | yadi bhūtapūrvagatir ihāśrīyate “ṛtvik śvaśuraḥ” ityādivat, dīkṣitabrahmacāriṇor apy evaṃ prasajyeta | tataś ca sarvasya bhūtapūrvagatyā brahmacāritvād ṛtvigdīkṣitagrahaṇam anarthakaṃ syāt | tasmāt sarva eva pravṛttakriyā gṛhyante | dīkṣito yajamānaḥ | brahmacārī yāvad brahmacaryam | upariṣṭāt kṣatriyādīnām upadeśād brāhmaṇaviṣayam idam | sūtakasya vakṣyamāṇatvād āśaucaṃ daśarātram ity ukta eva śavanimittasya siddhe śāvagrahaṇaṃ śavaprabhṛti divasagaṇaṇā kartavyety evamartham |
nanu ca smṛtyantare saṃskāraprabhṛti śrūyate:
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣipet [kṣapet?] tryaham eva vā | [MDh 5.69]
iti pūrvakālakriyāyām | tathā “āhitāgniś cet pravasan mriyeta punaḥ saṃskāraṃ kṛtvā śavavac chaucam” [VaDh 4.37] iti | tenedaṃ viruddham iti |
atrocyate – viṣayavibhāgenāvirodha iti | tathā vyāghradharme viṣayavibhāgapratipādanaparaṃ vākyaṃ śrūyate |
maraṇād eva kartavyaṃ saṃyogo yasya nāgnibhiḥ |
dahanād eva kartavyaṃ yasya vaitāniko vidhiḥ || iti |
“patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭi” [GDh 14.29] ityanena śavanimittāśaucasparśane snānavidhānād evāśucibhāve siddhe | āśaucagrahaṇm adhyayanāder apy anadhikārārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho yajñaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate || iti |
śāvāśaucam iti vaktavye śāvam āśaucam iti visamāsakaraṇaṃ śavāṅgabhūtānām asthnāṃ yāvat saṃcayanaṃ tāvad eva parasparasparśaṇaṃ praty aśucibhāva iti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvam aṅgasparśo vidhīyate | iti |
asthisaṃcayanasvarūpqṃ smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
prathame ca dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca caturthake |
asthisaṃcayanaṃ kuryād bandhubhir hitam īpsubhiḥ || iti |
tatra sadyaḥśaucādhikāriṇāṃ prathame ‘hni, ekāhasaṃbandhināṃ dvitīye ‘hni, tryahasaṃbandhināṃ tṛtīye ‘hni, caturahasaṃbandhināṃ daśāhasaṃbandhināṃ caturthe ‘hnīti draṣṭavyam | tathā tatraivāparaṃ vākyaṃ śrūyate |
caturthe pañcame caiva saptame daśame tathā |
asthisaṃcayanaṃ kuryād varṇānām ānupūrvyaśaḥ || iti |
tatra brāhmaṇasya caturthe kṣatriyasya pañcame vaiśyasya saptame śūdrasya daśama iti draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇasyoktam, evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor api vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣayā svalpāśaucānāṃ svalpam ity anena nyāyena kalpyam iti | brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ parivrājakādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ śāve cāpi tathaiva ca || iti |
dīkṣitagrahaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇādau pravṛttānām api grahaṇārtham | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na rājñām aghadoṣo ‘sti vratināṃ satriṇāṃ tathā |
aindrasthānam upāsīnāḥ brahmapūtā hi te sadā || iti | [VaDh 19.48]
“śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ” [GDh 14.24], “ekādaśarātraṃ kṣatriyasya” [GDh 14.2] iti ca liṅgād eva daśarātraṃ grahītuṃ śakyate | daśarātragrahaṇaṃ brāhmaṇasyāśauce kṣatriyāder api daśarātram eveti niyamārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kṣatraviṭchūdradāyādā ye syur viprasya bāndhavāḥ |
teṣām aśauce viprasya daśāhaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet || iti |
viprasyāśauce prāpte teṣāṃ daśāham eveti tatrārthaḥ | manur api |
sarve tūttamavarṇānām āśaucaṃ kuryur ādṛtāḥ |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena svaṃ tu śaucaṃ svayoniṣu || iti |
brāhmaṇāśaucae teṣāṃ daśāhopadeśāt teṣām āśauce viprasya smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ janmahānyoḥ svayoniṣu |
ṣaṭ trir ekaṃ tathā proktaṃ kṣatraviṭśūdrayoniṣu ||
iti || 14.1 ||
ekādaśarātraṃ kṣatriyasya || 14.2 ||
ṛtvigdīkṣitabrahmacārivarjitānāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ śāvam āśaucam iti vartate || 14.2 ||
dvādaśarātraṃ vaiśyasya || 14.3 ||
pūrvavat anuvartate || 14.3 ||
ardhamāsam eke || 14.4 ||
pañcadaśāham ity eke | vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | evaṃ ca brāhmaṇādīnām api vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣāstīti gamyate | tathā ca parāśaraḥ |
ekāhāc chudyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu nirguṇo daśabhir dinaiḥ || iti | [PārSm 3.5]
tathā ca manur api |
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
arvāk saṃcayanād asthnāṃ tryaham ekāham eva vā || iti | [MDh 5.59]
atra arvāk saṃcayanād iti caturahopalakṣaṇam | ekaguṇayoge caturaheṇaiva śuddhiḥ, dviguṇayoge tryaheṇa, sarvaguṇayoge tv ekāhena | sarvaguṇayoge sati vṛttisaṃkoco ‘pi bhavati tadā sadyaḥśaucam iti bhāṣyakṛtā vyākhyātam | tathā kṣatriyasyāpi vākyacatuṣṭayam ārabhyate – “ekādaśarātreṇa” [GDh 14.2] iti gautamaḥ | “pañcadaśarātreṇa” [VaDh 4.28] iti vasiṣṭhaḥ | “dvādaśarātreṇa” [YDh 3.22] iti yājñavalkyaḥ | “daśarātreṇa” iti parāśaraḥ | brāhmaṇasya daśarātravat kṣatriyasyāpi pañcadaśāha eva nityaḥ | tatraikaguṇayoge dvādaśarātram, dviguṇayoge ekādaśarātram, sarvaguṇayoge daśāham, nirguṇasya pañcadaśāhaḥ | tathā vaiśyasyāpi – ekaguṇayoge “ardhamāsam eke” ity ardhamāsaḥ, dviguṇayoge “dvādaśarātraṃ vaiśyasya” iti dvādaśāhaḥ, sarvaguṇayoge parāśaroktam ekādaśarātram, nirguṇasya viṃśatirātraṃ vasiṣṭhenoktam | tad eva nityam ity evaṃ kalpanā draṣṭavyā || 14.4 ||
māsaṃ śūdrasya || 14.5 ||
asya dīkṣādyabhāvāt sapiṇḍānām ity eva | vṛttādyabhāvāc caitad eva | asacchūdraviṣayaṃ cedam, “sacchūdrāṇām ardhamāsam” ity auśanasavacanāt || 14.5 ||
tac ced antaḥ punar āpatec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran || 14.6 ||
tad eva samānajātīyamaraṇe maraṇam, janane jananam iti draṣṭavyam,[62] tadgrahaṇāt | punaḥ bhūyo ‘pi anekaśo ‘pīty arthaḥ | āpated āgacchet | tataḥ pūrvāśaucāvaśiṣṭenaiva kālena śuddhiḥ syāt nāgatasya kālapratīkṣaṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | antargrahaṇaṃ vispaṣṭārtham “śeṣeṇa śudhyeran,” [GDh 6.7] “rātriśeṣe dvābhyām, prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ” [GDh 18.8] ity etair eva madhyamasya jñātuṃ śakyatvāt |
anye tu “janane ‘py evam” [GDh 14.14] ity atideśād eva siddhe tadgrahaṇaṃ samānakālāpekṣam āhuḥ | yathā daśarātrapramāṇāśauce daśarātrapramāṇāśaucam iti | yadi svalpapramāṇake mahatpramāṇam āgacchati tat pūrveṇa na gacched iti | tathā ca paiṅgigṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “aghavṛddhau paścimena samāpayet” iti | aghavṛddhāv ity uktaḥ alpāghaṃ gacched eveti draṣṭavyam || 14.6 ||
rātriśeṣe dvābhyām || 14.7 ||
yady ekarātryavaśiṣṭe pūrvāśauce tadanyad āpatet tato na pūrveṇaiva śuddhiḥ kiṃ tu dvābhyāṃ rātribhyām | evaṃ ca trirātraṃ parigṛhītaṃ bhavati || 14.7 ||
prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ || 14.8 ||
prabhāte snānāt pūrvam, saṅgavakālāt pūrvam ity arthaḥ, “aghabhājaḥ saṅgave snātvā grāmaṃ praviśeyuḥ” ity arthavādadarśanāt | yady āpatet tato na rātriśeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ | kiṃ tu tisṛbhiḥ rātribhiḥ || 14.8 ||
gobrāhmaṇahatānām anvakṣam || 14.9 ||
gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā hatānāṃ yāvad darśanam āśaucam | sadyaḥ śaucam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.95]
sadyaḥśaucādhikāre idam uktam | gobrāhmaṇahatānām anyatarapramāpitānāṃ vā | tathā ca uśanā: “gobhir hatānāṃ brāhmaṇena hatānāṃ ca sadyaḥśaucam” iti || 14.9 ||
rājakrodhāc cāyuddhe || 14.10 ||
anvakṣam iti vartate | caśabdo ‘piśabdasyārthe, bhinnakramaś ca | rājakrodhād ayuddhe ‘pīti | apiśabdāt yuddhe ‘pi | tathā ca manuḥ |
udyatair āhave śastraiḥ kṣatradharmahatasya tu |
sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate yajñas tadāśaucam iti sthitiḥ || iti | [MDh 5.98]
caśabdaḥ samuccayārtho vā | tataś ca ya rājā krodhāt ghātayed ayuddhe tasya sadyaḥśaucam | yuddhe akrodhenāpi hatasyeti |
kecid evaṃ vyācakṣate – rājakrodhād dhatānāṃ yuddhe ‘pi janapadakṣobhādibhiḥ hatānām iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca | iti | [MDh 5.95]
anye tu vivāhakālam āhuḥ | ayuddhe rājakrodhād dhatānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam, vivāhakāle ca sadyaḥśaucam iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṛcchre vivāhe yajñe ca saṃgrāme deśaviplave |
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
iti || 14.10 ||
prāyānāśakaśastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanaprapatanaiś cecchatām || 14.11 ||
anvakṣam iti vartate | buddhipūrvaṃ prāyādibhir ātmānaṃ vyāpadayatāṃ sadyaḥśaucam | prāyaḥ mahāprasthānam | tad anicchato ‘pi rājabhayādinā saṃbhavatīti icchatām ity uktam | anāśaka abhojanam, etad api durbhikṣādāv anicchato ‘pi saṃbhavatīti | śastraṃ khaḍgādi, tatrāpi pramādāt saṃbhavaty eva | udbandhanaṃ rajjvādinā, atrāpi parihāsādinā anicchato ‘pi saṃbhavati | prapatanaṃ parvatād vṛkṣād vā, tatrāpi daivād abuddhipūrvam api saṃbhavaty eva | evaṃ cābuddhipūrvam etair mṛtānāṃ yathāprāptam eveti draṣṭavyam | cakārād anyair apy evaṃbhūtair ātmāhananahetubhir iti draṣṭavyam || 14.11 ||
idānīṃ sapiṇḍasvarūpam āha |
piṇḍanivṛttiḥ saptame pañcame vā || 14.12 ||
ṣaṭpuruṣān atītya saptame prāpte | pitrādibhyas tribhyaḥ piṇḍadānam | teṣu jīvatsu yebhyaḥ pitā dadāti tebhya eva putro ‘pīty aparebhyas tribhya iti ṣaṭpuruṣā piṇḍasaṃbandhinaḥ | yadā jīvapitṛkasya “na jīvantam ati dadāti” it jīvapitā pitāmahāya prapitāmahāyeti dadyād ity āśrīyate tadā pañcame nivṛttiḥ | strīṇām apy etat tulyaṃ pitṛpakṣe vā saptame nivṛttiḥ | mātṛpakṣe pañcama iti || 14.12 ||
janane ‘py evam || 14.13 ||
daśāha eva atidiśyate | anyathā mātur api sadyaḥśaucaṃ prāpnoti | tad ācāreṇa virudhyate, gṛhyasmṛtyā ca “daśarātraṃ dampatī sūtakinau bhavataḥ” iti | tataḥ yad eva śāva upadiṣṭaṃ daśarātraṃ tat sapiṇḍānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavatīti pratipattavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
dantajāte ‘nujāte ca kṛtacaule ca saṃsthite |
aśuddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathocyate || iti | [MDh 5.58]
tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
sūtake tu sapiṇḍānāṃ pitror vā mātur eva vā |
āśaucaṃ daśarātraṃ syāt tryahaṃ toyapradāyinām || iti |
tatrāpi dīkṣitādivarjanaṃ draṣṭavyam | apiśabdāt “tac ced antaḥ punar āpatet” [GDh 14.6]
ityādi || 14.13 ||
mātāpitros tat || 14.14 ||
mātāpitror vā sūtakam iti | uparitanavāśabdo ‘trāpy adhyāhartavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘py atraiva vāśabdam uktavān: “mātāpitror vā tannimittatvāt” [VaDh 4.21–22] iti || 14.14 ||
mātur vā || 14.15 ||
tasyaiva vā jātasya mātur na sapatnyā ity arthaḥ | vyavasthitavikalpo ‘tra draṣṭavyaḥ – yadi sarve vṛttahīnās tadānīṃ sarveṣāṃ sūtakaṃ syāt | yadi sarve vṛttavantaḥ pitā vṛttahīnaḥ tadā pitroḥ | yadā pitā ca vṛttavān saṅkocavṛttiś ca tadā mātur eveti | yadi tu pitā agnihotrī saṅkocavṛttir na bhavati tadā upaspṛśyāgnihotraṃ hutvā punar āśaucaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti || 14.15 ||
garbhamāsasamā rātrīḥ sraṃsane garbhasya || 14.16 ||
garbhasraṃsane tu yāvān māso garbhas tāvanty ahorātrāṇi bhavanti | ā pañcamān māsāt garbhasraṃsanam, ata ūrdhvaṃ tu garbhapatanam iti veditavyam | kutaḥ? “tasmāt pañcame māsi garbhā vikriyante” iti brāhmaṇaliṅgāt | tataś ca yāvanmāso garbhaḥ sraṃsate tattulyāny ahorātrāṇi bhavantīty arthaḥ || 14.16 ||
tryahaṃ vā || 14.17 ||
dvaimāsikatraimāsikagarbhasraṃsane tryaham, ūrdhvaṃ tu māsatulyam ity vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | patane ‘pi māsatulyam evāśaucaṃ draṣṭavyam, “garbhe tanmāsasamam ahar āśaucam” iti paiṅgigṛhyavacanāt | āśaucaṃ mātur eva, tadadhikārāt | tathā ca devagṛhyavacanam: “garbhasthe prete mātur eva syād āśaucam” iti | māsatulyanyāyenaiva jananottarakālaṃ mṛtasyāpi daśarātraṃ siddham | garbhasthe prete mātur evety evakārāt jananottarakālaṃ pitror bhavatīti gamyate | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
bāle mṛte sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
daśāhenaiva dampatyoḥ sodarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
sodarāṇāṃ daśāhagrahaṇaṃ nāmakaraṇād ūrdhvam eva draṣṭavyam | tathā ca paiṅgigṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “garbhasthe prete mātur eva syād āśaucaṃ jāta ubhayoḥ | kṛtanāmni sodarāṇāṃ ca | akṛtanāmny apīty eke | dantajāte kṛtacūḍe sarveṣām” ityādi | dantajāte kṛtacaule sarveṣāṃ dvādaśaputrāṇāṃ daśāha ity eva draṣṭavyam | “māsasamā rātrīḥ sraṃsane garbhasya” [GDh 14.16] ity eva siddhe garbhagrahaṇaṃ garbhasaṃnikṛṣṭāyā mātur evedam āśaucam iti niyamārtham || 14.17 ||
śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ pakṣiṇīm || 14.18 ||
daśamīgrahaṇaṃ sarvāśaucapradarśanārtham | pakṣiṇy ahardvayamadhyagatā rātriḥ, rātridvayamadhyagatam ahar vā | sapiṇḍajananaṃ maraṇaṃ vā śrutvā pakṣiṇy āśaucaṃ bhavati | caśabdāt “sametya cākṛtacaulānāṃ tisṛbhiḥ” ity uktatvāt, kṛtacaulānām idaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrāpi caturahasaṃbandhinām idaṃ draṣṭavyam | tryahasaṃbandhināṃ tu “deśāntarasthe prete ūrdhvaṃ daśāhāc chrutvā ekarātram āśaucam” [VaDh 4.36] iti vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | ekāhasaṃbandhināṃ tu “bāle deśāntarasthe” ity anena sadyaḥśaucam | daśāhasaṃbandhināṃ tu,
atikrānte daśāhe tu trirātram āśucir bhavet | [MDh 5.76]
iti manunoktaṃ tryahaṃ draṣṭavyam | sarveṣām ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāt snānam eva,
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpo viśuddhyati | [MDh 5.76]
iti manuvacanāt || 14.18 ||
asapiṇḍe yonisaṃbandhe sahādhyāyini ca || 14.19 ||
asapiṇḍaḥ samānodakaḥ | tasmin mṛte samānodakānāṃ pakṣiṇī | idam api caturahasaṃbandhinaḥ | ekāhasaṃbandhinas tu sadhyaḥśaucam | “bāle deśāntarasthe” ity anena tryaham asaṃbandhina ekāhaḥ kalpyaḥ | daśāhasaṃbandhinaḥ “tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ” [MDh 64] iti tryaham ity eva draṣṭavyam | yonisaṃbandhinaḥ mātulamātṛbhāgineyapaitṛṣvasreyāḥ | teṣu mṛteṣu pakṣiṇī | tathā sahādhyāyini ca | sahādhyāyī ekopādhyāyakaḥ | caśabdāt suhṛdi ca || 14.19 ||
sabrahmacāriṇy ekāham || 14.20 ||
sabrahmacārī samānacaraṇaḥ, tasmin mṛte ekāhorātram āśaucaṃ gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ, sarvatra gṛhṇīyād ity adhyāhārāt || 14.20 ||
śrotriye copasaṃpanne || 14.21 ||
ekāṃ rātriṃ gṛhṇīyād iti vartate | śrotriye ṣaḍaṅgavedādhyāyini upasaṃpanne jñānānuṣṭhānasaṃpadupete asabrahmacāriṇy api | upasaṃpanno vā kenacit kāraṇena āśritaḥ samīpapāṭhī vā | caśabdād rājani ca || 14.21 ||
pretopasparśane daśarātram āśaucam abhisaṃdhāya cet
|| 14.22 ||
pretasya mṛtasya upasparśane nirharaṇa ity arthaḥ | upaśabdaprayogān na sparśanamātre | daśarātram āśaucam, abhisaṃdhāya ced yadi dṛṣṭam aṅgīkṛtya spṛśati, na dharmārthakāmato vā | saṃsargamātram atrāśaucam, na tu vakṣyamāṇam “adhaḥśayyāsaninaḥ” [GDh 2.27] ityādi, āśaucādhikāre punar āśaucagrahaṇāt | atraiva viṣayavibhāgaḥ pratipattavyaḥ – sapiṇḍānāṃ madhye yaḥ kaścid vahati saṃskartā vā bhavati tasya viśeṣo nāsti tatpratipādakavacanābhāvāt | samānodakānāṃ tu daśarātram,
ahnā caikena rātryā ca trirātrair eva ca tribhiḥ |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ || [MDh 5.64]
iti manuvacanāt, samānodakānāṃ ye pretanirharaṇaṃ kurvanti saṃskāraṃ vā kurvanti teṣāṃ daśarātram āśaucam itareṣṃ trirātram iti bhāṣyakṛtā vyākhyātatvāt | ye ca samānodakebhyo ‘nye teṣām api manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān ||
yady annam atti teṣāṃ yaḥ sa daśāhena śudhyati |
anadann annam ahnaiva na ca tasmin gṛhe vaset || iti | [MDh 5.101–102]
atra śavanirharaṇaṃ saṃskāraṃ vā kṛtvā kaṭakaraṇaṃ ca karoti tadannaṃ vāśnāti ced daśāhaḥ | kaṭakaraṇamātram eva kṛtvā tad annaṃ nāśnāti cet tryahaḥ | kaṭakaraṇaṃ ca na karoti tadgṛhe ca vasati ced ahorātram | grāmaṃ ca na praviśati ahnaiveti evakārāt sajyotir bhavati | sapiṇḍam asapiṇḍaṃ vā dṛṣṭārtham aṅgīkṛtya vahati tasyedaṃ sūtraṃ vijñeyam iti | brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇasparśana evaitat, vaiśyaśudrayoḥ pṛthagārambhāt | kṣatriyasyāpi kṣatriyopasparśane ātmīyam eva, ādyantābhidhāne madhyaprasiddheḥ || 14.22 ||
uktaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || 14.23 ||
samānajātīyapretopasparśane ātmīyam eva nityatvenopāttam āśaucaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ || 14.23 ||
ārtavīr vā || 14.24 ||
ṛtusamānasaṃkhyā vā rātrīr vaiśyaśudrayor āśaucam | ṣaḍ veti vaktavye ārtavīgrahaṇaṃ pañcartavo bhavantīti jñāpanārtham | tatra śūdrasya ṣaṭ, vaiśyasya pañceti draṣṭavyam | tatra vaiśyasya vṛttavataḥ pañca avṛttavataḥ ātmīyam | śūdraviṣaye ‘pi sacchūdrasya vṛttavataḥ ṣaṭ itarasyārdhamāsam, asacchūdrasya vṛttābhāvāt māsam eveti draṣṭavyam || 14.24 ||
pūrvayoś ca || 14.25 ||
pūrvayor brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor ārtvīr vā pūrvoktaṃ vā | vikalpānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ || 14.25 ||
tryahaṃ vā || 14.26 ||
pūrvayor iti vartate | tatra brāhmaṇasyāvṛttavato daśarātram, vṛttavataḥ pañca, āhitāgnes tryaham, evaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi kalpyam iti || 14.26 ||
ācāryatatputrastrīyājyaśiṣyeṣu caivam || 14.27 ||
ācārye tatputre tatpatnyāṃ ca pretāyāṃ yājye śiṣye ca mṛte | caśabdād ācāryācārye | evaṃśabdena tryaha evaiko ‘tidiśyate, “ācārye ca prete trirātram” [VaDh 13.39] ityādivasiṣṭhasmṛtidarśanāt
|| 14.27 ||
pūrvaṃ samānajātāv uktam | asamānajātau tu |
avaraś ced varṇaḥ pūrvaṃ varṇam upaspṛśet pūrvo vāvaraṃ tatra śavoktam āśaucam || 14.28 ||
avaro jaghanyaḥ kṣatriyādi | varṇagrahaṇaṃ pratilomasaṃsarge nāśaucamātram | kiṃ tarhi? prāyaścittam api bhavatīti | yady apy anulomā api na varṇaśabdavācyāḥ, tathāpi “pratilomās tu dharmahīnāḥ” [GDh 4.25] iti pratilomānām eva dharmahīnatvād anulomānāṃ mātṛvat kalpyam iti | pūrvaṃ varṇaṃ brāhmaṇādiṃ tam upaspṛśet vahet, upaśabdaprayogāt | pūrvo vā avaraṃ kṣatriyādim | tatra parasparavahane yac chāva uktam āśaucaṃ tad eva bhavati | tathā kṣatriyasya brāhmaṇasparśane daśarātram | tathā brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasparśane pañcadaśāha iti | evaṃ sarvatra | āśaucādhikāre punar āśaucagrahaṇaṃ tadvad āśaucārtham, na punaḥ svādhyāyādyabhāvamātram iti | śāvāśaucam iti vaktavye uktagrahaṇaṃ bhojanādivyavahāre ‘pi tadāśaucapraveśārtham | evaṃ ca “anirdaśāhe pāraśave” [VaDh 4.32] ityādi yat smṛtyantare prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate tadā śaucottarakālaṃ draṣṭavyam | atroktam āśaucam iti vaktavye śavagrahaṇaṃ śavanimittasya sarvasyānupraveśārtham | tataḥ dṛṣṭārtham aṅgīkṛtya parasparavahane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
avaraś ced varaṃ varṇaṃ avaraṃ vā varyo yadi |
vahec chavavad āśaucaṃ dṛṣṭārthe dviguṇaṃ bhavet || iti |
tathādṛṣṭārthe ‘pi |
anāthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ pretaṃ ye vahanti dvijātayaḥ |
pade pade yajñaphalam ānupūrvyāl labhanti te ||
ityādi | śavoktam āśaucam iti vaktavye tatragrahaṇaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣoktam āśaucaṃ mā bhūd iti | evaṃ ca yathā brāhmaṇasya daśāho nityaḥ, evaṃ kṣatriyādīnām api pañcadaśāhādayo veditavyāḥ | vahane yad uktam āśaucaṃ tad eva saṃskartary api veditavyam | kutaḥ? auśanase “saṃskartā caiva coḍhā ca trirātreṇa viśudhyati” iti samānopadeśāt || 14.28 ||
samānajātāv asamānajātāv api śavavahananimittāśaucam uktam | buddhipūrvaṃ tatsparśanamātre idānīm āha |
patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭyupasparśane sacelodakopasparśanāc chudhyate || 14.29 ||
patito brahmahādiḥ | caṇḍālaḥ uktalakṣaṇaḥ | sūtikā prasūtā asamāptāśaucakālā | udakyā rajasvalā | śavagrahaṇena tannimittaṃ sarvam ucyate | yac chavaṃ ye ca tadāśaucabhājaḥ ye ca kaṭakaraṇaṃ kurvanti ye ca vahanti ye ac saṃskurvantītyādi | upasparśanaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavopasparśane spṛṣṭyupasparśane tatspṛṣṭyupasparśaṇa iti | spṛṣṭī patitādīnāṃ sākṣāt spraṣṭā, tatspṛṣṭī patitādispṛṣṭispraṣṭā | upaśabdo buddhipūrvārthaḥ | śavasya buddhipūrvasparśanam alaṃkārādyarthaṃ kevalecchāyā vā | samānajātīyaśavam asamānajātīyaśavaṃ vā viśeṣābhāvāt | abuddhipūrve tu mānavoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
divākīrtyam udakyāṃ ca patitaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati ||
iti dvayor āśaucam āmnātam, gautamas tu trayāṇām apīti | sacelaḥ savastraḥ udakopasparśanāt | evaṃ ca snānād iti vaktavyam iti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ, pūrvaṃ daṇḍavad āplutya punar yathāvat snāyād ity evamarthaṃ “udakopasparśanāt” ity uktam | sacelam udakam upaspṛśed iti vaktavye, udakopasparśanād ity upādānaṃ naimittikasnāne ‘pi tarpaṇānupraveśārtham | tathā ca bhārgavīyam |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ snānam ucyate |
tarpaṇaṃ trividhasyāpi prajāpatir uvāca ha || iti || 14.29 ||
śunaś ca || 14.30 ||
upasparśane sacela udakopasparśanāt “śudhyet” iti vartate | spṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭinivṛttyarthaṃ pṛthag grahaṇam | śvagrahaṇaṃ sṛgālādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | cakārād asthi ca, tad api sasneham | yathāha manuḥ |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gāṃ spṛṣṭvā vīkṣya vā ravim || iti | [MDh 5.87]
etat tu abuddhipūrvasparśane | buddhipūrve tu vasiṣṭha āha: “mānuṣāsthi snigdhaṃ spṛṣṭvā trirātram āśaucam asnigdhe tv ahorātram” [VaDh 23.24] iti | cakārād raktādi ca |
kecic chavānugamanam āhuḥ | tatra sacelasnānaṃ ghṛtaprāśanam api draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacelaḥ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.103]
sarveṣāṃ svajātiviṣaya evedam | kṣatriyavaiśyānugamane tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā cāha: “asnigdhe tv ahorātraṃ śavānugamane caivam” [VaDh 23.25] iti | tatra śavānugamane trirātraikarātayor atideśaḥ | tataś ca kṣatriyānugamane ekarātram, vaiśyānugamane pakṣiṇīṃ śūdrānugamane trirātraṃ prāyaścittaṃ ca draṣṭavyam | tathāha aṅgirāḥ |
pretībhūtaṃ tu yaḥ śūdraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
nīyamānam anugacchet trirātram aśucir bhavet ||
trirātre tu tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtam prāśya viśudhyati || iti |
kṣatriyasya vaiśyānugamane vaiśyasya śūdrānugamane ca brāhmaṇavat kalpyam | kṣatriyasya śūdrāgamane ekarātrasahitaṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ draṣṭavyam || 14.30 ||
yad upahanyād ity eke || 14.31 ||
eke tu yad evāṅgam upaspṛśati śvā tasyaiva prakṣālaṇam icchanti | so ‘sya vikalpaḥ – uparikāyasparśane snānam, adhaḥkāyasparśane tu prakṣālanam iti draṣṭavyam | tathā pakṣisparśe ‘pi | tathā jātūkarṇiḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā yad aṅgaṃ spṛśate khagaḥ |
snānaṃ tatra vidhātavyaṃ śeṣe prakṣālya śudhyati || iti |
evaṃ ca śvaviṣaye ‘pi karavarjaṃ draṣṭavyam || 14.31 ||
udakadānaṃ sapiṇḍaiḥ kṛtajaṭasya || 14.32 ||
adhikārāt pretasya kṛtajaṭasya kṛtacaulasya sapiṇḍair udakaṃ deyam | na sarvair aśucibhiḥ | udakakriyācodanenāśaucam api draṣṭavyam, “udakakriyāśaucaṃ ca” [VaDh 4.9] iti vasiṣṭhena sahanirdeśāt | yasyodakakriyā tasyāśaucam api bhavatīti tatra sahanirdeśasya prayojanam | tadvat smṛtyantarāt trirātraṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
nivṛttacailakānāṃ tu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate | iti | [MDh 5.66]
kṣatriyādīnām apy etat tulyam eva, viśeṣābhāvāt | evaṃ ca vayo’vasthāpekṣayā ye tryahādayaḥ āśaucavikalpāḥ śrūyante te ‘pi sarveṣāṃ tulyarūpeṇa draṣṭavyāḥ, viśeṣābhāvād eva | evaṃ ca ye vṛttādyapekṣayā pañcadaśāhādaya parikalpitāḥ te kṛtopanayane pramīte veditavyāḥ | tathā deśāntarāśaucaṃ ca | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
tulyaṃ vayasi sarveṣām atikrānte tathaiva ca |
upanīte tu viṣamaṃ tasminn evātikālajam || iti |
uktād asamāptāśaucakālāt prāg yat śrūyate “yac cheṣaṃ daśarātrasya” [MDh 5.75] ityādi, tad upanītāt prāg api draṣṭavyam | kṛtajaṭasya saṃkāro ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ smṛtyantarāt | tathāha lokākṣiḥ |
tūṣṇīm evodakaṃ dadyāt tūṣṇīm evāgnim eva ca |
sarveṣāṃ kṛtacūḍānām anyatrāpīcchayā dvayam || iti |
evaṃ ca kṛtajaṭasya niyamato ‘gnisaṃskāraḥ udakadānaṃ ca, akṛtacaulasyāniyama iti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryur nāmni vāpi kṛt sati || [MDh 5.70]
iti || 14.32 ||
tat strīṇāṃ ca || 14.33 ||
tad udakadānam agnisaṃskārasahitam, tadgrahaṇāt | strīṇāṃ ca kartavyam | kṛtajaṭānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | āśaucalāghavārthaṃ pṛthag grahaṇam | tat smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | tatra,
aprauḍhāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
iti prāk caulakaraṇāt sadyaḥśaucam | prauḍhāśabdena cūḍākaraṇam ucyate |
ahas tv adattakanyāsu bāleṣu ca viśodhanam | [YDh 3.24]
iti cūḍākaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ pradānakālāt prāk ekāhaḥ |
strīṇām asaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahāc chuddhyanti bāndhavāḥ | [MDh 5.72ab]
ity anena pradānakālād ūrdhvam aprattāyāḥ trirātram iti |
yathoktenaiva kalpena śudhyanti tu sanābhayaḥ | [MDh 5.72cd]
ity atra daṇḍāpūpikayā strīviṣaye pitror daśāha eva draṣṭavyaḥ | sapiṇḍaviṣaye ‘pi tripūruṣaṃ draṣṭavyam, “aprattānāṃ tu strīṇāṃ tripūruṣam” [VaDh 4.18] iti vasiṣṭhavacanāt || 14.33 ||
eke prattānām || 14.34 ||
udakavāgbhyāṃ prattānām api varapakṣair eva kartavyam ity eke manyante | gautamas tu,
adbhir vācā ca dattāyāḥ mriyetādau varo yadi |
anyasmai vidhivad deyā yathā kanyā tathaiva sā ||
iti kanyāvad ityatideśāt pitrādibhir eva deyam iti | tatra pitṛpakṣābhāve varapakṣaiḥ kartavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 14.34 ||
idānīm āśauce kathaṃ varteyur ity ata āha |
adhaḥśayyāsanino brahmacāriṇaḥ sarve || 14.35 ||
adhaḥ bhūmāv eva śayīran, āsīraṃś ca, maithunaṃ ca varjayeyuḥ, yāvad āśaucam | sarvagrahaṇaṃ samānodakānām apy evam iti jñāpanārtham || 14.35 ||
na mārjayīran || 14.36 ||
ujjvalaveṣadhāriṇaś ca na syur ity arthaḥ | gātramalāpakarṣaṇaṃ vā varjayeyuḥ || 14.36 ||
māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayeyur ā pradānāt || 14.37 ||
ā śrāddhkaraṇād āmiṣaṃ prāṇātyayādāv api na bhakṣayeyuḥ || 14.37 ||
prathamatṛtīyapañcamasaptamanavameṣūdakakriyā
|| 14.38 ||
prathamādiṣv ahaḥsu sapiṇḍaiḥ kartavyam, putrais tu pratidinam iti draṣṭavyam | kriyāgrahaṇaṃ gṛhyoktodakadānavidhyupasaṃgrahaṇārtham | “putrāḥ pretāya sakṛd udakaṃ prohanti” ityādi
|| 14.38 ||
vāsasāṃ ca tyāgaḥ || 14.39 ||
udakadānakāladhāritānāṃ ca vastrāṇāṃ tyāgaḥ | anyāni dhāraṇīyānīty arthaḥ | caśabdāt keśādīni ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
dvitīye ‘hani kartavyaṃ kṣurakarma prayatnataḥ |
tṛtīye pañcame vāpi saptame vāpradānataḥ || 14.39 ||
ante tv antyānām || 14.40 ||
ante navame antyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ vāsasāṃ tyāga ity anuvartate | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | udakadānaṃ prathamādiṣu kartavyam | vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ anta eveti antyānām iti bahuvacanam anulomānāṃ savyavahāryāṇām apy evem eveti || 14.40 ||
dantajanmādi mātāpitṛbhyām || 14.41 ||
dantajanmaprabhṛti niyamato mātāpitṛbhyām udakadānaṃ kartavyam | evaṃ ca tayor api prāg aniyama iti draṣṭavyam || 14.41 ||
bāladeśāntaritapravrajitāsapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam
|| 14.42 ||
bālaḥ nāmakaraṇāt prāk | tataś ca nāmakaranād ūrdhvaṃ prāg dantajananāt smṛtyantaroktam ekāha draṣṭavyam |
nṝṇām akṛtacaulānām aśuddhir naiśikī smṛtā | iti | [MDh 5.67]
dantajananād ūrdhvaṃ prāk cūḍākaraṇāt tryaham |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapeyuḥ tryaham eva tu | iti | [MDh 5.69]
deśāntare yo mṛtaḥ tasminn eva kāle na śrūyate sa deśāntaritaḥ | pravrajitaḥ naiṣṭhiko vānaprasthaḥ parivrājakaś ca | tasmin mṛte asapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam iti | asapiṇḍaḥ samānodakaḥ, tasya cokto viṣāyaḥ || 14.42 ||
rājñāṃ ca kāryanirodhāt || 14.43 ||
sadyaḥśaucam iti vartate | rājñām iti bahuvacanaṃ rājasadṛśānām amātyānām api grahaṇārtham | cakārād yasya cecchati | tathā ca manuḥ: “yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ” [MDh 5.95] iti | kāryām rakṣaṇādi tasya virodhaḥ asaṃbhavaḥ || 14.43 ||
brāhmaṇasya ca svādhyāyānivṛttyarthaṃ svādhyāyānivṛttyartham || 14.44 ||
hetuvacanaṃ vṛttasvādhyāyāpekṣayā āśaucaṃ bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | cakārāt kṣatriyāder api svādhyāyanimittapraveśārthaṃ pratipāditāḥ viṣayāḥ | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā
|| 14.44 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye caturdaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha śrāddham amāvāśyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt || 15.1 ||
śrāddhaṃ pañcavidham, yathāha vyāghraḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tathāparam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tatrāhar ahar yat kriyate tan nityam, yathāha manuḥ |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan || iti | [MDh 3.82]
tathā tatraivoktam |
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitrarthaṃ pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati divjam || iti | [MDh 3.83]
tathā ca smṛtyantaram api |
brahmacaryam anadhyāyaṃ muktvā śrāddhaṃ tu naityakam | iti |
yad uktaṃ naimittikam ekoddiṣṭaṃ ekam evoddiśya kriyamāṇatvād ekoddiṣṭam, yathāha kātyāyanaḥ: “athaikoddiṣṭam eko dvijaḥ ekaṃ pātram ekaṃ pavitram eko ‘py arghyaḥ ekaḥ piṇḍo nāvāhanaṃ nāgnaukaraṇam” iti, tad āśaucānantaram ārabhya sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt prāk kartavyam, yathāha laukākṣiḥ:
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivam ādyam ekādaśe ‘hani || iti |
tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
ekādaśe ‘hni kartavyaṃ tripakṣe ca tathaiva ca |
ṣaṇmāse ca tathā kuryād ekoddiṣṭaṃ prayatnataḥ || iti |
tataḥ saṃvatsare paripūrṇe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ |
tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe tripakṣe vā tathaiva ca |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād arvāg vṛddhikaraṃ bhavet || iti |
tripakṣa iti āhitāgniviṣayam | kutaḥ? bṛhaspativacanāt | tathāha |
anantaram amāvāsyāṃ daśarātrasya ced bhavet |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tatra kuryād evāgnimān sutaḥ ||
tadalābhe tripakṣe tu sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
“tripakṣe sapiṇḍīkaraṇam” iti jābālau śrūyate śrutir iti | sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ saṃkrāntyādau piṇḍatrayanirvāpeṇa śrāddhaṃ kartavyam | pratisaṃvatsare vipanne ahany ekoddiṣṭaṃ kartavyam[63] | yathāha uśanā |
saṃkrāntyām uparāge ca parvāyanamahālaye |
trīn piṇḍān nirvapet tatra vipanne ‘hny ekam eva tu || iti |
pratisaṃvatsaram āgrahāyaṇyā ūrdhvam aṣṭakā kartavyā | tathāha gṛhyakāraḥ: “ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyās trayoparakṣās teṣām ekaikasminn ekaikāṣṭakā bhavati śākāṣṭakā māṃsāṣṭakāpūpāṣṭakā” ityādi | tatra kāmyaṃ kāmanayā yat kriyate, yathā “ekādaśyāṃ putrakāmaḥ” ityādi | vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ nāndīmukham | pārvaṇam nāma māsiśrāddham | atha vā piṇḍatrayanirvāpeṇa yad yat kriyate aṣṭakādi śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ tannaimittikam iti |
asmin pañcavidhe sthite – “atha śrāddham amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt” | athānantaraṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt | kutaḥ? “amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ dadyād” ity ārambhāt, idaṃ māsiśrāddham iti jñātaṃ śakyatvāt, smṛtyantare sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāk māsiśrāddhapratiṣedhāt | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāk pārvaṇaṃ yaḥ karoti tu |
krimir bhūtvā śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati || iti |
evaṃ tarhi sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāg yāni śrāddhāni tāny eva vaktavyānīti cet, keṣāṃcit tadabhāvasūcanārtham anantaraṃ pārvaṇam eva kartavyam iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
yatīnāṃ tu mṛtānāṃ ca kriyā kācin na dṛśyate |
ahany ekādaśe prāpte pārvaṇaṃ tu vidhīyate ||
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ muktānāṃ ca tathaiva ca |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ noktaṃ ekoddiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
śrāddham iti karmanāmadheyam, atiśayavacchraddhāsādhyatvāt | śraddadhāna eva hi pratipadyate, yathā “putrair dattenānnena yatratatrasthāḥ pitaras tadupabhogena sukham āsate” ity arthavādadarśanāt | amāvāsyāyām, sūryācandramasoḥ parasmin saṃnikarṣe pitṛbhyaḥ pitṝn uddiśya, bahuvacanāt pitāmahaprapitāmahayor api deyam || 15.1 ||
pañcamīprabhṛti vāparakṣasya yathāśrāddham || 15.2 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣasya vā pañcamyā ārabhya dadyāt | yathāśrāddhaṃ yathāśraddhāsti tathā kartavyam ity arthaḥ | aṣṭakāśrāddhaṃ pañcamīprabhṛti dadyād ity abhiprāyaḥ | aparapakṣagrahaṇam amāvāsyādhikārāt pūrvapakṣe mā bhūd iti || 15.2 ||
sarvasmin vā || 15.3 ||
sarvasmin vāparapakṣe pratipatprabhṛti vā kuryād ity arthaḥ | prabhūtadravyasyāyaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ
|| 15.3 ||
dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhāne vā || 15.4 ||
dravyasya rajatādeḥ | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
rajataṃ kālaśākaṃ ca khaḍgamatsyāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpyante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ || iti |
deśasya puṣkarādeḥ | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
puṣkareṣv akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurukṣetre tathaiva ca |
nadyāṃ madhodadhau caiva hrade goṣṭhe girau tathā || iti |
brāhmaṇānāṃ yatīnāṃ paṅktipāvanānām | yathā ca vyāsah |
svadhā ca rājataṃ pātraṃ dauhitraḥ kutapas tilāḥ |
vastraṃ ca pāvanīyāni tridaṇḍī yatir eva ca || iti |
saṃnidhāne upapattau | pūrvapakṣe vā kuryād ity abhiprāyaḥ || 15.4 ||
kālaniyamaḥ śaktitaḥ || 15.5 ||
yadā asya dravyāder asaṃbhavo ‘tyantaṃ tadā ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyā ity uktaḥ kālaniyamo nāsti | yasmin māse atyantasaṃbhavas tasminn eva kartavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | dravyādyasaṃbhave yathā pakṣaviśeṣo nāsti tathā māsaviśeṣo ‘pi nāstīty abhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ cānenaiva nyāyena tithiniyamasyāpy abhāvaḥ pratipāditaḥ || 15.5 ||
sarvadā ca śrāddhe |
prakarṣed guṇasaṃskāravidhīn annasya || 15.6 ||
prakarṣed atiśayena kuryāt, annasya bhakṣyabhojyādeḥ, guṇān jātiviśeṣān śākāpūpādīn saṃskārān susiddhatvasurabhitvādīn vidhīn darbhatilāpasavyatvādīn || 15.6 ||
navāvarān bhojayet || 15.7 ||
sati sāmarthye evam || 15.7 ||
ayujo vā yathotsāhaṃ vā || 15.8 ||
ayugmān yathotsāhaṃ yathāsāmarthyam, arvāg navabhyaḥ, pūrvatrāvaragrahaṇāt, ūrdhvaṃ navabhyaḥ prāptyabhāvāt |
nanu ca ayujo vety eva vaktavyam | tataś cordhvaṃ navabhyo ‘prāpitatvāt ūnaprāpaṇārtha evārambhaḥ syāt | tataś cāsāmarthyakṛta eva vikalpo bhaviṣyatīti yathotsāham ity atiriktam |
ucyate – utsāhe ‘pi sati satkriyādyavirodhena bhojayitavyam iti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
dvau daive pitṛkārye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ‘pi na prasajyeta vistare ||
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmāt taṃ parivarjayet || iti | [VaDh 11.27–28]
devāḥ viśvedevāḥ tadaṅgabhūtāḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
dakṣasya duhitā sādhvī viśvā nāma prakīrtitā |
tasyāḥ putrā mahātmāno viśve devā mahābalāḥ ||
kratur dakṣo vasuḥ satyaḥ kālaḥ kāmyaḥ śucī ruciḥ |
purūravādravau ceti vijñeyās te daśāmarāḥ ||
iṣṭiśrāddhe kratudakṣāv aṣṭakyāṃ kālakāmyakau |
nāndīmukhe vasusatyau kāmye cāpi śucī ruciḥ ||
purūravādravau caiva pārvaṇe tu prakīrtitau ||
āhvayen nāmatas teṣāṃ tatra tatra niyogataḥ || iti |
tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
devādyantaṃ bhavec chrāddhaṃ pitrādyantaṃ na tad bhavet |
pitrādyantaṃ yadi bhavet tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate || iti |
tathā bhārgavīyam api |
eka eva yadā vipro dvitīyo nopapadyate |
pitṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇe yojyaṃ daivaṃ haste niyojayet || iti |
tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvau daive ‘tharvaṇau viprau prāṅmukhau viniveśayet |
trīṃs tathodaṅmukhān pitrye ṛgyajuḥsāmagān dvijān ||
iti || 15.8 ||
kīdṛśān brāhmaṇān bhojayed ity āha |
śrotriyān vāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannān || 15.9 ||
śrotriyān chando’dhyāyinaḥ, vāksaṃpannān saṃskṛtabhāṣiṇaḥ, rūpasaṃpannān manojñavapuṣaḥ, vayaḥsaṃpannān pariṇatavayasaḥ, śīlasaṃpannān praśāntamanasaḥ, evaṃguṇān brāhmaṇān bhojayet || 15.9 ||
vayaḥsaṃpannatvasyāpavādam āha |
yuvabhyo dānaṃ prathamam || 15.10 ||
yat prathamam ekoddiṣṭaṃ tat taruṇebhyo deyam || 15.10 ||
eke pitṛvat || 15.11 ||
yadvayasaḥ pitrādayaḥ pramītāḥ tadvayobhya eva dadyād ity eke manyante | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe yad ekoddiṣṭaṃ tadviṣayam idaṃ veditavyam | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe ekoddiṣṭam uktam eva smṛtyantare | yathāha yamaḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇe prāpte kuryāc chrāddadvayaṃ tathā |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇe viprān bhojayet pārvaṇe yathā ||
pṛthak ca kalpayed annam ekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ || iti |
atha vā pitṛvad iti pitaram uddiśya taruṇaṃ pitāmaham uddiśya vṛddhaṃ prapitāmaham uddiśya vṛddhataraṃ bhojayed iti || 15.11 ||
na ca tena mitrakarma kuryāt || 15.12 ||
tena śrāddhena na mitrakāryaṃ kuryād anyonyam | cakārāt amitrasaṃvaraṇam api || 15.12 ||
putrābhāve sapiṇḍā mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ śiṣyāś ca dadyuḥ
|| 15.13 ||
ayaṃ tāvat prathamaḥ kalpaḥ, yat putrāḥ śrāddhaṃ kurvantīti | tadabhāve tu sapiṇḍā uktāḥ | tadabhāve mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ mātulādayaḥ | tadabhāve śiṣyāḥ saṃbandhiśabdatvāt ācāryāya, caśabdāḍ yājyaś ca ṛtvija iti | ekoddiṣṭaviṣayaṃ caitat, mātṛsapiṇḍaśiṣyayājyānāṃ dātṛtvenopadeśāt | na hy ekasya mātṛsapiṇḍaḥ tatpitur api mātṛsapiṇḍo bhavati | evaṃ śiṣyayājyāv apīti | evaṃ ca sapiṇḍīkaraṇādāv anyaḥ kramo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha uśanā: “putrābhāve sapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāḥ sagotrāḥ samānārṣeyāś ca” ityādi | ekenaivaikoddiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ nāśaucavat sarvair iti visamāsaḥ
|| 15.13 ||
tadabhāve ṛtvigācāryau || 15.14 ||
ṛtvig yājyāya ācāryaḥ śiṣyāya tadabhāve pūrvasyābhāve | pṛthakkaraṇe śrāddhaṃ kurvatā ṛtvigācāryāṇāṃ prāyaścittābhāvajñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca pūrveṣām avaśyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam iti draṣṭavyam | ṛtvigācāryāṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvatām abhyudayaś ca bhavati codanāsāmarthyāt | tadabhāvagrahaṇaṃ pūrvatra "mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ śiṣyāś ca" [GDh 15.13] ity atrāpy adhikārajñāpanārtham | yogyānām eva śrāddhaṃ draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
gobrāhmaṇahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca |
ātmanas tyāgināṃ caiva patitānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
sarpair hatānāṃ viprāṇāṃ carantīnāṃ ca kāmataḥ |
pāṣaṇḍam āśritānāṃ ca pretakarma na vidyate ||
snehāt kāryabhayādibhyo yadi kuryān narādhamaḥ |
caṇḍālena hatasyāpi tasya vakṣyāmi niṣkṛtim ||
eṣām anyatamaṃ pretaṃ yo vaheta daheta vā |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ matyā cet taptakṛcchrakam ||
tacchavaṃ kevalaṃ spṛṣṭvā pātayitvāśru vā tathā |
ekarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt trirātraṃ buddhipūrvake ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā caret kṛcchraṃ matyā sāntapanaṃ caret |
tathā śrāddhabhujāṃ caiva dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām ||
iti || 15.14 ||
na bhojayet stenaklībapatitanāstikatadvṛtti-vīrahāgredidhiṣūdidhiṣūpatistrīgrāma-yājakājapālotsṛṣṭāgnimadyapakucarakūṭasākṣi-prātihārikān || 15.15 ||
śrotriyādiguṇayogenaiṣāṃ prāptau satyāṃ pratiṣidhyate | steno hiraṇyastenādayaḥ, tasya patitatvād eva pratiṣedhāt | klībaḥ bhagnotsāhaḥ | tathā ca bhagavān vāsudevaḥ: “mā klaibhyaṃ gaccha kaunteya” [BhG 2.3] ityādi, na tṛtīyaprakṛtiḥ, tasya śrotriyatvābhāvena prāptyabhāvāt | patito brahmahādiḥ | nāstikaḥ paralokābhāvādyavasthitaprajñaḥ, so ‘pi manoduṣṭaḥ, kriyāduṣṭasya tu ninditakarmābhyāsāt patitatvopadeśāt | tadvṛttiḥ tenaiva ca nāstikyena ya upajīvati lokāyataśāstradvāreṇa | vīrahā puruṣamātraghātakaḥ, “avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ” [MDh 4.213] ityādau puruṣe vīraśabdaprayogāt | vṛthā somābhiṣavakṛd vā “vīrahā vā eṣa devānāṃ yaḥ somam abhiṣuṇoti” iti śrutidarśanāt | agredidhiṣūdidhiṣūpatiḥ, patiśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | jyeṣṭhāyāḥ pūrvaṃ prattā agredidhiṣūḥ, punaḥ sā jyeṣṭhā didhiṣūḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyā yā tūhyate ‘nujā |
sā cāgredidhiṣūr jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣūḥ smṛtā || iti |
tayor bhartā | atha vā patiśabdayuktaḥ sa eva, yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
bhrātur mṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo ‘nurajyeta kāmataḥ |
dharmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ ||
sa caiva jīvato bhrātuḥ sa cāgredidhiṣūḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
vyāghoktau vā |
parapūrvāpatiṃ dhīrā vadanti didhiṣūpatim |
iṣṭo ‘gredidhiṣūr vipraḥ saiva yasya kuṭumbinī ||
strīgrāmayājakaḥ, yājakaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | strīṇāṃ kāmadevādi prati yo yājayati sa strīyājakaḥ | grāmayājako bahuyājakaḥ | ajapālaḥ ajārakṣaṇopajīvakaḥ | utsṛṣṭāgniḥ śāstreṇa vinā śrautasya smārtasya vā agneḥ tyāgaṃ ya karoti sa utsṛṣṭāgniḥ | madyapaḥ nālikerāder madyasya rakaṣaṇadvāreṇopajīvakaḥ, surāvyatiriktamadyabhoktā vā | kucaraḥ kutsitācāraḥ kutsitācāratvam api putrācāryatvadevatādyarcanayācanaśīlatvādi | kūṭasākṣī prasiddhaḥ | prātihārikaḥ indrajālopajīvakaḥ | etān na bhojayet | patitānāṃ sparśapratiṣedhād eva bhojanapratiṣedhe siddhe kṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ bhojanapratiṣedhārtha ārambhaḥ || 15.15 ||
upapatir yasya ca saḥ || 15.16 ||
upapatiḥ jāraḥ | yasya ca sa iti vyabhicāriṇī yasya bhāryety arthaḥ | etau na bhojayet || 15.16 ||
kuṇḍāśisomavikrayyagāradāhigaradāvakīrṇigaṇa-
preṣyāgamyāgāmihiṃsraparivittiparivettṛparyāhita-
paryādhātṛtyaktātmadurvālakunakhiśyāvadacchivitri-
paunarbhavakitavājaparājapreṣyaprātirūpikaśūdrāpati-
nirākṛtikilāsikusīdivaṇikśilpopajīvijyāvāditratālanṛtta-
gītaśīlān || 15.17 ||
kuṇḍagrahaṇaṃ golakasyāpy upalakṣaṇam, tasyānnaṃ yo ‘śnāti sa kuṇḍāśī | kuṇḍāśipratiṣedhāc cārthataḥ kuṇḍagolakayor api pratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ | tayoḥ svarūpaḥ smṛtyantare jñātavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍas tu mṛte bhartari golakaḥ || iti | [MDh 3.174]
somavirakyī somalatāvikretā tatsādhyakarmavikretā vā | agāradāhī veśmoddīpakaḥ | viṣadravyasya dātā garadaḥ | avakīrṇī buddhipūrvaṃ khaṇḍitabrahmacaryo brahmacārī | yathāha manuḥ |
kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhur dharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ || [MDh 11.120]
gaṇapreṣyaḥ gaṇānāṃ preṣaṇakṛt | agamyāgāmī kanyādūṣakaḥ | samānapravarastrīgāmīty eke | “sakhisayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsnuṣāyāṃ gavi ca talpasamaḥ” [GDh 23.12] iti sāmyopadeśāt patitagrahaṇena gṛhyata iti | hiṃsraḥ prāṇināṃ vadharuciḥ | parivittiḥ, jyeṣṭhe akṛtavivāhe kanīyān yo vivāhaḥ karoti sa parivettā, tataḥ sa jyeṣṭhaḥ parivittiḥ | paryāhitaḥ, yasminn akṛtādhāne kanīyān ādhānaṃ karoti sa paryāhitaḥ, kaniṣṭhaḥ paryādhātā |
nanu ca parivittiparivettṛśabdau krameṇa paryāhitaparyādhātror api vartete, smṛtyantare tathā dṛṣṭatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo ‘graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ || iti | [MDh 3.171]
tataś ca paryāhitaparyādhātroḥ pṛthagārambho ‘narthaka iti |
