tatrocyate – yaś ca yājanaṃ karoti yaś ca kanyāṃ dadāti tayor api grahaṇārtham ubhayor upādānam | yathāha manuḥ |[64]
parivittiḥ parivettā yayā ca parividyate |
sarve te narakaṃ yānti dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ || iti | [MDh 3.172]
asyāpavādo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha uśanā |
pitṛvyaputrāḥ sāpatnyāḥ paradārasutās tathā |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge na doṣaḥ parivedane || iti |
tathāha vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klība eva ca |
rājayakṣmāmayāvī ca na nyāyyāḥ syuḥ pratīkṣitum || iti |
śātātapo ‘pi |
kḷibe deśavinaṣṭe ca patite pravrajite tathā |
yogaśāstraniyukte ca na doṣaḥ parivedane || iti |
tathā ca kaṇvaḥ |
vyasanāsaktacitto yo nāstiko vā bhaved yadi |
kanīyaso vṛttavato na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
ityādi |
tyaktātmā sāhasikaḥ | śastrapāṇir ity eke | durvālaḥ śipiviṣṭaḥ | hīnaprajanana ity eke | kunakhī akāraṇena vinaṣṭanakhaḥ | śyāvadan kṛṣṇadaśanaḥ rogeṇa vinā | paunarbhavaḥ svairiṇīputraḥ | kitavo dyūtakṛt | mṛnmayahastyaśvādivyavahārasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ajapa upanītamātro ‘śrotriyaḥ | rājapreṣyo rājñaḥ preṣaṇakṛt dūtādiḥ | prātirūpikaḥ kūṭatulāmānādivyāvahārikaḥ yena kenacit prakāreṇa vañcako vā | śūdrāpatiḥ saiva bhāryā yasya | nirākṛtiḥ na kvacid āśrame vyavasthitaḥ | kilāsī kuṣṭhī bhūmihartā vā | kusīdī vārdhuṣikaḥ pratiṣiddena mārgeṇa | vaṇik sati saṃbhave vaiśyavṛttyupajīvī | śilpopajīvī takṣacitrakarmādyupajīvī teṣāṃ sakāśe tadvidyāśikṣakaś ca, upaśabdaprayogāt | jyāśīlo dhanurvedopajīvī | vāditraśīlo vaṃśavīṇādyupajīvī | tālaśīlo bheryāditāḍanopajīvī | nṛttaśīlo nṛttopajīvī | gītaśīlo gītopajīvī | etān na bhojayet | etasmād eva pratiṣedhāt eteṣām akāryakāritvam api siddham | ataḥ steyādi na kartavyam || 15.17 ||
pitrākāmena vibhaktān || 15.18 ||
ye ca pitrā anicchatā vibhaktāḥ | caśabdān mātrā ca | tāṃś ca na bhojayet || 15.18 ||
śiṣyāṃś caike sagotrāṃś ca || 15.19 ||
caśabdād yājyāṃś ca | dvitīyāc cakārāt samānārṣeyāṃś ca | ekegrahaṇāt bhojanīyā eveti gautamaḥ | tatra yadi guṇavantaḥ tadā bhojayet | yadi guṇahīnās tadā na bhojanīyā iti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śiṣyān api guṇavato bhojayet” [VaDh 11.33] iti | tatrāpiśabdāt samānagotrāṇām api grahaṇam iti || 15.19 ||
bhojayed ūrdhvaṃ tribhyaḥ || 15.20 ||
“ayujo vā yathotsāham” [GDh 15.8] ity anenāviśeṣāt prāpte viśeṣārtham idam, asaty apy utsāhe tribhya ūrdhvam ayugmān bhojayed iti || 15.20 ||
guṇavantam || 15.21 ||
ekam api bhojayed iti, ekavacanaprayogāt | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
api vā bhojayed ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam |
śrutavṛttaśīlasaṃpannam avalakṣaṇavarjitam || iti | [VaDh 11.29]
vāśabdānupādānaṃ dvayor apy anujñānārtham | tathāha manuḥ |
ekaikam api vidvāṃsaṃ daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti nāmantrajñān bahūn api || [MDh 3.129]
iti || 15.21 ||
sadyaḥ śrāddhī śūdrātalpagaḥ tatpurīṣe māsaṃ nayati pitṝn || 15.22 ||
anena bhoktur dātuś ca brahmacaryam ucyate | tathā ca manuḥ |
nimantrito dvijaḥ pitrye niyatātmā bhavet sadā |
na ca chandāṃsy adhīyīta yasya śrāddhaṃ ca tad bhavet || iti | [MDh 3.188]
sadya iti kriyāviśeṣaṇaṃ sadya eva nayatīti | śūdrāgrahaṇaṃ kālaparimāṇārtham, itarāsv alpataraṃ kālam iti | talpagrahaṇaṃ bhāryārtham | tataś cānyasyāṃ viśiṣṭataro doṣaḥ | tasyāḥ samīpe māsaṃ pitṝn adhivāsya yāvad enas tāvatā doṣeṇa saṃbandhata ity arthaḥ || 15.22 ||
tasmāt tad ahar brahmacārī syāt || 15.23 ||
yasmād ayaṃ doṣas tasmāt tasminn ahani maithunavarjakaḥ syād ṛtāv api, hetuvacanāt || 15.23 ||
śvacaṇḍālapatitāvekṣaṇe duṣṭam || 15.24 ||
śvādibhir vīkṣitam annaṃ śrāddhaṃ vā doṣavad bhavati | annapakṣe abhojyam, śrāddhapakṣe tv akṛtaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ || 15.24 ||
tasmāt pariśrite dadyāt || 15.25 ||
yasmād evaṃ tasmāt pracchannapradeśe dadyāt | hetuvacanaṃ śrāddhād anyatrāpi pariśrita eva bhoktavyam iti || 15.25 ||
tilair vā vikiret || 15.26 ||
aparivṛtadoṣaśamanaṃ bhavati | smṛtyantarasāmārthyāt pitṝṇāṃ ca tṛptir bhavati | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
pānīyam api yad dattaṃ tilair miśraṃ dvijasya tu |
pitṛbhyaḥ kāmadhuk tat syāt pitṛgrāhyam idaṃ tataḥ || || 15.26 ||
paṅktipāvano vā śamayet || 15.27 ||
paṅktipāvano vakṣyamāṇaḥ, aparivṛtadoṣaṃ śamayet || 15.27 ||
paṅktipāvanāḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit jyeṣṭhasāmagas triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇaḥ pañcāgniḥ snātako mantrabrāhmaṇavid dharmajño brahmadeyānusantāna
iti || 15.28 ||
ete ca sati śrotriyatve adhikaguṇayogāt paṅktiṃ śodhayanti nirdoṣāṃ kurvantīti paṅktipāvanāḥ | ṣaḍaṅgavit ṣaṇṇām aṅgānām anyatamasyārthābhijñaḥ | tathā purāṇe |
caturdaśānāṃ vidyānām ekasyāḥ pārago ‘pi yaḥ |
ātmakaivalyavic caiva sarve te paṅktipāvanāḥ || iti |
jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ, jyeṣṭhasāma chandogānāṃ sāmaviśeṣaḥ, tadadhyāyī jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ | triṇāciketo nāma adhvaryūṇām ṛgviśeṣaḥ | trimadhur bahvṛcānāṃ sūktaviśeṣaḥ | trisuparṇo bahvṛcānāṃ taittirīyāṇāṃ ca | pañcāgniḥ sabhyāvasathyābhyāṃ saha | snātako vidyāvratasnātakaḥ | mantrabrāhmaṇavit vināpy aṅgavijñānena | dharmajño dharmaśāstrāthajñaḥ | brahmadeyānusantāno brāhmavivāhoḍhāsantatiḥ | itikāraḥ prakāravācaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaś caturmedhā vājasaneyī ṣaḍaṅgavic chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmiko mantrabrāhmaṇavidyaḥ svadharmān adhīte yasya ca daśapuruṣaṃ mātṛpitṛvaśaḥ śrotriyo vijñāyate vidvāṃsaḥ snātakāś caite paṅktipāvanā bhavanti” [VaDh 3.19] ity evamādīnāṃ smṛtyantaroktānām apy upalakṣaṇārtham || 15.28 ||
haviḥṣu caivam || 15.29 ||
haviḥṣu abhyudayakarmasu | bahuvacanaṃ na kevalaṃ pitryāṅgabhūte daiva evam | kiṃ tarhi? sarvābhyudayeṣv apīti | caśabdād eva siddhe evaśabdaḥ pariśritapaṅktipāvanārthaḥ || 15.29 ||
durvālādīn śrāddha evaike śrāddha evaike || 15.30 ||
durvālād ārabhya ye anukrāntās tān śrāddha eva na bhojayet, na tu daive | ekegrahaṇāt na tu gautamaḥ, saṃbhavāsaṃbhavakṛto vikalpa iti || 15.30 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye pañcadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ṣoḍaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
sāmānyo ‘yaṃ dvijātīnāṃ dharma ity ata āha |
śravaṇādi vārṣikaṃ proṣṭhapadīṃ vopākṛtyādhīyīta chandāṃsi || 16.1 ||
śravaṇena yuktā paurṇamāsī śravaṇā | taddhitavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | tadādita ārabhya varṣāsu bhavaṃ vārṣikam, etad api saṃjñārtham, tataś ca “abhito vārṣikam” [GDh 16.40] iti vyavahārasiddhiḥ | proṣṭhapadī vā paurṇamāsīm āditaḥ kṛtvā upākṛtya upākaraṇaṃ kṛtvādhīyīta | adhyayanaṃ ca apūrvādhigamo gṛhītābhyāsaś ca | chandāṃsi mantrabrāhmaṇam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – śrāvaṇyāṃ pauṣṭhapadyāṃ vopākṛtya tadādi vedānāṃ varṣākālasaṃbandhyadhyayanaṃ kurvīteti || 16.1 ||
kiyantaṃ kālam adhīyītety āha |
ardhapañcamān māsān pañca dakṣiṇāyanaṃ vā || 16.2 ||
upākaraṇottarakālaṃ vikalpaḥ kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ || 16.2 ||
adhīyānaḥ |
brahmacāry utsṛṣṭalomā na māṃsaṃ bhuñjīta || 16.3 ||
brahmacārī maithunavarjakaḥ, utsṛṣṭalomā akṛtaśmaśruḥ | lomagrahaṇena nakhādīnām api grahaṇam | na māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet devārcanaśiṣṭam api | gṛhasthasyāyaṃ niyamaḥ, brahmacāriṇaḥ prāptyabhāvāt | brahmacaryarakṣaṇam ṛtugamanam | kutaḥ? “gṛhasthasya ṛtāv eva gamanaṃ brahmacaryam upadiśanti” iti smṛteḥ | yady utsṛṣṭalomaśabdena kḷptalomatvaṃ parigṛhyate tadā “na rūḍhaśmaśrur akasmāt” [GDh 9.7] ity anenaiva siddhatvād anārambhaḥ prāpnoti | tasmād ārambhasāmarthyāt akḷptatvam eva gṛhyate || 16.3 ||
dvaimāsyo vā niyamaḥ || 16.4 ||
ayaṃ brahmacaryaniyamo māsadvayaṃ vā | śaktyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ | niyamagrahaṇam avaśyakartavyatārtham | evaṃ ca – niyamābhāve adhyayanaprasaṅganivṛttir draṣṭavyā || 16.4 ||
evam adhīyānaḥ |
nādhīyīta vāyau divā pāṃsuhare || 16.5 ||
yāvad divā pāṃsuvarṣuko vāyur vahati tāvan nādhīyīta | divā pāṃsuhara ity eva siddhe vāyugrahaṇaṃ vātyāyām api nādhīyītety evamartham | tathāha uśanā: “vātyāsau piśācikā tasmāt tasyāṃ nādhīyīta” iti | divāgrahaṇād rātrau na doṣaḥ, yadi rātrāv apīṣyate divety anārambheṇaiva sidhyatīti || 16.5 ||
karṇaśrāviṇi naktam || 16.6 ||
karṇaśrāviṇi naktaṃ rātrau tu yadā vāyuḥ karṇaśrāvī bhavati śrotraṃ śabdena pūrayati tadā nādhīyīta | naktagrahaṇād divā na doṣaḥ || 16.6 ||
vāṇabherīmṛdaṅgagartārtaśabdeṣu || 16.7 ||
vāṇo vīṇā, “vāṇaḥ śatatantur bhavati” iti mahāvrate darśanāt | bherīmṛdaṅgau prasiddhau | gartaśabdo rathaśabdaḥ, avyakto vā dhvanih | ārtaśabdaḥ kranditadhvaniḥ, ākrośādir vā | śabdaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate yāvad eteṣāṃ dhvanis tāvad anadhyāyaḥ 16.7 ||
śvasṛgālagardabhasaṃhrāde || 16.8 ||
hrādaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate, śvādīnāṃ samuditānāṃ śabde śvasaṃhrāde sṛgālasaṃhrāde gardabhasaṃhrāde ca, samity ārambhāt, “sam ity ekībhāve” saty bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt | daṇḍāpūpikayā trayāṇāṃ samuditānām api draṣṭavyam | evaṃ caikaikasya na doṣaḥ || 16.8 ||
lohitendradhanurnīhāreṣu || 16.9 ||
lohite indradhanuṣi nīhāre ca nādhīyīta lauhityam ākāśasya lohitavarṇatvaṃ tasmin dṛṣṭe nādhīyīta | tathendudhanuṣi, tat prasiddham | nīhāro himaḥ | yāvad eteṣāṃ darśanaṃ tāvan nādhīyīta || 16.9 ||
abbhradarśane cāpartau || 16.10 ||
anṛtau sodakameghadarśane, na yasya kasyacit | cakārād vidyuddarśane ca | yāvad darśanamātraṃ tāvad anadhyāyaḥ | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ: “vidyuddarśane nādhīyīta” iti || 16.10 ||
mūtrita uccāritaḥ || 16.11 ||
yāvan mūtroccārāv utpadyete anutsarge ‘py anadhyāyaḥ, kṛte tūtsarge “mānasam apy aśuciḥ” [GDh 16.47] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 16.11 ||
niśāsaṃdhyodakeṣu || 16.12 ||
niśāśabdenārdharātram ucyate, na rātrimātram, “vidyuti naktaṃ ca” [GDh 16.25] ityādiviśeṣānārambhāt | tenārdharātre saṃdhyāyāṃ codake cāvasthito nādhīyīta || 16.13 ||
varṣati ca || 16.13 ||
yāvad varṣakarma tāvan nādhīyīta | caśabdāt anṛtau varṣaty ahorātraṃ ca || 16.13 ||
eke valīkasantānam || 16.14 ||
eke acchinnadhāraṃ varṣati nādhīyītetīcchanti | adhīyīteti gautamaḥ | kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 16.14 ||
ācāryapariveṣaṇe || 16.15 ||
ācāryau surāsurayoḥ śukrabṛhaspatī, tayoḥ pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta | ācāryapariveṣaṇam upādhyāyasyānnāpaharaṇam ity eke || 16.15 ||
jyotiṣoś ca || 16.16 ||
ādityacandrasamo dvivacananirdeśāt, pariveṣaṇe nādhīyīta cakārād ācārye vyasanagate || 16.16 ||
bhīto yānasthaḥ śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaḥ || 16.17 ||
bhīto vartamānabhayaḥ, yānastha aśvādyārūḍhaḥ, śayānaḥ śayyām āsevamānaḥ, prauḍhapādaḥ pāde ekapādārūḍhaḥ, pīṭhikādyāropitacaraṇo vā | vṛkṣārūḍhasyāpi grahaṇartho visamāsaḥ
|| 16.17 ||
śmaśānagrāmāntamahāpathāśauceṣu || 16.18 ||
śmaśānaṃ śavadahanasthānam, tatsamīpe | grāmānto grāmasīmā tatsamīpe | mahāpatho rājamārgaḥ, aśaucam aśucisthānam | eteṣv avasthito nādhīyīta | āśaucaṃ jananamaraṇanimittam ity eke | tad ayuktam, pūrvam uktatvāt | sadyaḥśaucapakṣe ‘py adhyayanābhāvajñāpanārtham ity eke, sadyaḥśauce ‘pi dānapratigrahabhojanāny eva kartavyāni na tv adhyayanam | daśarātrād arvāg ity apare | mātāpitros tanmātur vety asmin pakṣe ‘pi sapiṇḍānām adhyayanābhāvajñāpanārtham iti keṣāṃcid darāsnam | āśaucavataḥ samīpe nādhyetavyam ity evamartha upadeśaḥ gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādivad iti | śmaśānagrahaṇaṃ ca bhūtapūrvagatyā pratiṣedhārtham, idānīṃtanasya “śmaśānābhyadhyayane caiva” [GDh 1.66] ity anenaivoktatvāt | tataś ca śravaṇaparaṃparayā yat sthānaṃ śmaśānam iti śrūyate tatra nādhyetavyam iti || 16.18 ||
pūtigandhāntaḥśavadivākīrtyaśūdrasaṃnidhāne || 16.19 ||
pūtigandho durgandhaḥ | antaḥśave grāme anirhṛtaprete | divākīrtyo nāpitaḥ śūdro ‘pi, na tu caṇḍālaḥ, tasya śūdrapratiṣedhād evārthataḥ siddheḥ, tasya samīpe na paṭhet | atha vā divākīrtyagrahaṇena tatkarma gṛhyate | tataḥ kṛtaśmaśrur akṛtaśauco nādhīyīteti | kecid divākīryaśabdena caṇḍālam evoktvā, antaḥśabdam adhikṛtya vyācakṣate – antaścaṇḍāle grāma iti | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “antaḥśavam antaścaṇḍālam abhinirhṛtānāṃ tu sīmny anadhyāyaḥ” [1.9.14–16] iti | śūdrasya saṃnidhāne badhirasyāpi kutaḥ? “atha hāsya vedam upaśṛṇvataḥ” ity anenaiva siddhe iha punar ārambhāt || 16.19 ||
śuktake codgāre || 16.20 ||
amle udgāre nādhīyīta | cakārād anyasmin jvarādau nādhīyīta || 16.20 ||
ṛgyajuṣāṃ ca sāmaśabdo yāvat || 16.21 ||
ṛgyajuṣāṃ cānadhyāyaḥ yāvat sāmadhvaniḥ śrūyate | śākhāntare sāmno ‘py anadhyāyārthaś cakāraḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “śākhāntare ca sāmnām anadhyāyaḥ” iti | dvivacanena vaktavye ṛgyajuṣām iti bahuvacanaṃ tadaṅgabhūtānāṃ sūtrādīnām apy upasaṃgrahārtham | dhvanimātraśravaṇe ‘pi nādhyetavyam iti yāvadgrahaṇam, anyathā ṛgyajuṣāṃ ca sāmaśabda ity avakṣyat || 16.21 ||
ākālikā nirghātabhūmikamparāhudarśanolkāḥ || 16.22 ||
yasmin kāle ete bhavanti tasmāt kālād ā dvitīyād anadhyāyakāriṇa ity ākālikāḥ | nirghātaḥ aśanipātaḥ, bhūmikalpo bhūmicalanam, rāhudarśanaṃ grahaṇam, ulkā ulkāpātaḥ |
tryahaṃ na kīrtayet brahma rājño rāhoś ca sūtake | [MDh 4.110]
ity evamādibhir adhikāhaḥpratipādakaiḥ smṛtyantaraiḥ kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 16.22 ||
stanayitnuvarṣavidyutaś ca prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu || 16.23 ||
prakāśiteṣv agniṣu agnihotrahomakāle saṃdhyāyām ity arthaḥ | ete stanayitnuprabhṛtayo bhavanta ākālikā bhavanti | sarvasamuccayārthaḥ cakāraḥ, ekaikasya dvayor vā mā bhūd iti
|| 16.23 ||
ahar ṛtau || 16.24 ||
varṣāsu saṃdhyāyām ete bhavanto nākālikāḥ | aharmātrānadhyāyakāriṇaḥ | ahaḥśabdena rātrir apy abhidhīyate “ahaś ca kṛṣṇam ahar arjunaṃ ca” ityādiśrutidarśanāt | tataś ca prātaḥsaṃdhyāyām upalabhyamāneṣu ahar anadhyāyaḥ, sāyaṃsaṃdhyāyāṃ rātrir iti draṣṭavyam
|| 16.24 ||
vidyuti naktaṃ cāpararātrāt || 16.25 ||
vidyuti saṃdhyāyām ṛtau dṛśyamānāyāṃ tribhāgād arvāg rātrir anadhyāyaḥ | cakārāt prātar upalabhyamānāyām ahaś ca sarvam anadhyāyaḥ na rātrivat tribhāgād arvāk, “vidyuti prātar ahar anadhyāyaḥ” iti jābāligṛhyadarśanāt || 16.25 ||
tribhāgādipravṛttau sarvam || 16.26 ||
yā saṃdhyāyām upalabhyate tasyā ahnaḥ pūrvabhāgāv atikramya tata ārabhya pravṛttir asti cet tataḥ sarvaṃ naktam anadhyāyaḥ || 16.26 ||
ulkā vidyutsamety ekeṣām || 16.27 ||
ulkā ca vidyuttulyā | yathā vidyuty anadhyāyaḥ tatholkāpāte ‘pi draṣṭavyam ity ekeṣāṃ mataṃ na tu gautamasya | kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 16.27 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe || 16.28 ||
stanayitnur aparāhṇe bhavan vidyutsamo bhavati | naktam āpararātrād anadhyāyaṃ karotīty arthaḥ || 16.28 ||
api pradoṣe || 16.29 ||
prathame ‘pi rātribhāge pravṛttaḥ pūrvavad āpararātrād anadhyāyaṃ karotīty arthaḥ || 16.29 ||
sarvaṃ naktam ārdharātrāt || 16.30 ||
prathamāt rātribhāgād ārabhyārdharātrāt pravṛttaḥ sarvaṃ naktam anadhyāyaṃ karoti || 16.30 ||
ahaś cit [cet] sajyotiḥ || 16.31 ||
prāg aparāhṇād yad ahaḥ stanayitnur bhavati tataḥ sajyotir anadhyāyaḥ, divasam evety arthaḥ
|| 16.31 ||
viṣayasthe ca rājñi prete || 16.32 ||
viṣayasthe svarāṣṭrasthe ity arthaḥ, rājani pramīte | ākālikānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | tataś ca ākāliko ‘nadhyāyaḥ || 16.32 ||
viproṣya cānyonyena saha || 16.33 ||
yadā śiṣyācāryau sahādhyāyinau vā parasparam aviyuktau pravaseyuḥ | tadā cākāliko ‘nadhyāyaḥ | cakārāt sametya ca || 16.33 ||
saṃkulopāhitavedasamāpticchardiśrāddhamanuṣya-yajñabhojaneṣv ahorātram || 16.34 ||
saṃkulaḥ kenacit kāraṇena grāmādyupadravaḥ | upāhito ‘gnisaṃbhramaḥ agnidāhaḥ | vedasamāptiḥ vedasthaprakaraṇasamāptiḥ, grāmo dagdha ityādivat | śākhāsamāptir vā | chardiḥ prasiddhā | śrāddham ekoddiṣṭādi, tatra bhoktuḥ | manuṣyayajñe mitramelake | bhojane anekabrāhmaṇabhojane | eteṣv ahorātram anādhyāyaḥ || 16.34 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ ca || 16.35 ||
ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | caśabdāt paurṇamāsyāṃ ca || 16.35 ||
dvyahaṃ vā || 16.36 ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca dvyahaṃ vā pūrvoktam ahorātraṃ vā anadhyāyaḥ | ubhayatra caturdaśyām iti draṣṭavyam | tatra vedasya dvyaham aṅgāder ahorātram eveti vyavasthitavikalpo draṣṭavyam | tathā cāha uśanā: “parvaṇītihāsavarjitānāṃ sarvāsāṃ vidyānām anadhyāyaḥ” iti
|| 16.36 ||
kārtikī phālguny āṣāḍhī paurṇamāsī || 16.37 ||
caśabdāt pūrvam anadhyāyaprāpaṇād iha paurṇamāsīśabdena pratipado gṛhyante | etāsv anadhyāyaḥ | evaṃ cānyāsu pratipatsu na doṣaḥ | tatra “pratipatsu na cintayet” ity auśanasavacanena kṛtārthākṛtārthāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 16.37 ||
tisro ‘ṣṭakās trirātram || 16.38 ||
tisra ūrdhvam āgrahāyaṇyā aparapakṣehvaṣṭakās trirātram anadhyāyakāriṇyaḥ | atas tāsu trirātram anadhyāyo bhavati, saptamyām aṣṭamyāṃ navamyām iti || 16.38 ||
antyām eke || 16.39 ||
antyām evāṣṭakām anadhyāyakāritvenecchanti | pūrvavad vikalpaḥ || 16.39 ||
abhito vārṣikam || 16.40 ||
ādāv ante cety arthaḥ | varṣākāle bhavaṃ vārṣikam iti tatkālasaṃbandhy adhyayanam ucyate | yat kṛtvā yat prārabhyate ādāv ante ca yat kriyate abhitaḥśabdena tad dvayaṃ gṛhyate | trirātram anadhyāya ity anuvartate | upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātram anadhyāya ity arthaḥ | tathā cāha uśanā: “upākarmaṇi cotsarge tryaham anadhyāyaḥ” iti || 16.40 ||
sarve varṣavidyutstanayitnusaṃnipāte || 16.41 ||
sarve ācāryā varṣādīnāṃ saṃnipāte trirātram anadhyāyam icchanti | gautamas tu “stanayitnuvarṣavidyutaś ca prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu” [GDh 16.23] ity ākālikam | tatra pūrvavad vikalpaḥ || 16.41 ||
prasyandini || 16.42 ||
vidyutstanayitnusaṃnipāte trirātram ity anuvartate | yasminn ahani viralaśo varṣati devas tasminn evāhani vidyudagnisaṃnipātaś ca tatrāpi trirātram icchanti || 16.42 ||
ūrdhvaṃ bhojanād utsave || 16.43 ||
utsave sarvābhyudaye bhojanād ūrdhvaṃ tad ahorātram anadhyāyaḥ | upanayanādāv utsave bhuktvā taduttarakālam anadhyāya ity arthaḥ || 16.43 ||
prādhītasya ca || 16.44 ||
prādhītasya ca adhyetuṃ prathamaṃ pravṛttasyānutsave ‘pi bhojanottarakālam anadhyāyaḥ | cakārād vedāntādhyetuś ca bhojanottarakālam anadhyāyaḥ || 16.44 ||
niśāyāṃ caturmuhūrtam || 16.45 ||
rātryāṃ prathamāṃś caturo muhūrtān nādhīyīta, smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt trayodaśyām | yathāha uśanā: “trayodaśyāṃ prathamāṃś caturo muhūrtān nādhīyīta” iti || 16.45 ||
nityam eke nagare || 16.46 ||
eke nagare grāme sadaivānadhyāyam icchanti | na tu gautamaḥ, grāmanivāsināṃ sadaivānadhyāyaprasaṅgāt | tatra śūdrādibhūyiṣṭhe anadhyāyaḥ, dvijātibahule adhyetavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 16.46 ||
mānasam apy aśuciḥ || 16.47 ||
aprayato manasāpy adhyayanaṃ na kuryāt | evaṃ cānyeṣv apy anadhyāyeṣu manasādhyayanaṃ na pratiṣidhyate | nityārthaṃ vā mānasagrahaṇam | aśuciḥ san naityakam api nādhīyīteti | evaṃ ca naityake anye ‘nadhyāyā na santīti draṣṭavyam | tathāha manuḥ |
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo homamantreṣu caiva hi |
naityake nāsty anadhyāyo brahmasatraṃ hi tat smṛtam || [MDh 2.105–06]
iti || 16.47 ||
śrāddhinām ākālikam || 16.48 ||
dātṝṇām, bhoktṝṇāṃ tu “saṃkulopāhita-” [GDh 16.34] ity uktatvāt | bahuvacanaṃ sarvaśrāddhasūcanārtham, ekoddiṣṭe dātṝṇām eva mā bhūd iti || 16.48 ||
akṛtānnaśrāddhikasaṃyoge ca || 16.49 ||
yadākṛtānnaṃ pitṝn uddiśya dīyate bhojanābhāve tad akṛtānnaśrāddhikam | tatsaṃyogaṃ dātṛpratigrahītror ubhayoḥ saṃyogavacanāt, ākāliko ‘nadhyāyaḥ | cakārād anyatrāpi pitrarthe | yathāha manuḥ |
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yat kiṃcic chrāddhikaṃ bhavet |
pratigṛhyāpy anadhyāyaḥ pāṇyāsyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | [MDh 4.117]
āmaśrāddhasyaitad eva liṅgam || 16.49 ||
pratividyaṃ ca yān smaranti yān smaranti || 16.50 ||
prativedaṃ pratiśākhaṃ ca yān anadhyāyān gṛhyakārāḥ smaranti cakārād dharmāntarev api, nādhīyīteti saṃbandhaḥ || 16.50 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye ṣoḍaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
saptadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
mānasam apy aśucir ity abhihitam | tadaśucitvam āhārajanitam api bhavatīti bhojyābhojyavivakṣayāha |
praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta
|| 17.1 ||
prakarṣeṇa stutānāṃ svakarmasu na kṣatriyādikarmasu arthakāmaparityāgena mukhyakarmānuṣṭānaratānām ity arthaḥ, teṣāṃ dvijātīnāṃ upanītānām | bhojanasyārthaprāptatvāt parisaṃkhyārtham idaṃ vacanam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijātipradarśanārtham | kṣatriyādīnām apraśastābhyanujñānārtham ity eke | bhuñjīta bhojanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | praśastānām ity ukte āhopuruṣikāmātreṇāpi praśastā bhavantīti svakarmagrahaṇam | praśastānāṃ sva ity eva siddhe karmagrahaṇam kāmyanivṛttyartham | svakarmasv iti bahuvacanaṃ jñānakarmasamuccayakāriṇāṃ prādhānyakhyāpanārtham || 17.1 ||
pratigṛhṇīyāc ca || 17.2 ||
eṣāṃ dhanam | etān adhyāpayed yājayec cety evamarthaś cakāraḥ || 17.2 ||
evam api |
edhodakayavasamūlaphalamadhvabhayābhyudyata-śayyāsanāvasathayānapayodadhidhānāśapharipriyaṅgu-sraṅmārgaśākāny apraṇodyāni sarveṣām || 17.3 ||
edhaḥ kāṣṭham, udakaṃ kumbhādyavasthitam api, yavasaṃ tṛṇāni, mūlaphale prasiddhe, madhu mākṣikam, abhayaṃ paritrāṇam, abhyudyataśayyāsanāni prasiddhāni, āvasathaṃ pratiśrayādiḥ, yānādīni prasiddhāni, śapharī matsyaviśeṣaḥ, priyaṅguḥ dhānyaviśeṣaḥ, srak grathitāni kusumāni, mārgaṃ mṛgamāṃsam, śākaṃ prasiddham, apraṇodyāni apratyākhyeyāni, ayācitāni, apraṇodyagrahaṇāt | asmin sūtre abhyudyataśabdena pratigrahītṛsakāśaṃ yad ānīyate tad ucyate | tatra jātiviśeṣo nāsti | tathāha manuḥ |
udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apraveditām |
bhojyāṃ prajāpatir mene api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ || iti | [MDh 4.248]
asyāpavādaḥ |
śraddadhānasya bhoktavyaṃ corasyāpi viśeṣataḥ |
na tv eva bahuyājyasya yaś copanayate bahūn || iti | [VaDh 14.17]
tathā cāparam |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ śalyakartuś ca pāśinaḥ |
ṣaṇḍasya kulaṭāyāś ca udyātāpi na gṛhyate || iti | [VaDh 14.19]
tatrāpi bhojyadravyam eva draṣṭavyam, “bhojyāṃ prajāpatir mene” [MDh 4.248] iti liṅgāt | evaṃ cābhyudyatam api bhojyam, na pratigrāhyam | apraṇodyagrahaṇāt praṇodane doṣaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
nāśnanti pitaras tasya daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate || iti | [VaDh 14.18]
tatrāpi bhojyadravyam eva pratigṛhṇīyāt apraśastebhyo dvijātibhyaḥ pṛthaggrahaṇāt | sarvagrahaṇāt śūdrād api | sarvebhya iti vaktavye sarveṣām ity upādānam ātmasaṃbandhijñāpanārtham | evaṃ ca parakīyaṃ muṣitvā yo dadāti tasyāgrahaṇe na doṣaḥ
|| 17.3 ||
pitṛdevagurubhṛtyabharaṇe cānyat || 17.4 ||
bharaṇavacanam āvaśyakakarmārtham | pitṛbharaṇam ekoddiṣṭādi, avicchedena śrāddhakaraṇam | devabharaṇaṃ sāyaṃ prātar agnihotrahomādi | guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ | bhṛtyāḥ putradāsādayaḥ | eteṣāṃ bharaṇaṃ bhaktācchādanādīnāṃ dānam | eteṣu nimitteṣv anyad apy apraṇodyam | caśabdād edhodakādi ca | bharaṇam ity uktatvāt na svayaṃ tato gṛhītvā puṣṭo bhavet | kecic cakāram ātithyārtham āhuḥ | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
gurvarthe dāram ujjihīrṣann arciṣyan devatātithīn |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ || iti | [VaDh 14.13]
āpadi tu “yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām” [GDh 7.4] ity anena yācitvā pratigrahaṇam uktam | iha tu labhyamānasyāpraṇodaḥ | evaṃ cāpady ayācitālābha eva yācitvā pratigrahaṇam iti pradarśitam | āpadviṣayatvād anāpadi na grāhyam iti draṣṭavyam || 17.4 ||
vṛttiś cen nāntareṇa śūdrāt || 17.5 ||
apraṇodyam iti vartate | na tu pūrveṇa vṛttiś ced iti | yadi jīvanaṃ śūdrapratigrahaṇam antareṇa na nirvarteta tatra śūdrāt pratigṛhṇīyāt | na tu yājanabhojanapravacanāni kuryāt | tathāh manuḥ |
nādhyāc chūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvān āśaucino ‘pi vā |
āmam evādadītāsmād avṛttāv ekarātrikam || [MDh 4.223]
ityādi | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śūdrānnenodarasthena yo ‘dhigacchati maithunam |
yasyānnaṃ tasya te putrā annāc chuklaṃ pravartate || iti | [VaDh 6.27]
yājane tu vakṣyati “śūdrayājakaṃ śūdrārthayājakam” ityādi [GDh 20.1] | tathādhyāpane tu “śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva” [MDh 3.156] ityādi || 17.5 ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
paśupālakṣetrakarṣakakulasaṃgatakārayitṛparicārakā bhojyānnāḥ || 17.6 ||
paśupālaḥ paśūnāṃ saṃrakṣitā sa paśusvāmino bhojyānnaḥ | tathā kṣetrakarṣakaś ca | tathāha uśanā: “svagopālo bhojyānnaḥ svakṣetrakarṣakaś ca” ityādi | kulasaṃgataḥ pāraṃparyeṇa mitrarūpeṇāgataḥ | kārayitā nāpitaḥ | paricārako dāsaḥ | etasmāl liṅgān nāpitasya śūdratvam adhyavasīyate | tathā cāha manuḥ |
kṣetrakaḥ kulamitraś ca gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnāḥ yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || [MDh 4.253]
ityādi | prakaraṇād yadi śūdrānnabhojanam antareṇa prāṇadhāraṇaṃ na syāt tata eteṣām annam adanīyam | kutaḥ kulasaṃgatakārayitṛparicārikāṇām annaṃ bhojyam iti vaktavye bhojyānnā iti liṅgāt bhoktavyam iti draṣṭavyam || 17.6 ||
vaṇik cāśilpī || 17.7 ||
bhojyānna ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | vāṇijyopajīvī ca śūdro bhojyānnaḥ | sa ced aśilpī kumbhakārādiko na bhavati | caśabdād aśūdro ‘pi śilpī bhojyānnaḥ || 17.7 ||
anenaiva prasaṅgenānyad apy abhojyam āha |
nityam abhojyam || 17.8 ||
yatrāhar ahar anāthāya dīyate tan nityaṃ na bhoktavyam | nityaṃ vā sarvadā paragṛhe anāpady abhoktavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
upāsate gṛhasthā ye parapākam abuddhayaḥ | [MDh 3.104]
ityādi || 17.8 ||
keśakīṭāvapannam || 17.9 ||
keśagrahaṇaṃ sarvagātralomopasaṃgrahaṇārtham | kīṭagrahaṇam apy aśuddhaprāṇipradarśanāṛtham | avapannaṃ pacanakāla eva siddham ity abhiprāyaḥ | abhojyaṃ ca | evaṃ ca bruvatā pacanottarakālaṃ kṛtaprāyaścittaṃ bhoktavyam iti jñāpitam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāmaṃ tu keśakīṭān uddhṛtyādbhiḥ prakṣālya bhasmanāvakīrya vācā praśasstam upayuñjīta bhojyam” [VaDh 14.23] iti | bahvannasya pacanakāla eva saṃbandhasyāpi prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
pakṣidagdhaṃ gavāghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || iti | [MDh 5.125]
atra pakṣigrahaṇaṃ kākād anyatra draṣṭavyam, tasya prāyaścittāntaradarśanāt | yathāha parāśaraḥ |
śṛtaṃ droṇādhikaṃ cānnaṃ śvakākair upapāditam |
na tyājyaṃ tasya śuddhyarthaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasreṇa mantrapūtena vāriṇā |
bhojyaṃ tat prokṣitaṃ vipraiḥ paryagnikṛtam eva ca || iti |
bahutvam api deśakālādyavasthayā draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
deśaṃ kālaṃ tathā mānaṃ dravyaṃ dravyaprayojanam |
upapattim avasthāṃ ca jñātvā śaucaṃ prakalpayet ||
iti || 17.9 ||
rajasvalākṛṣṇaśakunipadopahatam || 17.10 ||
abhojyam iti sarvatrānuvartate | rajasvalāgrahaṇaṃ sarvāśucipradarśanārtham | padagrahaṇam apy aśuciśarīrāvayavapradarśanārtham | etair upahataṃ spṛṣṭam || 17.10 ||
bhrūṇaghnā prekṣitaṃ gavopaghrātaṃ bhāvaduṣṭam
|| 17.11 ||
bhrūṇahā brahmahā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā bhrūṇahā bhavati” [VaDh 20.23] iti | tadgrahaṇaṃ śvacaṇḍālapatitānām apy upalakṣaṇam, “śvacaṇḍālapatitāvekṣaṇe duṣṭam” [VaDh 23.33] iti sahopadeśāt |
nanu ca yady evaṃ sahopadiṣṭānām api grahaṇaṃ syāt tadā “caṇḍālapatita” [VaDh 23.33] ity etasmin sūtre sahanirdeśāt, “śvacaṇḍāla” [VaDh 23.33] ity etasmin sūtre caṇḍālapatitayor anyataropdeśena itarasyāpi lakṣaṇayā siddhatvād ubhayor upādānam anarthakam iti |
atrocyate – niyamārtha ārambhaḥ, avekṣaṇe trayāṇām eva pratiṣedhaḥ, na patitasūtropāttānāṃ sūtikādīnām apīti | tasmād iha bhrūṇahagrahaṇena śvādīnām api grahaṇam ity uktaṃ yuktam eva | evaṃ sati virodhābhāvāt "rajasvalākṛṣṇaśakuni” [GDh 17.10] ity asmin sūtre rajasvalāgrahaṇenāpy ekasūtropāttānāṃ patitādīnām api grahaṇam | tataś ca rajasvalādibhiḥ spṛṣṭam eva nāvekṣitam, bhrūṇāhādibhir avekṣitaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ ceti draṣṭavyam | gavā ca sāmīpyenāghrātam, bhāvaduṣṭaṃ bhojayitrā apadhyānena dattam, bhoktur manaso tuṣṭikaraṃ vā
|| 17.11 ||
śuktaṃ kevalam adadhi || 17.12 ||
yad āmlarasaṃ bhavati tac śuktam, na tu svabhāvata evāmlam, adadhīty apavādadarśanāt | kevalaṃ dravyāntarāsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ kāñjikādivat, tad abhojyam | adadhi dadhi kevalam api dravyāntareṇa takrādinā saṃspṛṣṭam api bhojyam || 17.12 ||
punaḥ siddham || 17.13 ||
yat pūrvapākatulyatām āpadyate, na tu śītalatvād apakvatvād vā, punar grahaṇāt || 17.13 ||
paryuṣitam aśākabhakṣasnehamāṃsamadhūni || 17.14 ||
paryuṣitaṃ rātryantaritaṃ śākādivarjam | paryuṣitaṃ śākādi bhojyam | śākaṃ asnehasaṃyuktam api, bhakṣāḥ piṣṭādayaḥ, sneho ghṛtādi tatsaṃsṛṣṭam api draṣṭavyaṃ smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha manuḥ |
yat kiṃcit snehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet || iti | [MDh 5.24]
māṃsaṃ cāsnehasaṃyuktam api | snehagrahaṇenaiva madhunaḥ siddhe pṛthaggrahaṇam paryuṣitasyāpi madhunaḥ striyāḥ pratiṣedhārtham | tathā ca kalpakārāṇām apy upaniṣat – “tasmād uta striyo madhu nāśnanti” iti || 17.14 ||
saṃsargaduṣṭaṃ kāladuṣṭaṃ coktvā sāṃpratam āśrayaduṣṭam āha |
utsṛṣṭapuṃścalyabhiśatānapadeśyadāṇḍikatakṣa-kadaryabandhanikacikitsakamṛgayvaniṣucāryucchiṣṭa-bhojigaṇavidviṣāṇām || 17.15 ||
utsṛṣṭam asvāmikam | puṃścalī vyabhicāriṇī | anenaiva gaṇikā cārthasiddhā | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca” [VaDh 14.10] iti | abhiśastaḥ doṣenābhikhyātaḥ | anapadeśyaḥ strīliṅgena puṃliṅgena vā anirdeśyaḥ tṛtīyā prakṛtir ity arthaḥ | dāṇḍiko rājñā nigrahe niyuktaḥ | takṣo rathakāraḥ | kadaryaḥ asabhyabhāṣaṇarataḥ, “nāślīlaṃ kīrtayed yady aślīlaṃ kīrtayet taṃ kadaryam āhuḥ” iti brāhmaṇadarśanāt | bandhaniko guptipālaḥ | cikitsako vaidyo brāhmaṇaḥ, “tasmād brāhmaṇena bheṣajaṃ na kāṛyam” iti taittirīyāṇāṃ brāhmaṇe darśanāt | tathā ca vasiṣṭho ‘pi:
nānṛjur brāhmaṇo bhavati na vaṇiṅ na kuśīlavaḥ |
na śūdrapreṣaṇaṃ kurvan na steno na cikitsakaḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.3]
stenopajīvika ity eke | śalyakartety apare,
cikitsakasya mṛgayor śalyahartuś ca pāśinaḥ | [VaDh 14.19]
iti darśanāt | mṛgayur lubdhakaḥ, sa ced aniṣucārī[65] vāgurikaḥ | ucchiṣṭabhojī ucchiṣtānnabhojakaḥ | gaṇo janasamudāyaḥ | vidviṣāṇaḥ śatruḥ | eṣām annam abhojyam | praśastānām apraśastānām abhojanopadeśāt, āpady apy eṣām annaṃ na bhojyam || 17.15 ||
apāṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālāt || 17.16 ||
ye cāpāṅktyāḥ prāg upadiṣṭāḥ stenādayas tyaktātmaparyantās teṣām akṛtaprāyaścittānām annam abhojyam, brahmahādīnāṃ tu kṛtaprāyaścittānām api, “nāsyāsmin loke pratyāpattir vidyate kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhaṇyate” [ĀpDh 1.24.25] ity āpastambavacanāt || 17.16 ||
vṛthānnācamanotthānavyapetāni || 17.17 ||
vṛthānnaṃ yad ātithyādyanuddeśenātmārthaṃ pacyate | tathā bahvṛcānām ṛk |
mogham annaṃ vindate apracetāḥ
satyaṃ bravīmi vadha it sa tasya |
nāryamaṇaṃ puṣyati no sakhāyam
kevalāgho bhavati kevalādī || iti || [RV 10.170.6]
ācamanavyapetam, yad ardhaṃ bhuktvā ācamya punas tad eva bhuṅkte utthānavyapetam, yad ardhaṃ bhuktam evotthāya bhuṅkte etat trividham apy abhojyam | ācamanotthānavyapetam iti vaktavye bahuvacanam ekapaṅktāv anyatareṇācamane utthāne ca kṛte anyatarasyābhojyam iti | tathāha uśanā: “agurubhir ācamanotthāne kṛtaṃ ca” iti | evaṃ gurubhiḥ kṛte na doṣaḥ || 17.17 ||
samāsamābhyāṃ viṣamasame pūjātaḥ || 17.18 ||
samena vidyākulāditulyena saha viṣamāṃ pūjām āsanaparicaraṇādikāṃ kṛtvā asamena ca samāṃ kṛtvā yatrānnaṃ dīyate tatra pūjātaḥ viṣame ca same ca sati na bhoktavyam || 17.18 ||
anarcitaṃ ca || 17.19 ||
annamantreṇānamaskṛtam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “rocata iti sāyaṃ prātar aśanāny abhipūjayet” [VaDh 3.69] iti | cakārāt “ko bhokṣyate” ity abhikruṣṭam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “ko bhokṣyata iti cākruṣṭam” [VaDh 14.9] iti || 17.19 ||
goś ca kṣīram anirdaśāhāḥ sūtake || 17.20 ||
goḥ prasūtāyā anatikrāntadaśāhāyā kṣīram abhojyam | caśabdād dadhyādi ca || 17.20 ||
ajāmahiṣyoś ca || 17.21 ||
anirdaśāhayor ajāmahiṣyoḥ kṣīram apeyaṃ sadadhyādi | ajānām adhikārārthaś caśabdaḥ | pṛthag grahaṇaṃ gokṣīrasya kadācit saptarātrād ūrdhvam adoṣārtham || 17.21 ||
nityam āvikam apeyam auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ ca || 17.22 ||
nityagrahaṇaṃ na kevalaṃ daśāha eveti | vikārapratyayanirdeśāt mūtrādy apy āsām apeyam iti | evaṃ ca pūrveṣāṃ mūtrādeḥ peyatvam avagamyate | ekaśaphāḥ ekakhurā vaḍavādayaḥ | itare prasiddhāḥ | visamāso mṛgādīnām āraṇyānāṃ sarveṣām upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣaṃ vinā | iti | [MDh 5.9]
cakārān mānuṣastrīkṣīraṃ ca | prakṛtam apy apeyagrahaṇaṃ nityārthaṃ kriyate | tataś cājāmahiṣyor atyantāpady anirdaśāhayor apy anugrahaḥ siddhaḥ || 17.22 ||
syandinīyamasūsandhinīnāṃ ca || 17.23 ||
nityaṃ sadadhyādi kṣīram apeyam ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | syandinī nitāntagranthistanī | yamasūḥ yamalāpatyā | sandhinī ekāṃ velām antarīkṛtya duhyate vatsāntareṇa vā | garbhiṇī yā duhyate sā sandhinīty eke | caśabdād vyādhitānāṃ ca || 17.23 ||
vivatsāyāś ca || 17.24 ||
yāś ca vyapetavatsāḥ yāś ca nāśamaraṇābhyāṃ vatsair viyuktāḥ tāsām apeyam | pṛthag grahaṇaṃ dadhyādīnāṃ pratiṣedhābhāvārtham, kṣīramātrasyaiva pratiṣedhaḥ syād iti | ajāmahiṣyor apy upasaṃgrahārthaś cakāraḥ || 17.24 ||
sāṃprataṃ ye svarūpata evābhakṣyās ta ucyante |
pañcanakhāś cāśalyakaśaśaśvāviḍgodhākhaḍga-kacchapāḥ || 17.25 ||
abhakṣyā ity uttaratra vakṣyati | tasyehānukarṣaḥ | pañca nakhā yeṣāṃ hastacaraṇayoḥ, pañca pañcanakhāḥ sṛgālavānarādayaḥ, te cābhakṣyāḥ śalyakādi varjayitvā | caśabdaḥ payomūtrocchiṣṭānām upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | śalyakaḥ kaṇṭakavarāhaḥ | śaśaḥ prasiddhaḥ | śvāviṭ ulūkaḥ | godhā prasiddhā | khaḍgaḥ śṛṅgamṛgaḥ | kacchapaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ye abhakṣyās teṣāṃ mūtrādy apeyaṃ draṣṭavyam,
abhakṣyāṇāṃ tu yan mūtraṃ taducchiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca |
abhojyam iti nirdiṣṭaṃ viṣṭā caiva tathaiva ca ||
iti vatsavacanāt | pañcanakhāś ceti prathamānideśaḥ śarīrāvayavānāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti | pañcanakhāś ceti prathamānirdeśaḥ śarīrāvayavānāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti | yadi māṃsam eva abhīṣṭaṃ tadā ṣaṣṭhyā nirdeśo yukta iti | evaṃ ca rudhirādayo ‘py apeyā eva | evaṃ sarvatra draṣṭavyam || 17.25 ||
ubhayatodatkeśyalomaikaśaphakalaviṅkaplava-cakravākahaṃsāḥ || 17.26 ||
ubhayatodataḥ manuṣyādayaḥ | teṣāṃ pañcanakhatvād eva pratiṣedhe siddhe prāyaścittagauravārthaṃ punar abhidhānam | evam anyatrāpi viśeṣanirdeśe draṣṭavyam | keśinaḥ keśātiśayayuktāś camaryādayaḥ | alomāḥ sarpādayaḥ | ekaśaphāḥ aśvādayaḥ | kalaviṅko grāmacaṭakaḥ | plavaḥ śakaṭabilaḥ | cakravākahaṃsau prasiddhau || 17.26 ||
kākakaṅkagṛdhraśyenā jalajā raktapādatuṇḍā grāmyakukkuṭasūkarau || 17.27 ||
kākādayaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ prasiddhāḥ | jalajāḥ sarva evāviśeṣeṇa | raktapādāḥ pārāvatādayaḥ | raktatuṇḍāś cakorādayaḥ | grāmyaśabdaḥ kukkuṭasūkarābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate | evaṃ cāraṇyānām apratiṣedhaḥ | visamāsa anyeṣāṃ kravyādādīnām api pratiṣedhārtham || 17.27 ||
dhenvanaḍuhau ca || 17.28 ||
dhenuḥ payasvinī gau | anaḍvān balīvardaḥ | tayor grahaṇaṃ vatsādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | evaṃ tarhi gauś ceti vaktavyam iti ced, atrocyate – bhakṣyatvasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | tasmin bhakṣyatvapakṣe tayor eva grahaṇaṃ yathā syāt na vatsādīnām apīty evamartha upadeśaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “bhakṣyau tu dhenvanaḍuhau medhyau vājasaneyake vijñāyate” [VaDh 14.46] iti | tatrātithirūpeṇāgatasya dhenvanaḍuhau bhakṣyau itareṣām abhakṣyāv iti draṣṭavyam, vasiṣṭhaliṅgāt, yathā “pitṛdevātithipūjāyām apy evaṃ paśūn hiṃsyād iti mānavam” (VaDh 4.5), “athāpi brāhmaṇāya vā rājanyāya vābhyāgatāya mahokṣāṇaṃ mahājaṃ vā paced evam evāsmā ātithyaṃ kurvanti” (VaDh 4.8) iti || 17.28 ||
apannadadavasannavṛthāmāṃsāni || 17.29 ||
apannadat apatitadantaḥ | avasanno vyādhitaḥ | svayaṃ mṛta ity apare | vṛthāmāṃsam ātmārtham eva pakvam | vṛthāgrahaṇād eva siddhe prāyaścittagauravārthaḥ punarupadeśaḥ | etac ca bhakṣyaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam, abhakṣye prāptyabhāvāt || 17.29 ||
kisalayakyākulaśunaniryāsā lohitāḥ || 17.30 ||
kisalayaṃ sarveṣām agraprarohaḥ | kyākuḥ ahicchatraṃ chatrākam | laśunaṃ prasiddham | niryāsāḥ lohitāś cet | tathā ca manuḥ: “lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān” [MDh 5.6] ityādi | evaṃ ca hiṅgvādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ || 17 30 ||
vraścanāś ca || 17.31 ||
vraścanaprabhavāḥ vṛkṣāṇām anujā ity arthaḥ | te lohitāś cālohitāś cābhakṣyāḥ | evaṃ cāvraścanaprabhavatvād alohitatvāt karpūrādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ | kecit “lohitā vraścanāś ca” iti sūtrabhedaṃ kurvanti | teṣām api pakṣe karpūrādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ | anye niryāsā vārkṣā iti vyācakṣate | teṣāṃ hiṅgvādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | caśabdāt palāṇḍugṛñjanādīni smṛtyantaropadiṣṭāni gṛhyante || 17.31 ||
nicudārubakabalākāśukamadguṭiṭṭibhamāndhāla-naktaṃcarā abhakṣyāḥ || 17.32 ||
nicudāruḥ dārvāghāṭaḥ, madguḥ jalanimajjanaśīlaḥ, ṭiṭṭibhaḥ ṭiṭṭibhaśabdānukaraṇaśīlaḥ, mandhālo vāggudaḥ, naktaṃcarāḥ ulūkādayaḥ, anye prasiddhāḥ | abhakṣyāḥ iti kriyāpadaṃ pañcanakhā ityārabhya saṃbadhyata ity uktam || 17.32 ||
bhakṣyāḥ pratudaviṣkirājālapādāḥ || 17.33 ||
anyataropadeśād evānyatarasya siddheḥ ubhayavargopadeśo yeṣāṃ vidhiḥ pratiṣedho vā nāsti teṣām āpady anugrahārthaḥ | pratudāḥ pratudya pratudya mukhena ye bhakṣayanti mayūrādayaḥ | viṣkirāḥ vikīrya vikīrya ye bhakṣayanti lāvakādayaḥ | ajālapādāḥ ye ‘jālapādā na bhavanti tumbakādayaḥ || 17.33 ||
matsyāś cāvikṛtāḥ || 17.34 ||
vikṛtāḥ pāṇyantararūpāḥ | saśalkasamuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | saśalkāś cāvikṛtāś ceti ubhayaviśeṣaṇayuktā eva bhakṣyāḥ, naikaviśeṣaṇayuktā ity arthaḥ | pūrvam ajālapādā ity uktatvāt jālapādānām atra caikaviśeṣaṇayuktānāṃ ca pratiṣedho draṣṭavyaḥ | evam uttaratrāpi draṣṭavyam || 17.34 ||
vadhyāś ca dharmārthe || 17.35 ||
yajñādau dharmanimitte ye hatā pūrvam abhakṣyā api bhakṣyāḥ | cakārāt prāṇānām atyaye ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |
yathāvidhi niyuktasya prāṇānām eva cātyaye || iti | [MDh 5.27]
nanu ca yajñādau hatānāṃ śrutividhānād eva siddhatvād ihopadeśo ‘narthaka iti |
atrocyate – ye adṛṣṭam evoddiśya kriyante tacchiṣṭabhakṣaṇa eva prāyaścittābhāvaḥ na tu dṛṣṭaprayojane śiṣṭabhakṣaṇa iti | ata eva yajñārthe ity anuktvā dharmārthe ity uktam | anenaivāvakīrṇipaśor api māṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ nirastam, tasya prāyaścittārthatvāt, na tv adṛṣṭārthatvam iti | evaṃ ca yathā dṛṣṭaprayojanamāṃsabhakṣaṇe smārtaprāyaścittānupraveśaḥ tathā tadviṣayahiṃsāyām api bhavatīti gamyate | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa manunāpy uktam,
kuryāt ghṛtapaśuṃ saṅge kuryāt pitṣṭapaśuṃ tathā | [MDh 5.37]
ityādi || 17.35. ||
vyālahatādṛṣṭadoṣavākpraśastāny abhyukṣyopayuñjītopayuñjīta || 17.36 ||
vyālaiḥ siṃhādibhiḥ hatasya paśor māṃsam abhyukṣaṇamātraṃ kṛtvā bhakṣayet | adṛṣṭadoṣe vyālahataviśeṣaṇam, tathā vākpraśaste ca | vyālahatasya paśor māṃsaṃ bhakṣyaṃ tac ced adṛṣṭadoṣaṃ vākpraśastaṃ ca bhavatīti | adṛṣṭadoṣam abhakṣyavarge cānupadiṣṭam, bhakṣyatvopadeśād eva siddhatvāt | vākpraśastaṃ cānyaiḥ | evaṃ ca yad bhakṣyavarge abhakṣyavarge cānupadiṣṭam āpadi vihitaṃ tasyānāpdy anena prakāreṇābhyanujñānam | bahuvacanam api viśeṣaṇatrayayuktasyaiva śucitvaṃ naikaviśeṣaṇayuktasyeti | evaṃ “baiṣkam apy upayuñjīta” [GDh 3.31] ity atrāpy adṛṣṭadoṣatvādi draṣṭavyam | aśnīyād iti vaktavye upayuñjīteti bhakṣaṇād varjanam eva śreya iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā || [MDh 5.56]
iti || 17.36 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye saptadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
aṣṭādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
sāmānyā viśiṣṭāś cāśramadharmā uktāḥ | striya idānīṃ vaktavyā ity āha |
asvatantrā dharme strī || 18.1 |
asvatantrā parādhīnā dharme pāralaukike bhartrā sahāsyā dharmādhikāraḥ, na tu puruṣavat svātantryeṇa | tathāha manuḥ |
nāsti strīṇāṃ pṛthag yajño na vrataṃ nāpy upoṣitam |
patiṃ śuśrūṣate yena tena svarge mahīyate || iti | [MDh 5.155]
śaṅkhenāpy uktam: “na ca vratopavāsaniyamejyādānadharmo vānugrahakaraḥ strīṇām anyatra patiśuśrūṣāyāḥ kāmaṃ tu bhartur anujñayā vratopavāsaniyamādīnām abhyāsaḥ strīdharmaḥ” iti |
nanu ca śrautasmārtānāṃ puruṣoddeśena vihitatvāt svātantryeṇa striyāḥ prāptyabhāvāt pratiṣedhānupapattiḥ | athocyeta asvātantryavacanaṃ sahadharmakriyāyāṃ striyā api phalam astīty evamartham iti, tad ayuktam, sahadharmacāriṇītvād evāsyārthasya labdhatvāt | tathā asvatantreti na jñāyate kiṃtantrayā anayā bhavitavyam iti | bhartradhīnatayeti cet, na, upariṣṭāt “nāticared bhartāram” [GDh 18.2] ity anena bhartṛparatantratvasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt | tenāvyabhicāro vakṣyata iti cet, na, (ca) aticārasya dharmārthakāmaviṣayasya pratiṣiddhatvād iti |
atrocyate – upavāsadānāder dharmasādhanasya bhartur anujñāyāṃ satyāṃ striyāḥ prāpaṇārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ, āśramadharmasya bhartṛdvāreṇa prāptatvāt | tathā ca śaṅkhavacanaṃ yad darśitam “kāmaṃ tu bhartur anujñayā” ityādi | tathā dānaviṣaye ‘pi nārada āha |
strīkṛtāny apramāṇāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpati |
viśeṣato gṛhakṣetradānādhamanavikrayāḥ || [NSm 1.22]
ityādi | idaṃ bhartṛdhanaviṣayam,
strīdhanasyeśinī strī syād bhartā ca tadanujñayā |
bhoktuṃ rakṣayituṃ yogyo bhartuṃ nāśayituṃ na ca ||
iti kaṇvavacanād iti cet, na, smṛtyantaradarśanāt | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
na dātavyaṃ na hotavyaṃ strīdhanaṃ jātu kenacit |
patyanujñātapūrvaṃ cet striyā dātavyam eva tat || iti |
tathā ca lokākṣiḥ |
īśānā strī dhanasyoktā nādhidānakrayeṣu ca |
ādhyādi tatkṛtaṃ tasmān na sidhyati kadācana || iti |
evaṃ kaṇvavacane ‘pi dānādhamanavikrayaṃ varjayitvaiva svāmyaṃ draṣṭavyam | yat tūktaṃ “kiṃtantrayā bhavitavyam” iti, tatrāpi bhartṛputrapitradhīnayā yathākālam | tathāha manuḥ |
bāle pitur vaśe tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
putrasya sthavirībhāve na strī svātantryam arhati || iti | [MDh 5.148]
tathā ca tatraivāparam api |
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā sthaviryā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kāṛyaṃ kiṃcid gṛheṣv api || [MDh 5.147]
iti || 18.1 ||
evaṃ ca dharmād anyatra kāmacāre prāpta āha |
nāticared bhartāram || 18.2 ||
bhartur anujñayā vinā tatpratiṣiddhā vā na kvacid api pravartetety arthaḥ | patim iti vaktavye yo yo bharaṇaṃ karoti taṃ taṃ praty evam iti bhartṛgrahaṇam || 18.2 ||
kiṃ ca |
vākcakṣuḥkarmasaṃyatā || 18.3 ||
syād iti śeṣaḥ | yāvadarthasaṃbhāṣiṇī vāksaṃyatā | akāryakṛtām īkṣakādīnām aprekṣaṇī cakṣuḥsaṃyatā | gṛhavyāpārayuktā karmasaṃyatā || 18.3 ||
apatir apatyalipsur devarāt || 18.4 ||
apatir avidyamānabhartṛkā ayogyapatir vā | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
naṣṭe mṛte pravrajite klībe ‘tha patite patau |
pañcasv āpatsu nārīṇāṃ patir anyo vidhīyate || iti |
apatyalipsuḥ yady anapatyā saty apatyam icchati punar devarād utpādayed ity arthaḥ | devaro bhartur jyeṣṭho yavīyān vā || 18.4 ||
kena vidhinotpādayed ity āha |
guruprasūtā nartum atīyāt || 18.5 ||
gurubhir anujñātā bhartṛpakṣaiḥ pitṛpakṣaiś ca niyuktety arthaḥ | ṛtuṃ nātīyāt ṛtukālād anyatra na yujyetety arthaḥ | tatrāpy ekasmin dine yadi garbhotpattir bhavati tatraiva bhavati, no cet tasmin ṛtau na bhavatīti | tathā talavakārāṇāṃ brāhmaṇam: “yad vā prathamam aho retaḥ sicyate sa garbhaḥ saṃbhavati, ato yat tata sicyate mudhaiva tat parāsicyate” iti | tatrāpi ghṛtenābhyajyeti draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā: “niyuktāṃ sarvāṅgaghṛtābhyaktāṃ ghṛtena sarvāṅgam ātmānam abhyajya gacchet” iti || 18.5 ||
piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhebhyaḥ || 18.6 ||
uttarasūtre vāśabdasyābhāvavaikalpikārthasyānukarṣaḥ | tataś cādhikārād devarābhāve sapiṇḍamātrād apy apatyalipsuḥ syāt | tadabhāve sagotramātrāt | atra sagotraśabdena samānodako draṣṭavyaḥ, “saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ gṛhnīyāt” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | ṛṣisaṃbandhāt ṛṣisaṃbandhāḥ svakulyāḥ || 18.6 ||
yonimātrād vā || 18.7 ||
sarvābhāve brāhmaṇajātimātrād vā | mātraśabdo dharmavācī tataś ca guṇavadabhāve nirguṇād iti draṣṭavyam | pṛthag grahaṇaṃ kulīne sati paragāmitvapratiṣedhārtham | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “na khalu tatkulīne vidyamāne paragāminī syāt” [VaDh 17.80] iti || 18.7 ||
nādevarād ity eke || 18.8 ||
adevarāt sapiṇḍādeḥ sakāśād apatyaṃ na janayed ity eke manyante | devarābhāve anyato ‘pīty ācāryaḥ || 18.8 ||
nātidvitīyaṃ janayitur apatyam || 18.9 ||
dvitīyaṃ atītya tṛtīyaṃ notpādayet | evaṃ ca dvitīyam anujñātaṃ bhavati | yadi tṛtīyam utpādayati janayitur devarāder bhavati, na kṣetriṇaḥ | tataś ca tad ayogyaṃ bhavatīty etāvad atra vivakṣitam || 18.9 ||
samayād anyasya || 18.10 ||
samayaḥ “tava ratir mamāpatyam” ity evaṃrūpaḥ samayaḥ kṛtaś ced anyasya kṣetriṇo bhavati tṛtīyam api | atas tad aduṣṭam ity abhiprāyaḥ || 18.10 ||
jīvataś ca kṣetre || 18.11 ||
anyasyety anukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | jīvataḥ prajananāsamarthasya patyuḥ kṣetre bhāryāyāṃ yad apatyam utpadyate tat kṣetriṇa eva bhavati vināpi samayena dvitīyād adhikam apīty abhiprāyaḥ
|| 18.11 ||
parasmāt tasya || 18.12 ||
parasmāt devarādivyatiriktāt vyatikramād vā yadi jīvato ‘py apatyam utpadyate tat dvitīyād adhikam api tasyaiva janayitur eva bhavati na kṣetriṇaḥ || 18.12 ||
dvayor vā || 18.13 ||
evam utpannam apatyaṃ bījikṣetriṇor vā bhavati | dvyāmuṣyāyaṇo bhavatīty arthaḥ || 18.13 ||
rakṣaṇāt tu bhartur eva || 18.14 ||
rakṣaṇe bharaṇapoṣaṇasaṃskārādau kriyamāṇe bhartuḥ, parasmāt chidreṇa yad apatyam utpadyate tad bhartur eva bhavati | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | pravrajitanaṣṭapatitādeḥ arakṣito ‘pi bhavati | ya eva rakṣaṇasamarthaḥ tasya viśeṣeṇa bhavatīti || 18.14 ||
mṛte bhartari vidhir uktaḥ | idānīṃ naṣṭe āha |
naṣṭe bhartari ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ kṣapaṇam || 18.15 ||
deśāntaratiraskṛte bhartari ṣaḍvarṣāṇy apatyotpattir na kartavyā | tataḥ param icchantī guruniyogaṃ kārayitvā apatyam utpādayed ity abhiprāyaḥ | kutaḥ | “aniyuktāyām utpanna utpādayituḥ putro bhavatīty āhuḥ” [VaDh 17.63] iti vasiṣṭhasmṛtidarśanāt || 18.15 ||
śrūyamāṇe ‘bhigamanam || 18.16 ||
ṣaḍvarṣād ūrdhvaṃ yadi bhartā śrūyamāṇo bhavati tasmin śrūyamāṇe tam evābhimukhyena gacchet, nānyasmād apatyam utpādayed ity abhiprāyaḥ || 18.16 ||
pravrajite tu nivṛttiḥ prasaṅgāt || 18.17 ||
yadi pravrajito bhartā bhavati tasmin prasaṅgāt taṃ prati gamanān nivṛttiḥ | taṃ prati na gacched ity arthaḥ | atha vā prasaṅgād apatyotpādanān nivṛttiḥ, apatyaṃ notpādayet | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | abhilāṣābhāve notpādayet saṃyatayā bhavitavyam ity eke varṇayanti | pravrajite apatyaṃ notpādayet | tuśabdāt svayam api pravrajet, deśāntarasthasyābhiprāyo jñātum aśakya iti
|| 18.17 ||
dvāda śavarṣāṇi brāhmaṇasya vidyāsaṃbandhe || 18.18 ||
vidyādhigamārthaṃ proṣitasya brāhmaṇasya bhāryā dvādaśa varṣāṇi kṣapayet apatyotpattiṃ tadabhimukhagamaṇam ca, pravrajite tu nivṛttiṃ ca kuryād iti draṣṭavyam | atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt pūrvaṃ kṣatriyādiviṣayam iti gamyate | atha vātra vidyāgrahaṇād arthārthaṃ gatasya brāhmaṇasyaiva | smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt kāmaviṣaye kṣapaṇaṃ nāstīti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “proṣitapatnī pañca varṣāṇy upāsītordhvaṃ pañcabhyo varṣebhyo bhartṛsakāśaṃ gacched yadi dharmārthābhyāṃ pravased atha tu kāmād yathā prete evaṃ vartitavyaṃ syāt” [VaDh 17.75–77] iti | evaṃ ca kṣatriyāder vidyārthaṃ gatasyāpi brāhmaṇādibhyo ‘rdham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | arthārthaṃ gatasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “evaṃ brāhmaṇī pañcāprajātā ṣaṭ prajātā, catvāni rājakanyāprajātā pañca prajātā, trīṇi vaiśyā aprajātā catvāri prajātā, dve śūdrā aprajātā trīṇi prajātā” [VaDh 78] iti | anenaiva nyāyena vidyāsaṃbandhe ‘py aprajātāyā ekavarṣanyūnabhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ || 18.18 ||
bhrātari caivaṃ jyāyasi yavīyān kanyāgnyupayameṣu
|| 18.19 ||
bhrātary akṛtadāre anāhitāgnau ca proṣite jyeṣṭhe kanīyān evam eva kuryāt | evaṃśabdāt samastātideśaḥ śrūyamāṇe tadabhigamanam, pravrajite tu tadabhigamananivṛttiś ca tato draṣṭavyā | evaṃ tarhi pravrajite saṃyatatvam api prāpnotīti cet, na, smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha uśanā: “pravrajati jyeṣṭhe kṣapaṇaṃ nāsti sadya eva niviśet” ityādi | evam apy arthārthaṃ gatasya ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prāpnotīti cet, na, tatrāpi smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayor gataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātrā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || iti |
cakārāt smṛtyantaroktānām apy upasaṃgrahaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “aṣṭau daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaram aniviṣṭam apratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittī bhavati” iti | tatra bahuśaḥ śrūyamāṇe dvādaśa, punaḥ punar ity uktatvāt | kiṃcic chrūyamāṇe daśavarṣāṇi, sarvathā aśrūyamāṇe aṣṭāv iti draṣṭavyam | kṣatriyādeḥ pūrvavad ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | asyāpavādaḥ |
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klība eva ca |
rājayakṣmāmayāvī ca na nyāyyaḥ syāt pratīkṣitum ||
ityādismṛtyantarokto draṣṭavyaḥ | jyeṣṭhagrahaṇād eva kanīyaso ‘rthataḥ siddheḥ saṃbandhiśabdatvād ekamātṛkasyaiva doṣa iti khyāpanārtham | smārtasyāpy ager upsaṃgrahārthaṃ bahuvacanam || 18.19 ||
ṣaḍ ity eke || 18.20 ||
ṣaḍ eva varṣāṇi pratīkṣetety eke manyante | tatra proṣito ‘tyantavṛddhaś cet ṣaḍvarṣāṇi kṣapayet, no ced dvādaśavarṣāṇīti draṣṭavyam || 18.20 ||
prāsaṅgikam uktvā strīdharmam evāha |
trīn kumāry ṛtūn atītya svayaṃ yujyetāninditenotsṛjya pitryān alaṃkārān || 18.21 ||
trīn ṛtūn kumārī kanyā atītya yady etāṃ pitrādir na dadyāt svayam eva bhartāraṃ pratipadyeta aninditaṃ kulābhijanādisamanvitam ity arthaḥ | utsṛjya pitrādidattālaṃkārān | pitṛgrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | tathā cāha manuḥ |
alaṃkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃvarā |
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ syād yadi taṃ haret || [MDh 9.92]
iti || 18.21 ||
kasmin kāle pitrādibhir dātavyety ata āha |
pradānaṃ prāg ṛtoḥ || 18.22 ||
ṛtudarśanāt prāg enāṃ dadyāt || 18.22 ||
yataḥ |
aprayacchan doṣī || 18.23 ||
na kevalam aprayacchato dānanimittaguṇābhāvaḥ | kiṃ tarhi? dampatyoḥ brahmahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ bhavati | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yāvantaḥ kanyām ṛtavaḥ spṛśanti
tulyaiḥ sakāmām abhiyācyamānām |
bhrūṇani tāvanti hatāni tābhyāṃ
mātāpitṛbhyām iti dharmavādaḥ || [VaDh 17.71]
iti || 18.23 ||
prāg vāsasaḥ pratipatter ity eke || 18.24 ||
durvijñeyatvād ṛtoḥ vāsasaḥ pratipatteḥ prāg dadyāt, yadaiva lajjitā tadaiva dadyād ity arthaḥ | evem eke manyante | varasaṃbhavato vikalpaḥ || 18.24 ||
yadā pitrādir dadyāt tadā ācchādyālaṃkṛtāṃ dadyād ity uktam | tatra dravyābhāve katham ity ata āha |
dravyādānaṃ vivāhasiddhyartham || 18.25 ||
dravyasya parakīyasyādānam ananujñātasya svīkaraṇaṃ vivāhasiddhaye yāvatā vivāho nirvartate ātmīyābhāve tāvad apahṛtya dadyād ity arthaḥ || 18.25 ||
kiṃ ca |
dharmatantrasaṅge ca || 18.26 ||
dharmatantrasyāgnihotrādeḥ pravṛttasya vicchedaḥ saṅgaḥ, tasmiṃś ca sati parasvāpaharaṇaṃ kuryāt | tantragrahaṇaṃ yat prakrāntam avaśyakartavyaṃ tasyaiva saṅge, na tv apravṛttasya pravṛttyartham | siddhyartham ity asyānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | yāvatā nirvartate tāvad eva gṛhṇīyāt na tato ‘dhikam iti | evaṃ cādhikagrahaṇe steyam eva bhavati || 18.26 ||
kutas tad ādeyam ity ata āha |
śūdrāt || 18.27 ||
yajñārthaṃ hi dhanam | na ca śūdrasya vaidikeṣu karmasv adhikāraḥ | ataḥ prathamaṃ tāvac chūdrād ādadīta || 18.27 ||
tadalābhe |
anyatrāpi śūdrāt || 18.28 ||
anyatrāpīti vaktavye śūdragrahaṇaṃ vaiśyakṣatriyaprāpaṇārtham | tadalābhe vaiśyāt | tadalābhe kṣatriyād iti || 18.28 ||
tatrāpi |
bahupaśor hīnakarmaṇaḥ || 18.29 ||
hīnakarmā yaḥ ananurūpaṃ karma karoti, kiṃcid api na karotīti vā sa hīnakarmā | tatsakāśād ādadīta || 18.29 ||
śatagor anāhitāgneḥ || 18.30 ||
anyakarmakṛto ‘pi śatagoḥ agnyādhānam akurvataḥ | śatagrahaṇaṃ dravyaparimāṇopalakṣaṇārtham, śataniṣkasyety arthaḥ | evaṃ sarvatra || 18.30 ||
sahasragoś cāsomapāt || 18.31 ||
yaś cāhitāgnir api sahasraguḥ somaṃ na pibati tasmād apy ādadīta | caśabdād anyto ‘pi | yaḥ prabhūtadhanatve sati tadanurūpaṃ karma na karoti tasmād apy ādadīta || 18.31 ||
na kevalaṃ nimittadvayam eva paradravyādāne, kiṃ ca |
saptamīṃ cābhuktvānicayāya || 18.32 ||
saptamīṃ velām abhuktvā dhanakṣayāt paradravyādānaṃ kuryāt | tathāha manuḥ |
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣaḍ anaśnatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.16]
hīnakarmādhikārārthaḥ cakāraḥ | tathodāhṛtaṃ ca manuvacanam | anicayāya udarapūraṇamātram
|| 18.32 ||
apy ahīnakarmabhyaḥ || 18.33 ||
asyām avasthāyāṃ yathāvihitakarmakāribhyo ‘pi | apiśabdād āhitāgneḥ somapāc ca || 18.33 ||
yady ādadāno rājapuruṣeṇa nṛpasamīpaṃ nīyate cauryanimittaṃ ca pṛcchyate tadā –
ācakṣīta rājñā pṛṣṭaḥ || 18.34 ||
ācakṣīta kathayet na tu bhayād apahnuvīta pṛṣṭaḥ san | na tu svayaṃ gatvā kathayet || 18.34 ||
yasmāt |
tena hi bhartavyaḥ śrutaśīlasaṃpannaś cet || 18.35 ||
hiśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmāt tena hi bhartavyaḥ, “bibhṛyāt brāhmaṇān śrotriyān” [GDh 10.9] iti vacanāt | śrutaśīlasaṃpanno yadi bhavati | śrutena vedārthavijñānena, śīlena taccoditakarmānuṣṭhānenāpi saṃpannaḥ samyak stutaḥ | “bibhṛyāt brāhmaṇān śrotriyān” [GDh 10.9] iti śrotriyāṇāṃ bharaṇopadeśāt bhartavyavacanenaiva śrutaśīlasaṃpannatā siddheti cet, ucyate – aśrotriyāṇām api bharaṇopadeśāt “nirutsāhāṃś ca brāhmaṇān [cābrāhmaṇān]” [GDh 10.10] iti | evaṃ ca parasvāpaharaṇe abrāhmaṇo na bhartavya iti pradarśitam | teneti vacanaṃ rājño dharmārtham evāsya bharaṇaṃ na vṛttyartham iti || 18.35 ||
dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ tasya karaṇe ‘doṣo ‘doṣaḥ
|| 18.36 ||
yasmād bhartavyas tasmād asya dharmatantrapīḍāyām – dharmaśabdenāgnihotrādaya ucyante, tantraśabdena vivāhaḥ, pīḍāśabdenātmapīḍā saptamīṃ cābhuktvety anenoktā | dharmavicchede vivāhasiddhaye ātmapīḍāyāṃ cety arthaḥ | tasya cauryasya karaṇe adoṣaḥ | daṇḍo nāstīty arthaḥ | rājña upekṣayā dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ satyāṃ cauryaṃ kṛtvā svadoṣād āgate caurye brāhmaṇasya daṇḍapātanaṃ na yuktam ity abhiprāyaḥ |
kecid vyācakṣate — śrutaśīlasaṃpannaś cet bhartavyatvād ācakṣīta | aśrotriyo ‘py ācakṣīta | yasmāt tasmād aśrotriyasyāpi dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ daṇḍo nāstīti |
apare vyācakṣate — bibhṛyāc chrotriyān iti yo ‘sau bhartavyaḥ, so ‘sminn aparādhe ‘pi bhartavya eva | hiśabda evaśabdārthe | tadanurūpo ‘rthaḥ asyāpi dātavya ity arthaḥ | aśrotriyam api na daṇḍayet, yatas tasyāpi tasminn aparādhe daṇḍo nāstīti | kutaḥ? dharmatantrapīḍāyām ity asya sūtrasyāśrotriyārthatvād ārambhasyeti || 18.36 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye aṣṭādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ekonaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcavidho dharmo vyākhyeyatayā prakrāntaḥ | tatra caturvidha uktaḥ | idānīṃ pañcamo dharmo naimittiko vaktavyaḥ | tadabhidhānārtham uktopasaṃhāraṃ karoti |
ukto varṇadharmaś cāśramadharmaś ca || 19.1 ||
kiṃ punar anena sūtreṇa kriyate | anuktaprakaraṇopanyāsārtham uktānuvādaḥ kriyata iti cet, ayuktam, anupasaṃhāre ‘py uttarasyābhidhātuṃ śakyatvāt | tarhi śrotṝṇām adhikākāṅkṣānivṛttiḥ kriyata iti cet, tad apy ayuktam, naimittikārambhād eva tatsiddheḥ | kiṃ ca varṇāśramagrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam, tatsaṃbandhenaiva dharmābhidhānāt, ukto dharma ity etāvatāpi tadupasaṃhāraḥ kṛto bhavatīti, kṛtsnopasaṃhāreṇa hi varṇāśramadharmaguṇadharmayor apy abhidhānaṃ kartavyam iti |
niṣprayojanatvāc cakārāv apy anarthakau | tasmād idam asamañjasaṃ sūtram iti |
atra brūmaḥ —
kecit tāvad varṇayanti – ādhāraṃ prasidhyartham iti | idam ayuktam, “varṇā āśramāś ca” [GDh 11.31] iti sūtrārambhād eva jñāyamānatvāt |
apare punar varṇayanti – śūdrasyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittābhāvārtham idam iti | tad apy ayuktam – “praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnām” [GDh 17.1] ity atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ dvijātipradarśanārtham uktaṃ na śūdrapradarśanārtham api | kutaḥ? smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha manuḥ |
etad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhyakṣaym aśeṣataḥ | [MDh 5.26]
iti śūdrasya bhakṣyābhakṣyādhikārābhāvāt prāyaścittābhāvo ‘rthasiddha iti |
anye varṇayanti – upanayanāt prāg api kaiścid varṇadharmair adhikārārtham idam iti | tad apy asamyak, “varṇā āśramāś ca” [GDh 11.31] ity atraivoktatvāt |
apare varṇayanti – varṇāśramadharmāṇāṃ striyā api prāptyartham iti | tad apy ayuktam, vivāhāt prāg anupanītā sā bhavatīti “varṇā āśramāś ca” [GDh 11.31] ity atra varṇagrahaṇenaiva siddham, uttarakālam apy upanītety āśramagrahaṇād āśramadharmair adhikriyata iti | tatrāha manuḥ |
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇām aupanāyanikaḥ smṛtaḥ | [MDh 2.67]
ityādi | atas tata eva siddhe kim anena sūtreṇa kriyata iti |
apara āhuḥ – uttarārtho ‘yam anuvādaḥ | yeṣāṃ varṇavibhāgena dharmā uktās teṣām eva vakṣyamāṇo ‘pīti | tataś ca śūdrasyāpi prāyaścittasiddhir iti | tad apy ayuktam, dvayam eva prāyaścittānāṃ nidānaṃ vihitākaraṇaṃ pratiṣiddhasevanaṃ ca | tatra vihitākaraṇe manur āha |
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇām ativartane |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittam abhojanam || iti | [MDh 11.203]
pratiṣiddhasevāyāṃ ca bṛhaspatir āha |
pratiṣiddhaṃ tu yaḥ kuryād brāhmaṇas tu viśeṣataḥ |
ekarātram anādeśa ādiṣṭe tu pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
tataś ca śūdrasyāpy arthasiddhatvāt na tatprāpaṇārtham idam ārabdham iti |
atrocyate — āśaṅkānivṛttyartham idam, upariṣṭād vaidikāni punaḥstomādīny udāhariṣyante tāni śūdrasya na bhavanti | atas tadvad itareṣām api tannivṛttir mā bhūd ity āśaṅkāpanīyata iti | vayo’vasthāviśeṣāpekṣayā vā prāyaścittasyānugrahārthaṃ varṇagrahaṇam | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo rogiṇa eva ca || iti |
balaviśeṣāpekṣayā vā | yathāha bhṛguḥ |
durbalasyāpi yat proktaṃ tad uktaṃ bālavṛddhayoḥ |
saṃkocavṛttinā vāpi tathā kālaviparyaye || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
deśaṃ kālaṃ vayaḥ śaktiṃ jñānaṃ buddhikṛtaṃ tathā |
abuddhikṛtam abhyāsaṃ jñātvā niṣkramaṇaṃ vadet || iti |
caśabdo ‘py avāntarajopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tadupasaṃgrahaś ca teṣām api prāyaścittārdhaprāptyarthaḥ | tataś ca mātṛjātivat parikalpyam | āśramagrahaṇaṃ prāyaścittānāṃ saṃbhavaprāpaṇārtham, yathaiva pāpakṣayārthaṃ prāyaścittānāṃ kalpanā kriyate evam āśramāpakṣayāpi | tataś ca japādīnāṃ saṃbhavato viśeṣaḥ, na hi homadānayoḥ bhukṣuke saṃbhavaḥ, anagnitvād aparigrahatvāc ca | tathā brahmacāriṇāṃ dāne | dvitīyaś cakāraḥ strīdharmānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | varṇāśramagrahaṇenaiva tasya siddheḥ punar apy anuvādaḥ prāyaścittakalpanārthaḥ | tatrodāhṛtaṃ ca bhṛguvacanam: “aśītir yasya varṣāṇi” ityādi | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
nāry apy akāmataḥ prāptā pāpam ardhaṃ samācaret |
arvāk tu dvādaśād varṣāt puruṣo ‘py ardhabhāg bhavet ||
iti || 19.1 ||
idānīṃ prāyaścittakāraṇam āha |
atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpyena karmaṇā lipyate
|| 19.2 ||
nanu ca śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanaṃ ca prāyaścittakāraṇam | tac coparitanasūtre pratipādayiṣyata ity anarthakam evaitat sūtram iti |
atrocyate – ātmānam ajñātvā yaḥ karma karoti tasyaiva karmasaṃbandhaḥ, na tv ātmajñāninaḥ vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane vā lepasaṃbandho ‘stīti anātmajñānina eva prāyaścittādhikārārtham idam | tathā cātharvopaniṣat |
bhidyate hṛdayagranthiḥ chidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ |
kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare || iti |
tataś cājñānina idam |
atha karmānuṣṭhānāntaram, khalv ity avadhāraṇe ayam eva puruṣa iti | ayam iti pratyakṣeṇa śarīriṇaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ vyapadiśati, avadhāraṇayā paramātmānaṃ nivartayati | tathā copaniṣat |
dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte |
tayor anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvatty anaśnann anyo abhicākaśīti || iti | [MuṇḍUp 3.1.1]
puri śayanāt puruṣaḥ | tathopaniṣat: “yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ parame vyoman” iti [TaitUp 2.4.1] | yāpyena garhyeṇa karmaṇā lipyate saṃbadhyate | yady apy ayaṃ dharmeṇāpi lipyate, tathāpi dharmasaṃbandhe prāyaścittābhāvād adharmasyaivopādānam | “khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpena karmanā lipyate” ity ukte kṛtasya karmaṇo ‘vaśyasaṃbandhāt athaśabdo ‘narthaka iti cen, na, anupalakṣite ‘pi prāyaścittanimitte japāder anuṣṭhānārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || iti | [MDh 5.21]
evaṃ cābuddhipūrvake ‘pi prāyaścittam astīti jñāpitam | tathā ca manuḥ |
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
kāmakārakṛte ‘py āhur eke śrutinidarśanāt || iti | [MDh 11.46]
tataḥ buddhipūrve gurutaram abuddhipūrve laghutaram iti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca vakṣyati “antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ amatyā dvādaśarātram” [GDh 23.33–34] ityādi | pūrvajanmakṛtasyāpi saṃbandhajñāpanārthaṃ karmagrahaṇam | tathā ca manuḥ |
iha duścaritaiḥ kecit kecit pūrvakṛtais tathā |
prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam || iti | [MDh 11.48]
tatraivāha “brahmahā kṣayarogitvam” [MDh 11.49] ityādi | evaṃ ca kṣayādiliṅgadarśane pūrvajanmakṛtasyāpi prāyaścittavidhānaṃ kartavyam ity evamartham upadeśaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | pāpena karmaṇā lipyata iti vaktavye yāpyagrahaṇaṃ dharmo ‘pi lokaninditaś ced adharmāya bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
parityajed arthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavarjitau |
dharmaṃ cāpy asukhodarkaṃ lokasaṃkruṣṭam eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.176]
yāpyeneti visamāsaḥ, yady apy ātmanā na kṛtaṃ tathāpi parair garhitatvād abhiśaṃsanamātreṇāpi prāyaścittī bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśaṃsya patanīyenopapanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayet | [VaDh 23.39] pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate | etenābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ | [VaDh 23.37]
iti || 19.2 ||
kathaṃ yāpyena karmaṇā lipyata iti cet, tatsādhanasevanāt | kiṃ tatsādhanam iti cet, tad āha |
yathaitad ayājyayājanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanam
iti || 19.3 ||
yathaitad iti dṛṣṭāntārtham | ayājyayājanam ayājyānāṃ patitānāṃ yājanam | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ laśunādīnāṃ bhakṣaṇam | avadyavadanam anṛtādīnāṃ vadanam | śiṣṭasya vihitasya saṃdhyopāsanāder akriyā akaraṇam | pratiṣiddhasya hiṃsādeḥ sevanam karaṇam | itikaraṇaṃ parisamāptyartham, etāvad adharmasādhanam iti |
nanu ca pratiṣiddhagrahaṇād evāyājyayājanāder api saṃgrahaṇāt punar abhidhānam anarthakam iti |
ucyate – anujñātānām apy eteṣām akāryatvajñāpanārthaṃ svaśabdenopdānam iti | tataś cāpy avidyasyāpy āpady anuṣṭhāne prāyaścittaṃ siddham, tatra hy abhyanujñātam “yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām” [GDh 7.4] iti | tathā hy abhakṣyāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇam api bhakṣyavargānupadiṣṭānām apy āpady anujñātam | tathā āpady anṛtam api smṛtyantare abhyanujñātam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
yasyoktau vāṅmanaḥpīḍā nityaṃ bhavati cet tataḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ vyāghrasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
ity anujñānasāmarthyāl laghutaraprāyaścittam iti | tad api prathamakalpitāt caturbhāgarūpam | yathāha uśanā: “āpadvihitaiḥ karmabhir āpadaṃ tīrtvā punas teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ caturbhāgaṃ kuryāt” iti || 19.3 ||
tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryān na kuryād iti mīmāṃsante
|| 19.4 ||
tasmin yāpyakarmasaṃbandhe tanniryātanārthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam uta na kartavyam iti mīmāṃsante vicārayanti brahmavādinaḥ || 19.4 ||
na kuryād ity āhuḥ || 19.5 ||
eke tāvad upapattibalān na kuryād ity āhuḥ || 19.5 ||
āpatkalpaviṣaye tāvad vidhīyamānatvād adoṣaḥ | anāpady api vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane vā prāyaścittam anarthakam | kutaḥ |
na hi karma kṣīyata iti || 19.6 ||
hiśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmāt kṛtaṃ karma phalapradānam antareṇa na kṣīyate prāyaścittaiḥ, tataś cātmasaṃsthaṃ karma ā vināśāt phalaṃ dadāti | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ |
yathā pṛthivyāṃ bījāni ratnāni ca payonidhau |
evam ātmani karmāṇi tiṣṭhanti prabhavanti ca || iti |
tatphalapradānottarakālaṃ svayam eva vinaśyati | bījavīryam ivāṅkure | tathā śrutiḥ: “puṇyo vai puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpena” iti | na ceha kevalaḥ sukhopabhogaḥ kasyacid upalabhyate yenānumīyetāsya prāyaścittenāśubhaṃ hīyata iti | yāni tu śrautāni prāyaścittāni tāni “bhinne juhoti skanne juhoti” ity evamādīni nimitte karmāṅgāni na doṣanirharaṇārthāni | loke ‘pi ca ghaṭasya chidrasya jatvādinā kāryasāmarthyādhānamātraṃ kriyate na pūrvavat pratyāpattiḥ | na cāśubhasyātmānaṃ vyāpyāvasthitasyāpanodaḥ śakyate kartum, udakavyāpina iva lavaṇasya | prāyaścittajanitena dharmeṇādharmo ‘bhibhūyate na tu vināśyate | auṣṇyenevodakaśītatvam, punar api śītabhāvadarśanāt || 19.6 ||
kuryād ity aparam || 19.7 ||
kuryāt prāyaścittam ity aparaṃ darśanam | na vidyate asmāt param ity aparaṃ siddhānta ity arthaḥ | āpatkalpadharmānuṣṭhāne tāvad vacanena vidhīyamānasya vacanāntareṇa prāyaścittam api darśitam | anāpady api prāyaścittam arthavad eva | katham? japādinimittaduḥkhopabhogenaiva kṣīyate, tatkaraṇatvāj japādinimittasya duḥkhānubhavasya, dīrghakāliko ‘py alpakālikena kṣīyata eva, yathā dīrghakāliko rogo loke ‘lpakālikavyathenauṣadhapānena kṣīyate |
kathaṃ punar idam avagamyate yathā prabhūtaduḥkhaphalo ‘dharmo ‘lpena duḥkhopabhogena kṣīyata iti | āgamād avagamyate | taṃ ca darśayati: “punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥsavanam āyantīti vijñāyate,” “vrātyastomeneṣṭvā,” “tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajate,” “agniṣṭutābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayed iti ca” [GDh 19.8–11] |
ayam asyābhiprāyaḥ – āgamagamya evāyam arthaḥ idaṃ dharmasādhanam idam adharmasādhanam iti | asmin kṛte adharmaḥ kṣīyata iti nopapattigamyaḥ, pratyakṣādipramāṇāntarāviṣayatvāt | tena yāvatya upapattaya uktāḥ prāyaścittākaraṇe, tāḥ sarvā āgamavirodhād eva nirasyante |
yad uktaṃ na hi ghaṭasya chidritasya jatvādipratipūraṇena pratyāpattir astīti,
tatrocyate – tatra hi yogyatāpādanam eva vivakṣitam, na hi ghaṭotpattir ity avirodhaḥ | prāyaścittenāpi cādharmakṣayadvāreṇa puruṣasya yogyataiva kriyata iti | yat tūdakavyāpilavaṇavad ātmavyāpyadharmāpanayanam aśakyam iti, tad vastrarāgasyeva kṣārādibhir apanayanam ity aviruddham | yas tv abhibhava uktaḥ, asāv api “sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati” ity anenaiva śrutivacanena nirastaḥ | yady abhibhavamātraṃ kriyate tadā taratīti vācoyuktyanupapattiḥ | yat tu karmāṅgāni prāyaścittānīti, tan na sarvatra, yatra hi nimittaṃ vihitākaraṇaṃ pratiṣiddhasevanaṃ vā tatra taddoṣanirghātāya, yathā “tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajate” [GDh 19.10] iti | yatra tad ubhayaṃ nāsti tatra nimitte karmāṅgam iti grāhyam |
nanu ca yady ayam arthaḥ āgamena sādhyate tadā ekam evodāharaṇaṃ yuktam, udāharaṇabahutve prayojanaṃ vaktavyam iti |
ucyate viṣayabhedapradarśanārtho ‘nekodāharaṇopanyāsaḥ | punaḥstomodāharaṇena tāvad etat pradarśayati apratigrāhyapratigrahadoṣo naśyatīti, “apratigrāhyāt tu bahu pratigṛhya punaḥstomena yajeta” iti śruteḥ | apratigrāhyapratigrahavacanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇāvadyavadanayor api pradarśanārtham | “abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avadyavadanaṃ punaḥstomena tarati” iti śrutyantaradarśanāt || 19.7 ||
tataś ca tair yadā sarvakarmānadhikṛtā bhavanti tadā –
punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyantīti vijñāyate
|| 19.8 ||
savanaśabdena karmocyate | punas tadāpattiḥ punar api śrautasmārtakarmayogyo bhavatīty arthaḥ | āyantīti bahuvacanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇāvadyavadanapradarśanārtham | vijñāyata iti śrutisaṃsūcanārtham || 19.8 ||
vrātyastomaiś ceṣṭvā || 19.9 ||
cakāraḥ punaḥ savanam āyantīty asyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | vrātyastomair iti bahuvacanaṃ catvāro vrātyastomāḥ śrutiprasiddhā iti | vrātyā nāma atītopanayanakālāḥ, taiḥ yakṣyamāṇatvād vrātyastomāḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ:
patitasāvitrīka uddālakavrataṃ caret | dvau māsau yāvakena vartayet māsaṃ payasārdhmāsam āmikṣayāṣṭarātraṃ ghṛtena ṣaḍrātram acācitaṃ haviṣyaṃ bhuñjīta trirātram abbhakṣaṇam ahorātram upavaset | aśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ vā gacchet | vrātyastomena vā yajeta | iti | [VaDh 11.76–79]
vrātyagrahaṇam apy upapātakoplakṣaṇam, “apāṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛbrahmojjhatanmantrakṛdavakīrṇipatitasāvitrīkeṣūpapātakam” [GDh 21.11] iti vrātyasyopapātakamadhye pāṭhāt, “vrātyastomenopapātakaṃ vinaśyati” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | evaṃ cānenopapātakadoṣā vinaśyantīti pradarśitam || 19.9 ||
tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajata iti ca || 19.10 ||
sarvaṃ pāpmānam ity anena vihitākaraṇe pratiṣiddhasevane ca yat pāpaṃ tasya vināśaṃ darśayati | brahmahatyām api tarati mahāpātakasyāpi brahmahatyāyāḥ sarvādharmagarīyastvajñāpanārthaṃ pṛthag abhidhānam, anyathā pratiṣiddhasevanena gṛhyamāṇatvāt na vaktavyam | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
brahmahatyāśvamedhābhyāṃ na paraṃ puṇyapāpayoḥ ||
iti || 19.10 ||
agniṣṭutābhiśasyamānaṃ yājayed iti ca || 19.11 ||
agniṣṭududāharaṇena dehāntarādharmanimittadoṣakṣayaṃ darśayati, abhiśaṃsanaṃ hi na vinā puruṣāpacāreṇa bhavatīti | cakāraḥ vijñāyata ity asyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | itikaraṇaḥ pṛthak pṛthak śrutyantarasūcanārtham | evaṃ hi sarvatra viṣaye ‘pi śrutidarśanāt prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam eva |
nanu ca vacanagamye ‘rthe ko ‘yaṃ vicāraḥ karaṇākaraṇayoḥ prāyaścittasya – sa ca nopapadyate | yadi kriyate “upanayanaṃ brāhmaṇasya” ity ataḥ prabhrti kartavyam iti |
ucyate – mahāpātakānām abhyāse samuccaye ca prāyaścittābhāvajñāpanartho ‘yaṃ vicāraḥ | mahāpātakānām abhyāse samuccaye ca prāyaścittaṃ na kartavyam, “na hi karma kṣīyate” iti śrutidarśanāt, pṛthak pṛthak kartavyam iti | evaṃ cānyeṣām upapātakādīnām abhyāse samuccaye ca prāyaścittaṃ bhavati |
eke bruvate – prāyaścittastutyartho ‘yaṃ vicāra iti |
apare bruvate – akāmakṛta eva prāyaścittaṃ na kāmakrta iti |
tad ayuktam – “antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdo ‘matyā dvādaśarātraḥ” [GDh 23.33], “jñānapūrvaṃ cet trirātram” [GDh 20.9], “doṣavati ca karmaṇy abhisaṃdhipūrve” [GDh 25.10] ityādibhir virodhaprasaṅgāt |
anye bruvate – kāmakṛtasya gurutvakhyāpanārtho ‘yaṃ vicāra iti |
tad ayuktam, “atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣaḥ” [GDh 19.2] iti atraiva jñāpitatvāt || 19.11 ||
tasya niṣkrayaṇāni japas tapo homa upavāso dānam
|| 19.12 ||
tasya yāpyakarmaṇaḥ niṣkrayaṇāni saṃśodhanāni, yathaiva loke kāṃsyādīnāṃ bhasmādīni saṃśodhanāni evam ete adharmāṇām iti | tasyety asati saṃdehaḥ syāt kiṃ punaḥstomādīny uta japādīnīti | japo vakṣyamāṇasyopaniṣadāder abhyāsaḥ prakāśopāṃśumānasalakṣaṇaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
vidhiyajñāj japayajño viśiṣṭo daśabhir guṇaiḥ |
upāṃśuḥ syāc chataguṇaḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.85]
tapo vakṣyamāṇaṃ brahmacaryādi | homa ājyāder dravyasya vyāhṛtyādimantrapūrvo ‘gnau prekṣepaḥ | upavāsaḥ indriyāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃyamaḥ | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
vyāvṛttiś caiva doṣebhyaḥ sahavāso guṇair iha |
upavāsaḥ sa vijñeyo na śarīrasya śoṣaṇam || iti |
yat tu tapomadhye anāśakaṃ paṭhanti tad atyantabhaktaparityāgaḥ kleṣakaraiḥ sahopadeśāt anāśaka ity uktatvāc ca | dānaṃ hiraṇyāder dravyasya pātre pratipādanam | visamāsa ekaikasya niṣkrayaṇatvajñāpanārthaḥ || 19.12 ||
tatra tāvaj japasvarūpam āha |
upaniṣado vedāntāḥ sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā madhūny aghamarṣaṇam atharavaśiro rudrāḥ puruṣasūktaṃ rājanarauhiṇe sāmanī bṛhadrathantare puruṣagatir mahānāmnyo mahāvairājaṃ mahādivākīrtyaṃ jyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamad bahiṣpavamānaṃ kūśmāṇḍāni pāvamānyaḥ sāvitrī ceti pāvanāni || 19.13 ||
upaniṣado rahasyabrāhmaṇāni | vedāntā āraṇyakāni | sarvacchandaḥsu saṃhitā, na padāni kramo vā | sarvacchandaḥsv iti viśeṣaṇaṃ na svaśākhāyām eveti jñāpanārtham | madhūni “madhu vātā ṛtāyate” [RV 1.90.6] ityādīni madhuśabdasaṃyutāni | aghamarṣaṇaṃ “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” ityādi | atharvaśiro ‘tharvavede prasiddham | rudrāḥ “namas te rudra manyave” [VS 16.1] ityādirudraśabdayuktāḥ | puruṣasūktam “sahasraśīrṣāḥ puruṣaḥ” [RV 10.90] ityādi | rājarauhiṇe “indraṃ naro nemadhitā havante” [RV 7.27.1] ity asyām ṛci gīte sāmanī | yajuṣor apy evaṃnāmatvāt sāmagrahaṇam | bṛhat “tvām iddhi havāmahe” [RV 6.46.1] ity asyām ṛci gītam | rathantaram “abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ” [RV 7.32.22] ity asyām ṛci gītam | puruṣagatiḥ “aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya” [RV 7.27.1] ity asyām ṛci gīyete | “tvām iddhi havāmahe” [RV 6.46.1] ity ābhyāṃ bṛhat | “abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ” [RV 7.32.22] ity atra rathaṃtaram | “aham asmi prathamajā ṛtasya” [TaitBr 2.8.8.1] ity asyāṃ gītam | mahānāmnyaḥ “vidā maghavan” [AitĀ 4.15] ity asyāṃ sāma | mahāvairājam “pibā somam” [RV 6.17.1] ity asyām ṛci gītam | mahādivākīrtyam “vibhrāḍ bṛhat pibat” [RV 10.170.1] ity asyāṃ sāma | jyeṣṭhasāmnām anyatamat “śaṃ no devīḥ” [RV10.9.4] “citraṃ devānām” [RV 1.115.1] ity anayoḥ tṛceṣv ekaṃ sāma | bahiṣpavamānam “upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ” [RV 9.11.1] ityādyāḥ | kūśmāṇḍāni “yad devā devahelanam” [VS 20.14] ityādīni yajūṃṣi | pāvamānyaḥ pavamānaliṅgāni “svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā” [RV 9.1.1] ityādīni | sāvitrī prasiddhā | caśabdo ‘nuktopasaṃgrahārthaḥ, tataś ca sāmādīni smṛtyantaroktāni draṣṭavyāni | itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī evaṃprakārā eveti | tathā ca smṛtyantarapaṭhitānāṃ śivasaṃkalpādīnām eva grahaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, na cāpaṭhitānāṃ śrīsūktādīnām | visamāsaḥ ekaikasyaiva pāvanārthaḥ |
nanu ca japasya niṣkrayatvenoktatvāt tatsādhanatvāc caiṣāṃ pāvanatvaṃ siddham evety anarthakaṃ pāvanagrahaṇam |
ucyate – nimittād anyatrāpy eṣāṃ pavitratvajñāpanārhaṃ pāvanagrahaṇam iti || 19.13 ||
idānīm japādipravṛttasyāhāraniyamam āha |
payovratatā śākabhakṣatā phalabhakṣatā prasṛtayāvako hiraṇyaprāśanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ somapānam iti medhyāni || 19.14 ||
payovratatā kṣīrāhāratā | vratagrahaṇād upavāsanyāyo draṣṭavyaḥ | śākaṃ prasiddham | phalaṃ āmraphalādi | prasṛtayāvakaḥ, prakaraṇāt prasṛtayāvakāśanam iti vaktavye evam abhidhānaṃ smṛtyantaroktavidhyupasaṃgrahaṇārtham | tathāha uśanā:
snātaḥ śucir bhūtvoditeṣu nakṣatreṣu tāmrabhājane prasṛtayāvakaṃ śrapayet yathā yavāgūr bhavati | tasmin śrapaṇakāle rakṣāṃ kuryāt “namo rudrāya bhūtānāṃ pataye parvatānāṃ pataye tvam imāṃ rakṣasva” ity anena mantreaṇa | tataḥ praṇavenāvaropya “devasya tvā savituḥ” ityādinotpūya sāvitryābhidhārya tato ‘bhimantrayet:
yavo ‘si dhānyarājo ‘si vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā durvicintitam |
alakṣmī kālakaṇṭhī ca sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
mahāpātakasaṃyuktaṃ dāruṇaṃ rājakilbiṣam |
strībālavṛttadharmaṃ ca sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
suvarṇastainyam avratyam ayājyasya ca yājanam |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
śvasūkarāvadhūtaṃ ca kākādyucchiṣṭam eva ca |
mātāpitror aśuśrūṣāṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ śavasūtake |
corasyānnaṃ tathābhakṣyaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
ity etaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ, tato “brahmā devānām” iti prāśya, “prāṇāya tvā” ityādibhis tato yathākāmaṃ sarvaṃ prāśnīyāt ṣaḍrātraṃ tato niyamātikramajāt pratiṣiddhasevanajāt abhakṣyabhakṣaṇajād upapātakajāc ca sarvasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | saptarātraṃ pītvā bhrūṇahatyāṃ gurutalpasuvarṇastainyasurāpānādi punāti | ekādaśarātraṃ pītvā pūrvakṛtāni pāpāni nudati | ekaviṃśatirātraṃ pītvā gaṇān paśyati gaṇādhipatiṃ paśyati vidyāḥ paśyati vidyādhipatiṃ paśyati | evam ahar ahar anatyāhāro yavāgūṃ prāśnīyāt |
iti |
hiraṇyādīni prasiddhāni | itikaraṇena prakāravācinānyeṣāṃ pañcagavyādīnām api prāśanaṃ draṣṭavyam | punaḥ punaḥ kriyāpadābhyāsaḥ ekaikasya medhyatvajñāpanārthaḥ || 19.14 ||
idānīṃ japādīnāṃ sthānam āha |
sarve śiloccayāḥ sarvāḥ sravantyaḥ puṇyā hradās tīrthāni ṛṣinivāsagoṣṭhapariṣkandā iti deśāḥ || 19.15 ||
sarve śiloccayāḥ parvatāḥ, upariṣṭāt puṇyā ity upādānād apuṇyānām api grahaṇārthaṃ sarvagrahaṇam | puṇyāḥ kailāsādayaḥ, apuṇyāḥ avivakṣitāḥ | puṇyā nadya gaṅgādayaḥ, apuṇyāḥ kṣudranadyaḥ | puṇyā hradāḥ aśoṣyāḥ śrīpuṣkarādayaḥ | tīrthāni kanyātīrthādīni | ṛṣinivāsāḥ vasiṣṭhādyāśramāḥ | goṣṭhaṃ prasiddham | pariṣkandaḥ devālayaḥ,
surāṇāṃ ca pariṣkandaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca vanaspatim |
kuryāt pradakṣiṇaṃ nityaṃ vārāhaṃ vāmanaṃ tathā ||
iti śiṣṭasmaraṇāt | itikaraṇam anyeṣāṃ puṇyāraṇyādīnām upalakṣaṇam | evaṃ ca naimiśāraṇyādayo draṣṭavyāḥ | visamāsa uktaprayojanaḥ || 19.15 ||
idānīṃ tapaḥsvarūpam āha |
brahmacaryaṃ satyavacanaṃ savaneṣūdakopa-sparśanam ārdravastratādhaḥśāyitānāśaka iti tapāṃsi
|| 19.16 ||
brahmacaryādi prasiddham | savanaśabdena pūrvāhṇamadhyaṃdināparāhṇā ucyante, bahuvacanāt | itikaraṇaṃ prāṇāyāmādīnām upasaṃgrahārtham | tathāha kavaṣaḥ |
upasthapāyumanovākśrotratvagghrāṇacakṣurjihvākṛtānāṃ pāpānām ahar ahaḥ sāyaṃ prātas trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | śūdrānnabhojanatatstrīgamanatatpratigraheṣu samucciteṣu saptāhaṃ sapta prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | pṛthak pṛthak tryaham | abhakṣyābhojyāpeyālehyānāṃ prāśane apuṇyānāṃ madhumāṃsaghṛtatailalākṣālavaṇa-dadhikṣārakṣīravarjitānāṃ vikraye ca dvādaśāhaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | upapātakeṣv ardhamāsaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | pātakasamānāṃ caturmāsaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | atha patanīyeṣu ṣaṇmāsaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | gurutalpasamānāṃ saṃvatsaraṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | athāpy udāharanti |
savyāhṛtikāḥ sapraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api brūṇahanaṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ || iti |
evam anyeṣām ājasrikāṇāṃ japādīnāṃ kālaviśeṣo vijñātavyaḥ | iti |
tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
prāṇāyāmāḥ brahmacaryaṃ maunaṃ caiva nirāśanam |
ity etat tapaso rūpaṃ dhyānaṃ ca parameṣṭhinaḥ || iti |
homāḥ kūśmāṇḍagaṇahomādayaḥ smṛtyantare prasiddhāḥ | yathāha uśanā |
agamyāgamane suvarṇasteye nikṣepaharaṇe garhitānnādibhojane abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe parvamaithune rajasvalāgamane ‘yājyayājane paurṇamāsyāṃ luptakeśanakhasmaśruḥ snātaḥ tataḥ prabhṛti kūśmāṇḍair juhuyāc caturbhiḥ sakṛd ghṛtaṃ saptāhaṃ dvādaśāham ardhamāsaṃ māsam | iti |
yathāha vyāghraḥ:
pātakopapātakamahāpātakānām anyeṣāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ pāpānāṃ pūrvaṃ kṛtānām api gaṇahomaṃ kuryāt sakṛt kṛtenāpi sarvaṃ tarati kiṃ punar abhyāseneti | athāpy udāharanti |
vaiśvānarīṃ vrātapatīṃ pavitreṣṭiṃ tathaiva ca |
ṛtāv ṛtau prayuñjānaḥ punāti daśapauruṣam ||
iti vijñāyate vijñāyate | iti |
prasiddhatvād evātrānupādānam | tathaivopavāsasyāpi || 19.16 ||
idānīṃ dānasādhanaṃ darśayati |
hiraṇyaṃ gaur vāso ‘śvo bhūmis tilā ghṛtam annam iti deyāni || 19.17 ||
hiraṇyādīni prasiddhāni | itikaraṇaṃ ratnādīnām upasaṃgrahārtham || 19.17 ||
kālam idānīm āha |
saṃvatsaraḥ ṣaṇmāsāś catvāras trayo dvāv ekaś caturviṃśatyaho dvādaśāhaḥ ṣaḍahas tryaho ‘horāra iti kālāḥ || 19.18 ||
itikaraṇāt kevalā rātrir ahaś ca gṛhyate || 19.18 ||
idānīṃ pratipāpam ānantyāt prāyaścittopadeśo na śakyata ity ata āha |
etāny evānādeśe vikalpena kriyeran, enaḥsu guruṣu gurūṇi laghuṣu laghūni || 19.19 ||
etāny eva japādīni | evety avadhāraṇārthaḥ | yadi hi “etāny anādeśe” ity etāvad ucyeta tato yatra svaśabdena prāyaścittāni nopadiṣṭāni tatraiva japādīnāṃ kalpanā syāt | evakārād upadiṣṭe ‘py abhyāsāt pratipūraṇasiddhiḥ | anādeśo yatrānyat prāyaścittaṃ nopadiṣṭam ity arthaḥ | anādeśa ity asati upadiṣṭa eva taiḥ saha bhavatīti | vikalpena vividhābhiḥ kalpanābhir ity arthaḥ | kvacij japa eva kvacit tapa evetyādi kriyeran | etasminn asati yathopadiṣṭānāṃ japādīnāṃ dvisamuccyatrisamuccayādikalpanaṃ na syāt | sati tu tasmin kvacij japaḥ kvacit tapaś ca dānaṃ cetyādikalpanāsiddhiḥ | kva punar eṣāṃ kalpanety ata āha: enasi pāpe guruṇi gurūṇi mahānti prāyaścittāni laguni alpe laghūni alpāni prāyaścittāni | katham adharmasya gauravalāghavaṃ vijñāyata iti ced, ucyate – abhisaṃdhyanabhisaṃdhyabhyāsādibhiḥ | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptadoṣaphalam, saha saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ, evam anyeṣv api doṣavatsu karmasu, tathā puṇyaphaleṣu, yathā karmābhyāsaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.29.2–5, 18] iti | yatra tu pratiṣedha eva kevalaḥ, tatrāpi rājadaṇḍāpekṣayā, smṛtyantare vā nindārthavādaphalavipākakathanena pīḍādyatiśayāpekṣayā vā gurutvādi veditavyam iti || 19.19 ||
kṛcchātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittaṃ sarvaprāyaścittam || 19.20 ||
kṛcchrādisvarūpaṃ vakṣyati | sarvaprāyaścittaṃ na kevalam anādeśe ‘pīty arthaḥ | sāmarthyādyapekṣayā guruṇi laghuni vā prakāśe rahasye ceti | cāndrāyaṇasya pṛthagabhidhānaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchryoḥ sthāne cāndrāyaṇasyaiva kevalasya kalpanārtham | evaṃ ca tasyāpi sthāne tayor api kalpanāsiddhiḥ | vyastānām api kalpanārtham itikaraṇam | tataś ca kṛcchrātikṛcchrau saṃbhūya sarvaprāyaścittam, kevalaṃ cāndrāyaṇam api sarvaprāyaścittam iti | ekavacanaṃ samastānām api kalpanārtham | kecid itikaraṇaṃ prakārasyāpy upasaṃgrahārtham iti vyācakṣate | yathāha manuḥ |
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.215]
prājāpatyacāndrāyaṇayoḥ sthāne parāko draṣṭavyaḥ | kutaḥ? tulyadaṇḍadarśanāt | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
trayastriṃśat paṇānāṃ tu prājāpatyasya dāpayet |
śataṃ sapādaṃ dāpyaṃ syāt tathā sāntapanasya tu ||
atikṛcchrasya tu tathā saptaṣaṣṭiś catuḥśatam |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrayoś caiva paṇapañcaśataṃ tathā ||
dāpayec ca parākasya paṇapañcaśataṃ tathā |
trayastriṃśādhikaṃ daṇḍam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti |
prāyaścittaṃ niṣkrayaṇam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati sarvaniṣkrayaṇam iti vaktavyam ity cet, atrocyate – yan niṣkrayaṇārthaṃ kriyate tasya prāyaścittam iti saṃjñā bhavatīti jñāpanārtham | anvarthasaṃjñārthaṃ gurusaṃjñākaraṇam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ saṃyama ucyate |
tapaḥ saṃyamasaṃyuktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ ||
iti || 10.20 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye ekonaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
viṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
yāpyakarmasaṃbandhe prāyaścittavidhir uktaḥ | tatra yadi pitā yāpyakarmasaṃbandhī bhavati tadā putraḥ kiṃ kuryād ity ata āha |
tyajed pitaraṃ rājaghātakaṃ śūdrayājakaṃ śūdrārthayājakaṃ vedaviplāvakaṃ bhrūṇahanaṃ yaś cāntāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvased antāvasāyinyām || 20.1 ||
tyajet vakṣyamāṇena vidhinā | pitaram iti pitṛvacanād daṇḍāpūpikayā putrādīnām arthasiddhas tyāgaḥ | rājaghātakaṃ rājānaṃ nyāyavṛttam abhicāreṇa yo hinasti sa tyaktavyaḥ | evaṃ cānyāyavṛttam abhicāreṇa pramāpayato nāsti tyāgaḥ | tathā purāṇa uktam |
cāturvidaṃ hataṃ tena yo hinasti mahīpatim |
bahiṣkāryaḥ sa sarveṇa pāpakarmā na mantrataḥ || iti |
yat tu vakṣyati “na karhicit mātāpitror avṛttiḥ” [GDh 21.15] iti, tad anyatra draṣṭavyam | śūdrayājakaṃ śūdrasya yo niṣādasthapatīṣṭiṃ kārayati, tasyeṣṭividhānāt | evaṃ tarhi bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ sarvasya codanāvad draṣṭavyam | śūdrārthayajakaṃ ya śūdrād artham adhigamya yāgaṃ karoti | tathāha manuḥ: “ye śūdrād adhigamyārtham” [MDh 11.42] iti | vedaviplāvakam anadhyāpyādhyāpakam | bhrūṇahā brahmahā, tadgrahaṇaṃ patitopalakṣaṇam | yathāha manuḥ |
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍair bāndhavair bahiḥ |
nindite ‘hani sāyāhne jñātyṛtviggurusaṃnidhau || iti | [MDh 11.182]
“yaś cāntāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvaset” | antāvasāyinaḥ cāṇḍālādayaḥ | yathāha aṅgirāḥ |
caṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehakas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva saptaite ‘ntāvasāyinaḥ || iti |
taiḥ sahaikatrāvasthāne vasati, cakārāc cākrikādibhiś ca | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
cākrikaḥ pulkaso jambhaḥ kharajīvī pradīpakaḥ |
ete ca śūdraprabhavāḥ aspṛśyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || iti |
antāvasāyinyāṃ saṃvased iti saṃbandhaḥ, tayā saha maithunam ācaratīty arthaḥ | atha kim eteṣu rājaghātakatvādiṣu nimitteṣu satsu patitatvaṃ bhavati uta na bhavatīti saṃdehaḥ |
kiṃ cātaḥ? yadi tāvad bhavati tato “brahmahasurāpa” [GDh 21.1] ity atraivaiṣāṃ pāṭhaṃ kṛtvā “tyajet patitaṃ pitaram” ity etāvad eva vaktavyam, evaṃ ceha bhrūṇahanam iti na vaktavyaṃ bhaviṣyati | atha na bhavati, tatas tyāgo na yujyate | smṛtyantare ‘pi patitatvasaṃyoga eva tyāgavidhir uktaḥ | tathā ca darśito mānavaślokaḥ: “patitasyodakaṃ kāryam” [MDh 11.182] ityādi |
atrocyate – patitatvaṃ tāvat bhavaty eṣu nimitteṣu | tathāpi pātakimadhye na paṭhitaṃ prāyaścittalāghavārtham | bhrūṇahagrahaṇaṃ tatsāmyāpādanena tatsamaprāyaścittajñāpanārtham | yatra samīkriyate tatra hīnatāpi gamyate yathā rājasamo mantrīti | eṣāṃ madhye keṣāṃcit prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikam” [GDh 22.13] ityādi | tathā “antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ” [GDh 23.33] ityādi | yeṣāṃ nopalabyate teṣāṃ sarvaprāyaścittaṃ kalpyam | evaṃ sarvatra samīkaraṇaviṣaye sarvaprāyaścittam eva draṣṭavyam | evaṃ tarhi tulyaprāyaścittatvāt kauṭasākṣyādīnām iha pāṭhaḥ prasajyeteti cet, ucyate – eteṣv eva patiteṣu pitā tyaktavyo na tv anyatreti bhedārambhaḥ | atra rājaghātādiṣv abuddhipūrveṣv api patitatvaṃ draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? sahopadiṣṭānāṃ madhye ekasyābuddhipūrvaviṣaye ‘pi patitatvadarśanāt | yathāha manuḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175]
iti || 20.1 ||
athedānīṃ tasya tyāgavidhir ucyate |
tasya vidyāgurūn yonisaṃbandhāṃś ca saṃnipātya sarvāṇy udakādīni pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ || 20.2 ||
tasya tyājyasya nātmanaḥ vidyāguravaḥ ācāryagurūpādhyāyāḥ bahuvacanaprayogāt trīn api | yonisaṃbandhāḥ mātulamātāmahādayaḥ | tatrāpi bahuvacanm uktaprayojanam | cakārād ṛtvigyājyādayaś ca | saṃnipātya ekīkṛtya ayam evaṃkarmā tyakṣyāmy aham enam iti prakāśya putrādayo jñātayo jīvata eva sarvāṇy udakadānaśrāddhādīni pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ || 20.2 ||
pātraṃ cāsya viparyasyeyuḥ || 20.3 ||
