asya tyājyasya pātraṃ ca viparyasyeyuḥ ta eva jñātayaḥ | viparyāso ‘nudakīkaraṇam | udakadānena samuccayārthaś cakāraḥ | śrāddhakarmānantaraṃ mā bhūd iti tataś codakadānaṃ kṛtvā pātraviparyāsaṃ ca kṛtvā śrāddhaṃ kuryād iti draṣṭavyam || 20.3 ||
kathaṃ viparyasyeyur ity ata āha |
dāsaḥ karmakaro vāvakarād amedhyapātram ānīya dāsīghaṭāt pūrayitvā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ padā viparyasyed amum anudakaṃ karomīti nāmagrāham || 20.4 ||
dāso dhvjābhṛtādi | yathāha manuḥ[66] |
dhvajābhṛto bhaktadāso gṛhajaḥ krītadattrimau |
paitṛko daṇḍadāsaś ca saptaite dāsayonayaḥ || iti | [MDh 8.415]
karmakaro bhṛtakaḥ | saṃbhavataś cānayor vikalpaḥ | avakarāt varcasthānāt aśuci pātraṃ kiṃcid ādāya dāsī paricāriṇī tasyā ghaṭāt pūrayitvā | anenaivānyatra dāsīghaṭasyāśucitvaṃ draṣṭavyam | dakṣiṇāmukho bhūtvā padā pādena viparyasyet apasavyam utsṛjet “amuṃ devadattam anudakaṃ karomi” iti mantram uktvā | amum ity anenaiva nāmagrahaṇasya siddhatvāt nāmagrāham iti vispaṣṭārtham, na tūpāṃśu japed iti || 20.4 ||
taṃ sarve ‘nvālebheran prācīnāvītino muktaśikhāḥ
|| 20.5 ||
taṃ dāsaṃ karmakaraṃ vā, sarve sapiṇḍā na kevalaṃ putraḥ, anvālabheran saṃspṛśeyur ity arthaḥ | prācīnāvītinaḥ apasavyayajñopavītinaḥ | muktaśikhāḥ śikhāṃ vikīrya | anenaiva jñāpakenānyasminn ācamanādau śubhakarmaṇi muktaśikhasyāyogyatvaṃ draṣṭavyam || 20.5 ||
vidyāguravo yonisaṃbandhāś ca vīkṣeran || 20.6 ||
na tu spṛśeyur ity arthaḥ | cakārād ṛtvigādayaś ca || 20.6 ||
apa upaspṛśya grāmaṃ praviśanti || 20.7 ||
evam idaṃ karma kṛtvā tataḥ snātvā gṛhān gacchanti | ekasminn evāhani śrāddhādīni gamyante | grāmapraveśopadeśāc ca bahir idaṃ kartavyam iti || 20.7 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ tena saṃbhāṣya tiṣṭhed ekarātraṃ japan sāvitrīm ajñānapūrvam || 20.8 ||
atas tyāgād ūrdhvaṃ tena tyaktena saha saṃbhāṣya saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā | ajñānād idaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tiṣṭhed ity ekavacanāt jñātyāder anyo ‘pi, adhikārāt jñātaya eva | ekarātram ahorātraṃ na bhuñjītety arthaḥ | sāvitrīṃ japan || 20.8 ||
jñānapūrvaṃ cet trirātram || 20.9 ||
yas tu buddhipūrvam anena saṃbhāṣeta sa trirātram evaṃvidhoktaṃ[67] vidhim ātiṣṭhet | tāvac caitad evaṃ yāvad asāv akṛtaprāyaścittaḥ | kāryāntarasaṃbhāṣaṇam idaṃ na pathi praśnādau | tatra parāśaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
kṣute niṣṭhīvite caiva dantaspṛṣṭe tathānṛte |
patitānāṃ bhāṣaṇe ca dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || [PārSm 12.19]
iti || 20.9 ||
yas tu prāyaścittena śuddhyet tasmin śuddhe śātakumbhamayaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamād dhradāt pūrayitvā sravantībhyo vā tata enam apa upasparśayeyuḥ || 20.10 ||
tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇārthaḥ | yas tu prāyaścittena śuddhyet na rājadaṇḍena | evaṃ ca bruvatā anyatra rājadaṇḍasyāpi śuddhikāraṇatvam uktam | tataś cānyatra rājadaṇḍitasyāpi śuddhir veditavyā | tasmin śuddhe lokasamakṣaṃ caritaprāyaścittaḥ ity arthaḥ | śātakumbhamayaṃ pātraṃ sauvarṇapātram atiśayena puṇyād dhradād aśoṣyaprāyāt pūrayitvā sravantībhyo vā sadāpravāhābhyo nadībhyaḥ | bahuvacanāt bahvībhyaḥ | saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ | tatas tasmāt pātrād enaṃ kṛtaprāyaścittaṃ snāpayeyur vakṣyamāṇair mantraiḥ | sāmarthyāt pūrvaṃ parityaktāraḥ || 20.10 ||
athāsmai tat pātraṃ dadyus tat pratigṛhya japec chāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntaṃ śivam antarikṣaṃ yo rocanas tam iha gṛhṇāmīty etair yajurbhiḥ pāvamānībhis taratsamandībhiḥ || 20.11 ||
atha anantaram asmai snātāya tat pūrṇapātraṃ dadyuḥ | tat pratigṛhyemaṃ mantraṃ japet | kair mantriḥ? śānteti vakṣyati | etair evoktaiḥ śāntā dyaur ityādibhir yajurbhiḥ | pavamānībhiḥ “pavamānaḥ suvarjanaḥ” ity aṣṭābhiḥ taratsamandībhiś catasṛbhiḥ || 20.11 ||
kūśmāṇḍaiś cājyaṃ juhuyāt || 20.12 ||
juhuyād ity ekavacananirdeśād eka eva juhuyāt, na sarve jñātyādayaḥ | teṣāṃ madhye yogyasyedaṃ draṣṭavyam | kūśmāṇḍaiḥ “yad devā devaheḍanam” ityādibhir ājyaṃ juhuyāt | caśabdān mahāvyāhṛtibhiś ca || 20.12 ||
hiraṇyaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyād gāṃ vācāryāya ca
|| 20.13 ||
homānte suvarṇaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyād guṇātiśayayuktāya, brāhmaṇasyaiva pratigrahādhikāre brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt | kṛtaprāyaścittasyedam, na homakartuḥ | gāṃ vā ācāryāya ca | cakāro hiraṇyādhikārārthaḥ | ācāryāya homakartre gāṃ vā hiraṇyaṃ vā dadyāt | etasya dvayaṃ mukhyam, gaur hiraṇyam api draṣṭavyam, samavikalpodapeśāt || 20.13 ||
yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ mṛtaḥ śudhyet
|| 20.14 ||
yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittam upadiṣṭam, yatamāno mriyeta, asau maraṇād eva śudhyati | jñāyamānam apy etad ucyate – pratyuddhāre saty api sapiṇḍatāpratiṣedhārtham | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittam adhyavasya prāyaścittamadhye yadi mriyeta tadāpi śudhyati viśeṣataḥ kṛtaprāyaścitto mṛta iti | tathāha vyāsaḥ |
yatamānaḥ sadā dharme mriyate yadi madhyataḥ |
prāpnoty eva hi tat sarvaṃ atra me nāsti saṃśayaḥ ||
iti || 20.14 ||
sarvāṇy eva tasminn udakādīni pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ
|| 20.15 ||
sarvāṇy evodakaśrāddhādīni yāvanti pretasaṃskārakarmāṇi tāni sarvāṇi tasmin yo maraṇāntikaprāyaścittakaḥ pūto yaś ca pratyuddhṛto yaś cāntarā mriyate | arthād etadvyatiriktasya na kāryāṇi | sarvaśabdād āśaucam api sapiṇḍāḥ kuryuḥ || 20.15 ||
etad eva śāntyudakaṃ sarveṣūpapātakeṣūpapātakeṣu
|| 20.16 ||
etad eva yadantaram uktaṃ śāntyudakam iti śāntā dyaur ityādibhir abhimantritaṃ snānāya yaj jalam uktaṃ tad eva sarveṣu, sarvaśabdān mahāpātakasameṣv apy upapātakeṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣu tatprāyaścittādāv etad api kartavyam iti || 20.16 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye viṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ekaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
patitānāṃ tyāgavidhir uktaḥ | ke punas te ity ata āha |
brahmahasurāpagurutalpagamātṛpitṛyonisaṃbandhāga-stenanāstikaninditakarmābhyāsipatitātyāgyapatita-tyāginaḥ patitāḥ || 21.1 ||
brahmahā brāhmaṇavadhakṛt | surāpaḥ triprakārāyāṃ surāyāṃ yasya yā pratiṣiddhā tāṃ pītaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.94]
gurutalpagaḥ – guror ācāryasya, talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryā lakṣyate, tāṃ maithunāya yo gacchati | mātṛsaṃbandhāgaḥ mātṛpakṣe arvāk pañcamāt yā kanyā tāṃ varayitā | pitṛsaṃbandhāgaḥ pitṛpakṣe ‘pi saptamād arvāk | yonisaṃbandhāgaḥ bhaginībhartā | stenaḥ suvarṇacoraḥ | nāstikaḥ paralokādyabhāvavādī | ninditakarmābhyāsī ninditasya pratiṣiddhasya buddhipūrvaṃ sāṃtatyena sevakaḥ | karmagrahaṇāt kāyikasyaivedam, na vāṅmanasayor apacāreṇa | brahmahādīnāṃ patitānāṃ yas tyāgaṃ na karoty asau patitātyāgī | apatitānāṃ dveṣādinā yaḥ parityāgaṃ karoty asāv apatitatyāgī | ete brahmahādayaḥ patitāḥ || 21.1 ||
pātakasaṃyojakāś ca || 21.2 ||
ye pātakeṣu prayojayanti | caśabdād anumantā ca || 21.2 ||
taiś cābdaṃ samācaran || 21.3 ||
taiḥ saha saṃvatsaraṃ samācaran yānāsanaśayanaiḥ | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yānāsanaśayair nityam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti |
cakārād yājanādibhiḥ sadyo ‘pi | tathā ca vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ svādhyāyaṃ sahabhojanam |
kṛtvā sadyaḥ pataty eva patitena samācaran || iti |
aviśeṣāt buddhipūrve ‘buddhipūrve ca | tatra abuddhipūrve laghutaraṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
patitasaṃprayogaṃ ca brāhmeṇa vā yaunena vā yās tebhyaḥ sakāśān mātrā upalabdhās tāsāṃ parityāgas taiś ca na saṃvased udīcīṃ diśaṃ gatvānaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vjñāyate | iti | [VaDh 20.45–46]
tatra brāhmeṇa vā yaunena veti dvitīyo vāśabdo yānāsanaśayanānām apy upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | adhītavedasyaiva saṃhitādhyayanasaṃbhavāt, anadhītavedasya yāvatā kālena svaśākhyāyāḥ saṃhitādhyayanaṃ samāpyate tāvantaṃ kālaṃ sāvitryā japo draṣṭavyaḥ, “saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyīta tāvantaṃ kālaṃ sāvitrīṃ japet” iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | tathā ca taittirīyāṇāṃ svādhyāyabrāhmaṇaliṅgam – “anaśnan triḥ svādhyāyaṃ vedam adhīyīta trirātraṃ vā sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīm” ityādi | evaṃ brāhmaṇaliṅgadarśanāt vāsiṣṭhe aviśeṣite ‘pi triḥ saṃhitādhyayanaṃ draṣṭavyam | ekasyādhyayanasyaikaṃ prājāpatyaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
ekam adhyayanaṃ kuryāt prājāpatyam athāpi vā |
dadyād vā daśasāhasraṃ gavāṃ muṣṭiṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || iti |
buddhipūrve tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ |
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāt tatsaṃsargaviśuddhaye || iti | [MDh 11.181]
saṃvatsarād arvāk vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
patitena tu saṃsargam ajñātvā kurute yadi |
utsargaṃ tasya labdhasya kṛtvā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
buddhipūrve tu kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
patitena tu saṃsargas tv abhisaṃdhikṛto yadi |
kṛtvā labdhasamutsargaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam || iti |
sahabhojane tu lokākṣir āha |
patitaiḥ saha bhuktvānnaṃ parākena viśudhyati |
jñātvā tu tadvrataṃ kuryād iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
iti || 21.3 ||
kim eṣāṃ patitatvam ity āha |
dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam || 21.4 ||
dvijātikarmabhyaḥ śrautebhyo ‘gnihotrādibhyaḥ smārtebhyaś cāṣṭakādibhyaḥ hāniḥ tair anadhikāra ity arthaḥ | karmabhyo hānir iti vaktavye dvijātigrahaṇāc chūdravad avasthānaṃ darśayati | śūdrasyāpi ca patitatvaṃ pratilomavarṇavad avasthānam || 21.4 ||
na kevalaṃ patanam | kiṃ ca |
paratra cāsiddhiḥ || 21.5 ||
patanīyasevāyāḥ prāg api ca yad etena śreyaḥ samācaritaṃ tatphalenāsaṃbandha iti paratra cāsiddhiḥ | caśabdād iha cākīrtiḥ | tad etat pātakapadārthasvarūpāvadhāraṇaṃ dvijātikarmabhir anadhikārārthaṃ prāyaścittānuṣṭhānaprarocanārthaṃ ca || 21.5 ||
tam eke narakam || 21.6 ||
taṃ karmabhir anadhikāraṃ paratra ca śubhasyānupabhogaṃ narakaṃ manyante suhṛdjñātivarjitasyātīva duḥkhotpatteḥ narako ‘pi duḥkhakāraṇatvāt | yathāha niruktabhāṣyakāraḥ: “narakaṃ nyakkaraṇaṃ nīcair gamanaṃ nāsyāsmin ramaṇaṃ vidyate” iti | gautamas tu narakabhāg iti manyate || 21.6 ||
trīṇi prathamāny anirdeśyāni manuḥ || 21.7 ||
eṣāṃ pātakānām ādyāni trīṇi brahmahatyāsurāpānagurutalpagamanāni pātakāny anirdeśyāni, anirdeśyaprāyaścittatvāt | manur āha |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate | iti | [MDh 11.89]
tathā surāpāne ‘pi |
matipūrvam anirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti smṛtam | [MDh 11.145]
ity evamādi draṣṭavyam | pratyāpattyabhāvād vā anirdeśyānīty uktam | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “nāsyāsmin loke pratyāpattir vidyate kalmaṣaṃ tu nirhanyate” [ĀpDh 1.24.25] iti | manugrahaṇaṃ smṛtyarthasya samastasya manuḥ smarteti pradarśanārtham | tad āhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ |
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni mantrā ātharvaṇāś ca ye |
saptarṣibhis tu yat proktaṃ smārtaṃ tan manur abravīt ||
iti || 21.7 ||
na strīṣv agurutalpaḥ patatīy eke || 21.8 ||
strīṣu pravartamānasya agurutalpaṃ gurudārān varjayitvā anyastrīgamanāt na patatīy eke manyante | gautamas tu sarvatra patatīty eveti | so ‘yaṃ buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | nāgurutapa ity eva siddhe strīgrahaṇaṃ cāturvarṇyastrīṇām upasaṃgrahaṇārtham, pratilomāsu patitatvaṃ yathā syād iti | tathāha manuḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || iti | [MDh 11.175]
etat trayaṃ kṛtvā patati na gamanamātreṇeti cet, tad ayuktam – gamanamātra eva prāyaścittopadeśāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “athāparaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavased aśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ vā gacched etenaiva caṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.38, 40–41] iti | idam abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane draṣṭavyam | tatraivābhyāse gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryāt retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || iti | [MDh 11.170]
tasmād abhyāse sakṛdgamane ca patitaprāyaścittātideśāt gamanamātreṇāpi pataty eveti | strīṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ pūjārtham | strīṣu apatitastrīṣu pataty eva | yathāha kavaṣaḥ |
patitāṃ ca striyaṃ gatvā pataty ajñānato dvijaḥ |
talpavrataṃ tadabhyāse jñānatas tadvrataṃ caret || iti |
atrābuddhipūrve ‘bhyāse gurutalpavratātideśāt sakṛdgamane vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “gurvīsakhīṃ gurusakhīm apapātrāṃ patitāṃ ca gatvā kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caret” [VaDh 20.16] iti | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane ‘pi tadvrataṃ cared iti draṣṭavyam || 21.8 ||
bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇasevāyāṃ ca strī patati || 21.9 ||
yac caitat brahmahatyādy upadiṣṭaṃ etat puruṣavat striyā api draṣṭavyam | yat tu tasyā adhikaṃ tad ucyate – bhrūṇahanaṃ sevamānā patatīti | bhrūṇahagrahaṇaṃ patitopalakṣaṇam | hīnavarṇaḥ śūdraḥ | kecid upasargaprayogāt pratilomam āhuḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
catasras tu parityājyāḥ śiṣyagā gurugā ca yā |
patighnī ca viśeṣeṇa juṅgitopagatā ca yā || [VaDh 21.10]
iti tyāgopadeśāt, patitātyāge patitatvaprasaṅgāt | cakāro brahmahatyādibhiḥ samuccayārthaḥ | kecid garbhapatanam āhuḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
trīṇi striyāḥ pātakāni loke dharmavido viduḥ |
bhartur vadho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanam || iti | [VaDh 28.7]
tatra patitapratilomayoḥ saṃsarge puruṣavat prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam,
etam eva vidhiṃ vidyād yoṣitsu patitāsv api | [MDh 11.188]
iti manuvacanāt | śūdraṃ sevamānā śūdrasya saṃsargaṃ kurvāṇā yady api patati, tathāpi ā prajotpatteḥ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || iti | [VaDh 21.12]
tathā tatraiva prāyaścittam apy uktam |
prativarṇaṃ careyus tāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram | iti | [VaDh 21.13]
cāndrāyaṇottaraṃ cāndrāyaṇe uttare cāndrāyaṇāny uttarāṇīti | brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyānukrameṇa yojyam | “śūdrasaṃgatāḥ aprajātā viśudhyanti” [VaDh 21.12] ity uktatvāt kṣatriyavaiśyasaṃgatāḥ prajātā api viśudhyantīti draṣṭavyam | tatra kṣatriyeṇa saṃgatāyāś cāndrāyaṇam uttaraṃ vaiśyena saṃgatāyāś cāndrāyaṇe uttare iti kalpyam | tathā kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyena saṃgatāyāṃ cāndrāyaṇam uttaram iti kalpyam | puruṣāṇāṃ tu pratilomagamane “śūdro brāhmaṇīm” [VaDh 21.1] ityādi vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | anulomagamane tu vakṣyati – “dve paradāre” [GDh 22.31] ityādi | atra patitāsv api catasra eva parityājyāḥ | kutaḥ? “catasras tu” [VaDh 21.10] iti tuśabdena niyamavācinā catasṝṇām eva parityāgasya vasiṣṭhena pratipāditatvāt | itareṣāṃ tu manūktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
etam eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsu patitāsv api |
vastrānnam āsāṃ deyaṃ ca vaseyuś ca gṛhāntike || iti | [MDh 11.188]
ayam api vakṣyati “strī cāticāriṇī” [GDh 22.37] ityādi || 21.9 ||
kauṭasākṣyaṃ rājagāmi paiśunaṃ guror anṛtābhiśaṃsanaṃ pātakasamāni || 21.10 ||
kauṭasākṣyaṃ sakṣye anṛtavāditvam “anyatra nānṛtavacane doṣaḥ” [GDh 13.24] ity apavādāt | rājagāmi paiśunaṃ rājakule paradoṣakathanam | rājanīti vaktavye gāmīti vacanād rājakulād anyatrāpi yat pāraṃparyeṇa rājānaṃ gacchati tad api gṛhyate | guror ācāryasya kenacid anṛtadoṣeṇābhimukhyena śaṃsanam ākhyānam | etāni pātakatulyāni mahāpātakatulyānīti draṣṭavyam,
guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā | [MDh 11.55]
iti smṛtyantaradarśanāt | buddhipūrvavyatikrama evaiṣāṃ pātakasamatvam, na pramādamohādibhir iti visamāsakaraṇam | evaṃ ca pramādādibhir na doṣaḥ || 21.10 ||
apāṅktyānāṃ prāg durvālād gohantṛbrahmojjha-tanmantrakṛdavakīrṇipatitasāvitrīkeṣūpapātakam
|| 21.11 ||
durvālāt prāg ya upadiṣṭā abhojyānnāḥ patitakūṭasākṣivarjitāḥ | nāstikās tu pātakāt pṛthagbhūtā evābhojyānnānāṃ madhe paṭhitāḥ | kutaḥ? trividhā nāstikāḥ | tathāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ|
nāstikās trividhāḥ proktā dharmajñais tattvadarśibhiḥ |
kriyāduṣṭo manoduṣṭo vāgduṣṭaś ca tathaiva ca ||
upapātakas tu vāgduṣṭo manoduṣṭas tu pātakaḥ |
abhyāsāt tu kriyāduṣṭo mahāpātaka ucyate || iti |
atra vāgduṣṭa upapātakamadhye paṭhitaḥ, manoduṣṭaḥ pātakamadhye, kriyāduṣṭo “ninditakarmābhyāsi” [GDh 21.1] ity anenaivoktam iti | atra ya upapātakamadhye paṭhitaḥ tasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam: “nāstikaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā viramen nāstikyāt” [VaDh 21.29] iti | yas tu pātakamadhye paṭhitaḥ tasya āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “etad evānyeṣām api patanīyānām api” [ĀpDh 1,28,14] iti | yas tu mahāpātakaḥ tasyābhyāsāt prāk sarvaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | abhyāse tu brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ vasiṣṭhoktam “athāparaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ” [cf. VaDh 23.38] ityādi draṣṭavyam | alam atiprasaṅgena, prastutaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
gohantā goghātakaḥ | brahmojjhaḥ yo vedam adhītya pramādād vismarati sa gṛhyate | yas tu buddhipūrvam eva parityajati nāstika eva saḥ | tasya viśiṣṭataro doṣa iti | evaṃ cātra yaḥ pramādād vismarati tasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “brahmojjhaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar upayuñjīta vedam ācāryāt” [VaDh 20.12] iti | itarasya manūktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brahmojjhaṃ vedanindā ca kauṭasakṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || iti | [MDh 11.56]
tanmantrakṛt yo gohantṛbrahmojjayoḥ yājanādi karoti | avakīrṇī viplutabrahmacaryo brahmacārī | asyāpaṅktyatvena gṛhītatvāt punarvacanaṃ yo brahmacārī prajām utpādayaty asau kṛtaprāyaścitto ‘pi yāvad asau punar apy upapātakaprāyaścittam na karoti tāvad asau upapātakitvena varjanīya iti | kecid evaṃ varṇayanti – naiṣṭhikānaiṣṭhikatvena gurulaghuprāyaścittārthaṃ punar vacanam | upakurvāṇasya laghu naiṣṭhikasya gurv iti | tad api dviguṇaṃ naiṣṭhikasya “avakīrṇito dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ” iti kavaṣadharme dviguṇadaṇḍadarśanāt | patitasāvitrīko vrātyaḥ | eteṣu nimitteṣu upapātakaṃ bhavati | saṃjñākaraṇaṃ prāyaścittavidhānārtham || 21.11 ||
ajñānādhyāpanād ṛtvigācāryau patanīyasevāyāṃ ca heyau || 21.12 ||
ajñānād ṛtvik, adhyāpanād ācāryaḥ, etayor nimittayor api parityāgaḥ | anabhijñasyārtvijyāsaṃbhavāt taṃ tyajato yajamānasya nāsti doṣaḥ | evam ācāryo ‘py ālasyādibhiḥ samyaṅ nādhyāpayati tam api tyajato nāsti doṣaḥ śiṣyasya | patanīyasevāyāṃ ca patanīyasya karmaṇaḥ patitena saha yānāsanādeḥ sevāyām ācaraṇe | kutaḥ? patitau heyāv iti vaktavye patanīyasevāyām ity upādānāt | patitasyāpatitatyāgina ity eva siddhatvāt prāg ‘py abdāt parityāgārtham idaṃ vyākhyānam | atrāpy apatitatyāgadoṣaḥ prasajyeteti cet, atrocyate – naiva sahāsanādes tyāgo vidhitsitaḥ | kiṃ tarhi? ṛtvigantaropādānam ācāryāntaragamanaṃ ceti | tatra sahāsanādeḥ tyāgaṃ kurvataḥ sa doṣaḥ, nartvigācāryāntaragamanaṃ kurvata iti | cakārād upapātakināv api || 21.12 ||
anyatra hānāt patati || 21.13 ||
anyatra pūrvoktād ajñānādhyāpananimittād anyatra hānāt tyāgāt patati | tatas tayor anyatra tyāgo na kartavyaḥ || 21.13 ||
tasya ca pratigrahītety eke || 21.14 ||
tasya ca ṛtvijam ācāryaṃ vā tyajato yaḥ pratigrahītā ṛtvigācāryau vā tasyārtvjyam adhyāpanaṃ vā yaḥ karotīty arthaḥ | cakārāt tena sahādhyāyī ca patatīty eke manyante | jñānāt patati na tv ajñānād iti gautamaḥ || 21.14 ||
na karhicin mātāpitror avṛttiḥ || 21.15 ||
na kasyāṃcid apy avasthāyāṃ patitāvasthāyām apīty arthaḥ | avṛttir aśuśrūṣā sā na kartavyā | tasyām apy avasthāyāṃ śuśrūṣitavyāv ity arthaḥ | śuśrūṣā namaskārādiḥ || 21.15 ||
dāyaṃ tu na bhajeran || 21.16 ||
dāyaṃ tadīyaṃ dhanaṃ na bhajeran na gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ | mātur dāyaṃ strīdhanam | dāyābhāve ātmīyād api dravyād aśanācchādane dātavye iti tuśabdopādānam | niḥsvayoḥ patitāvasthāyām apy aśanācchādanacodanād apatitayor apy arthasiddham | atra patitāyā api mātus tyāgo nāstīti draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “mātā tu putraṃ prati na patati” [VaDh 13.47] ityādi | patitāvasthāyām api putreṇa mātā na tyaktavyety abhiprāyeṇa tad uktam iti || 21.16 ||
brāhmaṇābhiśaṃsane doṣas tāvat || 21.17 ||
yo doṣeṇa brāhmaṇam abhiyuṅkte “idam enas tvayā kṛtam” ity evamabhiyoge, yāvāṃs tasya adharmo bhavati abhiyoktur api tāvān eveti | tad yathā patita ity abhiyoge patita eva bhavati | sa yathā lokaiḥ pariharaṇīyaḥ tathā abhiyoktāpi pariharaṇīyaḥ | tadīyenāpi prāyaścittenādhikriyata iti || 21.17 ||
dvir anenasi || 21.18 ||
yas tv anṛtenābhiśaṃsanaṃ karoti tasya dviguṇo ‘bhiyoktur doṣo bhavati anṛtābhivadanād abhiśaṃsanāc ceti | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ cātra dvijātipradarśanārtham | tathā aviśeṣeṇaiva smṛtyantaram paṭhitam |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ |
vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syāt mithyā dvirdoṣatāṃ vrajet || iti | [VaDh 20.30]
tatrāpi patitagrahaṇam upapātakasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | smṛtyantare: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśasya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayan pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate, etenaivābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.39, 41] iti liṅgāt | evaṃ cātra pātakopapātakābhiśaṃsana eva doṣo na sarvatrety atrāpi draṣṭavyam | pūrvam ākhyātur evāyaṃ doṣaḥ na sarvair vyavahṛtasya | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “doṣaṃ buddhvā na pūrvaḥ parebhyaḥ patitasya samākhyātā syāt” [ĀpDh 1.21.20] iti || 21.18 ||
durbalahiṃsāyāṃ cāvimocane śaktaś cet || 21.19 ||
yatra durbalasya hiṃsā vināśo bhavati tatra tadvimocane vimokṣaṇe śaktaś cet tadavimocane yāvān hantur doṣas tāvān asyāpi bhavati | caśabdāt kṣudvyādhyādipīḍitasyāpi bhaktauṣadhācchādanādyadāne śaktau satyāṃ taddhanane yāvān doṣas tāvān evāsyāpi bhavati |
nanu cāhārārthaṃ yaḥ pramāpayati tadvicchede ‘pi doṣaḥ prāpnotīti |
ucyate – atulyatvād āhāraprāṇavicchedayoḥ | āhāravicchede mūlādibhir api kṣunnivṛttiḥ śakyā kartum, prāṇavicchede tu na kaścid asti pratīkāra iti || 21.19 ||
abhikruddhāvagoraṇe brāhmaṇasya varṣaśatam asvargyam || 21.20 ||
abhikruddhena, na parihāsādinā, avagoraṇe praharaṇodyamane brāhmaṇasyānaparādhinaḥ | aparādhinaḥ ātatāyitvaprasaṅgena na doṣa iti | varṣaśatam asvargyaṃ narakapatanam | samānajātīyaviṣayam idam | kṣatriyādibhiḥ kṛte –
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi vā |
kṣatraviṭchūdrajātīnāṃ brāhmaṇasya vadhe kṛte ||
iti prājāpatyasmṛtiliṅgāt | varṣāṇām api dviguṇatriguṇatvādi kalpyam | anenaiva nyāyena brāhmaṇena kṛte kṣatraviṭchūdrajātīnāṃ tripādam ardhaṃ pādam iti draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇakṣatriyavat kṣatriyavaiśyayor api, evaṃ kṣatryavaiśyavad vaiśyaśūdrayor api kalpyam | evaṃ sarvasyottamasyottamasya nīcena nīcena vadhe kṛte draṣṭavyam || 21.20 ||
nighāte sahasram || 21.21 ||
āyudhena pāṇinā vā nighāte sahasraṃ varṣāṇām asvargyam, adhikṛtatvāt || 21.21 ||
lohitadarśane yāvatas tat praskandya pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt saṃgṛhṇīyāt || 21.22 ||
rudhirotpādane kṛte tad rudhiraṃ brāhmaṇād avasṛtya yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt piṇḍīkuryāt tāvanti varṣasahasrāṇi narakapatanam, aparimitakālam ity arthaḥ | doṣaviśeṣakathanaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣajñāpanārtham | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
avagūrya caret kṛcchram atikṛcchaṃ nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam || iti |
prāyaścittaviśeṣāt daṇḍaviśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ || 21.22 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye ekaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
dvāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
prāyaścittanimittāny uktāni | athedānīṃ pratipāpaṃ prāyaścittāni vaktavyāni | tatrādyasya tāvad āha |
prāyaścittam | agnau saktir brahmaghnas trir avacchātasya || 22.1 ||
prāyaścittam ity adhikārārtham, prāyaścittam evaitan nādṛṣṭārtham iti | agnau saktiḥ saṅgaḥ patanam ity arthaḥ | trir iti patanaviśeṣaṇam | patane avākchirastvaṃ ca draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākchirāḥ | iti | [MDh 11.73]
kasyedaṃ prāyaścittam? brahmaghnaḥ brāhmaṇahantuḥ | avacchātasya avaśīrṇasya bhaktatyāgena karśitadehasyety arthaḥ | tathā ca kāṭhakaśrutiḥ: “anaśanena karśito ‘gnim ārohet” iti || 22.1 ||
lakṣyaṃ vā syāj janye śastrabhṛtām || 22.2 ||
lakṣyaṃ śaravedhyam, tad vā bhavej janye senāmadhye | śastrabhṛtām iti dhanuḥprāye yuddhe, nānyaśastraprāye iti || 22.2 ||
khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇir vā dvādaśa saṃvatsarān brahmacārī bhaikṣāya grāmaṃ praviśet karmācakṣāṇaḥ
|| 22.3 ||
khaṭvāṅgaṃ prasiddham | kapālaṃ śavaśiraḥ | yathāha manuḥ: “kṛtvā śavaśiro dhvajam” [MDh 11.72] iti | taddhāraṇaṃ ca cihnārtham | dvādaśasaṃvatsarān evaṃ caret | brahmacārī maithunavarjitaḥ | bhaikṣārtham eva grāmaṃ praviśet | tad eva cāsyāhāraḥ | bhaikṣacaraṇam api mṛnmayena pātreṇa | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “mṛnmayaṃ pātram” iti | anyadā araṇya evāvasthānam | brahmacaryabhaikṣopadeśāc ca vratatvam api draṣṭavyam | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
pavitrapāṇir daṇḍī ca paṅkadanto rajasvalaḥ |
tīrthavāsī kuśācchādī jaṭilo brahmahā bhavet || iti |
karmācakṣāṇaḥ brahmahāsmīti sarvadā kathayan || 22.3 ||
patho ‘pakrāmet saṃdarśanād āryasya || 22.4 ||
patho mārgād apeyāt dṛṣṭipātād āryasya dvijāteḥ | anenaiva nyāyena pṛṣṭhataḥ pārśvataś ca dūrata eva parihared iti draṣṭavyam | tatra govālavyajanād arvāk saṃnikṛṣṭe sacelasnānaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ caiva dūrataḥ parivarjayet |
govālavyajanād arvāk sacelaḥ snānam ācaret || iti |
śūdram api saṃsparśanāt parihared iti draṣṭavyam | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
mahāpātakisaṃsparśe varṇānāṃ snānam ucyate |
asnātvā bhojane caiva taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
trirātraṃ tu pibed ambu śaṅkhapuṣpīśritaṃ payaḥ |
evam ārtavacaṇḍālaśavānām api kīrtitam ||
iti || 22.4 ||
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharan savaneṣūdakopasparśī śudhyet
|| 22.5 ||
anyatra virodhibhyaḥ ācamanādikālebhyaḥ kṛtsnam ahas tiṣṭhet | evaṃ rātrim āsanena nayed iti | triṣavaṇasnānaṃ ca kurvan brahmahatyānimittād enaso mucyate | śudhyed ity upasaṃhārārtham
|| 22.5 ||
prāṇalābhe vā tannimitte brāhmaṇasya || 22.6 ||
brāhmaṇasya vā taddhetuke prāṇalābhe sati śudhyet, coravyāghrādibhiḥ pramāpyamānaṃ mocayitvety arthaḥ | ekasya vicchinnāḥ prāṇāḥ anyasmai dattā iti | anenaiva nyāyena sarveṣāṃ hanane tajjātīyaprāṇavicchede vimokṣaṇāt śuddhiḥ draṣṭavyā || 22.6 ||
dravyāpacaye vā tryavaraṃ pratirāddhaḥ || 22.7 ||
prakaraṇāt brāhmaṇasya yad dravyaṃ hiraṇyādi tad api yajjīvanāya paryāptam – draviṇam api prāṇarūpam “prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ” iti darśanāt – tasya dasyubhir apahriyamāṇasyāpahṛtasya vā apacaye pratimocane śudhyet | mocanāsaṃbhave triḥ pratirāddhaḥ triḥkṛtvā puruṣakāra ity arthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
tryavaraṃ pratirāddho vā sarvasvam apajitya vā |
viprasya tannimitte vā prāṇalābhe vimucyate || iti | [MDh 11.80]
asmād eva liṅgāt daridrasyākleśenānubhavaparyāptasya dhanasya pradānād api śuddhir jñātavyā
|| 22.7 ||
aśvamedhāvabhṛthe vā || 22.8 ||
avabhṛtagrahaṇaṃ kriyāntaranivṛttyartham | vacanasāmarthyād enam apa upasparśayeyuḥ | śudhyed iti vartate | vāśabdagrahaṇāt upasaṃhṛtasyābhidhānāc ca etāni svatantrāṇy eva prāyaścittānīti | kecit prakrāntadvādaśavārṣikasyety āhuḥ | tathā ca śaṅkho dvādaśavarṣe śuddhim āpnotīty uktvā āha: “antarāle vā brāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā gavāṃ dvādaśānāṃ paritrāṇāt” iti | tatrābhyāse prakrāntadvādaśavārṣikasya samāptiviśeṣaṇatvena draṣṭavyam, anabhyāse tu svatantrāṇy eveti || 22.8 ||
anyayajñe ‘py agniṣṭudantaś cet || 22.9 ||
anyo yajño yasya tad idam anyayajñam avabhṛthaṃ sa ca yajño yad agniṣṭudanto bhavati | agniṣṭunmadhya ity arthaḥ | sa ca sarvamedhaḥ sattraṃ vā tatrāpi snātvā śudhyet || 22.9 ||
na kevalaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ prāṇavicchedenaiva brahmahā bhavati | kiṃ tarhi?
sṛṣṭaś ced brāhmaṇavadhe ‘hatvāpi || 22.10 ||
sṛṣṭo niścito ‘dhyavasita ity arthaḥ | brāhmaṇo hantavya ity evaṃ yenādhyavasāyaḥ kṛto bhavati brāhmaṇahananaviṣaye asāv ahatvāpi brahmahā bhavati, tasyāpy etad eva prāyaścittaṃ tatsaṃkalpasya dṛṣṭatvād ity abhiprāyaḥ | anenaiva nyāyena sarvaprāṇivadhe tadīyam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇasya pradarśanārthatvād iti || 22.10 ||
ātreyyāṃ caivam || 22.11 ||
ātreyī ṛtumatī brāhmaṇī, yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “rajasvalām ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ | atra hy eṣyad apatyaṃ bhavatīti” [VaDh 20.35–36] | tasyāṃ ca hatāyāṃ brhamahā bhavati | kṣatriyavaiśyayor api savanagatayor upasaṃgrahārthaś cakāraḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
hatvā garbham avijñātam etad eva vrataṃ caret |
rājanyavaiśyau cejānau ātreyīm eva ca striyam || iti | [MDh 11.87]
evam iti samastaprāyaścittopadeśārtham | anye tu atrigotrām ātreyīm āhuḥ | ātreyīgrahaṇād anātreyyāṃ na brahmahā bhavatīti draṣṭavyam || 22.11 ||
garbhe cāvijñāte brāhmaṇasya || 22.12 ||
brāhmaṇasya garbhe strīpuṃnapuṃsakatvenāvijñāte hate auṣadhādinā brahmahā bhavati yadi brāhmaṇyām evāhito bhavati | vijñāte tu yathāliṅgam eveti draṣṭavyam | prāyaścittātideśārthaś cakāraḥ | tad etat brahmahatyāprāyaścittam anekaprakāraṃ hantuḥ hanyamānasya cābhisaṃdhyanabhisaṃdhyādyapekṣayā yojanīyam |
atra kecit bruvate – hantus tāvad yuktaṃ kuryān na kuryād ity atra kāmākāmakaraṇe gurulāghavasyoktatvāt | “ninditakarmābhyāsi” [GDh 21.1] ity anenābhyāse ‘pi doṣagurutvaśravaṇāt | yat tu hanyamānaṃ prati gurulaghubhāvakalpanaṃ tad ayuktaṃ jñāpakābhāvāt | api ca “brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ” ity atra brāhmaṇoddeśena hananapratiṣedho vidhīyate | na coddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣyate guṇavān nirguṇo veti yathā “daśāpavitreṇa grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭi” [ĀpŚr 12.4.8]ity atraikatvam avivakṣitam | tasmāt brāhmaṇatvajātinibandhana eva pratiṣedhaḥ nāṅgīkṛto guṇa iti |
tad etad ayuktam – hanyamānaguṇāpekṣayā doṣagurutvaprakhyāpakasya vidyamānatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam |
ātatāyinam āyāntaṃ hanyād evāvicārayan || iti | [MDh 8.350]
atrātatāyivadhābhyanujñānenaiva bahuśrutādīnām api vadhe siddhe bahuśrutādīnāṃ vadhe viśiṣṭataro doṣa iti jñāpanārthaṃ teṣām upādānam | yathā praśastakarmāpekṣayā ca gurutvam avagamyate yena rājanyavaiśyayor api savanagatayoḥ pramāpaṇe brahmahatyāprāyaścittam upadiṣṭaṃ “rājanyavaiśyau cejānau” [MDh 11.87] ity anena | yathā –
etad eva cared abdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyād caikaśataṃ gavām || [MDh 11.129]
iti vṛttasthavaiśyapramāpaṇe pṛthag uktam, itarasya tu “strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadhaḥ” [MDh 11.66] ity anenopapātakaprāyaścittam iti |
yat tūktam uddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇam avivakṣitam iti, tad apy ayuktam – prāyaścittavidhinaiva brāhmaṇavadhapratiṣedho ‘numīyate na hi brāhmaṇavadhapratiṣedhakaṃ vacanam asti yasmin viśeṣaṇam avivakṣitam ity ucyeta | yady api vidyate, tathāpi viśeṣaṇaṃ śakyata eva vivakṣitum uktajñāpakasāmarthyād eva | api ca yathā viśeṣaṇam avivakṣitaṃ tathā saṃkhyāpy avivakṣitaiva prāpnoti, tataś caikasmin hate dvayoś ca tulyam eva prāyaścittaṃ prāpnoti | na caitad iṣṭam | tasmāt “brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ” ity atrāpi guṇavato hanane viśiṣṭataro doṣa iti jñātuṃ śakyata evety uktaṃ sarvam upapannam eveti
|| 22.12 ||
rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam
|| 22.13 ||
kṣatriyavadhe kṛte ṣaṭsu varṣeṣu bhavaṃ ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tad api prākṛtaṃ svābhāvikaṃ khaṭvāṅgādirahitam ity arthaḥ | tataś ca brahmahatyāprāyaścittam eva ṣaḍvarṣāṇi kartavyam ity arthaḥ | buddhipūrvaviṣayam idaṃ vṛttavataś ca hanane | avṛttavato hanane,
“strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam | [MDh 11.66]
ity upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | evam uttaratrāpi | tatra buddhipūrvahanane yad vakṣyati “agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam” [GDh 22.34] iti tat draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avakīrṇī viśuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti | [MDh 11.117]
brāhmaṇasyety adhikārāt brāhmaṇaviṣayam idam | kṣatriyajātes tu dvādaśavarṣaṃ draṣṭavyam, vaiśyasyāṣṭādaśavarṣam, śūdrasya caturviṃśativarṣam ity evam uttaratrāpi kalpyam || 22.13  ||
vṛṣabhaikasahasrāś ca gā dadyāt || 22.14 ||
vṛttavataḥ | pūrvavat abuddhipūrvaviṣayam idaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
turīyo brahmahatyāyāḥ kṣatriyasya vadhe smṛtaḥ |
vaiśye ‘ṣṭamāṃśo vṛttasthe śūdre jñeyas tu ṣoḍaśaḥ || [MDh 11.126]
ity uktvā, punar apy āha |
akāmatas tu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ |
ṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
tryabdaṃ cared vā niyato jaṭilo brahmahavratam |
vasan dūratare grāmād vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ || [MDh 11.127–28]
iti draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatra cakākād dānasahitaṃ ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prākṛtabrahmacaryam api draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā: “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmahavrataṃ tasyānte ṛṣabhaikasahasragodānaṃ ca” iti | abhiṣekaguṇayuktasya buddhipūrvahanane idam | abuddhipūrve tu sa evāha: “aṣṭau varṣāni vā caret” iti || 22.14 ||
vaiśye traivārṣikam || 22.15 ||
prākṛtaṃ brahmcaryam | kṣatriyavadviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ || 22.15 ||
ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā dadyāt || 22.16 ||
pūrvavad buddhipūrvaviṣayam idam | tathā ca manunoktam udāhṛtaṃ ca: “etad eva cared abdam” [MDh 11.129] ityādi | cakārāt pūrveṇa samastam api | tad apy atyantaguṇavato buddhipūrvahanane | abuddhipūrvahanane tu “etad eva vrataṃ kuryuḥ” [MDh 11.117] ity etad eva draṣṭavyam | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tulyaprāyaścittasyānyāyyatvād avṛttavato na syād iti | evaṃ tarhi “agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam” [GDh 22.34] ity atrāvṛttavatoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayos tulatvaṃ prāpnotīti cet, atrocyate – tapasā pūraṇaṃ kartavyam iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
yasmin karmaṇy asya kṛte manasaḥ syād alāghavam |
tasmiṃs tāvat tapaḥ kuryād yāvat tuṣṭikaraṃ bhavet || iti | [MDh 11.233]
evaṃ sarvatra kalpayitavyam | evaṃ sthite vṛttavato vaiśyasyābuddhipūrvahanane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā: “vaiśyaṃ hatvā sapta māsān brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret” iti | sarvatra guṇādhikye ‘bhyāse ca japādīnām apy adhikakalpanā kartavyā | dadyād ity adhikārād eva siddhe punar dadyādgrahaṇaṃ gavām abhāve anyasyāpi dravyasya prāpaṇārtham | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
gavām abhāve niṣkaṃ vā niṣkārdhaṃ pādam eva vā |
yad vā sa pratigṛhṇīyāt tad dadyāc chrotriyāya tu ||
|| 22.16 ||
śūdre saṃvatsaram || 22.17 ||
dvijātidāsasya buddhipūrvahanane idam || 22.17 ||
tasyaivābuddhipūrve āha |
ṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gā dadyāt || 22.18 ||
gavām adhikṛtatvāt punar gograhaṇaṃ śuklavarṇaviśiṣṭagodānārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
pramāpya śūdraṃ ṣaṇmāsān etad eva vrataṃ caret |
ṛṣabhaikādaśāś cāpi dadyād viprāya gāḥ sitāḥ || iti | [MDh 11.130]
cakārāt pūrvayuktam api tasyaiva vṛttavato buddhipūrvahanane draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu “agnyutsādi” [GDh 22.34] ityādi | adārāsya buddhipūrvahanane etad eva vrataṃ kuryur iti | abuddhipūrve tu taptakṛcchraṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā: “śūdraṃ hatvā taptakṛcchram” iti | atrānulomapratilomayoḥ smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
anulomānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ tanmātṛhanane ca yat |
tad eva nirdiśed vidvān strīṇām ardhaṃ tathaiva ca ||
ātreyīhanane brūyād yan mātur upadiśyate |
garbhe caiva tathājñāte vyāghrasya vacanaṃ yathā || iti |
evam eva pratilomastrīṇāṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra puruṣāṇāṃ tv aṅgirā āha |
antyānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt bhagavān aṅgiro ‘bravīt || iti |
śūdraprabhavānām idaṃ buddhipūrve | abuddhipūrve tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
hanane pratilomeṣu śūdrajānāṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
jñānapūrve parākaḥ syād ajñāte tv aindavaṃ tathā || iti |
śūdraprabhavāṇāṃ “asamānāyāṃ tu śūdrāt patitavṛttiḥ” [GDh 4.27] iti samaguṇatvāt prāyaścittasāmyam api yuktam eva | itareṣām api tatraivāha |
itareṣāṃ caturbhāgaṃ pitur uktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
svajātīnāṃ vadhe yadvac chūdrasya vidhir ucyate || iti |
anulomapratilomayoḥ vadhe śūdrasya yat prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad eva svajātihanane ‘pi draṣṭavyam iti tatrārthaḥ | śūdrasya tu tatraivocyate |
sarvam ardhaṃ tripādaṃ ca pādaṃ caiva vrataṃ bhavet |
varṇamātrābhyantarajahiṃsāgamanabhojane ||
iti || 22.18 ||
anātreyyāṃ caivam || 22.19 ||
cakārād evātideśe siddhe evaṃkārakaraṇaṃ rājanyavadhaprāyaścittātideśārtham, anantaroktaṃ mā bhūd iti | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “anātreyīṃ rājanyahiṃsāyāṃ rājanyāṃ vaiśyahiṃsāyām” [VaDh 20.37–38] ityādi | tarhi tatraiva vaktavyam iti cet, ucyate – ante ‘bhidhānaṃ kṛte prāyaścitte ‘pi na saṃvased ity evamartham | tathā ca manuḥ |
brahmaghnāṃś ca kṛtaghnāṃś ca viśuddhān api dharmataḥ |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃś ca strīhantṝṃś ca na saṃvaset || [MDh 11.190]
iti || 22.19 ||
gāṃ ca vaiśyavat || 22.20 ||
gāṃ hatvā vaiśyavat prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | tatra vṛttavatyā hananam idam | buddhipūrvatvaṃ ca prakaraṇād adhikriyate | tatra bahukṣīraguṇayuktāyā buddhipūrvahanane vaiśyoktaṃ traivārṣikaṃ brahmacaryaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra ca dhanavata ṛṣabhaikasahasragodānam | kutaḥ? rājavadhe varṣatrayaṃ brahmacaryarakṣaṇasya gosahasradānasya pratinidhirūpeṇa dṛṣṭatvāt | tathaivodāhṛtaṃ ca manuvākyam “ṛaṣabhaikasahasrā gāḥ” [MDh 11.127] ityādi |
bahukṣīrayuktāyāṃ gurutaro doṣa iti kasmād avagamyata iti cet, ucyate –
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ | iti | [MDh 8.297]
tatra kṣudrapaśuśabdena alpaprayojanānāṃ gavāṃ grahaṇam, nājāvikānām,
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣakaḥ | [MDh 8.298]
iti teṣāṃ pṛthagdaṇḍadarśanāt | tasmāt tadviṣaye daṇḍalaghutvadarśanāt bahuprayojanānāṃ gurutaro doṣa iti gamyate | tasyā evābuddhipūrvahanane ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā dadyād iti draṣṭavyam | tadasaṃbhave abdaṃ vā brahmacaryam | yad vaiśyasya dānatapasoḥ samuccitayor uktaṃ tad atrāpi draṣṭavyam | tasyāpi viṣaya asyā eva brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyāḥ buddhipūrvahanane[68] | brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyāṃ gurutvam api,
goṣu brāhmaṇasaṃsthāsu sthūrikāyāś ca bhedane | [NSm 19.40]
iti liṅgāt | atra godānāśaktau niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhaṃ pādam eva veti draṣṭavyam | tathodāhṛtaṃ ca vyāghravacanam,
gavām abhāve niṣkaṃ vā niṣkārdhaṃ pādam eva vā |
ityādi | tatra rājño gosahasradānaṃ suvarṇasahasradānaṃ vā, tatsadṛśasya niṣkārdham ity ayaṃ pakṣaḥ | prākṛtasya tu pādam | kuta? asmin viṣayakaraṇe jātukarṇivacanāt |
gosahasraṃ suvarṇasya sahasraṃ rājña ucyate |
tadardhaṃ madhyamasyoktaṃ pādaṃ syāt prākṛtasya tu || iti |
tathā ca manur api kasmiṃścit prakaraṇe āha |
kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhaved daṇḍyaḥ yatrānyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ |
tatra rājā bhaved daṇḍyaḥ sahasram iti dhāraṇā || iti | [MDh 8.336]
anenāpi prākṛtajanād rājño ‘dhikaṃ gamyate | sarvāsaṃbhave varṣacatuṣṭayaṃ vā prākṛtabrahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatra | tasyā evābuddhipūrvahanane vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “gauś ced ghatā syāt tasyāś ca carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vātiṣṭhet” [VaDs 21.18] ityādi | tasya abuddhipūrvaviṣayaparijñānam “brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvam” [VaDh 21.16] ity atra aprekṣāpūrvam ity adhikārāt | tatra “ṛṣabhavehatau dadyātām” [VaDh 21.22] iti dvivacanāt dvau hantārau gamyete | tatra ṛṣabhahanane ṛṣabhaṃ gohanane gavādīti draṣṭavyam | vyāghroktaṃ vā |
gāṃ ced ghanyān naro ‘jñānāt paṇasaptaśataṃ tataḥ |
śrotriyāya daridrāya dadyāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ || iti |
gāṃ ceti cakārāc chūdroktam api draṣṭavyam | tatrālpaprayojanāyā hanane buddhipūrve vatsaratapaścaraṇasahitam ṛṣabhaikādaśagodānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tasyā evābuddhipūrve jātukarṇyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha:
gāṃ ced akāmato hanyāt brāhmaṇānujñayā tataḥ |
prājāpatyatrayaṃ kṛtvā sparśayet brāhmaṇāya gām || iti |
tasyā eva brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyā buddhipūrve hanane manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavān pibet |
kṛtavāso vased goṣṭhe carmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ||
caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇācaret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ ||
divānugacchet tā gās tu tiṣṭhann ūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet |
śuśrūṣitvā namaskṛtvā rātrau vīrāsanaṃ vaset ||
tiṣṭhantīṣv anutiṣṭhet tu vrajantīṣv anu ca vrajet |
āsīnāsu tathāsīta niyato vigataspṛhaḥ ||
āturām abhiṣiktāṃ vā coravyāghrādibhir bhayaiḥ |
patitāṃ paṅkalagnāṃ vā sarvaprāṇair vimocayet ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā svaśaktitaḥ ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ kṣetre gehe ‘tha vā khale |
bhakṣayantī na kathayet pibantaṃ caiva vatsakam ||
anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gā anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati ||
ṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || iti | [MDh 11.108–16]
abuddhipūrve tu saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ draṣṭavyam | yadi vatsasyākāmato mṛtyur bhavati tadā vṛṣabhaikādaśagodānaṃ draṣṭavyam | vatsaṃ prati laghutvam api,
vatsānām ardhadaṇḍaḥ syāt samo daṇḍaḥ payasvinīnām |
balīvardānāṃ vṛṣabhāṇāṃ ca tathaivoktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
iti prājāpatyasmṛtidarśanāt | tasya buddhipūrve tu,
vatsaṃ hatvā naro buddhyā tataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
paṇān pañcaśataṃ vāpi dadyāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
iti kavaṣasmṛtyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam | svāmine tanmūlyaṃ dāpyam “dāpyas tasya ca tadhanam” [MDh 8.176] iti liṅgāt | asmin vadhe śūdrāṇām ardhadaṇḍaḥ, lokākṣivacanāt:
dvijātīnāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ sarvo daṇḍo vidhīyate |
śūdrāṇām ardhadaṇḍaḥ syād iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
iti || 22.20 ||
maṇḍūkanakulakākabimbadaharamūṣakaśvahiṃsāsu
ca || 22.21 ||
atra kākagrahaṇaṃ manunā viḍvarāhaślokapaṭhitānāṃ kravyādaślokapaṭhitānāṃ biḍālaślokapaṭhitānāṃ mārjāraślokapaṭhitānāṃ copalakṣaṇam |
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || [MDh 11.154]
kravyādasūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ kukkuṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
narakākabakānāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || [MDh 11.156]
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibet brāhmīṃ suvarcalām || [MDh 11.159]
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkakākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || [MDh 11.131] iti |
eteṣāṃ samudāyavadhe śūdrahatyāvrataṃ cared iti draṣṭavyam | śūdrahatyāprāyaścittānukarṣaṇārthaś cakāraḥ | buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhede ‘pi śūdravad eva | pratyekavadhe tv abuddhipūrve viḍvarāhyādīnāṃ madhye keṣāṃcid āhatya vidhīyate smṛtyantare |
ghṛtakumbhaṃ varāhaṃ tu tiladroṇaṃ tu tittiram |
śukaṃ dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ krauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyanam ||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākaṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇam eva ca |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayed brāhmaṇāya gām || iti | [MDh 11.134–35]
idaṃ mahatānāṃ vadhe | pratyekavadhe tu kārṣāpaṇamātraṃ deyam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
haṃsānāṃ ca mayūrāṇāṃ jalasthānāṃ ca pakṣiṇām |
kapīnāṃ śyenabhāsānāṃ vadhe dadyāt paṇaṃ dvijaḥ ||
vāso dadyād dhayaṃ hatvā pañca nīlān vṛṣān gajam |
ajameṣāv anaḍvāhaṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam ||
kravyādāṃś ca mṛgān hatvā dhenuṃ dadyāt payasvinīm |
akravyādo vatsatarīm uṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṛṣṇalam || iti |
buddhipūrve daṇḍānurūpaṃ parikalpyam | yathāha daṇḍapāruṣye –
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ coravat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet |
prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsv ardhaṃ gomāyūṣṭrahayādiṣu ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ |
pramāpaṇe prāṇabhṛtāṃ tatra daṇḍo vicāritaḥ ||
pañcāśatsu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu |
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣakaḥ ||
māṣikas tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śvasūkaranipātane || iti | [MDh 8.296–98]
eṣām āhatya yuktāṇām –
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
upaspṛśet sravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet || [MDh 11.132]
iti draṣṭavyam | tatra buddhipūrve payaḥpānam adhvagamanaṃ vā draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu snānaṃ sūktaṃ japaś ceti | dakṣiṇācodanāvat | vatsānāṃ hanane ‘pi tāratamyaṃ draṣṭavyam | narāṇām uktaṃ varṇādibhedena | varāhāṇām api vakṣyati “varāhe ghṛtaghaṭaḥ” [GDh 22.26] iti | atraitad api draṣṭavyam | yat tu mūtrapurīṣaprāśane cāndrāyaṇam uktam, tatrāpy eteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇaṃ cāndrāyaṇam api buddhipūrve draṣṭavyam | tatra bhakṣaṇe taptakṛccham uktam | yatrāpy ucchiṣṭabhakṣaṇe brahmasuvarcalāpānam uktam atra sarvatrāpy eṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇam iti | atra cāndrāyaṇaṃ manuṣyamūtrapurīṣaprāśane pṛthag uktvā tadvarjitānāṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrāpi mūtragrahaṇe retaso ‘pi grahaṇam, manuṣyamūtrapurīṣaprāśane prāyaścittopadeśenāsyāpi samānopadeśāt |
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣipet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ reto viṇmūtram eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.222]
ayam api vakṣyati “mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ ca prāśane” [MDh 23.4] iti | buddhipūrve cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve tu sāntapanam, yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
varāhāṇāṃ kharoṣṭrāṇāṃ kapīnāṃ ca tathaiva ca |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi caret sāntapanaṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
atrāpy eṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ grahaṇam iti | taptakṛcchravidhānam api buddhipūrvaka eva | tatra punaḥ saṃskāraś ca draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāyaṃ vakṣyati punaḥsaṃskārasahitaṃ taptakṛcchram adhikṛtya “śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya” [GDh 23.5] iti | abuddhipūrve tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “śvakukkuṭagrāmasūkarakaṅkagṛdhrabhāsavāyasapārāvatamānuṣolūkānāṃ māṃsādane saptarātram abhojanaṃ niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca” [VaDh 23.30] iti | evaṃ cātrābhihitānāṃ ca tatrāntarbhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ | yatra tu brahmasuvarcalāpānaṃ vihitaṃ tatrāpi narocchiṣṭaṃ varjayitvā | yathāha uśanā:
brāhmaṇocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt kṣatriyocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmasahasraṃ vaiśyocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmadaśasāhasraṃ matipūrvaṃ cet prājāpatyam atikṛcchraṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchaṃ ca | śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane saptarātraṃ yavāgūpānaṃ matipūrvaṃ cet parākam | evaṃ dvijāter dvijātyucchiṣṭabhojane śūdrāṇāṃ ca brāhmaṇocchiṣṭānām apāṃ pāne ṣoḍaśaprāṇāyāmān dhārayet kṣatriyāṇāṃ triṃśat vaiśyānāṃ catvāriṃśat śūdrāṇāṃ kuśavāripānaṃ tryaham, buddhipūrvaṃ cet tryahaṃ caturahaṃ pañcāhaṃ saptāhaṃ ca krameṇa pañcagavyapānaṃ ca krameṇa | antyajānām ucciṣṭabhojane buddhipūrve mahāsāntapanam, abuddhipūrve cāndrāyaṇam | iti |
manunāpi kiṃcid uktam |
śūdrocchiṣṭā apaḥ pītvā kuśavāri pibet tryaham || [MDh 11.148]
abhojyānāṃ ca bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || [MDh 11.152] iti |
suvarcalāpānam apy abuddhipūrve aviśeṣād ekarātram eva | buddhipūrve tu sāntapanam | yathāha jātūkarṇiḥ |
jambūkakākamārjāraśvagodhānāṃ gavām api |
matyocchiṣṭaṃ dvijo bhuktvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
atra kavaṣoktam api draṣṭavyam |
viprārdhaṃ kṣatriyasya syād vaiśyānāṃ ca tadardhakam |
tadardham eva śūdrāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ || iti |
idaṃ sarvatra draṣṭavyam | hiṃsāgrahaṇam adhikārasiddham api kriyate hiṃsāyām eva na tu spṛṣṭasyāpīti || 22.21 ||
asthanvatāṃ sahasraṃ hatvā || 22.22 ||
anyeṣām asthimatāṃ sahasravadhe śūdrahatyāprāyaścittaṃ bhavati || 22.22 ||
anasthimatām anaḍudbhāre ca || 22.23 ||
anasthimatāṃ krimyādīnām anaḍudbhāramātre hate etac chūdrahatyāprāyaścittam | atra buddhipūrve śūdrahatyāprāyaścittam, netaratraitad iti draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sarvatrātideśe draṣṭavyam || 22.23 ||
api vāsthanvatām ekaikasmin kiṃcit kiṃcid dadyāt
|| 22.24 ||
atha na vā sahasrapūraṇam anadudbhārapūraṇaṃ vā anurudhyate, kiṃ tu anantaram ekaikasmin kiṃcit kiṃcit kākaṇikāmātraṃ dadyāt | vāśabdāt japādi vā kuryāt | apiśabdād anasthimatām api | tathāha manuḥ |
kiṃcid eva tu viprāya dadyād asthimatāṃ vadhe |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.141]
tatrāsthimatāṃ buddhipūrvahanane kākaṇikāvaramātraṃ deyam, abuddhipūrve tu ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ | anasthimatāṃ buddhipūrve prāṇāyāmatrayam, abuddhipūrve tv eka eva prāṇāyāmo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāha kaṇvaḥ |
hanane ‘sthimatāṃ dadyāt matyā kākaṇikāṃ naraḥ |
abuddhyā tu tatas teṣāṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt anasthnāṃ hanane matau |
abuddhyaikaṃ tataḥ kuryād iti dharmavido viduḥ ||
iti || 22.24 ||
ṣaṇḍe palālabhāraḥ sīsamāṣaś ca || 22.25 ||
ṣaṇḍe tṛtīyāprakṛtau, na tu klībe, tasya tu śrotriyatvena prāptau satyāṃ śrāddhe pratiṣedhāt sasaṃskārasaṃbandhitvāc ca | tasmin hate tasya jātiviśeṣo nāsti – palālabhāro gavāṃ sīsamāṣo brāhmaṇāya | asamāsanirdeśād vyastam, cakārāt samatvaṃ ca | tatrābuddhipūrve vyastaṃ buddhipūrve samastam iti || 22.25 ||
varāhe ghṛtaghaṭaḥ || 22.26 ||
sūkare hate ghṛtapūrṇo ghaṭo brāhmaṇāya deyaḥ | asya tūkto viṣayaḥ || 22.26 ||
sarpe lohadaṇḍaḥ || 22.27 ||
lohaśabdena kārṣṇāyasam ucyate | yathāha manuḥ |
abhrīṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ | iti | [MDh 11.133]
buddhipūrve idam, abuddhipūrve tu māṣamātraṃ deyam, “sarpaṃ hatvā māṣamātraṃ dayāt” ity auśanasavacanāṭ || 22.27 ||
brahmabandhvāṃ calanāyāṃ jīlaḥ || 22.28 ||
calanā vyabhicāriṇī | kṣatriyāder manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathāha |
jīlakārmukabastāvīn pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ || iti | [MDh 11.138]
jīlo dṛtiḥ | calanam api gūḍhatvena bahupuruṣasevitvam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
abhigacchati yā nārī bahubhiḥ puruṣair mithaḥ |
vyabhicāriṇīti sā jñeyā pratyakṣaṃ gaṇiketi ca || iti |
evaṃ ca puruṣatrayasaṃsargād arvāk pūrvoktaṃ hananaprāyaścittam eva draṣṭavyam, tata ūrdhvam idam iti | puruṣatrayasaṃsargād arvāg api pūrvoktād ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
vṛttasthāyāḥ striyāḥ sarvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
pratipūruṣaṃ pravṛttāyā ardhahīnaṃ bhavet tu tat || iti |
idaṃ buddhipūrve | abuddhipūrve tu vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
vyabhicāriṇyāḥ pāpārdhaṃ veśyānāṃ gamane bhavet |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārī hatvānavasthitām ||
śaṅkhaśuktyajam eṣāṃś ca kramād dayād viśuddhaye ||
iti || 22.28 ||
vaiśike na kiṃcit || 22.29 ||
vaiśyakarmaṇā jīvantyāṃ hatāyāṃ na kiṃcid api kartavyam | idam abuddhipūrve | buddhipūrve tu vyabhicāriṇyāṃ yad uktaṃ tadardhaṃ draṣṭavyam,
vyabhicāriṇyāḥ pāpārdhaṃ veśyānāṃ gamane bhavet |
caturṇām api varṇānām eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt | yad vā – veśyāsu kriyata iti vaiśikaṃ tat brahmabandhvā saha kṛte prakāśam ācarantyā maithune ācarite ity arthaḥ | na kiṃcit parastrīgamanaprāyaścittaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca gūḍham ācarantyā saha kṛte kiṃcit prāyaścittam astīti gamyate | tad api hiṃsāvat parikalpyam | atra paradāragamanaprāyaścittapratiṣedhād anyad asty evety avagamyate | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ |
paśuveśyāgamane prājāpatyaṃ caret dvijaḥ | iti |
tad api sakṛdgamane, abhyāse tu saṃvartoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
paśuveśyābhigamane prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
gogame tu naraḥ kuryāt kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram || iti |
tasyām atyantābhyāse manunoktam upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam | iti | [MDh 11.66]
tasyāṃ prasūtasya kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
prasūto yas tu veśyāyāṃ bhaikṣabhuṅ niyatendriyaḥ |
śatasāhasram abhyasya sāvitrīm eti śuddhatām || iti |
brahmabandhvā saha vaiśikācaraṇe yad uktaṃ tad eva kṣatrabandhvādibhir api vaiśikācaraṇe draṣṭavyam | tathāha kavaṣaḥ |
brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdrabandhubhir vaiśike kṛte |
prājāpatyaṃ cared vidvān abhyāse kṛcchram eva tu || iti |
kṣatriyādīnām ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam |
atra kecid vyācakṣate – brāhmaṇyāḥ śūdrasaṃparke pātityam uktam: “bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇasevāyāṃ ca strī patati” [GDh 21.9] iti | tasmāt yā brāhmaṇī śūdravarṇasaṃbandhā bhavati tayā sahānyeṣāṃ saṃbandhaṃ kurvatāṃ patitāgamanaprāyaścittam eva kartavyam iti |
tad ayuktam,
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175]
iti liṅgāt | tataś ca matyā śūdrādisaṃparke ‘pi brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ śūdrādisāmyam amatyā ca taddhīnatvam avagamyata iti | evam api yac chūdrayā avyabhicāriṇyā maithunācaraṇe prāyaścittaṃ tad eva brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrasaṃbandhāyām api prasajyeteti cet – atrocyate, sāmyavacanān na bhavatīti | tasmād uktam eva prāyaścittam iti |
anye vyācakṣate – abuddhipūrvaṃ śūdrādisaṃparkaṃ yā karoti tayā gamane pātakaprāyaścittam, buddhipūrve tu śūdrābhigamanaprāyaścittam, “jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati” [MDh 11.175] iti vacanād iti |
tad apy ayuktam | atrāpi buddhipūrvakṛtād abuddhipūrvakṛtasya gurutvaprāptau tad api “saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ” [ĀpDh1.29.3] ityādibhir virudhyeteti | evaṃ ca sati “caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā” [MDh 11.175] ity asyāpy ayam evārtho veditavyaḥ | caṇḍālādibhir abuddhipūrvasaṃparke patito bhavati dvijātikarmabhir nādhikriyata ity arthaḥ, na tu brahmahatyādipāpam iti | tad uktam “dvijātir karmabhyo hāniḥ patanam” [GDh 21.4] iti | na jāter apagamaḥ, tasmāt prāyaścittam astīti | buddhipūrve tu jāter apy apagamo bhavati tasmāt prāyaścittaṃ nāstīti | “pataty ajñānataḥ” [MDh 11.175] ity anena dvijātikarmānadhikṛta ity uktaṃ na brahmahatyādipāpam | sāmyavacanāc ca yena saṃparkaṃ karoti buddhipūrvaṃ tena sāmyaṃ gacchati na prāyaścittenādhikriyata iti, sāmyadvāreṇa prāyaścittābhāva iti | brāhmaṇyādīnāṃ śūdrasaṃbandhe buddhipūrve ‘pi prajātāyā eva tat sāmyaṃ draṣṭavyam, aprajātāyāḥ śuddhipratipādanāt |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || [VaDh 21.12]
iti śūdreṇa saṃgatāyā aprajātāyāḥ śuddhipratipādanād adhojātisaṃgatāyā aśucitavṃ draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśām ity uktatvāt śūdrastriyāḥ prajātāyā api śuddhiḥ | anyac ca –
prajātāyāṃ pratipuruṣam ardhahīnaṃ bhavet tu tat |
ity atra brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyasaṃparke ardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vaiśyasaṃbandhe tadardhaṃ śūdrasaṃbandha ity evaṃ parikalpyam | “madyapastrīniṣevaṇam” [MDh 11.66] ity atra codyate – brāhmaṇyā madyapāyā gamane patitāgamanaprāyaścittaṃ bhavitum arhati, nopapātakaprāyaścittaṃ madyapānena hi patitā bhavatīti |
atrocyate – pātityaṃ śūdratulyatvaṃ na brahmahatyāsamatvam, yathāha manuḥ |
yasya kāyagataṃ brahma madyenāplāvyate sakṛt |
tasya vyapaiti tad brahma śūdratvaṃ ca nigacchati || iti | [MDh 11.97]
ataḥ śūdratulyatvād uktam eva prāyaścittaṃ na patitāgamanaprāyaścittam iti | tathātrāpi coktam: “āryānāryayor vyatikṣepe karmaṇaḥ sāmyam” [GDh 10.66] iti | vyatikṣepas tu śūdrasyāpratiṣiddhaṃ madyapānaṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ tu pratiṣiddham | tatra śūdre madyapānavarjite brāhmaṇādau ca madyapānaprasakte sati tasmād dhetoḥ sāmyaṃ ca | evaṃ ca madyapo brāhmaṇādis tadvarjitaśūdratulya ity avagantavyaḥ | yady api śūdratulyaḥ tathāpy aspṛśya ity avagantavyaḥ, “patitacaṇḍāla” [GDh 14.29] ity atra patitagrahaṇena gṛhyamāṇatvāt | tadvarjitānāṁ grahaṇam iti cet, na, smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | tathāha kaṇvaḥ |
brahmahā madyapaḥ steno gurutalpaga eva ca |
aspṛśyāḥ patitā hy ete taiś cāvacaritaḥ samaḥ ||
|| 22.29 ||
talpānnadhanalābhavadheṣu pṛthag varṣāṇi || 22.30 ||
lābhavadhaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate, talpalābhavadhe dhanalābhavadha ityādi | talpaśabdena śayanavācinā bhāryocyate | taddhetutvāl lakṣaṇayā kanyāyā atra grahaṇam | annaṃ kṛtam akṛtaṃ vā | dhanaṃ suvarṇagavādi | lābhaḥ prāptiḥ | vadho vighnakaraṇam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – kanyānnadhaneṣu labhyamāneṣu vighnaṃ pratiṣedhaṃ kṛtvā saṃvatsaraṃ brahmacaryaṃ pṛthak pṛthak kartavyam iti | buddhipūrva idam | abuddhipūrve tu uśanasoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “kanyānnadhanavighne prājāpatyam” iti | brāhmaṇalābhavighnaṃ idam | kṣatriyādiṣv adharmadharmaṃ parikalpyam || 22.30 ||
dve paradāre || 22.31 ||
paradāragamane dve varṣe prākṛtabrahmacaryam | brāhmaṇīgamane garbhotpattāv idaṃ draṣṭavyam | garbhotpatteḥ prāk kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇīṃ gacched akāmāṃ yadi kāmataḥ |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe kuryād ardham eva pramādataḥ ||
ardham eva sakāmāyāṃ taptakṛcchraṃ sakṛd gatau |
ardham ardhaṃ nṛpādīnāṃ dāreṣu brāhmaṇaś caret ||
etad vrataṃ caret sārdhaṃ śrotriyasya parigrahe |
aśrotriyaś cet dviguṇaṃ guptāyām ardham eva ca || iti |
kṣatriyasya dāre garbhotpattau “dve paradāre” [GDh 22.31] ity asyārdhaṃ draṣṭavyam, “ardham ardhaṃ nṛpādīnām” iti liṅgāt | vaiśyadāre manunoktam upapātakaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, “etad eva vrataṃ kuryuḥ” [MDh 11.117] ityādi | śūdrasya dāre atikṛcchram, kaṇvavacanāt |
śūdradārān gato vipro hy atikṛcchraṃ samācaret |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ viśo rājñaḥ samaṃ tu brahmaṇi vratam || iti |
yadi brāhmaṇenaiva cāturvarṇyaprasūtāḥ krameṇa nirviṣṭāḥ tadānīṃ brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇīgamane yad uktaṃ tad eva pādahīnaṃ kṣatriyādigamane draṣṭavyam, vyāghravacanāt:
vipreṇaiva vinirviṣṭāś cāturvarṇyaprasūtakāḥ |
krameṇa pādato hīnaṃ vrataṃ tāsu gataś caret || iti |
ayam eva nyāyaḥ kṣatriyādiṣv api draṣṭavyaḥ | brāhmaṇabhāryāṃ śūdrāṃ brāhmaṇo gatvā prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt, vasiṣṭhavacanāt: “brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvakaṃ brāhmaṇadārān adhigacchen nivṛttadharmakarmaṇaḥ kṛcchro ‘nivṛttadharmakarmaṇo ‘tikṛcchraḥ” [VaDh 21.16] iti | idam abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane | buddhipūrve tu dviguṇam, “ardham eva pramādataḥ” iti liṅgāt | abhyāse tu pādahīnanyāyo draṣṭavyaḥ | brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyādibhāryāgamane yad uktaṃ tad eva kṣatriyādīnāṃ svajātibhāryāgamane ‘pi draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ?
vipro nṛpasya bhāryāyāṃ yat karoti samāgame |
tad eva kṣatriyaś cāpi kuryāt tatraiva saṃgataḥ ||
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt || 22.31 ||
trīṇi śrotriyasya || 22.32 ||
garbhotpattāv evāsyāṃ samāgame | garbhotpatteḥ prāg apy uktam:
etad vrataṃ caret sārdhaṃ śrotriyasya parigrahe | iti | [Vyāghra under 22.31]
garbhotpatter ūrdhvam etad eva ā prajāyā draṣṭavyam | bahuśo ‘bhyāse samānaprāyaścittatvam ayuktam iti cet, atrocyate – strīṇāṃ garbhotpatteḥ prāk bahuśo ‘bhyāse ‘pi samānatvaṃ dṛśyate,
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ | [VaDh 21.12]
ity atra | tadvad atrāpi na doṣa iti | tathā kavaṣavacanam api |
garbhotpattau yad uktaṃ tat tad evāmaraṇāc caret |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaś cāpi vaiśyaś śūdras tathaiva ca || iti |
japādibhir vā pratipūraṇaṃ kartavyam | tad uktaṃ ca mānave |
yasmin karmaṇy asya kṛte manasaḥ syād alāghavam | [MDh 11.233]
ityādi | tatraikasyāṃ duṣṭaḥ kṛtaprāyaścittaḥ saṃvatsare ‘tīte punar api tasyām eva yadi duṣṭo bhavati tadā pūrvaprāyaścittāt dviguṇaṃ tena kartavyam | kutaḥ?
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ | [MDh 8.373]
iti daṇḍadarśanāt | saṃvatsaraprabhṛti tasya tāvad eveti draṣṭavyam | saṃvatsarād arvāk prathame māsi dvādaśaguṇitasyaiva māsān māsi [māsānumāsi?] bhāgakalpaneti bhāṣyakāreṇa vyākyātam [=Bhāruci] | pratimāsaṃ traimāsikaṃ pūraṇaṃ kalpyam | kṛtaprāyaścittasyaivedam, abhiśastasya duṣṭasyeti padadvayopādānāt | akṛtaprāyaścittasya tu japādibhir eva pūraṇam | tatrātyantaśrotriyabrāhmaṇasya bhāryāṃ prajāvatīṃ pativratāṃ balād avaṣṭabhya kṛtasyācaturthād upagamanāt prāk pātityaṃ draṣṭavyam, āpastambavacanāt: “savarṇāyām anyapūrvāyāṃ sakṛt saṃnipāte pādaḥ patatīty upadiśanti | evam abhyāse pādaḥ pādaḥ| caturthe sarvam” [ĀpDh 2.27.11–13] iti || 22.32 ||
dravyalābhe cotsargaḥ || 22.33 ||
yadi ca parastrīto dravyaṃ labdhaṃ tasyotsargaḥ kāryaḥ | tyāgaś ca vinā pratigrahadharmeṇa parityāgaḥ | caśabdād yad annādy upabhuktaṃ tad api pratipādayet || 22.33 ||
yathāsthānaṃ vā gamayet || 22.34 ||
svāmina eva vā bhaktādikaṃ kenacid vyājena pratipādayed ity arthaḥ || 22.34 ||
pratiṣiddhamantrayoge sahasravākaś cet || 22.35 ||
pratiṣiddhasyāpraśastadvijāteḥ mantrayogaḥ yājanādhyāpanakriyā, tasmin kṛte saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | ajñānād idam, itaratra patitatvaṃ syād iti | yadi cāsau mantrayogaḥ sahasravākaḥ syāt | vākaśabdena padam ucyate | sahasraśabdo ‘pi bahutvavacanaḥ | ato yadi mahāntaṃ grantham abhyasyatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ cālpe pūrvoktasahitādhyayanaṃ draṣṭavyam | patitaviṣayam idam | upapātakiviṣaye tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
patitacaṇḍālaśavaśrāvaṇe trirātraṃ vāgyatā anaśnanta āsīran sahasraparamaṃ vā tad abhyasyantaḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyata ity etenaiva garhitādhyāpakayājakā vyākhyātā dakṣiṇātyāgāc ca pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate || iti | [VaDh 23.34–36]
atrāpy ajñānāt trirātraṃ vāgyatair anaśnadbhir bhavitavyaṃ sahasravākād arvāk | ūrdhvaṃ tu yāvad adhyāpitaṃ tāvataḥ sahasraparamo japo haviṣyāśino draṣṭavyaḥ | jñānaviṣaye tu sahasravākād ūrdhvam upapātakitā draṣṭavyā | tad uktaṃ ca “tanmantrakṛt” iti | tato ‘rvāk sahasratrayajapo draṣṭavyaḥ, “pratiṣiddhakṛtam anuvākaṃ sahasratrayam abhyaset” ity auśanasavacanāt |
anye tu mantrayogaṃ sahādhyayanaṃ sahayājanaṃ vācakṣate, tataś ca pratiṣiddhena sahaikatrādhyayanaṃ sahayājanaṃ ca kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam iti |
atrāpi pūrvoktaviṣayabhedena tad eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, sahādhyayanayājanayos tulyatvād iti || 22.35 ||
agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu caivam || 22.36 ||
yasya deśopaplavādinā śrautāgnīnāṃ bahukālaṃ viyogo bhavati so ‘gnyutsādī | nirākṛtir uktaḥ | nāsau upapātakatvena gṛhyate, durvālād ūrdhvam abhihitatvāt | utsṛṣṭāgnis tv anya eva vyākhyātaḥ śrautānām eva nāstikyena parityakteti | atraivaṃ viṣayavibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ – yo nāstikyena karmāṇi parityajati, deśopaplavādinā vā bahukālaṃ gacchati, tasya saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam, alpakāle tu prājāpatyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “yo ‘gnīn apavidhyet kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar ādhānaṃ kārayet” [VaDh 1.23] iti | ya ālasyādinā nāstikyadeśopaplavādyantareṇāpi parityajati tasya cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathāha manuḥ |
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat || iti | [MDh 11.41]
māsam apaviddhasyedam, tasmād uttaraṃ kṛcchrottaraṃ draṣṭavyam,
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn māsād ūrdhvaṃ tu kāmataḥ |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kuryād evāvicārayan ||
iti vacanāt | māsād arvāg api cāndrāyaṇam eva, “vispaṣṭārthaṃ vā māsagrahaṇam” iti bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt | sarvatra punarādhānam atītakālahomaś ca draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca jātukarṇiḥ |
atītakālaṃ juhuyād agnau viprāya vā vyayam |
naṣṭe ‘gnau vidhivad dadyāt kṛtvādhānaṃ punar dvijaḥ || iti |
idaṃ vākyaṃ śraute smārte ca draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca bahukālātikramaṇe prāyaścittasya gauravaṃ saṃpadyata iti nātra japādipūraṇaṃ kartavyam | smārtasya tu punar ādhānam atītakālahomaś caiva, na prāyaścittāntaram asti | nāstikye tu prājāpatyaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
yo ‘gnīṃs tyajati nāstikyāt prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
anyatra punarādhānaṃ dānam eva tathaiva ca || iti |
nirākṛter anye ye cāpāṅktatvena abhihitā upapātakavarjitās teṣāṃ manunoktaṃ ca draṣṭavyam |
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva ca |
homāś ca śālakā nityam apāṅktānāṃ viśodhanam || iti | [MDh 11.200]
upapātakeṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ smṛtyantaroktānām upalakṣaṇārtham | yathāha manuḥ |
govadho ‘yājyasaṃyājyapāradāryātmavikrayāḥ |
gurumātṛpitṛtyāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya ca ||
parivittatā cānujena parivedanam eva ca |
tayor dānaṃ kanyāyās tayor eva ca yājanam ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇaṃ caiva vārdhuṣyaṃ vratalopanam |
taṭākārāmadārāṇām apatyasya ca vikrayaḥ ||
vrātyatā bānadhavatyāgo bhṛtādhyāpanam eva ca |
bhṛtāc cādhyayanādānam apaṇyānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ ||
sarvākareṣv adhīkāro mahāyantrapravartanam |
hiṃsauṣadhīnāṃ stryājīvo ‘bhicāro mūlakarma ca ||
indhanārtham aśuṣkāṇāṃ drumāṇām avapātanam |
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho ninditānnādanaṃ tathā ||
anāhitāgnitā stainyam ṛṇānāṃ cānapakriyā |
asacchāstrādhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasya ca kriyā ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam || iti | [MDh 11.59–65]
atra keṣāṃcid āhatya prāyaścittaṃ smṛtyantare śrūyate | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ:
parivittaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet | atha parivividānaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet | agredidhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā niviśeta tāṃ caivopayacchet | didhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niviśet | [VaDh 20.7–10]
ityādi | govadhe atrāpy uktam, ayājyasaṃyājane ‘py uktaṃ pāradārye ‘py uktaṃ svādhyāyāgneḥ parityāge ‘py uktam | kanyādūṣaṇe ca vasiṣṭha āha: “gurvīsakhīṃ gurusakhīm apapātrāṃ patitāṃ ca gatvā kṛcchrābdapādaṃ caret” [VaDh 20.16] iti | etad akāmakṛte draṣṭavyam | buddhipūrve tu,
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || [MDh 11.170]
iti manunoktaṃ gurutalpavrataṃ draṣṭavyam | yady api sāmānyenoktam, tathāpi jātiviśeṣāt prāyaścittaviśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ: “brāhmaṇakanyāyāṃ sarvaṃ kṣatriyakanyāyām ardham” ityādi | vrātyatāyāṃ ca vasiṣṭhoktam udāhṛtam: “patitasāvitrīka uddālakavrataṃ caret” [VaDh 11.76] ityādi | manur apy āha |
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi |
tāṃś cārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhy upanāyayet || iti | [MDh 11.191]
tatra bahukālātikrama uddālakavratam, madhyame kṛcchratrayam, svalpakālātikrame vrātyastomayāga iti draṣṭavyam | bhṛtakādhyāpanam “vedaviplāvakam” [GDh 20.1] ity uktam | bahuśaḥ kṛte pātityaṃ tatra tyāgaś coktaḥ “tyajet pitaram” [GDh 20.1] ityādi | anyatra manur āha |
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya ca dvijaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhāras tat pāpam apasedhati || iti | [MDh 11.198]
abhicārasyāpi manur āha |
vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣām antyakarma ca |
abhicāram ahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrair vyapohati || iti | [MDh 11.197]
dvijātivadhe idam | śūdrādivadhe tu “śūdram abhicārya prājāpatyaṃ caret” iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt | ninditānnādane tu vakṣyati: “abhojyabhojane niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ” [GDh 23.23] ityādi | tatra buddhipūrvābhyāse surāpānasamatvam | yathāha manuḥ |
ninditānādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ | iti | [MDh 11.56]
evaṃ cābuddhipūrvābhyāse upapātakaṃ veditavyam | evaṃ yeṣām upapātakānām āhatya vidhānaṃ nāsti teṣām idaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ draṣṭavyam | abhyāsaviṣayaṃ cedam | itaratra manunoktam |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avakīrṇivarjaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti | [MDh 11.117]
tatrāpy abuddhipūrve cāndrāyanam itaratra govadhaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | cakārād evātideśe siddhe evaṃkārakaraṇaṃ samastātideśārtham | tataś ca “dravyalābhe cotsargaḥ” [GDh 22.33] ityādi draṣṭavyam || 22.36 ||
strī cāticāriṇī guptā piṇḍaṃ tu labheta || 22.37 ||
strī ca etad eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | aticāriṇī vyabhicāriṇī | śūdravyabhicāra uktatvāt dvijātivyabhicāra idam | buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane ced, anyatra “yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu” [MDh 11.176] ity uktatvāt, samānajātivyabhicāra idam | brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyavyabhicāre tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “vyavāye tu saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtapaṭaṃ dhārayet | gomayagarte kuśaprastare vā śayītordhvaṃ saṃvatsarād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavati” [VaDh 21.8] iti | vaiśyagamane tv auśanasaṃ draṣṭavyam: “vyabhicāriṇī kṛcchrābdaṃ caret” iti | uttamābhigamane ca nīcāyām ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | tathā saṃbhāṣaṇe ca śrūyate:
manasā bhartur aticāre trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ kṣīrodanaṃ vā bhuñjānādhaḥśayītordhvaṃ trirātrād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśatena śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vāksaṃbandha etad eva māsaṃ caritvordhvaṃ māsād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryāś caturbhir aṣṭaśataiḥ śirobhir juhuyāt | [VaDh 21.6–7] iti |
idam api sakṛdbhāṣaṇe | abhyāse tu manunoktam, punaḥśabdaśravaṇāt | yathāha |
sā cet punaḥ saṃpraduṣyet sadṛśenopayantritā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tad asyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 11.177]
hīnasaṃbhāṣaṇe taddviguṇam, uttamasaṃbhāṣaṇe ardhaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ vyabhicāriṇī yā na śuddhā sā guptā rakṣyamāṇā piṇḍaṃ prāṇadhāraṇamātram annaṃ labheta | tuśabdāt kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ vāsaś ca labheta || 22.37 ||
amānuṣīṣu govarjaṃ strīkṛte kūśmāṇḍair ghṛtahomo ghṛtahomaḥ || 22.38 ||
govarjitāsv amānuṣīṣu vaḍavādyāsu strīkṛte maithune ācarite kūśmāṇḍaiḥ “yad devā devaheḍanam” ityādibhir ghṛtena homaḥ kartavyaḥ abhyāsād dhomāvṛttir draṣṭavyā || 22.38 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye dvāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
trayoviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
adhunā kramaprāptaṃ surāpānaprāyaścittam āha |
surāpasya brāhmaṇasyoṣṇām āsiñceyuḥ surām āsye mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.1 ||
surāśabdenātra triprakārā surā gṛhyate, brāhmaṇagrahaṇāt, taṃ prati triprakārāyāś ca pratiṣiddhatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā na pātavyā tathā sarvā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.94]
“amatyā pāne” [GDh 23.2] iti vakṣyamāṇatvān matipūrva idam iti cāvagamyate | kṣatriyavaiśyayor api paiṣṭīpana idam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, tayos tasyā eva pratiṣiddhatvāt | yathāha manuḥ |
surā vai malam annānāṃ pāpmā ca malam ucyate |
tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaś ca na pibet surām || iti | [MDh 11.93]
tathā |
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohād agnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet |
tayā sa kāye nirdagdhe mucyate kilbiṣāṭ tataḥ || iti | [MDh 11.90]
prāyaścittavidhāne dvijagrahaṇāt | yasya yā pratiṣidhyate tasya tatpāne buddhipūrve tasyā evāgnivarṇāyāḥ pānam eva prāyaścittam ity avagamyate | atrāpi brāhmaṇasya gauḍīmādhvyor buddhipūrvābhyāse trayāṇāṃ paiṣṭyā buddhipūrve sakṛtpāne cedaṃ draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ? gauḍīmadhvyoḥ matipūrve sakṛtpāne prāyaścittāntaradarśanāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “madyapāne tv asurāyāḥ surāyāś cājñāne kṛcchrātikṛcchau ghṛtaprāśanaṃ punaḥsaṃskāraś ca” [VaDh 20.19] iti | atra “surāyāś cājñāne” ity uktatvān madyapānasya matipūrvatvam avagamyate | atikṛcchraṃ cātra taptakṛcchram, smṛtyantaradarśanāt, “madyaṃ pītvā kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vātiṣṭhet” iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt | uṣṇām agnivarṇām iti draṣṭavyam, “mṛtaḥ śudhyet” ityārambhāt | āsiñceyuḥ ābhimukhyenopadiśeyur ity arthaḥ | hetukartur vyapadeśaḥ na sākṣāt kartuḥ, pānasya kartā svayam eveti | yathāha āpastambaḥ: “surāpo ‘gnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet” [ĀpDh 1.25.3] iti | āsiñceyur iti bahuvacanam upadeṣṭṝṇāṃ bahutvasūcanārtham | tathāha manuḥ |
te yāṃ vedavido brūhus trayo ‘py enaḥsu niṣkṛtim |
sā teṣāṃ pāvanāya syāt pavitrā viduṣāṃ hi vāk || iti | [MDh 11.85]
śaṅkho ‘py āha |
parṣadānumataḥ samyag eno vikhyāpya tattvataḥ |
yad brūyus tadvidhānajñās tacchuddhikaram uttamam || iti |
āsye mukhe, mṛtaḥ śuddhyet maraṇād eva pūto bhavati | “surām asye” ity atra surāśabdo gomūtrādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārthaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
gomūtram agnivarṇaṃ vā pibed udakam eva vā |
payo ghṛtaṃ vāmaraṇāt gośakṛdrasam eva vā || iti | [MDh 11.91]
jātivacanatvāt brāhmaṇaśabdasya brāhmaṇyā api surāpāna etad eva prāyaścittam | yathā śaṅkhaḥ striya evādhikṛtyāha: “surālaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanamāṃsādīny abhakṣyāṇi varjayed āhāramayaṃ śarīraṃ tanmayatvād vā saṃkīrtyate” ityādi | tathā vasiṣṭho ‘pi:
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya yasya bhāryā surāṃ pibet |
patitārdhaśarīrasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti | [VaDh 21.15]
tathā tatraiva: “yā brāhmaṇī surāpā na tāṃ devāḥ patilokaṃ nayanti iha vai sā bhramati kṣīṇapuṇyāpsu jalūkā bhavati śuktikā vā” [VaDh 21.11] iti |
anye tu deśāntarācāradarśanāṭ,
pūrvaṃ striyaḥ surair bhuktāḥ somagandharmavahnibhiḥ |
gacchanti mānuṣān paścāt tasmān niṣkalmaṣāḥ striyaḥ ||
tāsāṃ somo ‘dadac chaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram |
agniś ca sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ tasmān niṣkalmaṣāḥ striyaḥ || [VaDh 28.5–6]
iti vasiṣṭhasmṛtidarśanāc ca brāhmaṇyāḥ naitat prāyaścittam icchanti |
tatra deśavyavasthayā vyavasthitavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 23.1 ||
amatyā pāne payo ghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuṃ pratitryahaṃ taptāni sa kṛcchraḥ || 23.2 ||
amatyā ajñānena gauḍīmādhvyoḥ, pānādhikāre punaḥ pānagrahaṇāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati |
matipūrvam anideśyaṃ prāṇāntikam iti sthitiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.146]
atrāpi “taptakṛcchrasahitena saṃskāreṇa” [Bhāruci] iti bhāṣyakāreṇa vyākhyātam | evaṃ ca paiṣṭyā amatyā sakṛtpāne brāhmaṇasya draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || [MDh 11.92]
kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu yathodāhṛtaṃ vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam “surāyāś cājñāne” [VaDh 20.19] ityādi | tasyā buddhipūrvābhyāse ‘pi trayāṇāṃ maraṇāntikam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
abhyāse tu surāyās tām agnivarṇāṃ pibed dvijaḥ | iti | [VaDh 20.22]
atra tuśabdo viśeṣavācī | dvijātīnāṃ paiṣṭyā abuddhipūrvābhyāse ‘tiviśeṣato ‘gnivarṇāyāḥ tasyāḥ pānaṃ kartavyam, brāhmaṇasya gauḍīmādhvyoś cābuddhipūrvābhyāse agnivarṇāyās tasyāḥ pāne maraṇād eva śuddhiḥ | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
matyā madyam amatyā vā punaḥ pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
tato ‘gnivarṇāṃ tat pītvā mṛtaḥ śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti |
kṣatriyavaiśyayos tu gauḍīmādhvyor apratiṣedhaḥ | tathāha vyāsaḥ |
ubhau madhvāsavakṣībau ubhau candanacarcitau |
ekaparyaṅkaśayanau dṛṣṭau me keśavārjunau || iti |
tataś cāmatipūrve gauḍīmādhvyor brāhmaṇasya sakṛtpāne kṣīrādīnāṃ tryahaṃ tryahaṃ kartavyam | payaādīnāṃ visamāsakaraṇaṃ kramanivṛttyartham | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
“tryaham uṣṇāḥ pibed āpaḥ tryaham uṣṇaṃ pibet payaḥ |
tryaham uṣṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ pītvā vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
iti taptakṛcchraḥ” [VaDh 21.21] iti udakaghṛtapayasām evoṣṇatvam, saṃbhavāt na vāyoḥ | “sa kṛcchraḥ” iti kṛcchravidheḥ upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] ityādi
|| 23.2 ||
tato ‘sya saṃskāraḥ || 23.3 ||
tata iti kṛcchrānantaram eva saṃskāra iti | asyagrahaṇaṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ yasya pānaṃ tasyeti | tad api paiṣṭyā buddhipūrve sakṛtpāne kṣatriyavaiśyayor api saṃskāraprāpaṇārthaṃ brāhmaṇādhikārāt tayor nivṛttir mā bhūd iti | uttarārthaṃ vā mūtrādiprāśane brāhmaṇasyaiva punaḥsaṃskāra iti | saṃskāra upanayanamātraṃ na tu vedavratadhāraṇādi | punaḥsaṃskārasvarūpaṃ smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca |
etāni tu nivartante punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || [MDh 11.151]
iti || 23.3 ||
mūtrapurīṣaretasāṃ ca prāśane || 23.4 ||
mūtrādīnāṃ ca prāśane etad eva prāyaścittam | cakāraḥ punaḥsaṃskārānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | idaṃ buddhipūrve | kutaḥ? manuvacanāt:
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva ca || iti | [MDh 4.222]
atra kṛcchraśabdena taptakṛcchro ‘bhihitaḥ iti bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt [Bhāruci], uttaratrānyeṣām abhakṣyāṇāṃ prāyaścittopadeśān mānuṣāṇāṃ mūtrādiprāśna ity avagantavyam | abuddhipūrve ca,
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet | [MDh 4.222]
ity anenaiva tryaho veditavyaḥ, buddhipūrve ‘tulyaprāyaścittadarśanāt | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane ‘matipūrvake |
nāśnīyāt tu tryahaṃ matyā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti |
amativiṣaye ‘pi punaḥsaṃskāro draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃsṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥsaṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.150]
kecid etad eva prāyaścittam icchanti, tad api sakṛtprāśane draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca yatra mūtrapurīṣaprāśane prāyaścittam uktam “kākakharoṣṭṛāṇāṃ ca” ityādau, tatra manuṣyavarjitasya draṣṭavyam || 23.4 ||
śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya || 23.5 ||
prāśana ity anuvartate | śvāpadāḥ śveva padāḥ śvāpadāḥ śvasadṛśapadāḥ sṛgālādayaḥ | uṣṭrakharau prasiddhau | teṣām aṅgasya māṃsacarmāder bhakṣaṇa etad eva prāyaścittam | śvasadṛśapādānāṃ vidhānād eva śuno ‘py arthasiddham, caśabdān manuṣyāṇāṃ ca || 23.5. ||
grāmyakukkuṭasūkarayoś ca || 23.6 ||
aṅgaprāśna etad eva prāyaścittam | pṛthagyogaḥ “pūrvaiś ca daṣṭasya” [GDh 23.8] ity atraitayor varjanārtham | asmin buddhipūrvādau pūrvam evoktam | cakāraḥ punaḥsaṃskārānukarṣaṇārthaḥ || 23.6 ||
gandhāghrāṇe surāpasya prāṇāyāmā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca
|| 23.7 ||
surāpasyāviśeṣitatvāt sarvasya | gandhāghrāṇe surāgandhāghrāṇe, na tu śarīragandhāghrāṇe | tatrāpi nānyāvasthitasurāgandhāghrāṇe, surāgandhāghrāna iti vaktavye surāpasyeti nirdeśāt | tataś cānyāvasthitasurāgandhāghrāṇe jātibraṃśakaratvaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtvā ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātibhaṃśakaraṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 11.67]
tathā prāyaścittam api |
jātabhaṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatarad icchayā |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā || iti | [MDh 11.124]
yasya yat pratiṣiddhaṃ tasya tad gandhaghrāṇa iti ca draṣṭavyam, aviśeṣitatvāt | dvijātīnāṃ sāmānyam eva jātibhraṃśakaraprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasya ity asyānupraveśo ‘tra nāsti, smṛtyantaradarśanāt | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi vaiśyaś cāpi tathaiva ca |
surāyā gandham āghrāya caret sāntapanavratam || iti |
prāṇāyāmās trayo draṣṭavyāḥ, tāvatā bahuvacanasya kṛtārthatvāt | ghṛtaprāśanaṃ kāyāplavanamātram, tat kṛtvā bhoktavyam upavāsavidhyabhavāt | visamāsacakārau vyastasamastakalpanārthau | ato brāhmaṇasya samastaṃ kṣatriyasya tu prāṇāyāmāḥ vaiśyasya ghṛtaprāśanam iti | tatrāpi – brāhmaṇasyāhitāgner viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇas tu surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
prāṇān apsu trir āyamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.149]
kṣatriyāder viśeṣābhāvo vacanābhāvāt || 23.7 ||
pūrvaiś ca daṣṭasya || 23.8 ||
pūrvaḥ śvāpadādibhiḥ samānuṣair daṣṭasya etad eva prāyaścittam | caśabdāt grāmyaiś ca kravyādibhiḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādibhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭrair varāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | [MDh 11.199]
atrāpy evaṃviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ | etair daṣṭasya brāhmaṇasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | [VaDh 23.31]
śvagrahaṇasyaiteṣām apy upalakṣaṇatvāt kṣatriyasyedaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ draṣṭavyam | vaiśyasya manunokta ekaḥ prāṇāyāmo draṣṭavyaḥ, prāṇāyāmenety ekavacananirdeśāt | śūdrasya tu na doṣaḥ | striyāś ca sacelasnānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha jātukarṇiḥ |
brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śunā ca śvāpadair api |
daṣṭā sacelam āplutya śudhyanti tu na saṃśayaḥ ||
iti || 23.8 ||
tapte lohaśayane gurutalpagaḥ śayīta || 23.9 ||
tapte agnivarṇe āyase gurubhāryāgāmī svapayāt || 23.9 ||
sūrmiṃ vāśliṣyej jvalantīm || 23.10 ||
sūrmiṃ lohamayīṃ strīprakṛtim antaḥsuṣirām | antaḥsuṣirakāṣṭhām ity apare | tāṃ jvalantīm āśliṣyet upagūhet || 23.10 ||
liṅgaṃ vā savṛṣaṇam utkṛtyāñjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇāpratīcīṃ vrajed ajihmam ā śarīranipātāt | mṛtaḥ śudhyet || 23.11 ||
liṅgaṃ puruṣavyañjanaṃ bījasahitaṃ samūlam utkṛtya hastadvaye sthāpayitvā nairṛṭīṃ diśaṃ vrajed ajihmaṃ kūpādyapariharan | ā śarīranipātāt ā vināśāt | mṛtaḥ śudhyet | ayaṃ ca sarvaśeṣaḥ, maraṇāntikatvāt | evaṃ ca teṣāṃ vikalpe siddhe vāgrahaṇaṃ vyavasthārtham | tataś ca tayor icchātaḥ saṃyoge prathamam, tayā protsāhitasya dvitīyam, puruṣeṇa svena protsāhitāyāṃ tṛtīyam iti | etad api brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇīgamane buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane | abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śmaśrulo nirjane vane |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchram abdam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.105]
tad apy ubhayor icchātaḥ saṃyoge | tayā protsāhitasya svena protsāhitāyām auśanasam iti draṣṭavyam: “gurutalpagāmī saṃvatsaraṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vā” iti | evaṃ brāhmaṇīgamane brāhmaṇasya buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane abuddhipūrve ‘bhyāse maranāntikam eva | kṣatriyāgamane vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca tathā kṛcchrātikṛcchrakam |
caren māsatrayaṃ vipraḥ kṣatriyāgamane guroḥ || iti |
tatrāpi buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttāv atikṛcchraḥ | tayā protsāhitasya kṛcchraḥ, svena protsāhitāyāṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ tatrāpi buddhipūrve abhyāse maraṇāntikam eva | yathāha kavaṣaḥ |
matyā gatvā guror bhāryāṃ punaḥ kṣatrasutāṃ dvijaḥ |
vṛṣaṇāvarjitaṃ liṅgam utkṛtya ca mṛtaḥ śuciḥ || iti |
abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca |
sakṛd gatvā guror bhāryām ajñānāt kṣatriyāṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchraḥ, tayā protsāhite atikṛcchraḥ, svena protsāhitāyāṃ cāndrāyaṇam iti | abhyāse tu –
guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ bhāryāṃ punar gatvā tv akāmataḥ |
vṛṣaṇamātram utkṛtya śudhyej jīvan mṛtaś ca saḥ ||
iti jātūkarṇyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam | vaiśyāgamane buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane kavaṣa āha |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathā sāntapanaṃ guroḥ |
bhāryāṃ vaiśyāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā māsaṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchaḥ, tayā protsāhitasya sāntapanam, svena protsāhitāyāṃ parākaḥ | abhyāse liṅgasyāgracchedaḥ kartavyaḥ | yathāha lokākṣiḥ |
guror vaiśyāṃ punar gatvā gatvā cāpi punaḥ punaḥ |
liṅgāgraṃ chedayitvā tu tataḥ śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti |
asmād eva jñāpakād abhyāse yad uktaṃ gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ tad eva bahuśo ‘bhyāse ‘pi draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane prajāpatir āha |
pañcarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt saptāṣṭau vā tathaiva ca |
vaiśyāṃ bhāryāṃ guror gatvā sakṛd ajñānato dvijaḥ || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau saptarātram, tayā protsāhitasya pañcarātram, svena prosāhitāyām aṣṭarātram iti | abhyāse tv ā maraṇāt brahmacaryarakṣaṇam | tathāha hārītaḥ |
abhyasya vipro vaiśyāyāṃ guror ajñānamohitaḥ |
saṣaḍaṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ sa cared yāvad āyuṣam || iti |
śūdrāgamane buddhipūrve jābālir āha |
atikṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā vipraś cared tataḥ || iti |
atrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchraḥ, tayā protsāhitasyātikṛcchraḥ, ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ parāka iti draṣṭavyam | abhyāse tu dvādaśavarṣaṃ brahmacaryarakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam | yathāha upamanyuḥ |
punaḥ śūdrāṃ guror gatvā buddhyā vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
brahmacaryam aduṣṭātmā sa cared dvādaśa samāḥ || iti |
ajñāne dīrghatapā āha |
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsakam |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā cared vipraḥ samāhitaḥ || iti |
atrāpy ubhayecchātaḥ pravṛttau sāntapanam, tayā protsāhitasya prājāpatyam, svena protsāhitāyāṃ saptarātram upavāsa iti | abhyāse tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasen niyatendriyaḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvāpi gurutalpāghanuttaye || iti | [MDh 11.106]
sādhāraṇastrīṇāṃ gurutalpadoṣo nāsti, vyāghravacanāt |
jātyuktaṃ paradāryaṃ ca kanyādūṣaṇam eva ca |
sādhāraṇastriyāṃ nāsti gurutalpatvam eva ca || iti |
ācāryavyatiriktāḥ pitrādayo guravaḥ, “ācāryaputraśiṣyabhāryāsu caivam” [VaDh 20.15] ity atideśād ācāryādīnāṃ bhāryāsu vasiṣṭhena || 23.11 ||
sakhīsayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsu snuṣāyāṃ gavi ca talpasamaḥ || 23.12 ||
sakhī mitram, sayoniḥ bhaginī, sagotrā ekārṣeyā | etāsu śiṣyabhāryāyāṃ putrabhāryāyāṃ ca maithunaṃ ācarite gurutalpatulyaṃ niṣkrayaṇam | visamāsaḥ sakhyādibhāryopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā sayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || iti | [MDh 11.170]
caśabda āśritādibhāryopasaṃgrahārthaḥ | tathāha vyāghraḥ |
āśritasyāpi viduṣa āhitāgneś ca yoginaḥ |
ācāryasya ca rājñaś ca bhāryāṃ pravrajitāṃ tathā ||
dhātrīṃ putrīṃ ca pautrīṃ ca sakhīṃ mātus tathaiva ca |
pituḥ sakhīṃ tathā gatvā gurutalpavataṃ caret ||
iti || 23.12 ||
avakara ity eke || 23.13 ||
eke tv avakīrṇivrataṃ kartavyam iti manyante na tu gurutalpavratam iti | itikaraṇaḥ saṃdehavyāvṛttyarthaḥ | yady evam ucyate[69] avakara eka iti tato ‘vakīrṇino ‘pi gurutalpasamo doṣa ity āśaṅkā syād iti | so ‘yaṃ vikalpo ‘bhisaṃdhyapekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | abhisaṃdhau gurutalpasamaḥ, anabhisaṃdhau tv avakīrṇivratam iti | evaṃ cātra pāradāryanimittaṃ kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā punar avakīrṇivratam iti draṣṭavyam | tathāha jātūkarṇiḥ |
ācāryādes tu bhāryāsu gurutalpavrataṃ caret |
avakīrṇivrataṃ caiva tataḥ kuryāt samāhitaḥ || iti |
gurutalpasame ‘pi pāradāryād adhikaṃ gurutalpād dhīnaṃ kalpyam | tad api paradāraprāyaścittam eva cāndrāyaṇād adhikaṃ draṣṭavyam | cāndrāyaṇasya ca varṇakrameṇa pādahīnakalpanā kartavyā | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
ācāryādeś tu bhāryāsu carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
krameṇa pādaśo hīnaṃ varṇānām iti ca sthitiḥ || iti |
avakīrṇiprāyaścittam api rahasyoktam “tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī” [GDh 25.1] ityādi vakṣyati | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ:
ācāryādes tu bhāryāsu gamane pāradāryaṃ parisamāpyāvakīrṇī varṇakrameṇa māsaṃ pakṣaṃ daśāhaṃ saptāhaṃ pañcāham ity agnāv ājyena juhuyāt, “kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smi kāma kamāya svāhā, kāmābhidrugdho ‘smy abhidrugdho ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāhā” iti dvābhyām | etaṃ homaṃ śrotriyabhāryāgamane dviguṇaṃ kuryād icchantyāṃ gamane ardham ity upadiśanti || iti |
sarvatra samīkaraṇaviṣaye sarvaprāyaścittam eva draṣṭavyam iti yad uktaṃ tad anyaprāyaścittānulabdhau draṣṭavyam || 23.13 ||
atra nihīnavarṇagamane striyāḥ pātityam uktam | tasyāḥ sāmānyataḥ patitaprāyaścitte
prāpta āha ||
śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā nihīnavarṇagamane striyam prakāśam || 23.14 ||
nihīnavarṇo vyākhyātaḥ “bhrūṇahani hīnavarṇasevāyāṃ ca” [GDh 21.9] ity atra | tadgamane tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā prakāśaṃ janasamakṣam | tathāha manuḥ |
bhartāraṃ laṅghayed yā tu jātistrīguṇadarpitā |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayed rājā saṃsthāne bahusaṃsthite || iti | [MDh 8.371]
abuddhipūrve ayaṃ rājadaṇḍaḥ, buddhipūrve vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
