śūdro brāhmaṇīm upagacchet vīraṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsyet, brāhmaṇyā śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kṛṣṇakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet, pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vaiśyaś cet brāhmaṇīm upagacchel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet, brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ gaurakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājanyaś cet brāhmaṇīm upagacchec charapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet brāhmanyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ śvetakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāyāṃ śūdraś ca rājanyavaiśyayoḥ || iti | [VaDh 21.1–5]
nihīnavarṇagamana ity uktatvāt kṣatriyavaiśyābhyāṃ buddhipūrvagamane brāhmaṇyāḥ kalpyam | yathāha manuḥ |
jaghanyaṃ sevamānāṃ tu saṃyatāṃ vāsayed gṛhe |
uttamāṃ sevamānas tu jaghanyo vadham arhati || iti | [MDh 8.366]
ayam eva kṣatriyāvaiśyāgamane ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ, sāmānyenoktatvāt | evaṃ ca nihīnavarṇa ity ayam api daṇḍo dvijātistrīṇāṃ sāmānyaḥ, sāmānyenoktatvād eva | anulomasaṃparke tu vyāghra āha |
varṇānām anulomānāṃ parasparasamāgame |
vyutkrameṇa tato rājā khādayed vānaraiḥ striyam ||
sṛgālair buddhipūrvam cet puruṣo vadham arhati |
ayam evānulomānāṃ svajātivyutkrameṣv api || iti |
pratilomasamāgame buddhipūrve cābuddhipūrve ca manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
pratilome vadhaḥ puṃso strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam | iti |
nanu ca:
etad eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsu patitāsv api | [MDh 11.188]
iti,
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu yac caināṃ cārayed vratam | [MDh 11.176]
iti ca siddhe ayaṃ daṇḍavidhir anarthaka iti |
atrocyate | yā svayam eva rājānaṃ gacchati tasyā daṇḍa eva, yayā tu balād ānīyate tasyā daṇḍaś ca prāyaścittaṃ ca, yā svayam api na gacchati na balād nānīyate tasyāḥ prāyaścittam eveti | ayam eva nyāyaḥ sarvatra daṇḍaprāyaścittayor draṣṭavyaḥ | atra pratilomānāṃ svajātivyutkrame “pratilomas tu dharmahīnaḥ” [GDh 4.25] iti prāyaścittābhāvād anyeṣāṃ saṃkaradoṣaparihārārthaṃ daṇḍaḥ kalpyaḥ | evaṃ ca,
pratilome vadhaḥ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ nāsādikartanam |
iti teṣām api draṣṭavyam | tathā pātakopapātakaviṣaye ‘pi brāhmaṇyā anulomānantarajasya yo daṇḍa uktaḥ tasyārthaṃ draṣṭavyam, tathaikāntaradvyantarayoś ca | kutaḥ?
caṇḍālasya samīpe tu nādhyetavyaṃ kadācana |
tathā pāraśavasyāpi caṇḍālārdho his sa smṛtaḥ ||
iti vyāghradharmaliṅgāt || 23.14 ||
pumāṃsaṃ ghātayet || 23.15 ||
prakāśam ity anuvartate | ghātanaprakāraś ca vasiṣṭhokto draṣṭavyaḥ | udāhṛtaś ca viśeṣāt | buddhipūrve ‘buddhipūrve sakṛdgamane abhyāse ca viśeṣavacanāntarābhāvāc ca sarvatra hananam eva draṣṭavyam | evaṃ varṇānām anulomānāṃ pratilomānāṃ ca svavargavyutkrame parasparavyutkrame ca hananam eva draṣṭavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantaravākyāni codāhṛtāni | evaṃ ca strīṇām api sakṛdgamane ‘bhyāse ca pūrvokta eva daṇḍa iti draṣṭavyam || 23.15 ||
yathoktaṃ vā || 23.16 ||
liṅgoddhāra ityādi yathoktaṃ vā śūdrasya draṣṭavyam | tatra sacchūdrasya yathoktam itarasyedam iti draṣṭavyam || 23.16 ||
gardabhenāvakīrṇī nirṛtiṃ catuṣpathe yajeta || 23.17 ||
avakīrṇī viplutabrahmacaryo brahmacārī | yathāha jātukarṇiḥ |
khaṇḍitaṃ vratinā reto yena syāt brahmacāriṇā |
kṣaraṇāt kāmataḥ prāhur avakīrṇīti taṃ budhāḥ || iti |
yatra tv anyat prāyaścittaṃ nāsti putrikākaraṇādau tatredaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ cānyatra kanyāgamanādiprāyaścittena saha prāyaścittadvayasiddhiḥ | gardabhena khareṇa kāṇenāvakīrṇī nirṛtidaivatyena catuṣpathe araṇya eva, na sarvatra laukike ‘gnau | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brahmacārī cet striyam upeyāt araṇye catuṣpathe laukike ‘gnau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabheta,  nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet” [VaDh 23.1–2] iti | tatrāśrotriyasya gardabhaḥ, śrotriyasya carur iti draṣṭavyam | sthālīpākavidhānena yajeta juhuyāt | rātrāv eva na divā | yathāha manuḥ |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
sthālīpākavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi || iti | [MDh 11.118]
atrāyaṃ homavidhir veditavyaḥ – ā pradhānayāgāt sthālīpākavidhānena homaṃ kṛtvā vasiṣṭhoktair mantraiḥ paśumāṃsair hotavyam, tatra “tasya juhuyāt” [VaDh 23.3]  ity uktatvāt | tatrāhutayaḥ – “kāmāya svāhā kāmakāmāya svāhā nirṛtyai svāhā rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāhā” [VaDh 23.3] ity catasraḥ | vapāhomatvāt sviṣṭakṛn nāsti “sviṣṭakṛd anyatra vapāhomājyahomābhyām” iti jaiminigṛhye pratipāditatvāt | evaṃ haviṣā hutvā punaḥ sarpiṣā vāyvādīnāṃ “saṃ mā siñcantu” ity anena mantreṇa hotavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
hutvāgnau vidhivad dhomān antataś ca sam ity ṛcā |
vāyvindraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ || iti | [MDh 11.119]
evam hutvā punar api sthālīpākavidhānena homaśeṣaṃ ninayed iti | abhyāsaviśeṣād dhomāvṛttir draṣṭavyā | yathāha kaṇvaḥ |
prathamadivase rātrāv avakīrṇī gardabhena tu |
yajed evaṃ yathābhyāsaṃ so ‘bdenaikena śudhyati || iti |
evaṃ cābhyāse homāvṛttir eva na kālāvṛttir iti || 23.17 ||
tasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya lohitapātraḥ sapta gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret karmācakṣāṇaḥ || 23.18 ||
tasya gardabhasyājinaṃ carma | adhikāreṇaiva siddhe tasya grahaṇaṃ yena gardabhena yāgaḥ tasyaiva carma nānyasyeti niyamārtham | tad api sāṅgam ūrdhvavālaṃ bahirloma, tad upari vasitvā | lohitapātraḥ tāmramayapātraḥ raktavarṇapātro vā | pratidinaṃ saptaiva gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret karmācakṣāṇaḥ “avakīrṇine bhikṣāṃ dehi” iti, evam “avakīrṇy asmi” iti kīrtayan | evaṃ ca bhaikṣācaraṇakāla eva carmaṇaḥ paridhānaṃ na homakāle ‘pi | tathā ca manur api homād ūrdhvam eva vakṣyati |
etasminn enasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam |
saptāgāraṃ caret bhaikṣaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan || iti | [MDh 11.122]
bhojanam apy ekakālam eva | yathāha manuḥ |
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayann ekakālikam |
upaspṛśan triṣavaṇam abdenaikena śudhyati || [MDh 11.123] iti || 23.18 ||
saṃvatsareṇa śudhyet || 23.19 ||
evam ācaran saṃvatsareṇa pūto bhavati | aviśeṣād dvijātīnām idaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathā ca manunā dvijātigrahaṃ kṛtam |
kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhur dharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.120]
evaṃ ca brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyety ayaṃ nyāyo ‘tra nāvatarati | yathāha śāṇḍilyo ‘pi |
avakīrṇī dvijo rājā vaiśyaś cāpi khareṇa tu |
iṣṭvā bhaikṣāśano nityaṃ śudhyaty abdāt samāhitaḥ || iti |
atra striyam antareṇa prayatnotsarge ‘pi homamātraṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “etad eva retasaḥ prayatnotsarge ‘pi” [VaDh 23.4] iti | tathā puruṣamaithune ‘pi | tathāha kāśyapaḥ |
puṃsi maithunam āsevya yatnotsarge kṛte tathā |
brahmacārī tathābhyāsāt snātvātha hiviṣā yajet || iti |
vānaprasthādīnāṃ cāyam eva kṛcchrādhiko draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vānaprastho dīkṣābhede kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā mahākakṣaṃ vardhayet, bhikṣur vānaprasthavat somavṛddhivarjam” iti | dvādaśarātragrahaṇam atra parākatrayopasaṃgrahaṇārtham | yathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva khaṇḍane sati kāmataḥ |
parākatrayasaṃyuktam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti |
caritaprāyaścittayor api saṃsargo na kartavyaḥ, yathāha kauśikaḥ |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ cāvakīrṇinām |
śuddhānām api loke ‘smin pratyāpattir na vidyate || iti |
yatīnāṃ vanasthānāṃ ca prayatnotsarge kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
yatnotsargaṃ gṛhī kṛtvā vāruṇībhir upaspṛśet |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva cared cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
gṛhyamantrais trir ācāmet bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti tribhiḥ |
yatir vanasthaś ca japet punar mām iti ṣoḍaśa || iti |
idam api svapnād anyatra | svapne ‘pi kāśyapa āha |
sūryasya trīn namaskārān svapne kṛtvā gṛhī caret |
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca triḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam || iti |
gṛhasthasya puruṣamaithune manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
maithunaṃ tu samāsevya puṃsi yoṣiti vā punaḥ |
goyāne ‘psu divā caiva sacelasnānam ācaret || iti |
evaṃ ca jātibhraṃśakaraprāyaścittam asmād anyatra draṣṭavyam | yoṣiti svabhāryāyām eva, ṛtau gamane ca | tathāha aṅgirāḥ |
ṛtau tu garbhaśaṅkitvāt snānaṃ mithuninaḥ smṛtam |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchec chaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat || iti |
divā maithune vāruṇībhiś ca apamārjanaṃ kartavyam | yathāha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ kṛtvā parvaṇi maithunam |
sacelaḥ snātvā divā ca vāruṇībhiś ca mārjayet ||
śāvāśauce tathā śrāddhe carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
saṃkrāntyām uparāge ca taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || iti |
puruṣamaithune vānaprasthaparivrājakayoḥ kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
puṃsi maithunam āsevya vānaprastho yatis tathā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kṛtvā śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt ||
iti || 23.19 ||
alam atiprasaṅgena | adhunā prastutam ārabhyate |
retaḥskandane ‘bhaye ‘roge ‘svapne ‘gnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni saptarātram akṛtvā ājyahomaḥ samidhor vā retasyābhyām || 23.20 ||
abuddhipūrvaviṣaya idam, buddhipūrve pūrvam uktatvāt | retaḥskande retasaḥ kṣaraṇe sati, abhaye aroge asvapne, agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇāni anāturaḥ saptarātram akṛtvā | tathā ca manuḥ: “akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam” [MDh 2.187] ityādi | ājyasamidhor vā homo retasyābhyām ṛgbhyām, “punar mām aitu, punar manaḥ” ity ābhyām | samidājyayoḥ saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ | svapne tu kṣaraṇe manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śuklam akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam arcayitvā triḥ punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || iti | [MDh 2.181]
bhayaroganimittābhyām api kṣaraṇe idam eva draṣṭavyam,
bhaye roge tathā svapne siktvā śuklam akāmataḥ |
ādityam arcayitvā tu punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet ||
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt | evaṃ ca vānaprasthaparivrājakayoś ca yad uktaṃ svapne retaḥseke tad eva bhayaroge ‘pi draṣṭavyam | naiṣṭhikasya yad uktaṃ brahmacāriṇas tad eva dviguṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha hārītaḥ |
upakurvāṇas tu yat kuryāt kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ kuryād brahmacārī ca naiṣṭhikaḥ || iti |
anye tu vyācakṣate – retaḥskandane svapne bhayarogau varjayitveti, teṣāṃ manunoktena tulyavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | svapnād anyatrāpi prāyaścittaṃ mṛgyam || 23.20 ||
sūryābhyudito brahmacārī tiṣṭhed ahar abhuñjāno ‘bhyastamitaś ca rātriṃ japan sāvitrīm || 23.21 ||
sūryābhyuditaḥ yasya śayānasya sūrya udetīty arthaḥ | brahmacārigrahaṇam adhikāralabdham api kriyate gṛhasthāder anyat prāyaścittam iti, teṣām api “uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi” [GDh 3.10] iti prāptir mā bhūd iti | gṛhasthasya tāvad āpastambhoktam ekīyaṃ mataṃ draṣṭavyam: “ā tamitoḥ prāṇam āyacched ity eke” [ĀpDh 2.12.15] iti | yativanasthayoś ca brahmakūrcasahita upavāsaḥ | yathāha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
vanasthaś ca yatiś caiva sūryeṇābhyuditau yadi |
brahmakūrcāśinau bhūtvā japetāṃ praṇavaṃ tv ahaḥ || iti |
brahmakūrcavidhānam api smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
pālāśaṃ padmapatraṃ vā tāmraṃ vāpi hiraṇmayam |
gṛhītvāsādayitvā ca tataḥ karma samārabhet ||
gāyatryā gṛhya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ||
śukram asi tejo ‘sīty ājyaṃ devasya tveti kuśodakam |
caturdaśyām upoṣyātha paurṇamāsyāṃ samācaret ||
gomayād dviguṇaṃ mūtraṃ sarpir dadyāc caturguṇam |
kṣīram aṣṭaguṇaṃ deyaṃ dadhi pañcaguṇaṃ tathā ||
sthāpayitvātha darbheṣu pālāśaiḥ patrakair atha |
tat samuddhṛtya hotavyaṃ devatābhyo yathākramam ||
agnaye caiva somāya savitre ca tathaiva ca |
praṇavena tathā hutvā sviṣṭakṛc ca tathaiva ca ||
evaṃ hutvā tataḥ śeṣaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā samāhitaḥ |
āloḍya praṇavenaiva nirmanthya praṇavena tu ||
uddhṛtya praṇavenaiva pibec ca praṇavena tu |
etad brahmakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ māsi māsi caran dvijaḥ ||
sarvapāpavimuktātmā svargalokaṃ sa gacchati |
yady apy asthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati dehinām |
brahmakūrco dahet sarvaṃ pradīptāgnir ivendhanam || iti |
tiṣṭhed ahar ahar abhuñjānaḥ nirāhāro ‘hani tiṣṭhet, rātrau tu bhoktavyam | evam abhyastam itaḥ yasya saṃdhyām upāsīnasya sato ravir astam eti asāv api rātrau nāśnīyāt | abhyāsanaṃ draṣṭavyam, “tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta” [GDh 26.6] iti liṅgāt | ubhayatra japan sāvitrīm | tathāha manuḥ |
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmakārataḥ |
nimroced vāpy avijñānāj japaṃs tūpavased dinam || iti | [MDh 2.220]
buddhipūrve abuddhipūrve ca idam eva, “nimroced vāpy avijñānāt” iti liṅgāt | atra sacelasnānaṃ draṣṭavyam, “sūryābhyuditanimruktaḥ sacelasnānaḥ sāvitrīṃ savanānugatāṃ japet” iti śaṅkhavacanāt | atrāpy “abhaye aroge” iti cānuvartate tatrādoṣakhyāpanārtham | tathāha jābāliḥ: “abhayarogasthaḥ sūryābhyuditaḥ prāyaścittīyo bhavati” iti | atra brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyety etad draṣṭavyam || 23.21 ||
aśuciṃ dṛṣṭvādityam īkṣeta prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā || 23.22 ||
aśuciḥ caṇḍālādiḥ, taṃ dṛṣṭvā ekaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā ādityaṃ paśyet | prakaraṇāt brahmacāriṇo niyamakāla idam | naiṣṭhikādīnāṃ manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed asucidarśane |
sauryān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.86]
gṛhasthasya praṇavo draṣṭavyaḥ, “aśucidarśane dvijaḥ praṇavaṃ japet” iti jābāligṛhyadarśanāt | sarveṣāṃ niyamakālād anyatrādityadarśanamātram eva, “aśucidarśana āditayadarśanaṃ brāhmaṇadarśanaṃ vā gavām agner vā” ity auśanasavacanāt | jābāligṛhye dvijagrahaṇāc ca dvijātīnām idaṃ sāmānyam, śūdrasya na vidhiḥ na pratiṣedhaḥ || 23.22 ||
abhojyabhojane niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ || 23.23 ||
abhojyānāṃ laśunādīnāṃ bhojane chardanena virecanena vā niṣpurīṣībhāvaḥ kartavyaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
abhojyam annaṃ nāttavyam ātmanaḥ śuddhim icchatā |
ajñātabhuktam udgāryaṃ śodhyaṃ vāpy āśu śodhanaiḥ || iti | [MDh 11.160]
jīrṇatvāt kālātikrame chardanavirecanāsaṃbhave smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā dadyād viprāya tadvyayam |
ārdravāsā dinaṃ tiṣṭhed yad vā dadyād gavāhnikam || iti |
parigrahaduṣṭabhojana idam, viprāya tadvyayaṃ dadyād yathābhyastaṃ tadasaṃbhave gavāhnikaṃ vā dadyāt, tasyāpy asaṃbhave haviṣyaṃ sakṛt bhuktvā jalārdravāsās tiṣṭhed yāvanti dināny abhyastāni tāvanty ahorātrāṇīti tatra kramaḥ | kālāśrayasvabhāvaduṣṭānāṃ vyāghroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
abhojyabhojanaṃ kṛtvā naktabhojanam ācaret |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ vāpi bhūñjīyāc ca dine dine ||
kapilāyās tu dugdhāyā dhāroṣṇaṃ goḥ payaḥ pibet |
eṣa vyāghrakṛtaḥ kṛcchraḥ śvapākam api śodhayet || iti |
atrāpi kāladuṣṭabhojane naktabhojanam, āśrayaduṣṭabhojane akṣāralavaṇānnabhojanam | svabhāvaduṣṭabhojane kapilākṣīrapānam iti | dine dine iti sarvaśeṣaḥ | yāvanti dināni bhuktāni tāvanti dinānīty arthaḥ | tatrāpi sakṛdbhojane pūrvaślokoktānāṃ madhye vyayadānādīnāṃ tāvanmātrasyaiva dānaṃ draṣṭavyam | ārdravāsastāpi dinamātram eveti | naktabhojanaśloke ‘pi sakṛdbhojane saṃpūrṇāhāratā dvirbhojane ardhāhārateti | kṣatriyavaiśyayos tv abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe ardhasyārdhārdhasya ca praveśe kavaṣokto nyāyaḥ | evaṃ ca sati vyayadānaślokoktānām ardham ardhaṃ parikalpyam | naktabhojanaśloke rasadravyarahitaṃ brāhmaṇasya, kiṃcidrasadravyasahitaṃ kṣatriyasya, tadbahulaṃ vaiśyasyeti draṣṭavyam | kṣīrapānaśloke ‘pi kṣatriyasya dviḥ pānam, vaiśyasya triḥ pānam iti | evaṃ caturvidhasya abhakṣyasyājñānabhojane chardanavirecanavyayadānāni kṛtvā vakṣyamānam “trirātrāvaram abhojanam” [GDh 23.24] ityādiprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | buddhipūrve vyayadānādi kṛtvā prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam, vyayadānādiśloke viśeṣābhāvāt | chardanavirecanaviṣaye “jñātaṃ dagdham” ity uktatvāc ceti
|| 23.23 ||
adhunā prāyaścittam āha |
trirātrāvaram abhojanam || 23.24 ||
“prāk pañcanakhebhyaḥ” [GDh 23.27] ity anenāśrayaparigrahaṇakāladuṣṭānāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt svabhāvaduṣṭānām idaṃ veditavyam | tatra laśunādīnāṃ paśūnāṃ ca “saptarātraṃ vā, svayaṃ śīrṇāni” [GDh 23.25–26] iti dvābhyāṃ sūtrābhyāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvāt, tebhyo ‘nyeṣām idam iti cāvagantavyam | tatrāpi buddhipūrve sakṛdbhojana idam, abuddhipūrve sakṛdbhojane manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ” [MDh 5.20] iti | atrāpi bakādīnāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ buddhipūrve sakṛdbhojane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “bakabalākacakravākahaṃsaplavakhañjarīṭakāraṇḍavavaṭahāracaṭakagṛhakapotaśuka-śārikakirakasārasaṭiṭṭibholūkakapotaraktapādajālapādacāṣabhāsamadgubhañjiśiṃśumāra-nakramakaratimitimiṅgilavalmīkalabhisamāṃsabhakṣaṇe dvādaśarātram anāhāraḥ pibed vā gomūtrayāvakam” iti | abuddhipūrve uśanā āha: “balākaplavahaṃsakāraṇḍavacakravāka-khañjarīṭagṛhakapotavaṭahāravīṭakaraktapādolūkaśukasārasaṭiṭṭibhabhāsamadgumahāṭiṭṭibha-cāṣabhāsajālapādanakrakulikavikṛtamatsyakravyādādīnāṃ māṃsabhakṣaṇe pañcagavyaṃ pibet trirātram” | avagūraṇāt sarvālpatvam ataś ca trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ veti sāmarthyāpekṣayā draṣṭavyam || 23.24 ||
saptarātraṃ vā || 23.25 ||
laśunādiviṣayam idam | yathāha manuḥ |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | [MDh 5.19]
evaṃ ca sāntapane na vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | abuddhipūrve sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsaś ca
|| 23.25 ||
svayaṃ śīrṇāny upayuñjānaḥ phalāny anatikrāman
|| 23.26 ||
vāśabdo ‘dhikriyate | śudhyed ity asyādhyāhāraḥ | phalāni svayaṃ śīrṇāni kākādibhir apāṭitāni | sthāvarahiṃsāpratiṣedhārthaṃ svayaṃśīrṇagrahaṇam | tāny upayuñjānaḥ anatikrāman guṇadoṣaparīkṣām akurvann ity abhiprāyaḥ | kṣutpramāpaṇam idam | iyam avasthā yāvatā kālena saṃpadyate tāvatā śudhyatīty arthaḥ | abhakṣyāṇām ekaśaphādīnāṃ paśūnaṃ bhakṣaṇe abuddhipūrve manunoktaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam |
jagdhvā tu śuṣkamāṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
ajñātaṃ caiva sūnāstham etad eva vrataṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.155]
buddhipūrve taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe vipraḥ paśūnāṃ buddhipūrvakam |
taptakṛcchreṇa sahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
iti || 23.26 ||
prāk pañcanakhebhyaḥ chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca
|| 23.27 ||
pañcanakhebhyaḥ prāg yad abhojyam uktaṃ tad abhyavahāre chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanam ca prāyaścittam, na pūrvavat prāyaścittāntaram api | niṣpurīṣībhāva ity anenaiva siddhe ārambhasāmarthyāc caśabdāt kvacid upavāsaś ca | asamāso doṣāpekṣayā vyastasamastakriyārthaḥ | tatra prāg abhiśastādibhyo ghṛtaprāśanamātram | ghṛtaprāśane cānyāhārapratiṣedhaḥ | tatprāśanaṃ ca yāvatā kāyāplavamātraṃ bhavati | prāg apāṅktyādibhyaś chardanaghṛtaprāśane, prāg gokṣīrāder ekarātropavāsaḥ, prāk pañcanakhebhyaś chardanamātram ity evaṃ kalpyam | apañcanakhānāṃ tūktam “śvāpadoṣṭrakharāṇāṃ cāṅgasya” [GDh 23.5] iti | abuddhipūrve sakṛdbhojana idam | buddhipūrve manunoktaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ draṣṭavyam |
bhuktvāto ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva vā || iti | [MDh 4.222]
atra abuddhipūrve chardanāsaṃbhave tryahopavāso draṣṭavyaḥ | eteṣām uktānāṃ caturvidhānāṃ buddhipūrvābhyāse paśulaśunādivarjitānāṃ pratilomapatitasūtakaśāvānnavarjitānāṃ ca manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha |
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || iti | [MDh 11.152]
aṅgirasoktaṃ vā |
abhakṣyāṇām apeyānām alehyānām ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
padmodumbarabilvānāṃ kuśāśvatthapalāśayoḥ |
eteṣām udakaṃ pītvā saptarātreṇa śudhyati ||
ity abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittam | śaktasya kṛcchro draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śva-kākāvalīḍhaśūdrocchiṣṭabhojaneṣv atikṛcchraḥ, kṛcchra itareṣu” [VaDh 14.33] iti | laśunādīnām abhyāse manunoktaṃ yaticāndrāyaṇam draṣṭavyam |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ || iti | [MDh 5.20]
paśūnām abhyāse cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ kuryād abhakṣyapaśubhakṣaṇe |
ekam eva sakṛt kṛtvā cared āha prajāpatiḥ || iti |
patito dviprakāraḥ – patito mahāpatita iti | mahāpatito mahāpātakī | yathāha manuḥ |
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ |
mahānti pātakāny āhuḥ saṃyogaṃ caiva taiḥ saha || iti | [MDh 11.53]
etebhyo ‘nyaḥ patitaḥ, tasya pātityaṃ dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ | tatra patita abhojyānnamadhye parigaṇitaḥ, aspṛśyatvād evetarasya siddhatvāt | evaṃ ca patito ‘spṛśyo na bhavati | yathāha uśanā: “patitopapātakāv abhojyānnau mahāpātako ‘spṛśyaś ca” iti | evaṃ ca mahāpatakina abhojyānnamadhye parigaṇitatvāt prāyaścittāntaraṃ draṣṭavyam | tatra pratilomamahāpātakinor akāmataḥ sakṛdbhojane atikṛcchro draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha hārītaḥ |
yad annaṃ pratilomasya śūdrajasyottamastriyāḥ |
mahāpātakinaś caiva yad annaṃ strīkṛtaghnayoḥ ||
ārūḍhapatitasyaiva sagotrābhartur eva ca |
pāṣaṇḍam āśritānāṃ ca yateś caiva tathaiva ca ||
atikṛcchraṃ cared bhuktvā pramādād brāhmaṇaḥ sakṛt |
matyā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryād āmaṃ ced ardham eva ca ||
abhojane tadardhaṃ ca triguṇaṃ sahabhojane |
caturguṇaṃ taducchiṣṭe pānīye ‘rdhārdham eva ca || iti |
evaṃ cāmaṃ ced ardham evetyādi sarvatra draṣṭavyam | amatyābhyāse ‘pi tathaiva śrūyate |
kṛcchrābdapādam ucchiṣṭam abhyāse ‘jñānabhojane |
matyābhyāse tathā kuryāt triṃśat kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ || iti |
tatra caṇḍālādīnāṃ matyābhyāse tatsāmyam eva draṣṭavyam | yathāha manuḥ: “caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā” [MDh 11.175] ityādi | mahāpātakaviṣaye ‘py āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam:
adhonābhy uparijānv ācchādya triṣavaṇam upaspṛśan akṣāralavaṇaṃ bhuñjāno dvādaśa varṣāṇi nāgāraṃ praviśet | atha siddhir atha saṃprayogaḥ syād āryair etad evānyeṣām api patanīyānām | iti | [ĀpDh 1.28.11–14]
pratilomānulomaviṣaye sarvatra prāyaścittaṃ kalpyam | pratilomānāṃ śūdraprabhavānām anulomānāṃ śūdrāyāṃ dvijātyutpannānāṃ ca śūdrānnabhojanavad draṣṭavyam | yathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
śūdrasya śūdrajasyaiva vyutkramāt tatstriyām api |
utpannasya dvijātīnāṃ samam annam akalpayan || iti |
śūdrānnabhojane tu prajāpatir āha |
brāhmaṇasyaiva śūdrānnam abhojyaṃ parikīrtitam |
sakṛd bhuktvā tad ajñānāt brahmakūrcaṃ sakṛt pibet ||
abhyāse tu tad abhyasyed āmam annam agarhitam |
matyā trirātraṃ kurvīta kṛcchram abhyāsakṛc caret || iti |
sūtakaśāvānnabhojane ‘pi smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha gargaḥ |
śāve ca sūtake caiva matyā bhuktvaindavaṃ caret |
matyābhyāse tataḥ kuryāt kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyanottaram ||
tryahaṃ tv akāmataḥ kuryād abhyāse kṛcchram eva ca || iti |
etad api brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇāśauce | kṣatriyādīnām āśauce ‘pi tatrivoktam |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi ca |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnām āśauce parikīrtitam || iti |
tatra brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇāśauce yat prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad eva kṣatriyādīnām api draṣṭavyam | dviguṇatriguṇakrameṇādhikaṃ hīnajātiṣu draṣṭavyam | uttamajātiṣu tatkrameṇaiva hīnam | tathā tatraivābhihitam |
samānajātiṣu sarveṣāṃ vipravan niṣkrayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kramād vṛddhaṃ kramād dhīnaṃ hīnajātyuttameṣv api || iti |
sarvatrāśaucottarakālam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam,
yady annam atti teṣāṃ yaḥ sa daśāhena śudhyati | [MDh 5.102]
iti liṅgāt | tatra niyogād bhuktasya vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
āśauce yas tu śūdrasya sūtake cāpi bhuktavān |
krimibhir bhūtvā svadehānte tāṃ viṣṭhām upajīvati ||
anirdaśāhe ca śave niyogāt bhuktavān dvijaḥ |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ tiryagyoniṣu jāyate || iti | [VaDh 4.31–32]
“dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān vā anaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate” [VaDh 4.33] iti | tatraivaṃ kalpyam – śūdrāśauce bhuktasya dvādaśa māsān vaiśyasya daśa māsān, kṣatriyasyāṣṭau, brāhmaṇasya dvādaśārdhamāsān iti | evaṃ laśunādīnāṃ pṛthag upalabdhatvāt tadvarjitānām eva manunoktaṃ saptarātraṃ yavāgūpānaṃ draṣṭavyam | upapātakamadhye pāṭho ‘pi tadvad asaṃvyavahārārthaṃ na prāyaścittapraveśārtham avakīrṇyādivat śāntyudakapraveśārthaṃ vā | buddhipūrvābhyāse surāpānasamaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam,
garhitānnādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ | [MDh 11.56]
iti manuvacanāt | tad api cāndrāyaṇadvayam | yathāha gargaḥ |
abhakṣyāṇām abhojyānāṃ kāmato bhojane kṛte |
cāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ kuryāt surāpānasamaṃ hi tat || iti |
bhojayitur dviguṇaṃ draṣṭavyam, “bhojayitur dvigunaṃ daṇḍaḥ” iti kātyāyanenoktatvāt
|| 23.27 ||
alam ativācālatayā | prastutaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
ākrośānṛtahiṃsāsu trirātraparamaṃ tapaḥ || 23.28 ||
ākrośe atyantapātakopapātakayuktād anyatra | anṛtaṃ sākṣipratiśrutyādānādau | hiṃsāyāṃ sthāvarāṇām, jaṅgamānām uktatvāt | trirātraparamaṃ sarvaṃ bahurātram iti yāvat | ata ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ trirātram iti kalpyam | tapo brahmacaryādy uktam | tat prāyaścittam | tatra brāhmaṇākrośe trirātram, kṣatriyākrośe dvirātram, vaiśyākrośa ekarātram, śūdrākośe ‘har iti | evam ākrośaviṣaye draṣṭavyam | anṛte ‘pi brāhmaṇādyarthe kramād draṣṭavyam | hiṃsāyāṃ ca phalavṛkṣe dvirātram, upajīvyacchāyāvṛkṣe ekarātram, evamādīnām aharmātram iti | idam api prayojanāntareṇa svāmyanujñayā vinā kṛtasyeti |
anye vyācakṣate – ākrośe dhvanimātreṇābhiśastena kenacit saṃmukham | saṃmukhābhiśaste tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśasya” ityādi | tatra pātakadhvanau trirātraṃ pātakasame dvirātram upapātaka ekarātram anyatrāharmātram iti | hiṃsāśabdena ca hiṃsāyāṃ kṛtavyavasāyina ucyante | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
jīvann ātmatyāgī kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret trirātraṃ copavasen nityaṃ snigdhena vāsasā prāṇān ātmani saṃyamya triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam iti | api caitena kalpena gāyatrīṃ parivartayed api vāgnim upasamādhāya kūśmāṇḍair juhuyāt ghṛtam ||
iti | [VaDh 23.19–21]
tatra brāhmaṇādīnāṃ śastrādinā vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvataḥ kṛcchraṃ buddhimātre trirātopavāsaḥ, kṣatriyasya vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvataḥ jalārdravāsasaḥ trirātram aghamarṣaṇapāṭho gāyatrījapo vā buddhimātre trirātraṃ tapomātram, vaiśyasya vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvato jalārdravāsasḥ haviṣyaṃ bhuktvā nityaṃ tris triḥ kūśmāṇḍair homaḥ buddhipūrve trirātraṃ tapomātram idam, śūdrasya vraṇādi kṛtvā jīvata ahorātraṃ buddhimātre ‘harmātram ity evaṃ kalpyam | anṛtaśabdārtha eṣām api |
apare vyācakṣate – ākrośaśabdena daṇḍapāruṣyam ucyata iti |
tad ayuktam, brāhmaṇasya tāvat brāhmaṇadaṇḍapāruṣyam uktaṃ prāyaścittam: “abhikruddhāvagūraṇam” [GDh 21.20] ityādi | tenaiva brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyety anenaiva sarveṣāṃ kalpayituṃ śakyam | ato vākpāruṣyaviṣaya evedam | tatrāpi śrotriyasyāśrotriyākrośa idam | aśrotriyasya śrotriyākrośe tu “śataṃ kṣatriyo brāhmaṇākrośe” [GDh 12.8] ityādi daṇḍānurūpaṃ parikalpyam | kṣatriyādīnāṃ svajātyākrośe ‘pi daṇḍānurūpaṃ kalpyam | daṇḍas tu manunoktaḥ |
asavarṇe dvijātīnāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame |
padeṣv avacanīyeṣu tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || iti | [MDh 8.269]
evaṃ śrotriyasyāpi prāyaścittānurūpo daṇḍaḥ parikalpyaḥ | yatra prāyaścittam eva vidhīyate daṇḍo noktaḥ tatra daṇḍaparikalpanā | yatra tu daṇḍa evokto na prāyaścittaṃ tatra prāyaścittaparikalpanā kartavyeti | evaṃ cāvacanīyapadeṣv api prāyaścittaṃ siddham | avacanīyam api kuṣṭhādibhir bahubhir ajātair abhiśaṃsanam, na pātakopapātakaviṣayam, tatra prāyaścittasya gurutvadarśanāt | anṛtadoṣaprāyaścittam apy āhitāgner arthaviṣaya idam | tathāha prajāpatiḥ |
anṛte somapaḥ kuryāt trirātraṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vā juhuyāt saptateti ghṛtena svāhā || iti |
anāhitāgner arthaviṣaye “vāṅmanasor apacāre vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca” [GDh 25.12] iti yad vakṣyati tad draṣṭavyam | tad rahasyam iti cet, na, “prāyaścittam aviśeṣāt” [GDh 25.9] ity uktatvāt rahasye prakāśe na bhavatīti | anarthaviṣaye dakṣiṇaśravaṇasparśanam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
anṛtoktau ṣṭhīvane ca dantasparśe kṣute tathā |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || iti |
sākṣibhāve tv anṛtenoktāv āhitāgnīnām anyeṣāṃ ca pātakasamatvam eva | yad uktaṃ “kauṭasākṣyam” [GDh 21.10] ityādi | pratiśrutyānṛtoktau hārīta āha |
pratiśrutyānṛtaṃ brūyān mithyā satyam athāpi vā |
sa taptakṛcchasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || iti |
brahmacāriṇo ‘pi viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha gargaḥ |
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā brahmacāry anṛte caret |
madhumāṃsāśane kṛcchraṃ śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti |
tatrārthaviṣaye trirātram, itaratraikarātram | māṃsasyābhakṣyasya bhakṣaṇe tu taptapūrvam idaṃ kartavyam | avakīrṇiprāyaścittavat abuddhipūrvaviṣayaṃ cet, buddhipūrvaviṣaye tu punaḥsaṃskāraś ca | tathāha prajāpatiḥ |
māṃsaṃ bhuktvā brahmacārī punaḥsaṃskāram ācaret |
abhyāse tv aindavaṃ caiva naiṣṭhiko dviguṇaṃ caret ||
vanasthaḥ triguṇaṃ kuryād yatiḥ kuryā caturguṇam |
māṃsāśane ‘nṛtoktau ca śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti |
śavanirharaṇaṃ tu brahmacāriṇa upādhyāyādivarjitānām | yathāha manuḥ |
ācāryaṃ svam upādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretaṃ na vratena viyujyate || iti | [MDh 5.91]
tatra svaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyata ity uktam | tataḥ svam ācāryam eva, nācāryasyācāryam, evaṃ sarvatra,
guror gurau saṃnihite guruvad vṛttir iṣyate | [MDh 2.205]
ity evaṃ mā bhūd iti | tataś ca tannirharaṇe vrataviyogo bhavaty eveti | tatra kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā punaḥsaṃskāra iti draṣṭavyam | ācāryādau vrataviyogābhāvād evāśaucasyāpy abhāvaḥ siddhaḥ, “śāvam āśaucaṃ daśarātram anṛtvigdīkṣitabrahmacāriṇāṃ sapiṇḍānām” [GDh 14.1] ity uktatvāt | daśāhād arvāg api piṇḍanirvāpaṇakāle aśucir bhavati | tathā śāṇḍilyaḥ |
brahmacārī yadā kuryāt piṇḍanirharaṇaṃ pituḥ |
tāvat kalam aśucaṃ syāt tataḥ snātvā viśudhyati || iti |
asnātvā bhojane prajāpatyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
asnātvaiva yadā bhuṅkte piṇḍaṃ datvā pitur vratī |
spṛṣṭvā śavam udakyāṃ vā caṇḍālaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā ||
akāmatas trirātraṃ syāt buddhyā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
gṛhasthādīnāṃ ca sūtikādisparśane bhojane smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
aspṛśyasparśanaṃ kṛtvā yadā bhuṅkte gṛhāśrami |
akāmatas trirātraṃ syāt ṣaḍrātraṃ kāmataś caret ||
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca kuryātāṃ tau tad eva tu || iti |
yatra tu brahmacāryādīnāṃ viśeṣo nāsti tatra sarveṣāṃ samānam eva draṣṭavyam | yathāha uśanā |
caṇḍālaśvapacau spṛṣṭvā viṇmūtrocchiṣṭam eva ca |
trirātreṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt bhuktvocciṣṭaṃ ṣaḍ ācaret || iti |
tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
caṇḍālodakyāsaṃspṛṣṭau snānam eva samācaret |
tenocchiṣṭena saṃspṛṣṭaḥ trirātraṃ tu samācaret || iti |
tathā yamo ‘pi |
caṇḍālodakabhāṇḍeṣu yaḥ pibet tṛṣito jalam |
tatkṣaṇāt kṣipate yas tu prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
yadi na kṣipet toyaṃ śarīre yasya jīryate |
prājāpatyaṃ na dātavyaṃ kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
tathā vyāso ‘pi |
caṇḍālabhāṇḍasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pibet toyam akāmataḥ |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
tataḥ snātvā tathā kuryāt kāmāt sāntapanaṃ caret |
teṣāṃ taṭāke kūpe ca tathaivoktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ || iti |
tathā vyāghro ‘pi |
viṇmūtrocchiṣṭakūpasthā apaḥ prāśya dvijātayaḥ |
trirātreṇaiva śudhyanti kumbhe sāntapanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tathā śātātapo ‘pi |
mūtroccāraṃ dvijaḥ kṛtvā akṛtvā śaucam ātmanaḥ |
mohād bhuktvā trirātraṃ tu buddhyā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
tathā vṛddhavasiṣṭho ‘pi |
ekapaṅktigato bhuṅkte na spṛśed itaraṃ yadi |
bhasmanā kṛtamaryādas tasya paṅktir na duṣyati || iti |
tathā śaṅkhaḥ: “antarudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe sacelasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca” iti | tathā tatraivāparam api “retomūtrapurīṣāṇy udake kṛtvā trirātropoṣita idam āpaḥ pravahata iti japet” iti | tatraiva “duḥsvapnāriṣṭadarśane ghṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ dadyāt” iti | evam anyāny api smṛtyantarāṇi draṣṭavyāni || 23.28 ||
satyavāk ced vāruṇīmānavībhir homaḥ || 23.29 ||
yadi tu satyavākyenāvaruṣṭo bhavati satyenākrośati vā | evaṃ ca pūrvam asatyeneti gamyate | tato vāruṇīmānavībhiḥ prajāpatidevatābhiś ca ājyena, pūrvoktaṃ vā trirātraṃ homaḥ kāryaḥ | aviśeṣāt sakṛd eva homaḥ | vāruṇībhis tisṛbhis tathā mānavībhiś ca | bahuvacanaprayogād vikalpitābhiḥ samuccitābhiś ca | tatra śrotriyākrośe aśrotriyasya samuccitābhiḥ viparīte vikalpitābhir iti draṣṭavyam || 23.29 ||
vivāhamaithunanarmārtasaṃyogeṣv adoṣam eke ‘nṛtam
|| 23.30 ||
vivāhakāle kanyāgateṣu varagateṣu vā avalakṣaṇeṣu śobhanakathane nānṛtakathanadoṣa ity abhiprāyaḥ | maithune gotraskhalanādau | narma parihāsaḥ | ārtena duḥkhitena ca saṃyoge yat tad duḥkhaśamanāyocyate, eteṣu nimitteṣu anṛtābhidhāne prāyaścittābhāvo doṣābhāvād ity eke manyante | gautamas tv eteṣv api doṣo ‘stīti manyate | tatra niyamasthasya gautamābhiprāyo draṣṭavyaḥ, itarasyaikīyaṃ matam iti || 23.30 ||
na tu khalu gurvartheṣu || 23.31 ||
guruprayojaneṣv avivāhādiṣv apy anṛtaṃ na vadet | khalv iti nipāto ‘vadhāraṇārthaḥ | na kadācid apy ekīymatenāpi | tuśabdo viśeṣārthaḥ | mātulādigurvartheṣv api na vadet viśeṣataḥ ācāryārtheṣv iti || 23.31 ||
kiṃ punaḥ tatra kāraṇam? idam eva |
sapta pūruṣān itaś ca parataś ca hanti manasāpi guror anṛtaṃ vadann alpeṣv apy artheṣu || 23.32 ||
sapta āgāminaḥ putrādīn itaḥ parataś cātītān api pitrādīn hanti pīḍayati pāpena yojayati | manasāpi gurvartham anṛtaṃ cintayann api, kim u vaktavyaṃ vadann ity arthaḥ | alpakeṣv api prayojaneṣu, kim uta mahatsu || 23.32 ||
antāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ || 23.33 ||
antāvasāyinī vyākhyātā “yaś cāntāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃviśet” [GDh 20.1] iti yatra | tasyāṃ maithuna ācarite sakṛdgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ | saṃvatasraṃ prājāpatyavidhināvasthānam ity arthaḥ || 23.33 ||
amatyā dvādaśarātraḥ || 23.34 ||
evaṃ ca pūrvaṃ buddhipūrva ity avagamyate | kṛcchra iti vaktavye dvādaśarātragrahaṇaṃ parākopasaṃgrahārtham | tathāha aṅgirāḥ |
antyajānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt bhagavān aṅgiro ‘bravīt || iti |
idam api retaḥsekāt prāg evoparatasya | kutaḥ? “retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu” [MDh 11.170], iti pūrvakālakriyāsmaraṇāt | tasmāt prāyaścittalāghavam avagamyata iti | evaṃ ca kumārīgamanādāv apy ardhadaṇḍaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | gamane tu nirvṛtte vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavaset | aśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ vā gacchet | etenaiva caṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” [VaDh 23.38, 40–41] iti | buddhipūrve ‘bhyāse gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrāpi samaśabdopādānāt manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “sahasraṃ tv antyajāṃ striyam” [MDh 8.385] iti daṇḍavidhānāt sahasradaṇḍatulyatvāc cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ draṣṭavyam | tatrābuddhipūrve dvitīyagamane yāvat prāyaścittaṃ tāvad evāgarbhotpatteḥ draṣṭavyam, nādhikakalpanāsti | kutaḥ?
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ |
vrātyayā saha saṃvāse caṇḍālyā tāvad eva ca || [MDh 8.373]
iti liṅgāt | kūśmāṇḍahomo vā draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha prajāpatiḥ |
sarvastrīṇāṃ ca saṃyogo gamane ca dvijottamaḥ |
prāyaścittād asaṃtuṣṭaḥ kūśmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam || iti |
garbhotpattau satyāṃ prāyaścittābhāvaḥ | yathāha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
antyastrīṣu prasūtasya dvijasyānicchayāpi ca |
niṣkṛtir nocyate tasya jātyā caṇḍāla eva saḥ || iti |
buddhipūrve ‘bhyāse ca prāyaścittābhāvaḥ |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || [MDh 11.175]
iti manuvacanāt | atrānnabhojane yad uktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tadardhaṃ pratigrahe ‘pi kalpyam | kutaḥ?
pakvād apakvam ardhaṃ syād yad anyat bhakṣaṇāgatam |
tasmād ardham idaṃ proktaṃ yad anyad dravyam ucyate ||
iti vyāghravacanāt || 23.34 ||
udakyāgamane trirātras trirātraḥ || 23.35 ||
striyā rajasvalāyā gamane trirātram abhojanam | gamanādhikāre punar gamanagrahaṇaṃ svadārād anyatra tadadhikapraveśārtham | svadāre cāsyaiva praveśārtham | tatraiva pratipattavyam – abuddhipūrve tu sakṛdgamane śātātapoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “anudakamūtrapurīṣagrahaṇe śvakākasparśane sacelasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca rajasvalāgamane caitad evam” iti | abuddhipūrve ‘bhyāse ca vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “rajasvalāgamane śuklaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt kṛṣṇaliṅgam” iti | buddhipūrve tu sakṛdgamana idam | abhyāse tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
amānuṣīṣu govarjam udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | [MDh 11.173]
atra kṣatriyāyāṃ dviguṇaṃ vaiśyāyāṃ triguṇam iti draṣṭavyam | tathā brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyasyetyādi veditavyam | gamanaṃ prati kūśmāṇḍahomaś ca draṣṭavyaḥ | dviruktir adhyāyaparisamāptyarthā || 23.35 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye trayoviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
caturviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
āviṣkṛtainasāṃ prāyaścittam uktam | anāviṣkṛtainasāṃ tu prāyaścittaṃ vaktavyam ity ata āha |
rahasyaṃ prāyaścittam avikhyātadoṣasya || 24.1 ||
rahasyam aprakāśaṃ kartavyaṃ yathānyair na jñāyate | prāyaścittagrahaṇam alpenāpi mahataḥ pāpasya niṣkṛtir bhavatīty evamartham | avikhyātadoṣasya lokenāvijñātainasaḥ | yenāntareṇa yat kartuṃ na śakyate tasmād anyair avijñātainasa ity arthaḥ | pāradāryapatitasaṃprayogādau na kevalaṃ kartur eva vijñānam, asaṃbhavāt | kathaṃ punar aprakāśe pāpe ‘lpadoṣatvam, na punar viṣabhakṣaṇādivad ekarūpateti | nātropapattir anveṣṭavyā, vacanagamyatvāt, matipūrvāmatipūrvabheda iva | atha vā avaśyakartavyatvād idam ucyate | yo hi prakāśaṃ pāpaṃ karoti sa ubhayaloke nirapekṣaḥ pravartate, tasya bahutaraḥ saṃkalpo bhavati, tataś ca doṣabhūyastvam, anyeṣāṃ pravṛttau hetubhāvaṃ ca pratipadyate gatānugatiko hi prāyaśo loka iti | tataś ca prakāśe lokanindānivṛttiḥ prāyaścittaṃ ca kartavye itaratra tu pāpakṣapaṇamātram eveti
|| 24.1 ||
caturṛcaṃ taratsamandīty apsu japed apratigrāhyaṃ pratijighṛkṣan pratigṛhya vā || 24.2 ||
“tarat sa mandi dhāvati” ity etāś catasra ṛco ‘ghamarṣaṇarūpeṇāpsu japet, anyathā anyair jñāyata iti | trirātraṃ japo draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs taratsamadhīyaṃ pūyate mānavas tryahāt || iti | [MDh 11.253]
haviṣyabhojane ca draṣṭavyam – apratigrāhyasya jātikarmaduṣṭasya yad dravyaṃ tat pratijighṛkṣan pratigrahītum icchan | evaṃ ca bruvann etad darśayati kāmakārakṛte ‘pi pāpe rahasyam asti icchāmātre ‘py apratigrāhyasya doṣo ‘stīti | “pratiṣiddhaṃ vāṅmanasor apacāre” [GDh 25.12] iti yad vakṣyati tasmād anyatra draṣṭavyam | pratigṛhya vā etad eva kuryāt | vāśabdād abhojyaṃ bhuktvā ca | tatra yadā pūrvam apratigrāhyo ‘yam iti  na jānāti tadā pratigṛhya japet, itaratra pūrvam iti draṣṭavyam | atrābhojyabhojane mūtrādivarjitasya draṣṭavyam, tatra śaṅkhenoktatvāt: “retomūtrapurīṣaprāśane oṃkāreṇābhimantryāpaḥ pibet” iti | yatra japaviśeṣo noktaḥ tatraikādaśāvaro draṣṭavyaḥ | tatra buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhede etāvān viśeṣaḥ – ‘buddhipūrvaṃ kṛto mayāpacāras tasyedaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhaved’ ity evaṃ saṃkalpya kartavyam, tāvatā ca gurur bhavatīti | tathāha āpastambaḥ: “yaḥ pramatto hanti prāptaṃ doṣaphalaṃ saha saṃkalpena bhūyaḥ” [ĀpDh 1.29.2–3] iti | abuddhipūrve tu saṃkalpena vinā prāyaścittamātram eva kartavyam iti | tathā vasiṣṭho ‘pi pāpasya dhyānena prāyaścittasya gauravaṃ darśayati: “manasā pāpaṃ dhyātvoṃpūrvā vyāhṛtīr aghamarṣaṇaṃ vā paṭhet triḥ” [VaDh 23.23] iti | tataś ca buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena prāyaścittakalpanā | abhyāse ‘pi tad evābhyaset,
japādipūraṇaṃ kuryāt khyātadoṣo dvijottamaḥ |
rahaḥ kṛtasya pāpasya tat tad evābhyaset tathā ||
iti prajāpativacanāt || 24.2 ||
abhojyaṃ bubhukṣamāṇaḥ pṛthivīm āvapet || 24.3 ||
abhojyaṃ caturvidhaṃ pūrvoktam[70] | tad bubhukṣamāṇo bhoktum icchan | āpatpradarśanārthaḥ bubhukṣamāṇa ity asyopanyāsaḥ | tataś ca tadupabhogam antareṇa yadi jīvanaṃ na saṃbhavatīti draṣṭavyam | pṛthivīm āvapet prakṣipet |
apare tu “pṛthivyām āvaped” iti paṭhanti | vyācakṣate ca pṛthivyāṃ prakṣiped iti |
tad ayuktam, evaṃ saty anyair[71] vijñāyata iti || 24.3 ||
ṛtvantarāramaṇa udakopasparśanāc chuddim eke
|| 24.4 ||
ekeśabdasaṃbandhārthā dvitīyā | ṛtumadhye āramaṇe uadkyāgamana ity arthaḥ | udakopasparśanāt sacelasnānāc chuddhim eka āhuḥ | gautamas tu sacelaṃ snātvā abliṅgābhir vāruṇībhiś cāpāmārjanam apīcchati | tathā ca vakṣyati: “anārjavapaiśuna” [GDh 25.10] ityādi | āramaṇa ity uktatvāt svadāraviṣayam idam | paradāreṣv etadadhikaṃ paradāraprāyaścittaṃ drṣṭavyam | tad api vakṣyati: “tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī praviśati” [GDh 25.1] ityādi || 24.4 ||
eke ‘strīṣu || 24.5 ||
eke astrīṣu vaḍavādyāsu govarjaṃ maithunam āsevya etad evodakopasparśanam icchanti | gautamas tu vakṣyati: “anārjava” [GDh 25.10] ityādau | gavi manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
tryahaṃ tūpavased yuktas trir ahno ‘bhyupayann apaḥ |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ trir japitvāghamaraṇam || iti | [MDh 11.259]
eke varṇayanti – strīṣu vyabhicāriṇīṣv etad eva prāyaścittam iti |
tad ayuktam,
na strī duṣyati jāreṇa na vipro vedakarmaṇā |
nāpo mūtrapurīṣeṇa nāgnir dahanakarmaṇā || [VaDh 28.1]
iti vasiṣṭhenoktatvāt | evaṃ cāpadi vedakarmaṇā duṣṭasya viprasyāpy adoṣo draṣṭavyaḥ || 24.5 ||
rahasye brāhmaṇavadha idānīm āha |
payovrato vā daśarātraṃ ghṛtena dvitīyam adbhis tṛtīyam || 24.6 ||
payovrataḥ kṣīrāhāraḥ | evaṃ prathamadaśarātram, dvitīyaṃ ghṛtena, tṛtīyam adbhir varteta | vāśabdād etat triṃśadrātraṃ haviṣyabhojano vā | śaktito vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 24.6 ||
tasya guṇavidhānam āha |
divādiṣv ekabhaktiko jalaklinnavāsāḥ || 24.7 ||
divādiṣu pūrvāhṇeṣu ekabhaktikaḥ sāyam abhuñjāna ity arthaḥ | bahuvacanāt triṣu daśarātreṣu payovratapakṣa idam | tataś ca haviṣyabhojane dvirbhojanam aviruddham | jalaklinnavāsāḥ ārdravāsāś ca syāt | idam api “tadvrata eva” [GDh 24.12] ityādau na bhavatīti draṣṭavyam, prakāśo mā bhūd iti || 24.7 ||
lomāni nakhāni tvacaṃ māṃsaṃ śoṇitaṃ snāyv asthi majjānam iti homā ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomītyantaḥ sarveṣām || 24.8 ||
lomādayo mantrāḥ | katham avagamyate? nāyaṃ dravyopadeśaḥ, itikaraṇāntopadeśāt, antaḥ sarveṣām ity anuṣaṅgārambhāt, homagrahaṇāc ca | tataś caitair mantrair ājyahomam agnau kuryāt | ātmano mukha ity anuṣaṅgaḥ sarveṣāṃ lomādīnām | “lomāny ātmano mukhe mṛtyor āsye juhomi” ityādiprayogaḥ || 24.8 ||
prāyaścittaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ || 24.9 ||
etad yathoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ | prāyaścittādhikāre punaḥ prāyaścittagrahaṇam adṛṣṭārtham evaitat na tu dṛṣṭārtham apīti | bhrūṇagha iti puruṣanirdeśe kartavye pāpanirdeśaḥ sakṛt kriyāyām evaitad iti jñāpanārtham | abhyāse tu gurutaraṃ kalpyam, yan manunoktam |
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣann apanodanam |
avety ṛcaṃ japed abdaṃ yat kiṃcedam itīti ca || [MDh 11.252] || 24.9 ||
athānyad, ukto niyamaḥ || 24.10 ||
athānyat prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate | pūrvaṃ vaikalpikam | pūrvaprāyaścittāt bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ anyad iti | ukto niyamaḥ “payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādi | viśeṣās tu vidhīyante || 24.10 ||
agne tvaṃ pārayeti mahāvyāhṛtibhir juhuyāt kūśmāṇḍaiś cājyam || 24.11 ||
“agne tvaṃ pāraya” [RV 1.189.2] ityādinā mantreṇa mahāvyāhṛtibhiś ca bhūrādyaiḥ kūśmāṇḍaiś ca “yad deva devaheḍanam” [GDh 27.7] ityādibhiś cānvahaṃ krameṇa sakṛd ājyaṃ juhuyāt | ājyagrahaṇaṃ siṃhāvalokitanyāyena pūrvatrāpy adhikartavyam | yatra yatra dravyānuddeśas tatra tatra sarvatreti kecit | cakārāt praṇavena ca || 24.11 ||
tadvrata eva vā brahmahatyāsurāpānasteyagurutalpeṣu prāṇāyāmais tānto ‘ghamarṣaṇaṃ japet samam aśvamedhāvabhṛthena || 24.12 ||
tenaiva coktena “payovrato vā” [GDh 24.6] ityādinā brahmahatyādicaturṣu pāpeṣu prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | steyaṃ cātra suvarṇasteyaṃ brahmahatyāsurāpānagurutalpasāhacaryāt | prāṇāyāmais tāntaḥ khinnaḥ aghamarṣaṇam “ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca” ityādi japet | samaṃ tulyam etad aśvamedhāvabhṛtena | surāpānādiṣv aśvamedhāvabhṛtasnānaṃ śuddhihetur ity etad anena darśayati | daṇḍāpūpikayāśavmedho ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭ sarvapāpapraṇodanaḥ |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || [MDh 11.260]
iti || 24.12 ||
sāvitrīṃ vā sahasrakṛtva āvartayet punīte haivātmānam
|| 24.13 ||
adhikārāt tadvrata evaiteṣv eva pāpeṣu māsaṃ sāvitrīṃ bahukṛtvo ‘bhyasan śīghraṃ śuddhim āpnoti | heti nipātaḥ vaidikārthatvapradarśanārthaḥ | evety avadhāraṇārthaḥ | vety etad vacanam anyeṣv api pāpeṣu sāvitryabhyāsaḥ śuddhihetur iti pradarśanārtham | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |
śuddhikāmaḥ prayuñjīta sarvapāpeṣv avasthitaḥ || iti | [VaDh 26.15]
avadhāraṇaṃ pāvanatamatvapradarśanārthaṃ draṣṭavyam | yathāha vyāghraḥ |
na sāvitrīsamaṃ japyaṃ na vyāhṛtisamaṃ hutam |
nānnatoyasamaṃ dānaṃ na cāhiṃsāsamaṃ tapaḥ ||
iti || 24.13 ||
antarjale vāghamarṣaṇaṃ trir āvartayan sarvapāpebhyo mucyate mucyate || 24.14 ||
tadvrata evodakasyāntas triṃśadrātram aghamarṣaṇaṃ trir abhyasan sarvasmāt pāpāt jñānājñānakṛtāt mucyate śudhyatīty arthaḥ || 24.14 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye caturviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
pañcaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktāni mahāpātakeṣu rahasyāni | athedānīm upapātakeṣu vaktavyāni | tatrāvakīrṇinaḥ upapātakitvāt prāyaścittasyānyatrātideśāc ca tasya pūrvam ucyate | tatra nindātiśayayajñāpanārthā śrutir upanyasyate |
tad āhuḥ katidhāvakīrṇī praviśatīti || 25.1 ||
taddoṣagurutvam asya paśyanto viśiṣṭā āhuḥ – kathidhā katiprakāram | avakīrṇiśabdo hy aviplutasya vācakaḥ, raktasyeti yāvat | tena kvāsya prāṇādi gacchatīty abhiprāyaḥ || 25.1 ||
evaṃ saṃśayam utpādya svayam evāha |
marutaḥ prāṇenendraṃ[72] balena bṛhaspatiṃ brahmavarcasenāgnim evetareṇa sarveṇeti || 25.2 ||
maruto vāyūn prāṇena pañcavṛttinā praviśati, indraṃ balena utsāhaśaktyā, bṛhaspatim adhītaśrutyādijanitena tejasā, agnim itareṇa | evakārān nānyaṃ manaādi | sarvagrahaṇāc cakṣuḥśrotrādinā | aviplato rikto ‘lpāyur nirutsāho brahmavarcasahīnaś cakṣurādihīnaś ca saṃpadyate | seyam udvegajananārthaṃ prāyaścittaprasaṅgārthaṃ vā śrutir udāhṛtā || 25.2 ||
idānīṃ prāyaścittam āha |
so ‘māvāsyāyāṃ niśy agnim upasamādhāya prāyaścittājyāhutīr juhoti || 25.3 ||
avakīrṇīti prastutatvāt sa ity anarthaka iti cet, rahasi khaṇḍitasyaiva parāmarśārthaḥ, anyathā śrutyudāharaṇāt sarveṣāṃ brahmacāriṇām idaṃ prāyaścittam ity āśaṅkā syād iti | amāvāsyāyāṃ kṛṣṇaparvaṇi niśi ardharātra iti draṣṭavyam, anyathā prakāśaḥ syād iti | agnim upasamādhāya kasmiṃścit kāṣṭhe mathitvety arthaḥ | itarathā juhotivacanād evāgneḥ siddhatvād avacanīyam evaitat syāt | prāyaścittagrahaṇaṃ vyatikramānantaraṃ prāyaścittānuṣṭhānajñāpanārtham, smṛtyantaroktasya vā
sāvitrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ | [MDh 11.225]
ityevamādeḥ sāmānyasya prāyaścittavidheḥ prāpaṇārtham | ājyagrahaṇaṃ ca niyamārtham | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiḥ: “catasra ājyaprakṛtayo bhavanty ūdhasyaṃ vā bāhyaṃ dadhi payo vā” ity āhutigrahaṇam avahitena homārtham || 25.3 ||
kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāhā | kāmābhidugdho ‘smy abhidugdho ‘smi kāma kāmāya svāheti || 25.4 ||
sakalaḥ mantrapāṭhaḥ pratyakṣaśrutau tathopalabhyamānatvāt || 25.4 ||
samidham ādhāyānuparyukṣya yajñavāstu kṛtvopotthāya saṃ mā siñcantv ity etayā trir upatiṣṭhet || 25.5 ||
mantradvayahomānta ekāṃ samidhaṃ tūṣṇīm eva hutvā tataḥ paryukṣya “adite ‘nvamaṃsthāḥ” ity evamādibhiḥ | tato yajñavāstu kṛtvā tat chandogānāṃ gṛhye paribhāṣitam | tatrāgisamīpe paścād utthāya “saṃ mā siñcantu” ity etayā prakṛtam agniṃ trir upatiṣṭhet || 25.5 ||
agnyupasthānastutyarthaṃ vaidikārthavādam āha |
trayo vā ime lokā eṣāṃ lokānām abhijityā abhikrāntyā
iti || 25.6 ||
trayo lokā ime bhūrbhuvaḥsvarlokākhyāḥ | teṣām abhijitiḥ apracyavanam abhikrāntis tatraivotpattiḥ | abhijityā abhikrāntyā iti ca bruvan akṛtaprāyaścittasya avakīrṇinas triṣv api lokeṣv anavakāśaṃ darśayti | vaiśabdo ‘narthakaḥ, “anarthakāni vaidikākṣarāṇī” ity uktatvāt | “ime lokāḥ” iti pratyakṣeṇa nirdeśa ādarārthaḥ || 25.6 ||
etad evaikeṣāṃ karmādhikṛtya yo pūta iva syāt sa itthaṃ juhuyād ittham anumantrayeta || 25.7 ||
etad eva karmādhikṛtya kṛtvety arthaḥ | ekeṣāṃ matena yo pūta iva syāt | adhikārād rahasyaprāyaścitte ‘pi kṛte yo pūtam ivātmānaṃ manyeta sa etam eva juhuyāt evam evāgnim upatiṣṭheta || 25.7 ||
varo dakṣiṇeti || 25.8 ||
ante karmaṇo deyaḥ, svayaṃkartṛkatvād anyasmai brāhmaṇāya deyā | gauravān mahāpātakaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | gautamasya tu tasyaiva prāyaścittasyāvartanam || 25.8 ||
prāyaścittam aviśeṣāt || 25.9 ||
upapātakināṃ sarveṣāṃ sāmānyam uktam eva prāyaścittam | kecid uparitanasūtraśeṣatvena vyācakṣate – prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyamāṇam aviśeṣād rahasye prakāśe ceti | tatra prakāśe vihitānāṃ śeṣatvena varṇanīyam, rahasye tu svātantryarūpeṇa | prāyaścittagrahaṇam alpe ‘pi nānādaraḥ kārya iti || 25.9 ||
anārjavapaiśunapratiṣiddhācārānādyaprāśaneṣu śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvāyonau ca doṣavati karmaṇy abhisaṃdhipūrve ‘py abliṅgābhir apa upaspṛśed vāruṇībhiḥ || 25.10 ||
anārjavam anṛjutvaṃ mānasaṃ karma śāṭhyaṃ vā | paiśunaṃ paraparivādaḥ vācikam | pratiṣiddhācāro niyamalopaḥ kāyikam | anādyasyānekavidhasyopabhogaḥ anādyasya prāśanam | śūdrāyāṃ ca retaḥ siktvā maithunaṃ samāsevya | pṛthagupadeśaḥ parigṛhītāyām apīty evamarthaḥ | asyopapātakaprāyaścittam eva yady api bhavati, tathāpy āhatyopadeśād idam eveti draṣṭavyam | evaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvam ālocya sarvatra vyavasthā kartavyā | caśabdo rajasvalāyām upasaṃgrahārthaḥ | ayonau ca āsyādau | caśabdāt prayatnotsarge ca | doṣavati ca karmaṇi parapīḍāhetau karmaṇi kṛte | caśabdād vihitākaraṇe ca | abhisaṃdhipūrve ‘pi tādarthyenopakalpite ‘pīty arthaḥ | anādyaprāśanādi yady api pratiṣiddhasevanam, tathāpi yatnato varjanārthas teṣāṃ pṛthagupadeśaḥ, prāyaścittagauravārtho vā | tataś ca bahubhir mārjanaṃ draṣṭavyam | doṣavat karma steyam ity eke | tatra suvarṇasteyād anyad iti draṣṭavyam, tasya “brahmahatyāsurāpāna” [GDh 24.12] ity uktatvāt | hiṃsety apare | tatrāpi pātakopapātakaṃ varjayitveti draṣṭavyam, tayor uktatvāt | abhisaṃdhipūrve ‘pīty apiśabdo ‘bhyāse ‘pīti kecid vyācakṣate | tad ayuktam, pratyabhyāsaṃ prāyaścittāvṛtter uktatvāt | anabhisaṃdhipūrve ‘pīty evamarthaṃ tatra laghutvaṃ mā bhūd iti | abliṅgābhiḥ abdevatābhir āpohiṣṭhetyādikābhiḥ | bahuvacanāt tryavarābhiḥ | vāruṇībhiḥ varuṇadevatābhiḥ “imaṃ me varuṇa” ityevamādibhiḥ | pūrvaṃ snātvāpo mūrdhni nikṣipet | visamāsanirdeśo vikalpārthaḥ | tataś cābliṅgābhir vāruṇībhir veti draṣṭavyam || 25.10 ||
anyair vā pavitraiḥ || 25.11 ||
anyair aghamarṣaṇādibhir vā pavitrāṇām eva grahaṇaṃ yathā syād iti pavitragrahaṇam | saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ || 25.11 ||
pratiṣiddhavāṅmanasor apacāre vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca
|| 25.12 ||
pratiṣiddhāpacāre vāṅmanasor apacāre ca trayāṇāṃ pūrvaprāyaścittam | viśeṣārtham idam – vyāhṛtayo bhūrādyāḥ puruṣasatyāntāḥ pañca abliṅgābhir mārjanānantaram ādityopasthānarūpeṇaikādaśavāram udāhartavyāḥ || 25.12 ||
sarvāsv apo vācāmed ahaś ca mādityaś ca punātv iti sāyam || 25.13 ||
sarvāsu kriyāsu anārjavādikarmasv ity arthaḥ | sarvāsu vyāhṛtiṣu pratyekam ācamanam iti kecit | tad ayuktam, sarvābhir iti prasaṅgāt | anantarāṇāṃ trayāṇām eva mā bhūd iti sarvagrahaṇam | abgrahaṇaṃ kṣārādinivṛttyartham | evaṃ sāyaṃ prātar abhimantrya pratipāpam ācāmet | saṃbhavato vikalpaḥ || 25.13 ||
aṣṭau vā samidha ādadhyād devakṛtasyeti hutvaiva sarvasmād enaso mucyate mucyate || 25.14 ||
aṣṭau vā samidha ādadhyāt “devakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | manuṣyakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | pitṛkṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | ātmakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | anyakṛtasyainaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā | yad diva ca naktaṃ cainaś cakṛma tasyāvayajanam asi svāhā | enasa enaso ‘vayajanam asi svāhā” ity etair mantrair hutvaiva anyad akṛtvety arthaḥ | asya prādhānyakhyāpanārtha evakāraḥ | tataś ca saṃbhave idam eva kartavyam | sarvasmād enasaḥ na kevalam anārjavādibhyaḥ, kiṃ tu ayājyayājanādibhyo ‘py enasaḥ pāpān mucyate || 25.14 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye pañcaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
ṣaḍviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti sarvaprāyaścittam iti | atra kṛcchrasvarūpanirūpaṇārtham idam āha |
athātaḥ kṛcchrān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 26.1 ||
athaśabdo maṅgalārthaḥ pūrvasyāśubhasaṃkīrtanasya vyavacchedārthaḥ | evaṃ caite dharmārthā api bhavanti | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “śaktitaḥ śarīraṃ śodhayed yamaniyamamantravratādibhiḥ” iti | vrataśabdena cātra kṛcchrāṇy abhidhīyanta iti | ataḥśabdo hetau yata evaite maṅgalārthā api bhavanti tato vyākhyāsyāma iti | kṛcchragrahaṇaṃ saṃjñārtham | anvarthasaṃjñā ceyam, kṛcchrād duḥkhāt pāpān mocayatīti || 26.1 ||
haviṣyān prātarāśān bhuktvā tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt || 26.2 ||
haviṣyān śuddhān vrīhyādibhir niṣpannān ghṛtādisaṃyuktān kṣāralavaṇavarjitān prātarāśān grāsān | āśān iti nātisauhityaṃ darśayati | tryaham abhyavahṛtya tatraiva tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt |
nanu ca sāyaṃprātar bhojanaprāptau satyāṃ prātaraśanavacanād eva rātribhojanapratiṣedhaḥ siddhaḥ |
ucyate – haviṣyān iti viśeṣārambhāt prātarhaviṣyabhojanaṃ sāyaṃ yathāprāptabhojanam ity āśaṅkā syād iti rātrau yathāprāptasyāpi pratiṣedhārthaṃ vacanam || 26.2 ||
athāparaṃ tryahaṃ naktaṃ bhuñjīta || 26.3 ||
athānantaram aparam api tryaham | aparagrahaṇāc ca pūrvam api tryahasiddhiḥ | prākṛtaṃ haviṣyānnaṃ naktam eva bhuñjīta pūrvavat || 26.3 ||
athāparaṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana yāceta || 26.4 ||
athety adhikārārtham | sakṛdbhojanasyāyācitalabhyasya na kālaviśeṣaḥ | aparagrahaṇaṃ pūrvatryahasādṛśyopapādanārtham | tataś ca haviṣyatvaṃ nātisauhityaṃ cātrāpi draṣṭavyam | na kaṃcana jñātim api prārthayet | evaṃ ca ayācitalabdhasyāpratiṣedhaḥ |
kecid bruvate – bhojane labhyamāne yadi brūyād ahaviṣyaṃ na bhokṣya iti tato ‘rthād dhaviṣyabhojanam iti yācñā kṛtā bhavatīty ato ‘tra haviṣyabhojananiyamo nāstīti |
anye tv āhuḥ – pratigraho ‘nena pratiṣidhyate yājñāyā asvīkṛtasvīkaraṇaviṣayatvān na haviṣyabhojanapratiṣedha iti | evaṃ cātmīyasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | na yāceteti siddhe na kaṃcanety ārambhasāmarthyāt paricārakādiviṣayāpi yācñā na kartavyā || 26.4 ||
athāparaṃ tryaham upavaset || 26.5 ||
athaśabda uktārthaḥ | anantaraṃ tryahaṃ nirāhāraḥ syāt | ihāparagrahaṇaṃ prakārāntareṇāpi tryahaprayogaprāpaṇārtham | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ahaḥ prātar ahar naktam ahar ekam ayācitam |
ahaḥ parākaṃ tatraiva evaṃ caturahau parau ||
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca śiśukṛcchram uvāca ha || iti | [VaDh 23.43]
upavāsaṃ kartum aśaktasya bhāradvājoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
prājāpatyaṃ caran vipro yady aśakto dine dine |
viprān pañcāvarān śuddhān bhojayet samyag arcitān ||
iti || 26.5 ||
idānīṃ guṇavidhim āha |
tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta kṣiprakāmaḥ || 26.6 ||
bhojanādyaviruddheṣu kāleṣu ahani tiṣṭhet, rātrāv āsīta | evaṃ ca nidrāyā avaśyabhāvitvād āsīna eva nidrāṃ seveteti draṣṭavyam | diva rātrau ca kṣiprakāmaś ca syāt | kāma āsthā | sarvadhānubhavam utpannam api nāśayed ity arthaḥ | atha vā sarvaprayatnena śīghraṃ śuddhaḥ syām iti satataṃ saṃjātamatiḥ syāt |
kecid vyācakṣate – śīghraṃ śuddhikāmaḥ tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāv āsīta, śanaiḥ śaktitaḥ śuddhiṃ karomīti yasya matiḥ syāt tasyāniyama iti | evaṃ ca guṇabāhulyāt prāyaścittagurutvam anumīyate | tathā ca prājāpatyadvayasthāna idaṃ gautamoktam itikartavyatāsahitaṃ prājāpatyaṃ draṣṭavyam | prājāpatyasthānamātre itikartavyatārahitam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tryahaṃ prātas tathā sāyam ayācitṃ parāka iti kṛcchraḥ” [VaDh 24.2] iti |
anye bruvate – yeṣāṃ mantrādhikāro nāsti strīśūdrādīnāṃ teṣāṃ mantrarahitaṃ vasiṣṭhādyuktena mārgeṇa draṣṭavyam | yeṣāṃ tadadhikāro ‘sti teṣām idam iti |
apare “akṣiprakāmaḥ” iti paṭhanti, vyācakṣate ca – akṣiprakāma evaṃ prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt kṣiprakāmas tu vasiṣṭhoktaṃ kuryād iti | tathā cāha |
atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
rātrau sthito jale vyuṣṭaḥ prājāpatyena tatsamam ||
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japyaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvair yadi na bhrūṇahā bhavet || [VaDh 27.17–18]
|| 26.6 ||
satyaṃ vadet || 26.7 ||
satyaṃ yathābhūtaṃ vadet | vivāhādiṣv apy ekeṣāṃ pakṣam āśritya anṛtaṃ na vaktavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ ca satyavacanenāpi pāpakṣapaṇaṃ bhavatīty avagamyate || 26.7 ||
anāryair na saṃbhāṣeta || 26.8 ||
anāryā dvijātibhyo ‘nye varṇāḥ | varṇāpaśadāś ca | bahuvacanāt strīpuṃnapuṃsakaiś ca || 26.8 ||
rauravayodhājaye nityaṃ prayuñjīta || 26.9 ||
rauravayodhājaye sāmanī “punānaḥ soma dhāraya” ity asyām ṛci gīyete | nityam ity asya sarvatrānuṣaṅgo draṣṭavyaḥ “tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityādau, japed iti vaktavye prayuñjīteti vacanāt | svādhyāyarūpeṇa nityaśabdād anusaṃdheyam ity eke | punaḥ punar bahuśa ity anye || 26.9 ||
anusavanam udakopasparśanam āpohiṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhir mārjayīta hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucaya ity aṣṭābhiḥ || 26.10 ||
anusavanaṃ pūrvāhṇamadhyāhnāparāhṇeṣu udakopasparśanaṃ snānam upaśabdaprayogāt | āpohiṣṭhetyādibhis tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhiḥ “pavitraṃ te vitatam” ityādibhiś catasṛbhir eva, anuktasaṃkhyātvāt tritvaṃ vinā bahuvacanasyākṛtārthatvāc ca | “hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ” ity aṣṭābhiś ca mārjayīta salilam abhimantrya mūrdhani prakṣipet | anye karaṇavibhaktinirdeśād ebhir eva snānam iti varṇayanti | mārjanaśabdasya madhyopadeśād anyaiś ca pavitraiḥ || 26.10 ||
athodakatarpaṇam || 26.11 ||
atha anantaram, anusavanaprāpaṇārthaṃ tasyopādānam | evaṃ ca naimittikeṣv api snāneṣūdakatarpaṇam astity avagamyate | tathāha bhṛguḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ snānam ucyate |
tarpaṇaṃ trividhasyāsyāpy eṣā eva vidhiḥ sadā || iti |
udakopasparśanam iti yatra udakādhikāre punar udakagrahaṇam udakasthasyāpi tarpaṇaprāpaṇārtham | tathā ca smṛtyantare viruddham abhidhāya punaḥ samarthitam | yathā |
nodakeṣu na pātreṣu na kruddho naikapāṇinā |
nopatiṣṭhati tat toyaṃ yad bhūmyāṃ na pradīyate ||
tathā |
udake nodakaṃ kuryāt pitṛbhyas tu kadācana |
uttīrya tu śucau deśe kuryād udakatarpaṇam ||
ity abhidhāyoktam |
āpo devagaṇāḥ sarve āpa pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tasmād apsu jalaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
tathā |
devātānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca jale dadyāj jalāñjalim |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ sthale dadyāj jalāñjalim ||
iti || 26.11 ||
tat punaḥ kebhyaḥ kathaṃ vety ata āha |
namo hamāya mohamāya maṃhamāya dhūnvate tāpasāya punarvasave namaḥ | namo mauñjyāyormyāya vasuvindāya sarvavindāya namaḥ | namaḥ pārāya supārāya mahāpārāya pāradāya pārayiṣṇave namaḥ | namo rudrāya paśupataye mahate devāya tryambakāyaikacarāyādhipataye harāya śarvāyeśānāyogrāya vajriṇe ghṛṇine karpardine namaḥ | namaḥ sūryāyādityāya namaḥ | namo nīlagrīvāya śitikaṇṭhāya namaḥ | namaḥ kṛṣṇāya piṅgalāya namaḥ | namo jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya vṛddhāyendrāya harikeśāyordhvaretase namaḥ | namaḥ satyāya pāvakāya pāvakavarṇāya kāmāya kāmarūpiṇe namaḥ | namo dīptāya dīptarūpiṇe namaḥ | namas tīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇarūpiṇe namaḥ | namaḥ saumyāya supuruṣāya mahāpuruṣāya madhyamapuruṣāyottamapuruṣāya brahmacāriṇe namaḥ | namaś candralalāṭāya kṛttivāsase nama iti || 26.12 ||
nāyam eko mantraḥ, etā evājyāhutaya iti bahuvacananirdeśāt | kiṃ tarhi trayodaśaite mantrā namaskārādayo namaskārāntāś ca draṣṭavyāḥ, śrutau tathaiva pāṭhdarśanāt | tataś caitair namaskārāntair mantair udakatarpaṇaṃ kartavyam |
anye punar āhuḥ – saṃpradānavibhaktyantāḥ ṣaṭpañcāśad ete mantrā iti |
apare namaskārādaya eva na namaskārāntās tathāpāṭhadarśanād ity āhuḥ ||
|| 26.12 ||
etad evādityopasthānam || 26.13 ||
etad eva yajuḥ ādityopasthānam |
nanu ca upasthānaśabdasya kriyāvacanatvād yajuṣā saha sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattiḥ, ata eva vaktavyam etair evādityopasthānam iti |
naiṣa doṣaḥ – upatiṣṭhate ‘nenety upasthānam ity evaṃ yajur evābhidhīyata ity adoṣaḥ | tataś cādityopasthānam ity arthāt kriyācodaneti | evaśabda udakatarpaṇe ‘py ayam eva mantra iti sūcanārthaḥ | itarathā etad ādityopasthānam ity ukte udakatarpaṇaṃ gṛhyoktena mantreṇa prasajyeteti | evaṃ ca nityād adhikam idaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathāsamāmnāyaprayogārtho vā | evaṃ codakatarpaṇādy anyathety avagamyate | raudreṇa mantreṇādityastutir ayukteti cet, naiṣa doṣaḥ | na hi vacanasyātibhāra ity atibhārābhāvād rudrāṃśatvāc cādityasya | tathā purāṇa uktam: “kṣitijalapavanahutāśanayajamānākāśasomasūryākhyā mūrtayo bhagavataḥ śarvasyāṣṭau” iti | etad eva viruddham, tatraivādityo viṣṇur iti darśanād iti cet, dvayor abhedatvād adoṣaḥ | tathā ca bhagavadgītāsu “rudrāṇāṃ śaṃkaraś cāsmi” [BhG 10.23] iti | tarpaṇānantaram upadeśāt trir ādityopasthānam anusavanaṃ draṣṭavyam |
anye vyācakṣate – “athodakatarpaṇam ādityopasthānaṃ ca” iti vaktavye pṛthagyogakaraṇāt pratyahaṃ sakṛd evādityopasthānam iti |
apare prāyaścittād anyatrāpi praveśārthaṃ pṛthagyogakaraṇam iti | vacanavad ekavacanatvād uktabahutve ‘pi na virodhaḥ || 26.13 ||
etā evājyāhutayaḥ || 26.14 ||
ājyāhutayaś ceti vaktavye etā evety abhidhānam āhutibahutvapratiṣedhārtham | atas trayodaśaitā ājyāhutayo draṣṭavyāḥ | pūrvavat samāmnāyaprayogārtha evaśabdaḥ | tataś ca namaskārādayo mantrāḥ svāhākārāntāś ca draṣṭavyāḥ | kutaḥ? āhutigrahaṇasāmarthyāt | yady api puṃliṅgā mantrāḥ, tathāpy āhutisāmānādhikaraṇyād etā iti strīliṅganirdeśaḥ |
kecid vyācakṣate – etāḥ saṃpradānavibhaktayaḥ ṣaṭpañcāśad ājyāhutaya iti, saṃpradānavibhaktiparāmarśārthaḥ strīliṅganideśa iti | etad api sakṛt sakṛd ādityopasthānavad anusavanaṃ draṣṭavyam, ānantaryānabhidhānāt | evaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ varteta || 26.14 ||
dvādaśarātrasyānte caruṃ śrapayitvaitābhyo devatābhyo juhuyāt || 26.15 ||
dvādaśarātrasyānte, na pratidinam | trayodaśe vāhani, ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśāt | caruṇā juhuyād ity eva siddhe śrapayitvāgrahaṇam utpūya śrapaṇam abhidhāryāvaropaṇam ity evamartham | juhuyād iti homavidhyartham | atra svagṛhyoktena sthālīpākavidhānena draṣṭavyam | “etābhyo devatābhyaḥ” iti na vaktavyam, vakṣyamāṇatvād eva siddham iti cet, dhyānapūrvārtha upadeśa ity adoṣaḥ
|| 26.15 ||
tā āha |
agnaye svāhā somāya svāhāgnīṣomābhyām indrāgnibhyām indrāya viśvebhyo devebhyo brahmaṇe prajāpataye ‘gnaye sviṣṭakṛta iti || 26.16 ||
dviḥsvāhākārapāṭhaḥ sarvatrānuṣaṅgārthaḥ | itikaraṇaḥ prakārāvarodhārthaḥ | ata indrāyety evamādau na puruhūtāyety evamādi yojyam | kiṃ tu tad eveti || 26.16 ||
tato brāhmaṇatarpaṇam || 26.17 ||
homānantaraṃ nāparasmin divase, śaktito brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ kartavyam | tatrāpi tryavarān bhojayed iti draṣṭavyam || 26.17 ||
etenaivātikṛcchro vyākhyātaḥ || 26.18 ||
etenaiva prājāpatyavidhinā atikṛcchro ‘py atiśayapīḍotpādako vyākhyātaḥ | kṛcchrān iti bahuvacanād evaitat siddham iti cet, ucyate – agniṣṭomavat prathamasya prakṛtitvakhyāpanārtham abhidhānam iti | tataḥ sāntapanādāv api “tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityādyavirodhakalpanā kartavyā | tatsvarūpaṃ tu smṛtyantarād avagantavyam | tathāha bodhāyanaḥ |
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ smṛtam ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ caiva kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tathā |
pañcarātraṃ tadāhāraḥ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
gomūtrādibhir abhyastam ekaikaṃ tritrisaptakam |
mahāsāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ vadanti brahmavādinaḥ ||
kaṇapiṇyākatakrāṇi tathā cāmānilāśanaḥ |
ekatripañcasaptāhaṃ pāpaghno ‘yaṃ tulāpumān ||
yāvakaḥ saptarātreṇa vṛjinaṃ hanti dehinām |
saptarātropavāso vā dṛṣṭam etan manīṣibhiḥ ||
pauṣabhādrapadajyeṣṭhā ārdrākāśātapāśrayāḥ |
trīn śuklān mucyate pāpāt patanīyād ṛte dvijaḥ ||
amāvāsyāṃ nirāhāraḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ tilāśanaḥ |
śuklakṛṣṇakṛtāt pāpān mucyate ‘bdasya parvabhiḥ ||
baikṣāhāro ‘gnihotribhyo māsenaikena śudhyati |
yāyāvaravanasthebhyo daśabhiḥ saptabhir dinaiḥ ||
ekāhaṃ dhanino ‘nnena dinenaikena śudhyati |
kāpotavṛttiniṣṭhasya pītvāpaḥ śudhyate dvijaḥ ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmavedānāṃ vedasyānyatamasya vā |
pārāyaṇaṃ trir abhyasyānaśnan pāpāt pramucyate ||
yo ‘nnadaḥ satyavādī ca sarvabhūteṣv ahiṃsakaḥ |
pūrvoktamantraśuddhibhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ so ‘tiricyate ||
trimadhucchandaso rudrā gayatri praṇavānvitā |
saptavyāhṛtayaś caiṣāṃ japaḥ pāpapraṇāśanaḥ ||
mṛgāreṣṭiḥ pavitreṣṭiḥ pāvamānyo ‘pi ceṣṭayaḥ |
etāḥ pāpapraṇāśinyo vaiśvānaryā samanvitāḥ ||
idaṃ caivāparaṃ guhyam ucyamānaṃ nibodhata |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mahadbhiḥ pātakair vinā ||
pavitrair mārjanaṃ kurvan rudraikādaśinīṃ japan |
vyāhṛtībhir ghṛtair juhvan prayacchan hemagotilān ||
prāśnīyād yāvakaṃ pakvaṃ gomūtraiś ca śakṛdrasaiḥ |
sadadhikṣīrasarpiṣyaṃ mucyate so ‘ṃhasaḥ kṣaṇāt ||
prasūto yaś ca śūdrāyāṃ yenāgamyā ca laṅghitā |
saptarātrāt pramucyete vidhinaitena tāv ubhau ||
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane ‘bhojyabhojane |
paryādhānejyayor etat parivitteś ca bheṣajam ||
apātakāni karmāṇi kṛtveha ca bahūny api |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo ity etad vacanaṃ satām ||
mantramārgapramāṇaṃ ca vidhānaṃ samudāhṛtam |
bharadvājādayo yena brahmaṇaḥ sātmatāṃ gatāḥ ||
prasannahṛdayo vipraḥ payodadhyāśy karmaṇā |
kāmāṃs tāṃs tān avāpnoti ye ye kāmā hṛdi sthitāḥ ||
nirvṛttiḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ pravṛttaḥ puṇyakarmaṇi |
bho viprās tasya sidhyanti vinā yatnair api kriyāḥ ||
brāhmaṇā ṛjavas tasmād yad yad icchanti cetasā |
tat tad āsādayanty āśu saṃśuddhā ṛjukarmiṇaḥ ||
evam etāni yantrāṇi tāvat kāryāṇi dhīmatā |
kālena yāvatāpaiti vigrahāśuddhir ātmanaḥ ||
ebhir yantrair viśuddhātmā trirātropoṣitaḥ śuciḥ |
tad ārabheta yenarddhiṃ karmaṇāṃ prāptum icchanti ||
kṣāpavitraṃ sahasrākṣaḥ mṛgāro ‘ṃhomucau gaṇau |
pāvamānyaś ca kūśmāṇḍyaḥ vaiśvānarya ṛcaś ca yāḥ ||
ghṛtaudanena tā juhvan saptāhaṃ savanatrayam |
maunavṛttir haviṣyāśī nigṛhītendriyakriyaḥ ||
siṃhe ma ity apāṃ pūrṇe pātre ‘vekṣya catuṣpathe |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mahataḥ pātakād api ||
vṛddhatve yauvane bālye yaḥ kṛtaḥ pāpasaṃcayaḥ |
pūrvajanmasu vā jātas tasmād api vimucyate ||
bhojayitvā dvijān ante pāyasena sasarpiṣā |
gobhūmitilahemāni bhuktavadbhyaḥ pradāpayet || [cf. BDh 4.5.15–7.9]
ityevamādi | alam atiprasaṅgena | prastutam abhidhīyate || 26.18 ||
yas tv asya viśeṣaḥ sa ucyate |
yāvat sakṛd ādadīta tāvad aśnīyāt || 26.19 ||
yāvanmātraṃ haviṣyam ekena pāṇinā sakṛd ādātuṃ śaknuyāt tāvanmātraṃ teṣv evāhaḥsu aśnīyāt | grāsamātracodanaiṣā | tathāha manuḥ |
ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt tryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat |
tryahaṃ copavased antyam atikṛcchraḥ sa ucyate || iti | [MDh 11.213]
abbhakṣas tṛtīyaḥ, sa kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | pūrvatra ya eva bhojanakāla uktaḥ, tatraiva kevalam udakaṃ pibet | sa evaṃrūpas tṛtīyaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchro bhavati | tṛtīyagrahaṇaṃ dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyam iti kevaloktāv api tatpratipattyartham | tathā ca vakṣyati: “prathamaṃ caritvā” [GDh 26.20] ityādi | adbhis tṛtīya iti vaktavye bhakṣagrahaṇam apām apy annakāryakaraṇapradarśanārtham | tataś copadiṣṭakālavyatirekeṇa udakapānābhāvaḥ | saṃjñākaraṇaṃ vyavahārārtham || 26.19 |
idānīm[73] eteṣāṃ phalam āha |
prathamaṃ caritvā śuciḥ pūtaḥ karmaṇyo bhavati
|| 26.20 ||
prathamaṃ prājāpatyaṃ caritvā śucir apagatavihitākaraṇanimittadoṣaḥ pūtaḥ pratiṣiddhācaraṇajanitādharmakṣayaś ca, karmaṇyaḥ kāmyanityakarmayogyaś ca bhavati saṃpadyate |
nanu ca idam anarthakam, prāyaścittavidhānād evaitasya siddhatvāt, kuryād ity atra pāpakṣapaṇahetutvasya pratipāditatvāt | tatrāpi prathamād ity eva siddhatvāc caritvāgrahaṇam apy anarthakam | tathā ca śuciḥ pūta ity anenādharmāvāntarabhedakathanaṃ mandaphalam, pūta ity etāvataivobhasiddheḥ | tathā karmaṇya ity etad api | yadi tāvad apetādharmatvena karmādhikārābhprāyaḥ tato ‘rthasiddhatvād avacanīyam | atha viśiṣṭataraphalaprāptyabhiprāyaḥ, tad apy ayuktam | yasya karmaṇo yat phalaṃ tad eva, na phalātiśayo bhavatīti |
atrocyate – dehāntarakṛtobhayavidhādharmakṣapaṇasādhanatvajñāpanārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | caritvāgrahaṇaṃ sakṛtprayogeṇa bhavatīti jñāpanārtham, upariṣṭāt “yad anyan mahāpātakebhyaḥ” [GDh 26.21] ity abhidhānāt, pratiṣiddhasevana eva prāyaśaḥ prāyaścittavidhānāt | pūtaśabdena ca pratiṣiddhaviṣaya evāśaṅkā syād iti śucigrahaṇam | tasya ca jñātasyeti draṣṭavyam, ajñātasya vedoditānām ity uktatvāt |
nanv etad api sarvaprāyaścittābhidhānāt siddham, tasya buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvakṛtānāṃ sāmānyatvāt |
ucyate – nirdiṣṭaviṣayam idam | anirdiṣtaviṣayaṃ tad iti | evaṃ pratiṣiddhaviṣaye ‘pi draṣṭavyam | karmaṇya ity etad apy adharmasaṃbandhe sati karmaphalapratibandho bhavatīty asyārthasya jñāpanārtham || 26.20 ||
dvitīyaṃ caritvā yad anyan mahāpātakebhyaḥ pāpaṃ kurute tasmāt pramucyate || 26.21 ||
atikṛcchraṃ caritvā mahāpātakavyatiriktapāpanirmukto bhavatīti | evaṃ ca pūrveṇa yamaniyamalopapāpakṣayaḥ kriyate, anena tūpapātakakṛtapāpakṣapaṇam iti pūrvasahitasya ca sarvaprāyaścittavacanān mahāpātakam apy anabhisaṃdhikṛtaṃ kṣapayatīti draṣṭavyam || 26.21 ||
tṛtīyaṃ caritvā sarvam eno vyapohati || 26.22 ||
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ caritvā mahāpātakam apy anabhisaṃdhikṛtaṃ vināśayati | kutaḥ? asya pūrvayos tulyatvāt, cāndrāyaṇasahitasya sarvaprāyaścittasya abhisaṃdhikṛtasya mahāpātakasya vināśahetutvāc ca | evaṃ ca pūrvayoḥ sthāna idam api kartavyam | dvitīyam ityādy api tadviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | sarvaprāyaścitte sarvasamuccayo mā bhūd ity upadeśaḥ || 26.22 ||
athaitāṃs trīn kṛcchrāṃś caritvā sarveṣu vedeṣu snāto bhavati sarvair devair jñāto bhavati yaś caivaṃ veda yaś caivaṃ veda || 26.23 ||
atha anantaram apy avyavahitān etāṃs trīn eva na taptakṛcchrādīn | kṛcchrān iti pīḍātiśayenānuṣṭhānārtham | evaṃ cārdravastrādīny api draṣṭavyāni | tathā japahomādīny api | sarvān vedān adhītya snātasya yat phalaṃ tattulyaphalasaṃbandho bhavatīty abhiprāyeṇoktaṃ sarveṣv ityādi | sarvair devair jñāto bhavati tallokagamanasamartha ity arthaḥ | yaś caivaṃ veda yaś ca kṛcchrasvarūpetikartavyatāphalaṃ vijānāti | cakāraḥ pūrveṇa samuccayārthaḥ | jñātvā kṛtasya pūrvoktaṃ phalaṃ na karmamātreṇa jñānamātreṇa veti | evaṃ ca pratyekam api phalam ānurūpyeṇa draṣṭavyam |
kecit pratyekam apīcchanti |
tad ayuktam, anuṣṭhānād eva pāpakṣapaṇaṃ na jñānamārad iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
phalaṃ katakavṛkṣasya yady apy ambuprasādakam |
na nāmagrahaṇād eva tasya vāri prasīdati || [MDh 6.67] || 26.23 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye ṣaḍviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
saptaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
uktaṃ kṛcchātikṛcchrau cāndrāyaṇam iti | tatra kṛcchrātikṛcchravidhir uktaḥ | adhunā cāndrāyaṇavidhivivakṣayāha |
athātaś cāndrāyaṇam || 27.1 ||
vyākhyāsyāmaḥ iti śeṣaḥ | athaśabdo ‘dhikārārthaḥ | kṛcchrātikṛcchraphalasya tattulyatvād anantaroktaṃ na bhavatīti | athaḥśabdo hetau, yata etat phalasaṃyuktam iti | cāndrāyaṇam iti saṃjñā | anvarthatvāt candragativaśāt bhavatīti | jātyām ekavacanam | evaṃ ca smṛtyantaroktasya yaticāndrāyaṇādeḥ sarvaprāyaścittatvaṃ jñāpayati | tathāha manuḥ |
aṣṭāv aṣṭau samaśnīyāt piṇḍān mādhyaṃdine sthite |
niyatātmā haviṣyasya yaticāndrāyaṇam caret ||
caturaḥ prātar aśnīyād dvijaḥ piṇḍān kṛtāhnikaḥ |
caturo ‘stamite sūrye śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
yathā kathaṃcit piṇḍānāṃ tisro ‘śītiḥ samāhitaḥ |
māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyaiti salokatām ||
ityādi || 27.1 ||
tasyokto vidhiḥ kṛcchre || 27.2 ||
tasya sarvaprakārasya, tacchabdopādānāt | ukto vidhiḥ itikartavyatā | kṛcchre prathame “tiṣṭhed ahani” [GDh 26.6] ityādi || 27.2 ||
yas tv iha viśeṣaḥ sa ucyate |
vapanaṃ vrataṃ cet || 27.3 ||
vapanaṃ keśādyapanayanaṃ kartavyaṃ vrataṃ cet | prāyaścittārtham asyaitad bhavati cet | vrataśabdena smṛtyantare prāyaścittam uktam iti | yathāha manuḥ |
etair dvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratair āviṣkṛtainasaḥ || iti | [MDh 11.226]
evaṃ ca yady adṛṣṭārthaṃ tadānīṃ vapanam antareṇāpi tad bhavatīti jñāpitam |
anye tu brahmacārivratam āhuḥ, “caturo vratino ‘pi” [MDh 11.121] ityādau tathā darśanāt | asmin pakṣe gṛhasthasya tadantareṇāpi bhavati | ayaṃ nyāyaḥ kṛcchrādāv api draṣṭavyaḥ
|| 27.3 ||
svarūpābhidhānārtham āha |
śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavaset || 27.4 ||
upavāso bhaktatyāgaḥ | taṃ caturdaśyāṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 27.4 ||
ā pyāyasva saṃ te payāṃsi navo nava iti caitābhis tarpaṇam ājyahomo haviṣaś cānumantraṇam || 27.5 ||
“ā pyāyasva sametu te saṃ te payāṃsi samuyantu vājāḥ, navo navo bhavati jāyamānaḥ” ity etābhis tisṛbhir udakatarpaṇaṃ ghṛtena ca homo haviṣaś ca vakṣyamāṇasyānumantraṇaṃ kartavyam |
tatraika āhuḥ – aṅgabhūyastve phalabhūyastvābhyupagamāt kṛcchravidhyatideśāc ca kṛcchre raudreṇa yajuṣā ya udakatarpaṇādaya upadiṣṭāḥ taiḥ saha samuccīyanta iti |
tad ayuktam | “atideśaprāptaṃ hi pratyakṣaśrutena nivartyate” iti nyāyān nivṛttir eva nyāyyeti | atha kim etābhiḥ yathāsaṃkhyenaikaikaṃ kartavyam, uta samuccitābhiḥ sarvam |
anya āhuḥ – ājyahoma ity ekavacananirdeśād ekaikaṃ kuryāt, samuccaye hi mantrabahutvāt karmabahutvam iti homapṛthaktve ca bahuvacanaṃ prasajyeteti || 27.5 ||
upasthānaṃ ca candramasaḥ || 27.6 ||
caśabdād etābhir eva kartavyam | yady asau na dṛśyate tadā ādityasya kāryam, “suṣumnaḥ sūryaraśmiḥ” iti mantraliṅgadarśanāt | atra bhinnārthatvāt[74] kṛcchravidhyatideśaprāptam ādityopasthānaṃ nivartate || 27.6 ||
yad devā devaheḍanam iti ca catasṛbhir juhuyāt || 27.7 ||
“yad devā devaheḍanam” iti ṛgbhiḥ | anādeśād ājyaṃ juhuyāt | strīliṅgasaṃkhyānirdeśa ṛgarthaḥ, yajūṃṣy apy evamādīni yajurvede paṭhyanta iti tannivṛttyartham | ataḥ pūrveṇa sahitāḥ pañcājyāhutayaḥ sapta vā || 27.7 ||
devakṛtasyeti cānte samidbhiḥ || 27.8 ||
“devakṛtasya” ityādaya uktāḥ [GDh 25.14] | tair mantrair aṣṭau samiddhomān kuryāt | ante cāndrāyaṇasamāptau | caśabdād ādau ca || 27.8 ||
oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar tapaḥ satyaṃ yaśaḥ śrīr ūrgiḍ aujas tejaḥ puruṣo dharmaḥ śiva ity etair grāsānumantraṇam || 27.9 ||
praṇavādayaḥ pañcadaśaite mantrāḥ | teṣām ekaikenānupūrvyā grāsānumantraṇaṃ kartavyam | pūrvaṃ haviṣo ‘numantraṇam uktam | yadā cāntyaḥ piṇḍo vyāvartate tadāntyamantro ‘pīti mantrānto draṣṭavyaḥ || 27.9 ||
pratimantraṃ manasā namaḥ svāheti vā sarvān
|| 27.10 ||
mantraṃ mantraṃ prati namaskāraṃ svāhākāraṃ vānte kṛtvā sarvān grāsān manasānumantrayet | icchāto vikalpaḥ sarvagrahaṇād vibhaktānām anumantraṇam, na punar avibhaktānām | anumantraṇamantrāś caite na bhakṣaṇamantrāḥ | ato ‘nnam evam abhimantrya bhuñjīta || 27.10 ||
grāsapramāṇam āsyāvikāreṇa || 27.11 ||
yāvatā grāsena mukhaṃ na vikriyate, nātivivṛtam ity arthaḥ, tāvad eva pramāṇaṃ syāt || 27.11 ||
carubhaikṣasaktukaṇayāvakaśākapayodadhighṛtamūla-phalodakāni havīṃṣy uttarottaraṃ praśastāni || 27.12 ||
dvādaśaitāni dravyāṇi | eteṣāṃ havirdravyāṇāṃ yad yad uttaraṃ tat tat pūrvasmāt pūrvasmāt praśastataram, pīḍātiśayahetutvāt | carur haviṣyaṃ svayaṃ śrapayitavyam | bhaikṣaṃ yasyopapadyate brahmacāryānītaṃ vā | śeṣāṇi prasiddhāni | havirgrahaṇaṃ haviṣa iva saṃskārārtham | tato lavaṇaguḍādivarjanasiddhiḥ | tatra dravyāṇāṃ puṭikayā grāsakalpanā, tatra “guruṣu gurūṇi” [GDh 19.19] ity anena nyāyena kalpanā kartavyā || 27.12 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān bhuktvaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāt || 27.13 ||
caturdaśyām upavāsaṃ kṛtvā eteṣām anyatamasya haviṣaḥ pañcadaśyāṃ pañcadaśagrāsān aśitvā tataḥ pratipadam ārabhya ekaikāhny ā kṛṣṇapakṣam aśnīyāt | aparapakṣagrahaṇaṃ vyāptyartham | sakalaṃ pakṣam anena prakāreṇa vyāpnuyāt, na tv aniyamena saṃkhyāṃ pūrayed iti | sakṛdbhojanārthaṃ vā gamyamānasya punarvacanam || 27.13 ||
amāvāsyāyām upoṣya ekoccayena pūrvapakṣam || 27.14 ||
tato ‘māvāsyāyām upavāsaṃ kṛtvā pratipatprabhṛty ekaikasyoccayena vṛddhyā śuklapakṣam aśnīyāt | tathā paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsā bhavanti | madhyatanutvāt pipīlikāmadhyam idam
|| 27.14 ||
viparītam ekeṣām || 27.15 ||
ekeṣām ācāryāṇāṃ matena viparītam idam eva bhavati | caturdaśyām upavāsaṃ kṛtvāmāvāsyāyām ekaṃ piṇḍam aśnīyāt | tataḥ pratipadam ārabhyaikaikavṛddhyā śuklapakṣam aśnīyāt | tathā ca caturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśa bhavanti | tataḥ paurṇamāsyām upavāsaḥ | pratipadi pañcadaśa grāsāḥ | tata ekaikāhny ā kṛṣṇapakṣam aśnīyād iti|
anye tv anyathā viparītam ācakṣate – paurṇamāsyām upoṣya pratipadam ārabhaikaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāt, tato ‘māvāsyāprabhṛty ekaikoccayena pūrvapakṣam aśnīyād iti | madhyapṛthutvād yavamadhyam idam | tasmāt pūrvokto ‘yaṃ ca pipīlikāmadhyaḥ
|| 27.15 ||
eṣa cāndrāyaṇo māsaḥ || 27.16 ||
candro yena hrāsavṛddhibhyām anugamyate tac cāndrāyaṇam | tatsahacarito ‘pi māsaḥ cāndrāyaṇa ity ucyate || 27.16 ||
etam āptvā vipāpo vipāpmā sarvam eno hanti || 27.17 ||
etam evaṃvidhaṃ cāndrāyaṇam āptvā vipāpaḥ vihitākaraṇanimittasyainasaḥ kṣayeṇa | vipāpmā pratiṣiddhācaraṇanimittakilbiṣavināśāt | anyad api dehāntaragatam api sarvaprakāram eno hanti | evaṃ ca vihitākaraṇe ca kuṣṭhādyupaghāte sati cāndrāyanaṃ kartavyam iti sūcitaṃ bhavati
|| 27.17 ||
dvitīyam āptvā daśa pūrvān daśāparān ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśaṃ paṅktiṃ ca punāti || 27.18 ||
nigadavyākhyānaṃ sūtram || 27.18 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tv āptvā candramasaḥ salokatām āpnoty āpnoti || 27.19 ||
tuśabdo ‘vyavahitārthaḥ | salokatāṃ sālokyaṃ gacchati || 27.19 ||
iti maskarīye gautamabhāṣye saptaviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
aṣṭāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
idānīṃ “svāmī rikthakraya-” [GDh 10.38] iti, “rikthabhāja ṛṇam” [GDh 12.37] iti ca sūcitaṃ dāyavibhāgam āha |
ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ putrā rikthaṃ bhajeran || 28.1 ||
nanu kasmād itaravat prāyaścittāt prāg anabhidhānam |
tatraika āhuḥ – yadi putrā pitaraṃ balād vibhāgāya pravartayanti, pitā vā putrāṇāṃ karmādhikāre sati yathāprāptaṃ vibhāgaṃ na karoti, tato ‘nayoḥ prāyaścittaṃ nāstīty evamartham iti |
na punar etad vicāraṇīyaṃ yena na kvacid api vyatikrame prāyaścittābhāva iṣyate | ataḥ prāyaścittagauravārtham evam abhidhānam |
sūtram idānīṃ vivriyate — ūrdhvaṃ jīvanāt pituḥ prete tasminn ity arthaḥ | ekaśeṣaś ca draṣṭavyaḥ, “pitā mātrā” iti | tathāha manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ saha |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ riktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ || iti | [MDh 9.104]
pitṛśabdasya saṃbandhiśabdatvāt putragrahaṇaṃ gamyamānam api kriyate | puruṣāṇām eva vibhāga ity evamartham | tathā ca strīṇāṃ tāvat taittirīyakaśrutau śrūyate: “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādīḥ” [TaitS 6.5.8.10] iti | tathā napuṃsakānāṃ vasiṣṭha āha: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ klībonmattapatitāś ca” [VaDh 17.52] iti | “jīvati vecchati” [GDh 28.2] iti vacanād ūrdhvam iti gamyamānam api abhidhīyate | ekoddiṣṭādisaṃskārakaraṇād ūrdhvam iti pratipādanārtham | rikthaṃ paitṛkaṃ dravyaṃ kṛṣyādilabdhaṃ rūḍhyā |
“vibhajeran” | atra vicāryate – svatve sati vibhāgaḥ, uta vibhāge sati svatvam?
atra kecid āhuḥ vibhāge satīti |
tad ayuktam, sarveṣāṃ vibhāgaprāpteḥ | vacanāt putrāṇām eva niyamya iti cet, “anīśās te jīvatoḥ” [MDh 9.104] iti liṅgāt | kutaḥ? ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ceti niyamād eva prāgavibhāge siddhe – punar īśatvamātrapratiṣedhāt svatvāstitvaṃ jñāpayati | itarathā anarthakaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ prāptyabhāvāt | tasmāt svatve saty eva vibhāgaḥ | evaṃ tarhi jātaputrasya putrasaṃkramitatvāt parasvādānaṃ prāpnotīti cet, jātivad ubhayatra vartata ity adoṣaḥ | īśatvapratiṣedhāt tu putrāṇāṃ dānābhāvo ‘rthasiddhaḥ || 28.1 ||
nivṛtte rajasi mātur jīvati vecchati || 28.2 ||
uparatarajaskāyāṃ vā mātari jīvaty api pitari tadanujñayā vibhāgaḥ, icchā ca pituḥ na putrāṇām, jīvatīti sāmānādhikaraṇyāt | so ‘yaṃ vibhāgaḥ putrāṇāṃ karmādhikārāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ
|| 28.2 ||
sarvaṃ vā pūrvajasyetarān bibhṛyāt pitṛvat || 28.3 ||
jyeṣṭha eva vā sarvaṃ dhanam ādāya itarān yavīyaso bhrātṝn bibhṛyāt poṣayet na vibhāgaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | pitṛvat yathā pitā bibharti | ayam api yavīyasāṃ karmānuṣṭhānasāmarthyābhāve jyeṣṭhavṛttitve sati draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.4 ||
vibhāge tu dharmavṛddhiḥ || 28.4 ||
tuśabdo hetvarthaḥ | yasmāt pratyekam atithidānādikarmānuṣṭhānena dharmavṛddhis tasmād vibhāga eva yuktatara iti | arbhakāvasthāyām api bhinnabuddhitve sati vibhāgaḥ kartavya ity evamartha upadeśaḥ | vṛddhivacanāc cāṃśapradātur api dharmo ‘sty eveti jñāpayati || 28.4 ||
adhunā kathaṃ punaḥ saṃvibhājya ity āha |
viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya mithunam ubhayatodadyukto ratho govṛśaḥ || 28.5 ||
sarvasmāt pitṛdhanād viṃśatitamo bhāgo jyeṣṭhasya | mithunaṃ gomithunaṃ prasiddham | ubhayatodataḥ aśvāśvataragardabhāḥ, teṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavam anyatamābhyāṃ yukto rathaḥ | govṛṣaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ayam uddhāraḥ sati saṃbhave jyeṣṭhasya || 28.5 ||
kāṇakhorakūṭavaṇṭā madhyamasyānekāś cet || 28.6 ||
kāṇaḥ prasiddhaḥ | khoro vṛddhaḥ | khoṭa iti pāṭhe tu pādavikalaḥ | kūṭaḥ śṛṅgavikalaḥ | vaṇṭo vilopitavāladhiḥ | aviśeṣitatvāt gavāśvādīnām api sarveṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavo draṣṭavyaḥ | madhyamasyāyam uddhāraḥ, te ca yadi bahavo bhavanti | itareṣām api santīty arthaḥ || 28.6 ||
avir dhānyāyasī gṛham anoyuktaṃ catuṣpadāṃ caikaikaṃ yavīyasaḥ || 28.7 ||
aviḥ prasiddhaḥ | ekavacanād ekam, dhānyaṃ vrīhyādi | ayo lohaḥ | ānurūpyeṇa dhānyāyasoḥ kalpanārthaṃ dvivacanam | evaṃ ca na sarvaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | gṛham ekaṃ śakaṭayuktaṃ catuṣpadāṃ ca gavādīnām ekaikaṃ pṛthak pṛthag ānurūpyeṇa yavīyasaḥ kaniṣṭhā gṛhṇīyuḥ | ayaṃ kaniṣṭhoddhāraḥ || 28.7 ||
samadhetarat sarvam || 28.8 ||
evam uddhṛtoddhāraśiṣṭaṃ samaṃ vibhaktavyam |
nanu ca vibhāge samadhaiva sarvasmin prāpte vacanād uddhāra ukte śeṣaṃ samadhaiva prāpnotīti nārtho ‘nena sūtreṇa |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, yadi hi pitā vibhāgaṃ karoti tadoddhārapratiṣedhārtham idam iti |
anye tu pitur uddhārapratiṣedhārtham idam iti bruvate | ayaṃ ca prakāro jyeṣṭhe kaniṣṭhe ca guṇavati draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.8 ||
dvyaṃśī vā pūrvajasya || 28.9 ||
dvāv aṃśau dvyaṃśam, tad asya vidyata iti dvyaṃśī, bhāgadvayaṃ jyeṣṭhasyoddhāra ity arthaḥ
|| 28.9 ||
ekaikam itareṣām || 28.10 ||
ayaṃ vibhāgo yadi jyeṣṭha eva guṇavān anye nirguṇāḥ tadā draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.10 ||
ekaikaṃ vā dhanarūpaṃ kāmyaṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvo labheta
|| 28.11 ||
ekaikaṃ vārthajātam abhipretaṃ jyeṣṭhānukrameṇa gṛhṇīyuḥ | tatrābhipretārthaviśeṣagrahaṇam evoddhāraḥ | ayam api sarve guṇavantaś ced draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.11 ||
kiṃ ca |
daśataṃ paśūnām || 28.12 ||
dhanarūpam ity anena kāmyasyaikasya paśor grahaṇe prāpte saṃkhyāniyamena grahaṇārtham idaṃ paśūnāṃ daśa grāhyā iti || 28.12 ||
tatrāpi |
naikaśaphadvipadānām || 28.13 ||
aśvādīnāṃ stryādīnāṃ ca na daśa daśa grāhyā ity arthaḥ | yadi sarve nirguṇās tadānīṃ samam eva vibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ | atra viśeṣānupādānāt, smṛtyantare ca dvirukteḥ pūrvasya tatprayojanatvāt | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “atha bhrātṝṇāṃ dāyavibhāgaḥ” [VaDh 17.40] iti | yadā kecit guṇavantaḥ kecin nirguṇās tadā jyeṣṭho daśamam uddhāraṃ gṛhītvā anyat samaṃ vibhajet | yathāha uśanā: “jyeṣṭhasya daśamoddhāraḥ kaniṣṭhasyābhipreto bhāgaḥ” iti | asmin deśe ayam eva vibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ, samācārāt || 28.13 ||
samānajātīyānām ekamātṛkāṇāṃ vibhāga uktaḥ | adhunā samānajātīyatve ‘py anekamātṛkāṇāṃ vaktum āha |
ṛṣabho ‘dhiko jyeṣṭhasya || 28.14 ||
kanīyasyāḥ putrasya jyeṣṭhasya ṛṣabha uddhāraḥ | samam anyat || 28.14 ||
ṛṣabhaṣoḍaśā jyaiṣṭhineyasya || 28.15 ||
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ jāto jyaiṣṭhineyaḥ | tasya pañcadaśa gāvaḥ, vṛṣabhaś caika uddhāraḥ | samam itarat
|| 28.15 ||
samadhā vājyaiṣṭhineyena yavīyasaḥ || 28.16 ||
sama evāyaṃ vibhāgo bhavati | ajyaiṣṭhineyena kaniṣṭhāyāṃ jātena jyeṣṭhena saha yavīyasaḥ jyeṣṭhāyāḥ putrasya kanīyasaḥ | ekasya mātṛto jyaiṣṭhyam anyasya janmataḥ | ayam[75] api guṇavadviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.16 ||
pratimātṛ vā svavarge bhāgaviśeṣaḥ || 28.17 ||
yadā tu bahvyaḥ samānajātīyā asamānāpatyāś ca jāyā bhavati, tadā putrāśrayasya vibhāgasya duṣkaratvān mātṛta eva vibhajyate | sve sve varge bhāgaviśeṣo ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | sa ukto “viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya” [GDh 24.5] ityādinā || 28.17 ||
putravatas tāvad eva, aputravatas tu |
pitotsṛjet putrikām anapatyo ‘gniṃ prajāpatiṃ ceṣṭvāsmadartham apatyam iti saṃvādya || 28.18 ||
pitā na tu mātāpi | utsṛjet arpayet na tu dadyāt śāstreṇa putratvāt,
pāṇigrāhaṇikā mantrā kanyāsv eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ | [MDh 8.226]
iti coktatvāt | putrikāṃ duhitaram | anapatyaḥ avidyamānaputraḥ | āgneyīṃ prājāpatyāṃ ceṣṭiṃ kṛtvā anāhitāgniś cet sthālīpākavidhānena āhutidvayaṃ hutveti draṣṭavyam | tato dānakāle ‘smadartham apatyaṃ yad asyāṃ bhaviṣyatīty evaṃ vareṇa saha saṃvādaṃ kṛtvā | tata eva pratipāditāyāṃ yaḥ putraḥ sa mātāmahasya putro bhavati, notpādayitur ity abhiprāyaḥ || 28.18 ||
abhisaṃdhimātrāt putrikety ekeṣām || 28.19 ||
yadā tu pūrvasya vidheḥ karaṇāsaṃbhavaḥ dānāt pūrvaṃ varaṇādinā saṃkalpam eva karoti – yad asyām apatyaṃ bhaviṣyati tan mameti | anenaiva putratvaṃ bhavatīty eke manyante | saṃbhavāpekṣo vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.19 ||
tatsaṃśayān nopayacched abhrātṛkām || 28.20 ||
