tadāśaṅkayā na gṛhṇīyāt kasyacid apy anapatyasya sarvā duhitaraḥ | kutaḥ? tām iti vaktavye abhrātṛkām ity ārambhāt || 29.20 ||
yasya tu putrikāpi na saṃbhavati tasya –
piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhā rikthaṃ bhajeran || 28.21 ||
piṇḍasaṃbandhāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ, tadabhāve gotrasaṃbandhāḥ sagotrāḥ, tadabhāve ṛṣisaṃbandhāḥ samānārṣeyāḥ | evam āsannasyābhāve viprakṛṣṭas taddravyabhāg iti || 28.21 ||
strī cānapatyasya || 28.22 ||
putrikāputrasyāpy abhāva etat | kutaḥ? “putrikām anapatyaḥ” [GDh 28.18] ity anapatyādhikāre punar anapatyagrahaṇāt | te yadaiva piṇḍādisaṃbandhā rikataṃ bhajeran tadā stry api bhāgaṃ labheta | caśabdād ācāryaḥ śiṣyaś ca, sāmarthyāt sarvābhāve | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tadabhāve ṛtvigācāryau” iti || 28.22 ||
bījaṃ vā lipseta || 28.23 ||
yad vā – sapiṇḍādibhyo bījam icched apatyam utpādayed ity arthaḥ | icchāto vikalpaḥ niyogato vā || 28.23 ||
devaravatyām anyajātam abhāgam || 28.24 ||
vidyamānadevarāyām anyenotpāditam apatyam abhāgārham, kāmajatvāt | kṣetrajāpavādo ‘yam
|| 28.24 ||
strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca
|| 28.25 ||
strīdhanaṃ ṣaṭprakāram | yathāha manuḥ |
adhyagnyadhyāvāhanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītikarmaṇi |
mātṛbhrātṛpitṛprāpaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam || iti | [MDh 9.194]
yac cānyat tayā karmaṇopāttaṃ tat tasyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tasyā eva duhitṝṇām adattānām anapatyānāṃ ca bhavati |
anya āhuḥ – apratiṣṭhitānāṃ putrāṇām akṛtavivāhānām iti |
apare niḥsvānām iti |
caśabdāt bhartuś ca | atra adattānāṃ duhitṝṇām abhāve putrā niḥsvā gṛhṇīyuḥ, tadabhāve akṛtavivāhāḥ, tadabhāve anapatyāḥ striyaḥ, sarveṣām abhāve bharteti kramo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.25 ||
bhaginīśulkaṃ sodaryāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ || 28.26 ||
bhaginīpradānanimittaṃ yad dravyaṃ labdham ārṣāsuravivāhayoḥ tat sodaryāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ, tasyāṃ mṛtāyām ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca pitur abhāve mātaiva tasyeṣṭa ity uktaṃ bhavati
|| 28.2 ||
pūrvaṃ caike || 28.27 ||
prāg api mātṛmaraṇāt bhaginīśulkaṃ sodaryāṇāṃ bhavatīty eke manyante | tasyā vṛttāpekṣayā vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | atha vā pūrvam eva yad uktam “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām” [GDh 28.25] iti, tac cordhvam eva mātur na jīvantyām ity eke manyante | ukto viṣayaḥ || 28.27 ||
asaṃsṛṣṭivibhāgaḥ pretānāṃ jyeṣṭhasya || 28.28 ||
asaṃsṛṣṭino vibhaktāḥ santaḥ pṛthak pṛthak yadā bhrātaro vyavaharanti teṣāṃ yaḥ praiti tasya anapatyasya | kutaḥ? anapatyādhikārāt | yo bhāgaḥ saḥ svasvajyeṣṭhasyaiva bhavati na sarveṣāṃ, sa pitṛsama iti | dvaimātṛkāṇāṃ samānajātīyānām asamānajātīyānām abhinnamātṛkāṇāṃ ca yo bhāgaḥ saḥ svasvavargajyeṣṭhasyaiva bhavati || 28.28 ||
saṃsṛṣṭini prete saṃsṛṣṭī rikthabhāk || 28.29 ||
yas tu vibhakto ‘pi punaḥ kāryavaśāt saṃsṛjyate sa eva tadbhāgabhāg bhavati | punaḥ pretagrahaṇaṃ sodaryārtham anyeṣāṃ yathāprāptam eveti || 28.29 ||
vibhaktajaḥ pitryam eva || 28.30 ||
“nivṛtte rajasi mātuḥ” [GDh 28.2] ity anena putrāṇāṃ vibhajya punaḥ dārakriyayā yadi paścāt putro jāyate sa pitryam eva labheta | evaṃ jīvadvibhāgapakṣe pitur apy aṃśabhāktvam arthasiddham | tadāpi “dvau bhāgau pituḥ” iti smṛtyantarād dvau bhāgau pitur draṣṭavyau || 28.30 ||
svayam ārjitam avaidyebhyo vaidyaḥ kāmaṃ na dadyāt
|| 28.31 ||
yat tu pitur dravyam anupaghnatā svayam upāttaṃ bhavati, vaidyagrahaṇād vā pratigrahādinā, tad itarebhyaḥ kāmam icchāto na dadyāt | yathāha manuḥ |
anupaghnan pitur dravyaṃ śrameṇa yad upārjayet |
svayam īhitalabdaṃ tan nākāmo datum arhati ||
vidyādhanaṃ tu yad yasya tat tasyaiva dhanaṃ bhavet || [MDh 9.206, 208]
ityādi || 28.31 ||
avaidyāḥ samaṃ vibhajeran || 28.32 ||
yat tu mūrkhāḥ kṛṣyādinopārjayeyuḥ tat samam eva vibhajeyuḥ | tathā cāha |
avaidyānāṃ tu sarveṣām īhātaś ced dhanaṃ bhavet |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syād apitrya iti dhāraṇā || iti | [MDh 9.205]
arthasiddham api caitad ucyate niyamārtham | tataś ca vaidya asamam api na dadāti avaidyopāttaṃ cet vaidyo ‘pi samam eveti || 28.32 ||
“putrā rikthaṃ bhajeran” [GDh 28.1] ity abhidhānād auraseṣv eva saṃpradāyo mā bhūd ity ata āha |
putrā aurasakṣetrajadattakṛttrimagūḍhotpannāpaviddhā rikthabhājaḥ || 28.33 ||
aurasaḥ svasya bhāryāyāṃ svayam utpādyate | tathāha manuḥ[76] |
svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāṃ tu svayam utpādayet tu yam |
tam aurasaṃ vijānīyāt putraṃ prathamakalpitam || iti | [MDh 9.166]
kṣetrajo yo niyogadvāreṇotpādyate | yathāha manuḥ |
yas talpajaḥ pramītasya klībasya vyādhitasya vā |
svadharmeṇa niyuktāyāṃ sa putraḥ kṣetrajaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.167]
datto yaḥ pratigrahenānyato labdhaḥ | yathāha manuḥ:
mātā pitā vā dadyātāṃ yam adbhiḥ putram āpadi |
sadṛśaṃ prītisaṃyuktaṃ sa jñeyo dattrimaḥ sutaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.168]
kṛtrimo yaḥ kriyate tvaṃ mama putra iti | yathāha manuḥ |
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuyād yaṃ guṇadoṣavivarjitam |
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ sa vijñeyas tu kṛtrimaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.169]
gūḍhotpanno nigūḍham utpāditaḥ | yatra bījī na jñāyate so ‘yaṃ bījina avijñānān mātṛjātīyaḥ | evaṃ sahoḍhakānīnāv api |
utpadyate gṛhe yasya na vijñāyeta kasya saḥ |
sa gṛhe gūḍha utpannaḥ tasya syād yasya talpajaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.170]
apaviddho yaḥ parityakto ‘nyena gṛhyate vijñānena | yathāha |
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā |
yaṃ putraṃ pratigṛhṇīyād apaviddhaḥ sa ucyate || iti | [MDh 9.171]
ete rikthabhājaḥ || 28.33 ||
kānīnasahoḍhapaunarbhavaputrikāputrasvayaṃdatta-krītāḥ gotrabhājaḥ || 28.34 ||
kānīnaḥ kanyāyām utpannaḥ, yaś ca tām udvahate tasyāsau bhavati | tathāha |
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayed rahaḥ |
kānīnaṃ taṃ vaden nāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavam || iti | [MDh 9. 172]
sahoḍhaḥ sagarbhāyām ūḍhāyām utpadyate | yathāha manuḥ |
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jātājñātāpi vā satī |
voḍhuḥ sa garbho bhavati sa sahoḍha ihocyate || iti | [MDh 9.173]
paunarbhavo vyabhicāriṇīputraḥ | yas tāṃ punaḥ saṃskṛtya tasyāṃ putram utpādayati, tasyāsau bhavati | yathāha manuḥ |
yā patyā saṃparityaktā vidhavā svecchayāpi vā |
utpādayet punar bhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate ||
sā ced akṣatayoniḥ syāt gatapratyāgatāpi vā |
paunarbhavena bhartrā sā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati || iti | [MDh 9.175–76]
putrikāputraḥ pūrvoktaḥ | svayaṃdatto yaḥ svayam evātmānaṃ pratipādayati | tathāha |
mātāpitṛvihīno yaḥ tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yasmai svayaṃdattas tu sa smṛtaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.177]
krītaḥ dhanena svīkṛtaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā || iti | [MDh 9.174]
ete gotrabhājaḥ udakadānādinā putrārthaṃ kurvantīty arthaḥ | evaṃ caite na rikthabhājaḥ, pūrve tu rikthabhājo gotrabhājaś ca | kuta etat? aurasena sahābhidhānāt smṛtyantarāc ca |
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś caiva dattaḥ kṛtrima eva ca |
gūḍhotpanno ‘paviddhaś ca dāyādā bāndhavāś ca ṣaṭ || iti | [MDh 9.159]
bāndhavā gotrabhāja ity arthaḥ | putratvād eva gotrabhāktve prāpte tadvacanam iha rikthabhāktvapratiṣedhārtham | aurasādisadbhāve ca teṣāṃ rikthabhāktvapratiṣedhād aurasādisadbhāve caite grāsācchādanamātram arhanti putratvād itareṣām | yathāha |
sarveṣām api tu nyāyyaṃ dātuṃ śaktyā manīṣiṇā |
grāsācchādanam atyantaṃ patito hy adadad bhavet || iti | [MDh 9.202]
tadabhāve tu bhavaty eva rikthabhāktvam, putrapratinidhitvāt | yathāha |
kṣetrajādīn sutān etān ekādaśa yathoditān |
putrapratinidhīn āhuḥ kriyālopān manīṣiṇaḥ || iti | [MDh 9.180]
pratinidhiś ca mukhyadravyābhāve samagraphala eva bhavati, somābhāve pūtīkavat | putrikāputrasyehābhidhānaṃ gotrabhāktvaprāpaṇārtham | rikthabhāktvaṃ tv aurasasadbhāve ‘pi bhavaty eva | yathāha manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nujāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāt jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ || iti | [MDh 9.134]
śūdrāputrasyehānabhidhānam anaurasatvād arikthabhāktvād vā | tathāha |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāputro na rikthabhāk |
yad evāsya pitā didyāt tad evāsya dhanaṃ bhavet || [MDh 9.155] || 28.34 ||
caturthāṃśino vaurasādyabhāve || 28.35 ||
ṣaṇṇām aurasādyānām abhāve kānīnādayaś caturthāṃśabhājaḥ, samastabhājo va, vāśabdāt | tathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “upārjitaṃ jyeṣṭhāya tadabhāve kṣetrajaputrikāputrayos tayor apy abhāve trayāṇām itareṣāṃ teṣām apy abhāve ṣaḍbhyaḥ” iti |
anye tu “aurasādyabhāve” ity etad uttarasūtrārtham āhuḥ | tataś ca kānīnādayaś caturbhāgārhā jīvanamātrasaṃbandhino veti || 28.35 ||
savarṇāputrāṇāṃ vibhāga uktaḥ | asavarṇāsutānāṃ tu viśeṣam āha |
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannas tulyabhāk || 28.36 ||
brāhmaṇasya rājanyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannaś ca yaḥ sa brāhmaṇīputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāk || 28.36 ||
jyeṣṭhāṃśahīnam anyat || 28.37 ||
jyeṣṭhāṃśo ya uddhāraḥ pūrvokto “viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya” [GDh 28.5]ityādinā tadvirahitam anyad dravyaṃ samaṃ labheta, uddhāro na bhavatīty arthaḥ | yady api dravyaparo nirdeśaḥ, tathāpi jyeṣṭhāṃśapratiṣedhamātram evātra vivakṣitam, ārambhasāmarthyāt | anyathā hi “guṇasaṃpanno jyeṣṭhabhāk” ity evaṃ vaktavyam āsīd iti guṇahīnaḥ nāsau tulyabhāk | tatra manūktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
caturo ‘ṃśān hared vipras trīn aṃśān kṣatriyāsutaḥ |
vaiśyāputro hared dvyaṃśam aṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || iti | [MDh 9.153]
śūdrāputrasya pratiṣedha ukta iti cet, ucyate – kṣatayonyām utpannasya pratiṣedhaḥ | akṣatayonyām utpannasya aṃśabhāktvam iti | paunarbhavādidvāreṇa kṣatayonyām api saṃbhavaty eveti | yeṣām aurasādyabhāva iti śeṣaḥ | te aurasādyabhāve tulyabhāktvam asya vyācakṣate | teṣu satsu smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “tryaṃśaṃ brāhmaṇyāḥ putro haret dvyaṃśaṃ rājanyāyāḥ putraḥ samam itare bhajeran” [VaDh 17.48–50] iti | vṛttāpekṣayā viṣayavikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ || 28.37 ||
rājanyāvaiśyāputrasamavāye yathā sa brāhmaṇīputreṇa
|| 28.38 ||
yadā vaiśyāputro guṇasaṃpanno jyeṣṭhaḥ, kṣatriyāsuto yavīyān, brāhmaṇīsutas tu nasty eva, tadā yathā sa brāhmaṇīputreṇa kṣatriyāputras tulyabhāk evaṃ kṣatriyāputreṇa vaiśyāputras tulyabhāk jyeṣṭhāṁśahīnaḥ | evaṃ ca kṣatriyāputrasya jyeṣṭhasya asmin viṣaye jyeṣṭhāṃśo ‘stīti jñāpitaṃ bhavati || 28.38 ||
kṣatriyāc cet || 28.39 ||
vaiśyāputro jyeṣṭho guṇasaṃpannas tulyabhāg iti vartate | kṣatriyād api vaiśyāputro jyeṣṭhaś cet kṣatriyāputreṇa yavīyasā tulyabhāg bhavatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ vaiśyāc chūdrāputrasya draṣṭavyam ity eke | tad ayuktaṃ manunoktatvāt || 28.39 ||
śūdrāputro ‘py anapatyasya śuśrūṣuś cel labheta vṛttimūlam antevāsividhinā || 28.40 ||
avidyamānadvijātiputrasya brāhmaṇasyāpi śūdrayonyām utpannaḥ yāvatā kṛṣyādikarmānuṣṭhānasamartho bhavati tāvanmātraṃ labheta sa yadi śiṣyavac chuśrūṣāparo bhavati | bhrātaraṃ tadbhāryāṃ ca gurum ivānuvartetety arthaḥ, antevādividheḥ śuśrūṣāviśeṣaṇatvāt | caturthabhāge tadapatye vṛttītyādi, kutaḥ? kṣatayonyā grahaṇād iti cet, garhārthāpiśabdahetor asyāṃśabhāktvaṃ ca || 28.40 ||
sarvarṇāputro ‘py anyāyavṛtto na labhetaikeṣām
|| 28.41 ||
samānavarṇāputo ‘pi, apiśabdāj jyeṣṭho ‘pi durvṛttaḥ patata iti yāvat | na labheta ity eke manyante | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ klībonmattapatitāś ca” [VaDh 17.52–53] iti | labhetety ācāryaḥ | śakyavineyatvāpekṣayā vikalpaḥ || 28.41 ||
śrotriyā brāhmaṇasyānapatyasya rikthaṃ bhajeran
|| 28.42 ||
śrotriyāḥ traividyavṛddhāḥ, na tu chandogamātrādhyāyinaḥ | tathāha manuḥ: “traividyāḥ śucayo dāntāḥ” [MDh 9.188] ityādi | anapatyasya avidyamānaputrasya dhanabhāja ity arthaḥ | rājñānujñātā rikthaṃ bhajeran, uttaratra rājagrahaṇāt || 28.42 ||
rājetareṣām || 28.43 ||
kṣatriyādīnāṃ rājaiva haret sarvābhāve || 28.43 ||
jaḍaklībau bhartavyau || 28.44 ||
jaḍaḥ upahatamanāḥ | klībaḥ tṛtīyāprakṛtiḥ | etāv aśanācchādanena bhartavyau | mūkādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam idam | tathāha manuḥ |
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā |
unmattajaḍamūkāś ca ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ || [MDh 9.201] iti || 28.44 ||
apatyaṃ jaḍasya bhāgārham || 28.45 ||
jaḍasya ya apatyaṃ tad bhāgayogyaṃ bhavati | arhagrahaṇād yadi yogyaṃ bhavati, na tu jaḍāpatyatayaiva | jaḍāpatyam iti vaktavye visamāsaḥ klībādīnāṃ kṣetrajāpatyaprāpaṇarthaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
yady arthitā tu dāraiḥ syāt klībādīnāṃ kathaṃcana |
teṣām utpannatantūnām apatyaṃ bhāgam arhati || iti | [MDh 9.203]
asmād eva liṅgāt teṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ saṃskārānumānam | anyathā sāvitrīpatitatve sati tadapatyasyātyayogyatvam eveti | kṛtaprāyaścittasya vā draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca yat pūrvam api patitasyāpy anujñātaṃ tad api kṛtaprāyaścittasya draṣṭavyam | evaṃ bruvann etat jñāpayati – vibhāgottarakālam api tad apatyasya bhāgo dātavya iti || 28.45 ||
śūdrāputravat pratilomāsu || 28.46 ||
pratilomāsu strīṣu jātānām api śuśrūṣūṇāṃ śūdrāputravat vṛttimūlaṃ dātavyam ity arthaḥ | tatra caṇḍālādīnāṃ śuśrūṣāsaṃbhavāt hīnataraṃ deyam iti draṣṭavyam | evaṃ pratilomānām apy ayam eva dharma iti draṣṭavyam || 28.46 ||
udakayogakṣemakṛtānnneṣv avibhāgaḥ || 28.47 ||
udakaṃ kūpādikam | yogaḥ iṣṭam, kṣemaḥ pūrtam | tathāha lokākṣiḥ |
kṣemaṃ pūrtaṃ yogam iṣṭam ity āhus tattvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye tu te prokte śayanaṃ tv annam eva ca || iti |
kṛtānne utsavārtham upakalpite | kṛtagrahaṇāt taṇḍulādau na doṣaḥ | eteṣu vibhāgo nāsti | tatra śayanavat taṇḍulādīny ekasyaiva dātavyānīti kecit | jyeṣṭhasya kaniṣṭhasya vecchanti | anyāni samudāya eva sthāpyānīti draṣṭavyam || 28.47 ||
strīṣu ca saṃyuktāsu || 28.48 ||
yāś ca striyo maithunopabhogārthena parigṛhītāḥ paralokavītabhayaiḥ pitṛbhiḥ | nanu ca doṣābhāvaḥ kasmān na gṛhyata iti cet, “pratilome vadhaḥ puṃsām” [GDh 28.46] ity anena prāyaścittaśravaṇāt | tāsu ca putrair na vibhāgaḥ | tāsāṃ vṛddhānām aśanācchādanaṃ dātavyam, anyābhir gantavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | cakārād anyad apy ekatvād aśakyam | yathāha manuḥ |
vastraṃ patram alaṃkāraṃ kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ |
yogakṣemaṃ pracāraṃ ca na vibhājyaṃ pracakṣate || [MDh 9.219]
iti || 28.48 ||
anājñāte daśāvaraiḥ śiṣṭair ūhavadbhir alubdaiḥ praśastaṃ kāryam || 28.49 ||
ājñātaṃ jñātaṃ tadviparītam anājñātam, tasmin yathāvad anavagate vā saṃdigdhe vā daśāvaraiḥ sarvālpair ity arthaḥ | śiṣṭair avagatavedārthaiḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
dharmeṇādhigato yais tu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ || iti | [MDh 12.109]
ūhavadbhir ūhāpohasamarthaiḥ mīmāṃsādiśāstrakṛtabuddhibhir ity arthaḥ | alubdhaiḥ vītatṛṣṇaiḥ praśastaṃ stutam, idam atra yuktam iti | tat kāryam anuṣṭheyam | tatsaṃnidhāne kartavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ || 28.49 ||
catvāraś caturṇāṃ pāragā vedānāṃ prāguttamāḥ traya āśramiṇaḥ pṛthag dharmavidas traya etān daśāvarān pariṣad ity ācakṣate || 28.50 ||
brāhmaṇā eva, dharmapravacane teṣām evādhikārāt | tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇo dharmān prabrūyāt” [VaDh 1.40] iti | catvāraḥ, naikaś caturvedo ‘pi | caturṇāṃ vedānāṃ pāragāḥ sarahasyam adhyetāraḥ vedānāṃ sāṅgānām | uttamāḥ prāk traya āśramiṇaḥ tṛtīyādhyāya uktāḥ | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ mā bhūd ity āśramagrahaṇam | ete ‘pi vedārthajñā eva parigṛhyante, ajñānām anadhikārāt | svadharmānuṣṭhānāpekṣayā tv evam uktam | pṛthagdharmaśāstrajñāḥ trayaḥ, naikaḥ sarvadharmajño ‘pi | te ca vedavittve saty āśramitve ca tātparyam aṅgīkṛtya pṛthag ucyante | etān daśāvarān pariṣad iti śiṣṭā ācakṣate | etad api smṛtyantareṇa sahaikavākyatvārtham | tad apy eṣām asaṃbhave tryavarādīnām upasaṃgrahārtham | tathā ca manuḥ |
daśāvarā vā pariṣad yaṃ dharmaṃ parikalpayet |
tryavarā vāpi vṛttasthāḥ taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || iti | [MDh 12.110]
tathā vasiṣṭho ‘py āha |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur vedapāragāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netareṣāṃ sahasraśaḥ || iti | [VaDh 3.7]
tatra bahutvāsaṃbhave svalpaṃ grāhyam iti draṣṭavyam || 28.50 ||
asaṃbhave tv eteṣāṃ śrotriyo vedavic chiṣṭo vipratipattau yad āha || 28.51 ||
tat kāryam iti śeṣaḥ | tuśabdo ‘vadhāraṇe | asaṃbhava eva eteṣām, na yathāsaṃbhavaṃ katipayānām apy asaṃbhava iti | aśrotriyo vety abhāvavikalpena vāśabdenaiva siddhau evam upadeśaḥ pūrvoktānāṃ sarveṣām asaṃbhave, na daśāvarāsaṃbhavamātra iti | śrotriyaḥ samastavedapāragaḥ, na smṛtyantaroktamantrabrāhmaṇādhyāyī bahupāṭhasyābhīṣṭatvāt | paricchede vedavit tadarthajñaḥ | vināpi ca granthaprakarṣeṇa tadarthajñānasya darśanāc chrotriyagrahaṇam | śiṣṭaḥ svadharmāvasthitaḥ | vipratipattau abhiprāyabhede eko ‘pi pramāṇam
|| 28.51 ||
atra kāraṇam āha |
yato yamaprabhavau bhūtānām || 28.52 ||
yasmād vedāt, yamaprabhavau, yamaśabdena saṃsāranivṛttir ucyate, prabhavaśabdena tasyaiva pravṛttiḥ, ubhayasādhanasya ca vedād adhigamaḥ | tena vedaḥ[77] pramāṇam vipratipattau | yathāha manuḥ |
cāturvarṇyaṃ trayo lokāś catvāraś cāśramāḥ pṛthak |
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyac ca sarvaṃ vedāt prasiddhim ait || [MDh 12.97]
ādau ca yamagrahaṇaṃ saṃsāranivṛttiḥ paramapuruṣārtha iti jñāpanārtham || 28.52 ||
kiṃ ca |
hiṃsānugrahayogeṣu || 28.53 ||
veda eva pramāṇam iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | hiṃsāsaṃbandhe — ete paśvādayo yajñe hiṃsyā ity atra, anugrahayoge — sarve brāhmaṇādayo 'nugrāhyā ity atrāpi veda eva pramāṇam | taddvāreṇa ca vedavid ity abhiprāyaḥ || 28.54 ||
sāṃprathaṃ dharmavidhaḥ phalam āha |
dharmināṃ viśeṣeṇa svargaṃ lokaṃ dharmavid āpnoti jñānābhiniveśābhyām || 28.54 ||
dharmī[78] kevaladharmī | viśeṣeṇa ā pralayād ity abhyprāyaḥ | dharmavit jñātvānuṣṭhāyī | kutaḥ? jñānagrahaṇāt | abhiniveśaḥ tadanuṣṭhānam | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “dhārmikaḥ” [VaDh] ityādi || 28.54 ||
iti dharmo dharmaḥ || 28.55 ||
“vedo dharmamūlam” (GDh 1.1) ity ārabhya etatpartyantaṃ dharmasaṃjñā gautamasyeti vākyaśeṣaḥ || 28.55 ||
iti purāṇamaskarīye gautamabhāṣye aṣṭāviṃśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
samāptaṃ cedaṃ gautamīyaṃ nibandhanaṃ
maskarīyaṃ bhāṣyam
